DFHack plugin overlay state/action fields: installed Lua source extraction

This material records readable installed Lua source, not Lua bytecode decompilation.
Native plugin getters/setters require separate binary decompilation evidence.
No executable Lua was loaded, no game window was accessed, and this is not a
test, regression, smoke test, check report, or observation of the final game UI.

Source root: D:/program/steam/steamapps/common/DFHack/hack/lua/plugins/
plugin-fields.tsv records constructor-level fields and their literal expressions.
Escaped \n in the TSV preserves original expression newlines. Expressions and
dynamic captions remain expressions; literal_texts is an auxiliary field and
does not claim to resolve user-supplied names or runtime option generators.
source_lines are one-based source lines. The numbered original source sections
below preserve callbacks, helper functions, dynamically generated choices,
parent visibility gates, and all conditional branches from the selected modules.

Scope:
registered-overlay = widget constructed in a registered plugin OverlayWidget;
registered-overlay-embedded-helper = helper explicitly embedded in such overlay;
unregistered-overlay-template = source overlay template with no active registration;
popup-gui-or-helper = companion large GUI/modal/helper, recorded separately.
Static companion labels and symbols are included so a state branch is not
translated without its panel heading and conditional action captions.
This is a plugin-family inventory across possible game contexts, not an assertion
that all panels share a fixed coordinate. Default positions and context filters
are source metadata; user configuration can move overlays.
An overlay class is expanded to its registered descendants when inherited
controls apply to both (for example JusticeOverlay -> interrogation/conviction).
sort BurrowAssignmentOverlay is presently commented out of OVERLAY_WIDGETS and
is therefore kept as an unregistered template, not claimed as an active overlay.

Shared rendering:
ToggleHotkeyLabel defaults to On=true/COLOR_GREEN and Off=false unless a
constructor supplies options. CycleHotkeyLabel displays every option label,
not only the first or the screenshot state. key and key_back are dynamic
interface-key displays; default key_sep is ': ', option_gap=1. Hover normally
changes pens only; option captions are still obtained from getOptionLabel().
HelpButton '?' invokes another gui/launcher command-help screen; DFHack frame
signature is a brand. These symbols and user-provided names preserve their form.

Semantic branch families:
logistics.autoretrain: fixed Autoretrain livestock: plus On/Off from native feature.
suspendmanager.toggle: Suspendmanager is plus Enabled/Disabled from isEnabled().
suspendmanager.status: Not suspended. versus Suspended because: <native description>.
dig warm/damp toolbar: four truth combinations of warm and damp flags select icon
pens/tiles; hover exposes instructions; the opened config panel has Damp dig:,
Warm dig:, inherited On/Off plus current-level mark/unmark action captions.
spectate.followpanel: normal Label concatenates ' spectate:' with ' on '/'off ';
click chooses disable/enable from native isEnabled(), not ToggleHotkeyLabel.
stockpiles.overlay: enabled and disabled Melting/Trading/Dumping/Training use
identical words with different pens; Forbid(0)/Forbid(1)/Claim(2) is three-state,
all driven by logistics_getStockpileConfigs. Minimize/restore and configuration
modal are separate presentation/action paths.
stockflow.stockflow: Order: + selected job or No job selected; Trigger: Never
fallback and every named trigger from the source triggers table. Trigger changes
are Lua persistent stockpile state, while isEnabled gates overlay visibility.
stocks.overlay: all_collapsed() chooses expand all versus collapse all; companion
expand all/remove empties/Shift+Scroll/: fast scroll are retained.
burrow.designation: Flood fill on double click: Off/2D fill enabled/3D fill enabled.
buildingplan.planner: Hollow area No/Yes; Engraved only/Empty only/Do now On/Off;
Show favorites/Hide favorites; Choose items With filters/Last used (<material or
pick manually>)/Manually; Building safety Any/Magma/Fire; stair subtype choices;
weapon quantity 1..10; Set filter/Edit filter selected by hasFilter; minimize/
restore captions; dynamic favorite slot names remain user-provided names.
buildingplan.mechanisms: source/lever and target safety Any/Magma/Fire and
dynamic next-mechanism action captions; mechanism_unlink mode includes every
Don't auto free/Free local mechanism/Free target mechanism/Free local and target.
buildingplan filter popup: quality/material and global settings choices are in
popup-gui-or-helper scope, including all native-setting On/Off combinations.
preserve-rooms.reserved: unreserved track-roles branch shows Autoassign to holder
of role: with None and generated role names; reserved branch shows reservation
name and Clear reservation; hover/list/pause masking are source-defined branches.
sort overlays: squad/work-animal filters include all cycles, not merely bools;
sort.interrogation Interviewed: Include/Exclude; sort.location_selector Hide
established: On/Off; sort.slab Show only citizens who need a slab: On/Off. Names
from dfhack.military.getSquadName(), named burrows and other user data remain
their source names. Squad aliases must not be translated or inferred by spelling.
zone.animal_actions: Butcher/Geld/Adopt/Has Trainer each No/Yes; valid-unit panel
visibility and pet/geldable/adoption/tamable enabled gates restrict interactions
but do not add another option. Slaughter excludes geld/adoption. AssignAnimal
large GUI filter cycles are retained separately in popup-gui-or-helper scope.
Other selected module widgets are included as conditional/action companions.

Native state/function follow-up (not claimed decompiled here):
logistics: logistics_getFeature, logistics_setFeature,
           logistics_getStockpileConfigs, logistics_setStockpileConfig.
dig: getDampPaintEnabled, getWarmPaintEnabled, setDampPaintEnabled,
     setWarmPaintEnabled, getCurLevelDesignatedCount,
     toggleCurLevelDampDig, toggleCurLevelWarmDig.
suspendmanager: isEnabled, suspendmanager_suspensionDescription.
spectate: isEnabled (state branch); spectate_followPrev/spectate_followNext actions.
buildingplan: getSpecials/setSpecial; getChooseItems/setChooseItems;
              getHeatSafetyFilter/setHeatSafetyFilter; hasFilter;
              getGlobalSettings/setSetting; getMaterialFilter/getMaterialMaskFilter;
              getAvailableItemsByHeat (mechanism availability captions).
buildingplan.getDoNow/setDoNow are readable Lua wrappers over job.flags.do_now;
they are not independent native getters despite their plugin namespace.
preserve-rooms: preserve_rooms_isReserved, preserve_rooms_getFeature,
                preserve_rooms_getRoleAssignment, preserve_rooms_getReservationName,
                reservation/role-assignment setters used by source callbacks.
stockflow: isEnabled only for overlay visibility; trigger names/settings are Lua.
zone state reads use native dfhack.units.isMarkedForSlaughter,
isMarkedForGelding, isAvailableForAdoption, isMarkedForTraining and related
unit flags/eligibility helpers in dfhack.dll rather than a zone-plugin feature getter.
sort/stocks/burrow filter/selection values are predominantly Lua state or DF
structures; native unit predicates filter choices, not an additional plugin On/Off
string branch.

Readable source sections follow. They are complete selected module contents,
so callbacks and dynamic option generation omitted from a single constructor
are retained without executing them.

13: local function get_cur_filters()
14:     return dfhack.buildings.getFiltersByType({}, uibs.building_type,
15:             uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type)
16: end

34: -- returns whether the items matched by the specified filter can have a quality
35: -- rating. This also conveniently indicates whether an item can be decorated.
36: local function can_be_improved(idx)
37:     local filter = get_cur_filters()[idx]
38:     if filter.flags2 and filter.flags2.building_material then
39:         return false;
40:     end
41:     return filter.item_type ~= df.item_type.WOOD and
42:             filter.item_type ~= df.item_type.BLOCKS and
43:             filter.item_type ~= df.item_type.BAR and
44:             filter.item_type ~= df.item_type.BOULDER
45: end
46: 
47: local function mat_sort_by_name(a, b)
48:     return a.name < b.name
49: end
50: 
51: local function mat_sort_by_quantity(a, b)
52:     return a.quantity > b.quantity or
53:             (a.quantity == b.quantity and mat_sort_by_name(a, b))
54: end
55:

256: local MAT_ENABLED_PEN = to_pen{ch=string.char(251), fg=COLOR_LIGHTGREEN}
257: local MAT_DISABLED_PEN = to_pen{ch='x', fg=COLOR_RED}
258: 
259: local function make_cat_choice(label, cat, key, cats)
260:     local enabled = cats[cat]
261:     local icon = nil
262:     if not cats.unset then
263:         icon = enabled and MAT_ENABLED_PEN or MAT_DISABLED_PEN
264:     end
265:     return {
266:         text=label,
267:         key=key,
268:         enabled=enabled,
269:         cat=cat,
270:         icon=icon,
271:     }
272: end
273: 
274: local function make_mat_choice(name, props, enabled, cats)
275:     local quantity = tonumber(props.count)
276:     local text = ('%5d - %s'):format(quantity, name)
277:     local icon = nil
278:     if not cats.unset then
279:         icon = enabled and MAT_ENABLED_PEN or MAT_DISABLED_PEN
280:     end
281:     return {
282:         text=text,
283:         enabled=enabled,
284:         icon=icon,
285:         name=name,
286:         cat=props.category,
287:         quantity=quantity,
288:     }
289: end
290: 
291: function QualityAndMaterialsPage:refresh()
292:     local summary = self.desc
293:     local subviews = self.subviews
294: 
295:     local buildingplan = require('plugins.buildingplan')
296: 
297:     local heat = buildingplan.getHeatSafetyFilter(uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type)
298:     if heat >= 2 then summary = 'Magma safe ' .. summary
299:     elseif heat == 1 then summary = 'Fire safe ' .. summary
300:     else summary = 'Any ' .. summary
301:     end
302: 
303:     local specials = buildingplan.getSpecials(uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type)
304:     if next(specials) then
305:         local specials_list = {}
306:         for special in pairs(specials) do
307:             table.insert(specials_list, special)
308:         end
309:         summary = summary .. ' [' .. table.concat(specials_list, ', ') .. ']'
310:     end
311: 
312:     local quality = buildingplan.getQualityFilter(uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, self.index-1)
313:     subviews.decorated:setOption(quality.decorated ~= 0)
314:     subviews.min_quality:setOption(quality.min_quality)
315:     subviews.max_quality:setOption(quality.max_quality)
316: 
317:     local cats = buildingplan.getMaterialMaskFilter(uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, self.index-1)
318:     local category_choices={
319:         make_cat_choice('Stone', 'stone', 'CUSTOM_SHIFT_S', cats),
320:         make_cat_choice('Wood', 'wood', 'CUSTOM_SHIFT_O', cats),
321:         make_cat_choice('Metal', 'metal', 'CUSTOM_SHIFT_M', cats),
322:         make_cat_choice('Glass', 'glass', 'CUSTOM_SHIFT_G', cats),
323:         make_cat_choice('Gem', 'gem', 'CUSTOM_SHIFT_E', cats),
324:         make_cat_choice('Clay', 'clay', 'CUSTOM_SHIFT_C', cats),
325:         make_cat_choice('Cloth', 'cloth', 'CUSTOM_SHIFT_L', cats),
326:         make_cat_choice('Silk', 'silk', 'CUSTOM_SHIFT_K', cats),
327:         make_cat_choice('Yarn', 'yarn', 'CUSTOM_SHIFT_Y', cats),
328:     }
329:     self.subviews.materials_categories:setChoices(category_choices)
330: 
331:     local mats = buildingplan.getMaterialFilter(uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, self.index-1)
332:     local mat_choices = {}
333:     local hide_zero = self.subviews.hide_zero:getOptionValue()
334:     local enabled_mat_names = {}
335:     for name,props in pairs(mats) do
336:         local enabled = props.enabled == 'true' and cats[props.category]
337:         if not cats.unset and enabled then
338:             table.insert(enabled_mat_names, name)
339:         end
340:         if not hide_zero or tonumber(props.count) > 0 then
341:             table.insert(mat_choices, make_mat_choice(name, props, enabled, cats))
342:         end
343:     end
344:     table.sort(mat_choices, self.subviews.mat_sort:getOptionValue())
345: 
346:     local prev_filter = self.subviews.search.text
347:     self.subviews.materials_mats:setChoices(mat_choices)
348:     self.subviews.materials_mats:setFilter(prev_filter)
349: 
350:     if #enabled_mat_names > 0 then
351:         table.sort(enabled_mat_names)
352:         summary = summary .. (' of %s'):format(table.concat(enabled_mat_names, ', '))
353:     end
354: 
355:     self.summary = summary
356:     self.dirty = false
357:     self:updateLayout()
358: end
359: 
360: function QualityAndMaterialsPage:toggle_category(_, choice)
361:     local cats = {}
362:     if not choice.icon then
363:         -- toggling from unset to something is set
364:         table.insert(cats, choice.cat)
365:     else
366:         choice.enabled = not choice.enabled
367:         for _,c in ipairs(self.subviews.materials_categories:getChoices()) do
368:             if c.enabled then
369:                 table.insert(cats, c.cat)
370:             end
371:         end
372:     end
373:     require('plugins.buildingplan').setMaterialMaskFilter(uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, self.index-1, cats)
374:     self.dirty = true
375: end
376: 
377: function QualityAndMaterialsPage:toggle_material(_, choice)
378:     local mats = {}
379:     if not choice.icon then
380:         -- toggling from unset to something is set
381:         table.insert(mats, choice.name)
382:     else
383:         for _,c in ipairs(self.subviews.materials_mats:getChoices()) do
384:             local enabled = c.enabled
385:             if choice.name == c.name then
386:                 enabled = not c.enabled
387:             end
388:             if enabled then
389:                 table.insert(mats, c.name)
390:             end
391:         end
392:     end
393:     require('plugins.buildingplan').setMaterialFilter(uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, self.index-1, mats)
394:     self.dirty = true
395: end
396: 
397: function QualityAndMaterialsPage:invert_materials()
398:     local mats = {}
399:     for _,c in ipairs(self.subviews.materials_mats:getChoices()) do
400:         if not c.icon then return end
401:         if not c.enabled then
402:             table.insert(mats, c.name)
403:         end
404:     end
405:     require('plugins.buildingplan').setMaterialFilter(uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, self.index-1, mats)
406:     self.dirty = true
407: end
408: 
409: function QualityAndMaterialsPage:clear_filter()
410:     require('plugins.buildingplan').clearFilter(uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, self.index-1)
411:     self.dirty = true
412: end
413: 
414: function QualityAndMaterialsPage:set_decorated(decorated)
415:     local subviews = self.subviews
416:     require('plugins.buildingplan').setQualityFilter(uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, self.index-1,
417:             decorated and 1 or 0, subviews.min_quality:getOptionValue(), subviews.max_quality:getOptionValue())
418:     self.dirty = true
419: end
420: 
421: function QualityAndMaterialsPage:set_min_quality(idx)
422:     idx = math.min(6, math.max(0, idx-1))
423:     local subviews = self.subviews
424:     subviews.min_quality:setOption(idx)
425:     if subviews.max_quality:getOptionValue() < idx then
426:         subviews.max_quality:setOption(idx)
427:     end
428:     require('plugins.buildingplan').setQualityFilter(uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, self.index-1,
429:             subviews.decorated:getOptionValue() and 1 or 0, idx, subviews.max_quality:getOptionValue())
430:     self.dirty = true
431: end
432: 
433: function QualityAndMaterialsPage:set_max_quality(idx)
434:     idx = math.min(6, math.max(0, idx-1))
435:     local subviews = self.subviews
436:     subviews.max_quality:setOption(idx)
437:     if subviews.min_quality:getOptionValue() > idx then
438:         subviews.min_quality:setOption(idx)
439:     end
440:     require('plugins.buildingplan').setQualityFilter(uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, self.index-1,
441:             subviews.decorated:getOptionValue() and 1 or 0, subviews.min_quality:getOptionValue(), idx)
442:     self.dirty = true
443: end
444: 
445: function QualityAndMaterialsPage:onRenderFrame(dc, rect)
446:     QualityAndMaterialsPage.super.onRenderFrame(self, dc, rect)
447:     if self.dirty then

102:     self.subviews.item2:reset()
103:     self.subviews.item3:reset()
104:     self.subviews.item4:reset()
105:     reset_inspector_flag = false
106: end

112: 
113: local RESUME_BUTTON_FRAME = {t=15, h=3, r=73, w=25}
114: 
115: local function mouse_is_over_resume_button(rect)
116:     local x,y = dfhack.screen.getMousePos()
117:     if not x then return false end
118:     if y < RESUME_BUTTON_FRAME.t or y > RESUME_BUTTON_FRAME.t + RESUME_BUTTON_FRAME.h - 1 then
119:         return false
120:     end
121:     if x > rect.x2 - RESUME_BUTTON_FRAME.r + 1 or x < rect.x2 - RESUME_BUTTON_FRAME.r - RESUME_BUTTON_FRAME.w + 2 then
122:         return false
123:     end
124:     return true
125: end
126: 
127: function InspectorOverlay:onInput(keys)
128:     if not require('plugins.buildingplan').isPlannedBuilding(dfhack.gui.getSelectedBuilding(true)) then
129:         return false
130:     end
131:     if keys._MOUSE_L and mouse_is_over_resume_button(self.frame_parent_rect) then
132:         return true
133:     elseif keys._MOUSE_L or keys._MOUSE_R or keys.LEAVESCREEN then
134:         self:reset()
135:     end
136:     return InspectorOverlay.super.onInput(self, keys)
137: end
138: 
139: function InspectorOverlay:render(dc)
140:     if not require('plugins.buildingplan').isPlannedBuilding(dfhack.gui.getSelectedBuilding(true)) then
141:         return
142:     end
143:     if reset_inspector_flag then
144:         self:reset()
145:     end
146:     InspectorOverlay.super.render(self, dc)
147: end
148: 
149: return _ENV

SOURCE D:/program/steam/steamapps/common/DFHack/hack/lua/plugins/buildingplan/itemselection.lua
1: local _ENV = mkmodule('plugins.buildingplan.itemselection')
2: 
3: local gui = require('gui')
4: local pens = require('plugins.buildingplan.pens')
5: local utils = require('utils')
6: local widgets = require('gui.widgets')
7: local caravan = reqscript('internal/caravan/common')
8: 
9: local uibs = df.global.buildreq
10: local to_pen = dfhack.pen.parse
11: 
12: local BUILD_TEXT_PEN = to_pen{fg=COLOR_BLACK, bg=COLOR_GREEN, keep_lower=true}
13: local BUILD_TEXT_HPEN = to_pen{fg=COLOR_WHITE, bg=COLOR_GREEN, keep_lower=true}
14: 
15: -- map of building type -> {set=set of recently used, list=list of recently used}
16: -- most recent entries are at the *end* of the list
17: local recently_used = {}
18: 
19: function get_automaterial_selection(building_type)
20:     local tracker = recently_used[building_type]
21:     if not tracker or not tracker.list then return end
22:     return tracker.list[#tracker.list]
23: end
24: 
25: function get_item_description(item_id, item, safety_label)
26:     item = item or df.item.find(item_id)
27:     if not item then
28:         return ('No %s safe mechanisms available'):format(safety_label:lower())
29:     end
30:     return dfhack.items.getReadableDescription(item)
31: end
32: 
33: local function sort_by_type(a, b)
34:     local ad, bd = a.data, b.data
35:     return ad.item_type < bd.item_type or
36:             (ad.item_type == bd.item_type and ad.item_subtype < bd.item_subtype) or
37:             (ad.item_type == bd.item_type and ad.item_subtype == bd.item_subtype and a.search_key < b.search_key) or
38:             (ad.item_type == bd.item_type and ad.item_subtype == bd.item_subtype and a.search_key == b.search_key and ad.quality > bd.quality)
39: end
40: 
41: local function sort_by_recency(a, b)
42:     local tracker = recently_used[uibs.building_type]
43:     if not tracker then return sort_by_type(a, b) end
44:     local recent_a, recent_b = tracker.set[a.search_key], tracker.set[b.search_key]
45:     -- if they're both in the set, return the one with the greater index,
46:     -- indicating more recent
47:     if recent_a and recent_b then return recent_a > recent_b end
48:     if recent_a and not recent_b then return true end
49:     if not recent_a and recent_b then return false end
50:     return sort_by_type(a, b)
51: end
52: 
53: local function sort_by_name(a, b)
54:     return a.search_key < b.search_key or
55:             (a.search_key == b.search_key and sort_by_type(a, b))
56: end
57: 
58: local function sort_by_quantity(a, b)
59:     local ad, bd = a.data, b.data
60:     return ad.quantity > bd.quantity or
61:             (ad.quantity == bd.quantity and sort_by_type(a, b))
62: end
63: 
64: local function sort_by_value(a, b)
65:     local ad, bd = a.data, b.data
66:     return ad.value > bd.value or
67:             (ad.value == bd.value and sort_by_type(a, b))
68: end
69: 
70: ItemSelection = defclass(ItemSelection, widgets.Window)
71: ItemSelection.ATTRS{
72:     frame_title='Choose items',
73:     frame={w=56, h=24, l=4, t=7},
74:     resizable=true,
75:     get_available_items_fn=DEFAULT_NIL,
76:     desc=DEFAULT_NIL,
77:     quantity=DEFAULT_NIL,
78:     autoselect=DEFAULT_NIL,
79:     on_submit=DEFAULT_NIL,
80:     on_cancel=DEFAULT_NIL,
81: }
82: 
83: function ItemSelection:init()
84:     self.num_selected = 0
85:     self.selected_set = {}
86:     local plural = self.quantity == 1 and '' or 's'
87: 
88:     local choices = self:get_choices(sort_by_recency)
89: 
90:     if self.autoselect then
91:         self:do_autoselect(choices)
92:         if self.num_selected >= self.quantity then
93:             self:submit(choices)
94:             return
95:         end
96:     end
97: 
98:     self:addviews{
99:         widgets.Panel{
100:             view_id='header',
101:             frame={t=0, h=3},
102:             subviews={
103:                 widgets.Label{
104:                     frame={t=0, l=0, r=16},
105:                     text={
106:                         self.desc, plural, NEWLINE,
107:                         ('Select up to %d item%s ('):format(self.quantity, plural),
108:                         {text=function() return self.num_selected end},
109:                         ' selected)',
110:                     },
111:                 },
112:                 widgets.Label{
113:                     frame={r=0, w=15, t=0, h=3},
114:                     text=widgets.makeButtonLabelText{
115:                         chars={
116:                             '               ',
117:                             '   Autoselect  ',
118:                             '               ',
119:                         },
120:                         pens=BUILD_TEXT_PEN,
121:                         pens_hover=BUILD_TEXT_HPEN,
122:                     },
123:                     on_click=self:callback('submit'),
124:                     visible=function() return self.num_selected < self.quantity end,
125:                 },
126:                 widgets.Label{
127:                     frame={r=0, w=15, t=0, h=3},
128:                     text=widgets.makeButtonLabelText{
129:                         chars={
130:                             '               ',
131:                             '    Continue   ',
132:                             '               ',
133:                         },
134:                         pens=BUILD_TEXT_PEN,
135:                         pens_hover=BUILD_TEXT_HPEN,
136:                     },
137:                     on_click=self:callback('submit'),
138:                     visible=function() return self.num_selected >= self.quantity end,
139:                 },
140:             },
141:         },
142:     }
143: 
144:     self:addviews{
145:         widgets.Panel{
146:             view_id='body',
147:             frame={t=self.subviews.header.frame.h, b=4},
148:             subviews={
149:                 widgets.EditField{
150:                     view_id='search',
151:                     frame={l=1, t=0},
152:                     label_text='Search: ',
153:                     on_char=function(ch) return ch:match('[%l -]') end,
154:                 },
155:                 widgets.CycleHotkeyLabel{
156:                     frame={l=1, t=2},
157:                     key='CUSTOM_SHIFT_R',
158:                     label='Sort by:',
159:                     options={
160:                         {label='Recently used', value=sort_by_recency},
161:                         {label='Amount', value=sort_by_quantity},
162:                         {label='Value', value=sort_by_value},
163:                         {label='Name', value=sort_by_name},
164:                     },
165:                     on_change=self:callback('on_sort'),
166:                 },
167:                 widgets.Panel{
168:                     frame={l=0, t=3, r=0, b=0},
169:                     frame_style=gui.FRAME_INTERIOR,
170:                     subviews={
171:                         widgets.FilteredList{
172:                             view_id='flist',
173:                             frame={t=0, b=0},
174:                             choices=choices,
175:                             icon_width=2,
176:                             on_submit=self:callback('toggle_group'),
177:                         },
178:                     },
179:                 },
180:             },
181:         },
182:         widgets.Panel{
183:             view_id='footer',
184:             frame={l=1, r=1, b=0, h=3},
185:             subviews={
186:                 widgets.HotkeyLabel{
187:                     frame={l=0, h=1, t=0},
188:                     key='KEYBOARD_CURSOR_RIGHT_FAST',
189:                     key_sep='----: ', -- these hypens function as "padding" to be overwritten by the next Label
190:                     label='Use one',
191:                     auto_width=true,
192:                     on_activate=function() self:increment_group(self.subviews.flist.list:getSelected()) end,
193:                 },
194:                 widgets.Label{
195:                     frame={l=6, w=5, t=0},
196:                     text_pen=COLOR_LIGHTGREEN,
197:                     text='Right', -- this overrides the "6----" characters from the previous HotkeyLabel
198:                 },
199:                 widgets.HotkeyLabel{
200:                     frame={l=1, h=1, t=1},
201:                     key='KEYBOARD_CURSOR_LEFT_FAST',
202:                     key_sep='---: ', -- these hypens function as "padding" to be overwritten by the next Label
203:                     label='Use one fewer',
204:                     auto_width=true,
205:                     on_activate=function() self:decrement_group(self.subviews.flist.list:getSelected()) end,
206:                 },
207:                 widgets.Label{
208:                     frame={l=7, w=4, t=1},
209:                     text_pen=COLOR_LIGHTGREEN,
210:                     text='Left', -- this overrides the "4---" characters from the previous HotkeyLabel
211:                 },
212:                 widgets.HotkeyLabel{
213:                     frame={l=6, t=2, h=2},
214:                     key='SELECT',
215:                     label='Use all/none',
216:                     auto_width=true,
217:                     on_activate=function() self:toggle_group(self.subviews.flist.list:getSelected()) end,
218:                 },
219:                 widgets.HotkeyLabel{
220:                     frame={r=5, t=0},
221:                     key='LEAVESCREEN',
222:                     label='Go back',
223:                     auto_width=true,
224:                     on_activate=self:callback('on_cancel'),
225:                 },
226:                 widgets.HotkeyLabel{
227:                     frame={r=4, t=2},
228:                     key='CUSTOM_SHIFT_C',
229:                     label='Continue',
230:                     auto_width=true,
231:                     on_activate=self:callback('submit'),
232:                 },
233:             },
234:         },
235:     }
236: 
237:     self.subviews.flist.list.frame.t = 0
238:     self.subviews.flist.edit.visible = false
239:     self.subviews.flist.edit = self.subviews.search
240:     self.subviews.search.on_change = self.subviews.flist:callback('onFilterChange')
241: end
242: 
243: -- resort and restore selection
244: function ItemSelection:on_sort(sort_fn)
245:     local flist = self.subviews.flist
246:     local saved_filter = flist:getFilter()
247:     flist:setFilter('')
248:     flist:setChoices(self:get_choices(sort_fn), flist:getSelected())
249:     flist:setFilter(saved_filter)
250: end
251: 
252: local function make_search_key(str)
253:     local out = ''
254:     for c in str:gmatch("[%w%s]") do
255:         out = out .. c
256:     end
257:     return out
258: end
259: 
260: function ItemSelection:get_choices(sort_fn)
261:     local item_ids = self.get_available_items_fn()
262:     local buckets = {}
263:     for _,item_id in ipairs(item_ids) do
264:         local item = df.item.find(item_id)
265:         if not item then goto continue end
266:         local desc = get_item_description(item_id, item)
267:         local value = dfhack.items.getValue(item)
268:         local key = desc .. "_" .. tostring(value)
269:         if buckets[key] then
270:             local bucket = buckets[key]
271:             table.insert(bucket.data.item_ids, item_id)
272:             bucket.data.quantity = bucket.data.quantity + 1
273:         else
274:             local entry = {
275:                 search_key=make_search_key(desc),
276:                 icon=self:callback('get_entry_icon', item_id),
277:                 data={
278:                     desc=desc,
279:                     item_ids={item_id},
280:                     item_type=item:getType(),
281:                     item_subtype=item:getSubtype(),
282:                     quantity=1,
283:                     quality=item:getQuality(),
284:                     value=value,
285:                     selected=0,
286:                 },
287:             }
288:             buckets[key] = entry
289:         end
290:         ::continue::
291:     end
292:     local choices = {}
293:     for key,choice in pairs(buckets) do
294:         local data = choice.data
295:         local obfuscated_value = caravan.obfuscate_value(data.value)
296:         choice.text = {
297:             {width=8, text=function() return ('%d/%d'):format(data.selected, data.quantity) end},
298:             {width=9, gap=2, text=function() return ('%s%s'):format(obfuscated_value, caravan.CH_MONEY) end},
299:             {gap=2, text=data.desc},
300:         }
301:         table.insert(choices, choice)
302:     end
303:     table.sort(choices, sort_fn)
304:     return choices
305: end
306: 
307: function ItemSelection:do_autoselect(choices)
308:     if #choices == 0 then return end
309:     local desired = get_automaterial_selection(uibs.building_type)
310:     if choices[1].search_key ~= desired then return end
311:     self:toggle_group(1, choices[1])
312: end
313: 
314: function ItemSelection:increment_group(idx, choice)
315:     local data = choice.data
316:     if self.quantity <= self.num_selected then return false end
317:     if data.selected >= data.quantity then return false end
318:     data.selected = data.selected + 1
319:     self.num_selected = self.num_selected + 1
320:     local item_id = data.item_ids[data.selected]
321:     self.selected_set[item_id] = true
322:     return true
323: end
324: 
325: function ItemSelection:decrement_group(idx, choice)
326:     local data = choice.data
327:     if data.selected <= 0 then return false end
328:     local item_id = data.item_ids[data.selected]
329:     self.selected_set[item_id] = nil
330:     self.num_selected = self.num_selected - 1
331:     data.selected = data.selected - 1
332:     return true
333: end
334: 
335: function ItemSelection:toggle_group(idx, choice)
336:     local data = choice.data
337:     if data.selected > 0 then
338:         while self:decrement_group(idx, choice) do end
339:     else
340:         while self:increment_group(idx, choice) do end
341:     end
342: end
343: 
344: function ItemSelection:get_entry_icon(item_id)
345:     return self.selected_set[item_id] and pens.SELECTED_ITEM_PEN or nil
346: end
347: 
348: local function track_recently_used(choices)
349:     -- use same set for all subtypes
350:     local tracker = ensure_key(recently_used, uibs.building_type)
351:     for _,choice in ipairs(choices) do
352:         local data = choice.data
353:         if data.selected <= 0 then goto continue end
354:         local key = choice.search_key
355:         local recent_set = ensure_key(tracker, 'set')
356:         local recent_list = ensure_key(tracker, 'list')
357:         if recent_set[key] then
358:             if recent_list[#recent_list] ~= key then
359:                 for i,v in ipairs(recent_list) do
360:                     if v == key then
361:                         table.remove(recent_list, i)
362:                         table.insert(recent_list, key)
363:                         break
364:                     end
365:                 end
366:                 tracker.set = utils.invert(recent_list)
367:             end
368:         else
369:             -- only keep most recent 10
370:             if #recent_list >= 10 then
371:                 -- remove least recently used from list and set
372:                 recent_set[table.remove(recent_list, 1)] = nil
373:             end
374:             table.insert(recent_list, key)
375:             recent_set[key] = #recent_list
376:         end
377:         ::continue::
378:     end
379: end
380: 
381: function ItemSelection:submit(choices)
382:     local selected_items = {}
383:     for item_id in pairs(self.selected_set) do
384:         table.insert(selected_items, item_id)
385:     end
386:     if #selected_items > 0 then
387:         track_recently_used(choices or self.subviews.flist:getChoices())
388:     end
389:     self.on_submit(selected_items)
390: end
391: 
392: function ItemSelection:onInput(keys)
393:     if keys.LEAVESCREEN or keys._MOUSE_R then
394:         self.on_cancel()
395:         return true
396:     elseif keys._MOUSE_L then
397:         local list = self.subviews.flist.list
398:         local idx = list:getIdxUnderMouse()
399:         if idx then
400:             list:setSelected(idx)
401:             local modstate = dfhack.internal.getModstate()
402:             if modstate & 2 > 0 then -- ctrl
403:                 local choice = list:getChoices()[idx]
404:                 if modstate & 1 > 0 then -- shift
405:                     self:decrement_group(idx, choice)
406:                 else
407:                     self:increment_group(idx, choice)
408:                 end
409:                 return true
410:             end
411:         end
412:     end
413:     return ItemSelection.super.onInput(self, keys)
414: end
415: 
416: ItemSelectionScreen = defclass(ItemSelectionScreen, gui.ZScreen)
417: ItemSelectionScreen.ATTRS {
418:     focus_path='dwarfmode/Building/Placement/dfhack/lua/buildingplan/itemselection',
419:     force_pause=true,
420:     pass_movement_keys=true,
421:     pass_mouse_clicks=false,
422:     defocusable=false,
423:     get_available_items_fn=DEFAULT_NIL,
424:     desc=DEFAULT_NIL,
425:     quantity=DEFAULT_NIL,
426:     autoselect=DEFAULT_NIL,
427:     on_submit=DEFAULT_NIL,
428:     on_cancel=DEFAULT_NIL,
429: }
430: 
431: function ItemSelectionScreen:init()
432:     self:addviews{
433:         ItemSelection{
434:             get_available_items_fn=self.get_available_items_fn,
435:             desc=self.desc,
436:             quantity=self.quantity,
437:             autoselect=self.autoselect,
438:             on_submit=self.on_submit,
439:             on_cancel=self.on_cancel,
440:         }
441:     }
442: end
443: 
444: return _ENV

SOURCE D:/program/steam/steamapps/common/DFHack/hack/lua/plugins/buildingplan/mechanisms.lua

97: local item_selection_dlg
98: local function reset_dlg()
99:     if item_selection_dlg then
100:         if item_selection_dlg:isActive() then
101:             item_selection_dlg:dismiss()
102:         end
103:         item_selection_dlg = nil
104:     end
105: end
106: 
107: local function is_reachable(bld_pos, item_id)
108:     if not bld_pos then return false end
109:     local item = df.item.find(item_id)
110:     if not item then return false end
111:     return dfhack.maps.canWalkBetween(xyz2pos(dfhack.items.getPosition(item)), bld_pos)
112: end
113: 
114: local function sort_by_safety(item_ids, safety)
115:     if safety == 2 then return item_ids end
116:     local safety_ids = {[safety]=utils.invert(item_ids)}
117:     for sort_safety=safety+1,2 do
118:         local safety_item_ids = require('plugins.buildingplan').getAvailableItemsByHeat(
119:             df.building_type.Trap, df.trap_type.Lever, -1, 0, sort_safety)
120:         safety_ids[sort_safety] = utils.invert(safety_item_ids)
121:     end
122:     local sorted_ids = {}
123:     for _,item_id in ipairs(item_ids) do
124:         for safety_idx=2,safety,-1 do
125:             if safety_ids[safety_idx][item_id] then
126:                 table.insert(ensure_key(sorted_ids, safety_idx), item_id)
127:                 break
128:             end
129:         end
130:     end
131:     local ret = {}
132:     for safety_id=safety,2 do
133:         if sorted_ids[safety_id] then
134:             table.move(sorted_ids[safety_id], 1, #sorted_ids[safety_id], #ret+1, ret)
135:         end
136:     end
137:     return ret
138: end
139: 
140: -- returns mechanisms ordered by heat safety, so the least heat safe mechanisms are used first
141: -- when heat safety isn't important
142: local function get_available_items(safety, other_mechanism)
143:     local item_ids = require('plugins.buildingplan').getAvailableItemsByHeat(
144:         df.building_type.Trap, df.trap_type.Lever, -1, 0, safety)
145:     local bld = dfhack.gui.getSelectedBuilding(true)
146:     local bld_pos = bld and xyz2pos(bld.centerx, bld.centery, bld.z)
147:     for idx=#item_ids,1,-1 do
148:         local item_id = item_ids[idx]
149:         if item_id == other_mechanism or not is_reachable(bld_pos, item_id) then
150:             table.remove(item_ids, idx)
151:         end
152:     end
153:     return sort_by_safety(item_ids, safety)
154: end
155: 
156: function MechanismOverlay:save_id(which, item_id)
157:     local saved_id = ('saved_%s_id'):format(which)
158:     local ui_id = ('linking_lever_mech_%s_id'):format(which)
159:     item_id = item_id or -1
160:     view_sheets[ui_id] = item_id
161:     self[saved_id] = item_id
162: end
163: 
164: function MechanismOverlay:choose_mechanism(which, autoselect)
165:     local widget_id = 'safety_' .. which
166:     local safety = self.subviews[widget_id]:getOptionValue()
167:     local ui_other_id = ('linking_lever_mech_%s_id'):format(which == 'lever' and 'target' or 'lever')
168:     local available_item_ids = get_available_items(safety, view_sheets[ui_other_id])
169: 
170:     if autoselect then
171:         self:save_id(which, available_item_ids[1])
172:         return
173:     end
174: 
175:     -- to integrate with ItemSelection's last used sorting
176:     df.global.buildreq.building_type = df.building_type.Trap
177: 
178:     local desc = self.subviews[widget_id]:getOptionLabel()
179:     if desc ~= 'Any' then
180:         desc = desc .. ' safe'
181:     end
182:     desc = desc .. ' mechanism'
183: 
184:     item_selection_dlg = item_selection_dlg or itemselection.ItemSelectionScreen{
185:         get_available_items_fn=function() return available_item_ids end,
186:         desc=desc,
187:         quantity=1,
188:         autoselect=false,
189:         on_cancel=reset_dlg,
190:         on_submit=function(chosen_ids)
191:             self:save_id(which, chosen_ids[1] or available_item_ids[1])
192:             reset_dlg()
193:         end,
194:     }:show()
195: end
196: 
197: function MechanismOverlay:onInput(keys)
198:     if MechanismOverlay.super.onInput(self, keys) then
199:         return true
200:     end
201:     if keys._MOUSE_L then
202:         if self:getMousePos() then
203:             -- don't let clicks bleed through the panel
204:             return true
205:         end
206:         -- don't allow the lever to be linked if mechanisms are not set
207:         return view_sheets.linking_lever_mech_lever_id == -1 or
208:             view_sheets.linking_lever_mech_target_id == -1
209:     end
210: end
211: 
212: function MechanismOverlay:onRenderFrame(dc, rect)
213:     MechanismOverlay.super.onRenderFrame(self, dc, rect)
214:     if self.saved_lever_id ~= view_sheets.linking_lever_mech_lever_id then
215:         self:choose_mechanism('lever', true)
216:     end
217:     if self.saved_target_id ~= view_sheets.linking_lever_mech_target_id then
218:         self:choose_mechanism('target', true)
219:     end
220: end
221: 
222: return _ENV

53: local function is_choosing_area()
54:     return uibs.selection_pos.x >= 0
55: end

57: -- TODO: reuse data in quickfort database
58: local function get_selection_size_limits()
59:     local btype = uibs.building_type
60:     if btype == df.building_type.Bridge
61:             or btype == df.building_type.FarmPlot
62:             or btype == df.building_type.RoadPaved
63:             or btype == df.building_type.RoadDirt then
64:         return {w=31, h=31}
65:     elseif btype == df.building_type.AxleHorizontal then
66:         return uibs.direction == 1 and {w=1, h=31} or {w=31, h=1}
67:     elseif btype == df.building_type.Rollers then
68:         return (uibs.direction == 1 or uibs.direction == 3) and {w=31, h=1} or {w=1, h=31}
69:     end
70: end
71: 
72: local function get_selected_bounds(selection_pos, pos)
73:     selection_pos = selection_pos or uibs.selection_pos
74:     if not is_choosing_area() then return end
75: 
76:     pos = pos or uibs.pos
77: 
78:     local bounds = {
79:         x1=math.min(selection_pos.x, pos.x),
80:         x2=math.max(selection_pos.x, pos.x),
81:         y1=math.min(selection_pos.y, pos.y),
82:         y2=math.max(selection_pos.y, pos.y),
83:         z1=math.min(selection_pos.z, pos.z),
84:         z2=math.max(selection_pos.z, pos.z),
85:     }
86: 
87:     -- clamp to map edges
88:     bounds = {
89:         x1=math.max(0, bounds.x1),
90:         x2=math.min(df.global.world.map.x_count-1, bounds.x2),
91:         y1=math.max(0, bounds.y1),
92:         y2=math.min(df.global.world.map.y_count-1, bounds.y2),
93:         z1=math.max(0, bounds.z1),
94:         z2=math.min(df.global.world.map.z_count-1, bounds.z2),
95:     }
96: 
97:     local limits = get_selection_size_limits()
98:     if limits then
99:         -- clamp to building type area limit
100:         bounds = {
101:             x1=math.max(selection_pos.x - (limits.w-1), bounds.x1),
102:             x2=math.min(selection_pos.x + (limits.w-1), bounds.x2),
103:             y1=math.max(selection_pos.y - (limits.h-1), bounds.y1),
104:             y2=math.min(selection_pos.y + (limits.h-1), bounds.y2),
105:             z1=bounds.z1,
106:             z2=bounds.z2,
107:         }
108:     end
109: 
110:     return bounds
111: end
112: 
113: local function get_cur_area_dims(bounds)
114:     if not bounds and not is_choosing_area() then return 1, 1, 1 end
115:     bounds = bounds or get_selected_bounds()
116:     if not bounds then return 1, 1, 1 end
117:     return bounds.x2 - bounds.x1 + 1,
118:             bounds.y2 - bounds.y1 + 1,
119:             bounds.z2 - bounds.z1 + 1
120: end
121: 
122: local function get_selected_volume(bounds)
123:     local w, h, depth = get_cur_area_dims(bounds)
124:     return w * h * depth
125: end
126: 
127: local function is_pressure_plate()
128:     return uibs.building_type == df.building_type.Trap
129:             and uibs.building_subtype == df.trap_type.PressurePlate
130: end
131: 
132: local function is_weapon_trap()
133:     return uibs.building_type == df.building_type.Trap
134:             and uibs.building_subtype == df.trap_type.WeaponTrap
135: end
136: 
137: local function is_spike_trap()
138:     return uibs.building_type == df.building_type.Weapon
139: end
140: 
141: local function is_weapon_or_spike_trap()
142:     return is_weapon_trap() or is_spike_trap()
143: end
144: 
145: local function is_construction()
146:     return uibs.building_type == df.building_type.Construction
147: end
148: 
149: local function is_siege_engine()
150:     return uibs.building_type == df.building_type.SiegeEngine
151: end
152: 
153: local function tile_is_construction(pos)
154:     local tt = dfhack.maps.getTileType(pos)
155:     if not tt then return false end
156:     if df.tiletype.attrs[tt].material ~= df.tiletype_material.CONSTRUCTION then
157:         return false
158:     end
159:     local construction = df.construction.find(pos)
160:     return construction and not construction.flags.top_of_wall
161: end
162: 
163: local ONE_BY_ONE = xy2pos(1, 1)
164: 
165: local function can_reconstruct(bounds)
166:     return get_selected_volume(bounds) == 1 or require('plugins.buildingplan').getGlobalSettings().reconstruct
167: end
168: 
169: local function can_place_construction(reconstruct, pos)
170:     return dfhack.buildings.checkFreeTiles(pos, ONE_BY_ONE) and (reconstruct or not tile_is_construction(pos))
171: end
172: 
173: local function is_interior(bounds, x, y)
174:     return x ~= bounds.x1 and x ~= bounds.x2 and
175:         y ~= bounds.y1 and y ~= bounds.y2
176: end
177: 
178: -- adjusted from WeaponSpiketrapPanel (HotKey & Slider) on the planner panel
179: local weapon_quantity = 1
180: 
181: local function get_quantity(filter, hollow, bounds)
182:     if is_pressure_plate() then
183:         local flags = uibs.plate_info.flags
184:         return (flags.units and 1 or 0) + (flags.water and 1 or 0) +
185:                     (flags.magma and 1 or 0) + (flags.track and 1 or 0)
186:     elseif (is_weapon_trap() and filter.vector_id == df.job_item_vector_id.ANY_WEAPON) or is_spike_trap() then
187:         return weapon_quantity
188:     end
189:     local quantity = filter.quantity or 1
190:     bounds = bounds or get_selected_bounds()
191:     local dimx, dimy, dimz = get_cur_area_dims(bounds)
192:     if quantity < 1 then
193:         return (((dimx * dimy) // 4) + 1) * dimz
194:     end
195:     if bounds and is_construction() then
196:         local reconstruct = can_reconstruct(bounds)
197:         local count = 0
198:         for z = bounds.z1, bounds.z2 do
199:             for y = bounds.y1, bounds.y2 do
200:                 for x = bounds.x1, bounds.x2 do
201:                     if hollow and is_interior(bounds, x, y) then goto continue end
202:                     if can_place_construction(reconstruct, xyz2pos(x, y, z)) then
203:                         count = count + 1
204:                     end
205:                     ::continue::
206:                 end
207:             end
208:         end
209:         return quantity * count
210:     end
211:     -- job item quantities are totals for the whole building, not per-tile
212:     return quantity
213: end
214: 
215: local function cur_building_has_no_area()
216:     if uibs.building_type == df.building_type.Construction then return false end
217:     local filters = dfhack.buildings.getFiltersByType({},
218:             uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type)
219:     -- this works because all variable-size buildings have either no item
220:     -- filters or a quantity of -1 for their first (and only) item
221:     return filters and filters[1] and (not filters[1].quantity or filters[1].quantity > 0)
222: end
223: 
224: local function is_tutorial_open()
225:     local help = df.global.game.main_interface.help
226:     return help.open and
227:             help.context == df.help_context_type.START_TUTORIAL_WORKSHOPS_AND_TASKS
228: end
229: 
230: local function is_plannable()
231:     return not is_tutorial_open() and
232:             get_cur_filters() and
233:             not (is_construction() and
234:                 uibs.building_subtype == df.construction_type.TrackNSEW)
235: end
236: 
237: local function is_slab()
238:     return uibs.building_type == df.building_type.Slab
239: end
240: 
241: local function is_cage()
242:     return uibs.building_type == df.building_type.Cage
243: end
244: 
245: local function is_stairs()
246:     return is_construction()
247:             and uibs.building_subtype == df.construction_type.UpDownStair
248: end
249: 
250: local function is_single_level_stairs()
251:     if not is_stairs() then return false end
252:     local _, _, dimz = get_cur_area_dims()
253:     return dimz == 1
254: end
255: 
256: local function is_multi_level_stairs()
257:     if not is_stairs() then return false end
258:     local _, _, dimz = get_cur_area_dims()
259:     return dimz > 1
260: end
261: 
262: local direction_panel_frame = {t=4, h=13, w=46, r=28}
263: 
264: local direction_panel_types = utils.invert{
265:     df.building_type.Bridge,
266:     df.building_type.ScrewPump,
267:     df.building_type.WaterWheel,
268:     df.building_type.AxleHorizontal,
269:     df.building_type.Rollers,
270:     df.building_type.SiegeEngine,
271: }
272: 
273: local function has_direction_panel()
274:     return direction_panel_types[uibs.building_type]
275:         or (uibs.building_type == df.building_type.Trap
276:             and uibs.building_subtype == df.trap_type.TrackStop)
277: end
278: 
279: local pressure_plate_panel_frame = {t=4, h=38, w=50, r=28}
280: 
281: local function has_pressure_plate_panel()
282:     return is_pressure_plate()
283: end
284: 
285: local function is_over_options_panel()
286:     local frame = nil
287:     if has_direction_panel() then
288:         frame = direction_panel_frame
289:     elseif has_pressure_plate_panel() then
290:         frame = pressure_plate_panel_frame
291:     else
292:         return false
293:     end
294:     local v = widgets.Widget{frame=frame}
295:     local rect = gui.mkdims_wh(0, 0, dfhack.screen.getWindowSize())
296:     v:updateLayout(gui.ViewRect{rect=rect})
297:     return v:getMousePos()
298: end
299: 
300: local function compress(str, len)
301:     if #str <= len then
302:         return str
303:     else
304:         local no_vowels = str:gsub('[aeiou]','')
305:         if #no_vowels <= len then
306:             return no_vowels
307:         else
308:             return no_vowels:sub(1,len-3)..'...'
309:         end
310:     end
311: end
312: 
313: local function filter_string(mats, cats, length)
314:     local enabled_mat_names = {}
315:     local enabled_cat_names = {}
316:     for name, props in pairs(mats) do
317:         local enabled = props.enabled == 'true' and cats[props.category]
318:         if enabled then table.insert(enabled_mat_names, name) end
319:     end
320:     if #enabled_mat_names == 1 then
321:         return '['..compress(enabled_mat_names[1], length)..']'
322:     elseif #enabled_mat_names > 1 then
323:         for cat, _ in pairs(cats) do
324:             if cat ~= 'unset' and cats[cat] then
325:                 table.insert(enabled_cat_names, cat)
326:             end
327:         end
328:         if #enabled_cat_names == 1 then
329:             return '[' .. enabled_cat_names[1]:gsub("^%l", string.upper) .. ']'
330:         else
331:             return '['..#enabled_cat_names..' mat. categories]'
332:         end
333:     else
334:         -- can result from selecting wood and then toggling "fire safe" etc.
335:         return '[impossible filter]'
336:     end
337: end
338: --------------------------------
339: -- ItemLine
340: --
341: 
342: -- number of characters for item filter summary (excluding surrounding [ ])
343: local item_filter_chars = 17
344: 
345: ItemLine = defclass(ItemLine, widgets.Panel)
346: ItemLine.ATTRS{
347:     idx=DEFAULT_NIL,
348:     is_selected_fn=DEFAULT_NIL,
349:     is_hollow_fn=DEFAULT_NIL,
350:     on_select=DEFAULT_NIL,
351:     on_filter=DEFAULT_NIL,
352:     on_clear_filter=DEFAULT_NIL,
353: }
354: 
355: function ItemLine:init()
356:     self.frame.h = 2
357:     self.visible = function() return #get_cur_filters() >= self.idx end
358:     self:addviews{
359:         widgets.Label{
360:             view_id='item_symbol',
361:             frame={t=0, l=0},
362:             text=string.char(16), -- this is the "►" character
363:             text_pen=COLOR_YELLOW,
364:             auto_width=true,
365:             visible=self.is_selected_fn,
366:         },
367:         widgets.Label{
368:             view_id='item_desc',
369:             frame={t=0, l=2},
370:             text={
371:                 {text=self:callback('get_item_line_text'),
372:                  pen=function() return gui.invert_color(COLOR_WHITE, self.is_selected_fn()) end},
373:             },
374:         },
375:         widgets.Label{
376:             view_id='item_filter',
377:             frame={t=0, l=28},
378:             text={
379:                 {text=self:callback('get_filter_text'),
380:                  width=item_filter_chars+2,
381:                  rjustify=true,
382:                  pen=function() return
383:                          self:is_impossible() and COLOR_RED or
384:                          gui.invert_color(COLOR_LIGHTCYAN, self.is_selected_fn()) end},
385:             },
386:             auto_width=true,
387:             on_click=function() self.on_filter(self.idx) end,
388:         },
389:         widgets.Label{
390:             frame={t=0, l=47},
391:             text='[clear]',
392:             text_pen=COLOR_LIGHTRED,
393:             auto_width=true,
394:             visible=self:callback('has_filter'),
395:             on_click=function() self.on_clear_filter(self.idx) end,
396:         },
397:         widgets.Label{
398:             frame={t=1, l=2},
399:             text={
400:                 {gap=2, text=function() return self.note end,
401:                  pen=function() return self.note_pen end},
402:             },
403:         },
404:     }
405: end
406: 
407: function ItemLine:reset()
408:     self.desc = nil
409:     self.available = nil
410: end
411: 
412: function ItemLine:onInput(keys)
413:     if keys._MOUSE_L and self:getMousePos() then
414:         self.on_select(self.idx)
415:     end
416:     return ItemLine.super.onInput(self, keys)
417: end
418: 
419: function ItemLine:get_item_line_text()
420:     local idx = self.idx
421:     local filter = get_cur_filters()[idx]
422:     local quantity = get_quantity(filter, self.is_hollow_fn())
423: 
424:     local buildingplan = require('plugins.buildingplan')
425:     self.desc = self.desc or buildingplan.get_desc(filter)
426: 
427:     self.available = self.available or buildingplan.countAvailableItems(
428:         uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, idx - 1)
429:     if self.available >= quantity then
430:         self.note_pen = COLOR_GREEN
431:         self.note = (' %d available now'):format(self.available)
432:     elseif self.available >= 0 then
433:         self.note_pen = COLOR_BROWN
434:         self.note = (' Will link next (need to make %d)'):format(quantity - self.available)
435:     else
436:         self.note_pen = COLOR_BROWN
437:         self.note = (' Will link later (need to make %d)'):format(-self.available + quantity)
438:     end
439:     self.note = string.char(192) .. self.note -- character 192 is "└"
440: 
441:     self.quantity = quantity
442: 
443:     return ('%d %s%s'):format(quantity, self.desc, quantity == 1 and '' or 's')
444: end
445: 
446: function ItemLine:has_filter()
447:     return require('plugins.buildingplan').hasFilter(
448:         uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, self.idx-1)
449: end
450: 
451: function ItemLine:get_filter_text()
452:     local buildingplan = require('plugins.buildingplan')
453:     if not buildingplan.hasFilter(
454:             uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, self.idx - 1)
455:     then
456:         return '[any material]'
457:     end
458:     local mats = buildingplan.getMaterialFilter(
459:         uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, self.idx - 1)
460:     local cats = buildingplan.getMaterialMaskFilter(
461:         uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, self.idx - 1)
462:     return filter_string(mats, cats, item_filter_chars)
463: end
464: 
465: -- short circuit version of the '[impossible filter]' case above
466: function ItemLine:is_impossible()
467:     local buildingplan = require('plugins.buildingplan')
468:     local mats = buildingplan.getMaterialFilter(
469:         uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, self.idx-1)
470:     local cats = buildingplan.getMaterialMaskFilter(
471:         uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, self.idx - 1)
472:      for _,props in pairs(mats) do
473:         local enabled = props.enabled == 'true' and cats[props.category]
474:         if enabled then return false end
475:     end
476:     return true
477: end
478: 
479: function ItemLine:reduce_quantity(used_quantity)
480:     if not self.available then return end
481:     local filter = get_cur_filters()[self.idx]
482:     used_quantity = used_quantity or get_quantity(filter, self.is_hollow_fn())
483:     self.available = self.available - used_quantity
484: end
485: 
486: local function get_placement_errors()
487:     local out = ''
488:     for _,str in ipairs(uibs.errors) do
489:         if #out > 0 then out = out .. NEWLINE end
490:         out = out .. str.value
491:     end
492:     return out
493: end
494: 
495: --------------------------------
496: -- QuickFilter
497: --
498: 
499: -- Used to store a table of the following format:
500: -- table<string, { label: string, mats: string[] }>
501: -- string: quick filter slot (must be strings because of the way persistence works)
502: -- label: string representation of the filter
503: -- mats: list of material names allowed by the filter
504: BUILDINGPLAN_FILTERS_KEY = "buildingplan/quick-filters"
505: 
506: -- old saves may use numbers as keys, which we convert to string keys on load
507: dfhack.onStateChange[BUILDINGPLAN_FILTERS_KEY] = function(sc)
508:     if sc ~= SC_MAP_LOADED or df.global.gamemode ~= df.game_mode.DWARF then
509:         return
510:     end
511:     local saved_filters = dfhack.persistent.getSiteData(BUILDINGPLAN_FILTERS_KEY, {})
512:     local new_filters = {}
513:     for k, v in pairs(saved_filters) do
514:         if type(k) == 'number' then
515:             new_filters[tostring(k)] = v
516:         elseif type(k) == 'string' then
517:             new_filters[k] = v
518:         end
519:     end
520:     dfhack.persistent.saveSiteData(BUILDINGPLAN_FILTERS_KEY, new_filters)
521: end
522: 
523: QuickFilter = defclass(QuickFilter, widgets.Panel)
524: QuickFilter.ATTRS{
525:     idx=DEFAULT_NIL,
526:     on_click_fn=DEFAULT_NIL,
527:     is_selected_fn=DEFAULT_NIL
528: }
529: 
530: function QuickFilter:init()
531:     local label = ('%d.'):format(self.idx)
532:     self.frame.w = 27
533:     self.renaming = false
534: 
535:     self:addviews {
536:         widgets.Label {
537:             frame = { t = 0, l = 0 },
538:             text = string.char(16), -- this is the "►" character
539:             text_pen = COLOR_YELLOW,
540:             auto_width = true,
541:             visible = self.is_selected_fn,
542:         },
543:         widgets.Label { frame = { t = 0, l = 2 }, text = label },
544:         widgets.Label {
545:             frame = { t = 0, l = 5, w = item_filter_chars + 2 },
546:             text = { { text = self:callback('get_label_text'), pen = function() return COLOR_CYAN end } },
547:             visible = function() return self.renaming == false end,
548:             on_click = self:callback("on_click"),
549:         },
550:         widgets.EditField {
551:             view_id = 'edit_field',
552:             frame = { t = 0, l = 5, w = item_filter_chars + 2 },
553:             text = "",
554:             visible = function() return self.renaming == true end,
555:             on_submit = function(text) self:submit_name(text) end,
556:         },
557:         widgets.Label {
558:             frame = { t = 0, r = 0, w = 3 },
559:             text = "[x]",
560:             text_pen = COLOR_LIGHTRED,
561:             visible = self:callback("slot_used"),
562:             on_click = self:callback("clear")
563:         }
564:     }
565: end
566: 
567: function QuickFilter:onInput(keys)
568:     if keys.LEAVESCREEN or keys._MOUSE_R then
569:         if self.renaming then
570:             self.subviews.edit_field:setFocus(false)
571:             self.renaming = false
572:             editing_filters_flag = false
573:             return true
574:         else
575:             return false
576:         end
577:     end
578:     return QuickFilter.super.onInput(self, keys)
579: end
580: 
581: function QuickFilter:on_click()
582:     if dfhack.internal.getModifiers().shift and
583:         self:slot_used() and not editing_filters_flag
584:     then
585:         self.subviews.edit_field:setText(self:get_label_text())
586:         self.renaming = true
587:         editing_filters_flag = true
588:         self.subviews.edit_field:setFocus(true)
589:     else
590:         self.on_click_fn(self.idx) -- save/apply filter based on selected ItemLine
591:     end
592: end
593: 
594: function QuickFilter:slot_used()
595:     local quick_filters = dfhack.persistent.getSiteData(BUILDINGPLAN_FILTERS_KEY, {})
596:     return quick_filters[self.idx] ~= nil
597: end
598: 
599: function QuickFilter:clear()
600:     local quick_filters = dfhack.persistent.getSiteData(BUILDINGPLAN_FILTERS_KEY, {})
601:     quick_filters[self.idx] = nil
602:     dfhack.persistent.saveSiteData(BUILDINGPLAN_FILTERS_KEY, quick_filters)
603: end
604: 
605: function QuickFilter:get_label_text()
606:     local quick_filters = dfhack.persistent.getSiteData(BUILDINGPLAN_FILTERS_KEY, {})
607:     local set = quick_filters[self.idx]
608:     if not set then
609:         return "empty"
610:     else
611:         return set.label
612:     end
613: end
614: 
615: function QuickFilter:submit_name(text)
616:     local quick_filters = dfhack.persistent.getSiteData(BUILDINGPLAN_FILTERS_KEY, {})
617:     quick_filters[self.idx].label = compress(text, item_filter_chars+2)
618:     dfhack.persistent.saveSiteData(BUILDINGPLAN_FILTERS_KEY, quick_filters)
619:     self.renaming = false
620:     editing_filters_flag = false
621: end
622: --------------------------------

624: --
