1060: function PlannerOverlay:show_favorites()
1061:     return not self.state.minimized and self.subviews.show_favorites:getOptionValue()
1062: end
1063: 
1064: function PlannerOverlay:show_hide_favorites(new)
1065:     local errors_frame = {t=16+(new and 9 or 0), l=0, r=0}
1066:     self.subviews.errors.frame = errors_frame
1067:     self:updateLayout()
1068: end
1069: 
1070: function PlannerOverlay:save_restore_filter(slot)
1071:     self.selected_favorite = slot
1072:     local buildingplan = require('plugins.buildingplan')
1073:     local quick_filters = dfhack.persistent.getSiteData(BUILDINGPLAN_FILTERS_KEY, {})
1074:     if quick_filters[slot] then -- restore saved filter
1075:         buildingplan.setMaterialFilter(
1076:             uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, self.selected - 1,
1077:             quick_filters[slot].mats
1078:         )
1079:     else -- save current filter
1080: 
1081:         if not buildingplan.hasFilter(
1082:                 uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, self.selected - 1)
1083:         then return end
1084: 
1085:         local mats = buildingplan.getMaterialFilter(
1086:             uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, self.selected - 1)
1087:         local cats = buildingplan.getMaterialMaskFilter(
1088:             uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, self.selected - 1)
1089:         local label = filter_string(mats, cats, item_filter_chars)
1090:         local enabled_mats = {}
1091:         for mat, props in pairs(mats) do
1092:             if props.enabled == "true" and cats[props.category] then
1093:                 table.insert(enabled_mats, mat)
1094:             end
1095:         end
1096:         if #enabled_mats > 0 then
1097:             quick_filters[slot] = { label = label, mats = enabled_mats }
1098:             dfhack.persistent.saveSiteData(BUILDINGPLAN_FILTERS_KEY, quick_filters)
1099:         end
1100:     end
1101: end
1102: 
1103: 
1104: function PlannerOverlay:is_minimized()
1105:     return self.state.minimized
1106: end
1107: 
1108: function PlannerOverlay:is_not_minimized()
1109:     return not self.state.minimized
1110: end
1111: 
1112: function PlannerOverlay:toggle_minimized()
1113:     self.state.minimized = not self.state.minimized
1114:     config:write()
1115:     self:reset()
1116: end
1117: 
1118: function PlannerOverlay:reset()
1119:     self.subviews.item1:reset()
1120:     self.subviews.item2:reset()
1121:     self.subviews.item3:reset()
1122:     self.subviews.item4:reset()
1123:     reset_counts_flag = false
1124: end
1125: 
1126: function PlannerOverlay:set_filter(idx)
1127:     filterselection.FilterSelectionScreen{index=idx, desc=require('plugins.buildingplan').get_desc(get_cur_filters()[idx])}:show()
1128: end
1129: 
1130: function PlannerOverlay:clear_filter(idx)
1131:     desc=require('plugins.buildingplan').clearFilter(uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, idx-1)
1132: end
1133: 
1134: function PlannerOverlay:queue_order(idx)
1135:     local item = self.subviews['item'..tostring(idx)]
1136:     if not item or not item.available or not item.quantity or item.available >= item.quantity then return end
1137:     local missing = item.quantity - item.available
1138:     if missing <= 0 then return end
1139: 
1140:     local filter = get_cur_filters()[idx]
1141: 
1142:     local job_name = "ConstructBlocks"
1143:     local item_type = nil
1144:     if filter.item_type and filter.item_type ~= -1 then
1145:         item_type = filter.item_type
1146:     elseif filter.vector_id and filter.vector_id ~= -1 then
1147:         local mapping_vector = {
1148:             [df.job_item_vector_id.ANY_WEAPON] = df.item_type.WEAPON,
1149:             [df.job_item_vector_id.ANY_ARMOR] = df.item_type.ARMOR,
1150:         }
1151:         item_type = mapping_vector[filter.vector_id]
1152:     end
1153: 
1154:     if item_type and ITEM_TO_JOB[item_type] then
1155:         job_name = ITEM_TO_JOB[item_type]
1156:     end
1157: 
1158:     local order_json = {
1159:         job = job_name,
1160:         amount_total = missing
1161:     }
1162: 
1163:     local buildingplan = require('plugins.buildingplan')
1164:     local cats_list = {}
1165:     if buildingplan.hasFilter(uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, idx - 1) then
1166:         local cats = buildingplan.getMaterialMaskFilter(uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, idx - 1)
1167:         for cat, enabled in pairs(cats) do
1168:             if enabled and cat ~= 'unset' then
1169:                 table.insert(cats_list, cat)
1170:             end
1171:         end
1172:     end
1173: 
1174:     if #cats_list == 0 then
1175:         -- df manager requires a material category for generic jobs or it queues "unknown material" orders
1176:         table.insert(cats_list, JOB_DEFAULTS[job_name] or 'stone')
1177:     end
1178: 
1179:     local mat_cats = {}
1180:     for _, cat in ipairs(cats_list) do
1181:         if VALID_MAT_CATS[cat] then
1182:             table.insert(mat_cats, cat)
1183:         elseif cat == 'stone' then
1184:             order_json.material = "INORGANIC"
1185:         elseif cat == 'glass' then
1186:             order_json.material = "GLASS_GREEN"
1187:         elseif cat == 'metal' or cat == 'iron' then
1188:             order_json.material = "IRON"
1189:         end
1190:     end
1191: 
1192:     if #mat_cats > 0 then
1193:         order_json.material_category = mat_cats
1194:     end
1195: 
1196:     dfhack.run_command_silent('workorder', json.encode(order_json))
1197: 
1198:     local desc = item.desc or "item"
1199:     dfhack.gui.showAnnouncement('Work order queued for ' .. tostring(missing) .. ' ' .. desc .. '.', COLOR_YELLOW, true)
1200: end
1201: 
1202: local function get_placement_data()
1203:     local direction = uibs.direction
1204:     local bounds = get_selected_bounds()
1205:     local width, height, depth = get_cur_area_dims(bounds)
1206:     local _, adjusted_width, adjusted_height = dfhack.buildings.getCorrectSize(
1207:             width, height, uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype,
1208:             uibs.custom_type, direction)
1209:     -- get the upper-left corner of the building/area at min z-level
1210:     local start_pos = bounds and xyz2pos(bounds.x1, bounds.y1, bounds.z1) or
1211:             xyz2pos(
1212:                 uibs.pos.x - adjusted_width//2,
1213:                 uibs.pos.y - adjusted_height//2,
1214:                 uibs.pos.z)
1215:     if uibs.building_type == df.building_type.ScrewPump then
1216:         if direction == df.screw_pump_direction.FromSouth then
1217:             start_pos.y = start_pos.y + 1
1218:         elseif direction == df.screw_pump_direction.FromEast then
1219:             start_pos.x = start_pos.x + 1
1220:         end
1221:     end
1222:     local min_x, max_x = start_pos.x, start_pos.x
1223:     local min_y, max_y = start_pos.y, start_pos.y
1224:     local min_z, max_z = start_pos.z, start_pos.z
1225:     if adjusted_width == 1 and adjusted_height == 1
1226:             and (width > 1 or height > 1 or depth > 1) then
1227:         max_x = min_x + width - 1
1228:         max_y = min_y + height - 1
1229:         max_z = math.max(uibs.selection_pos.z, uibs.pos.z)
1230:     end
1231:     return {
1232:         x1=min_x, y1=min_y, z1=min_z,
1233:         x2=max_x, y2=max_y, z2=max_z,
1234:         width=adjusted_width,
1235:         height=adjusted_height
1236:     }
1237: end
1238: 
1239: function PlannerOverlay:save_placement()
1240:     self.saved_placement = get_placement_data()
1241:     if (uibs.selection_pos:isValid()) then
1242:         self.saved_selection_pos_valid = true
1243:         self.saved_selection_pos = copyall(uibs.selection_pos)
1244:         self.saved_pos = copyall(uibs.pos)
1245:         uibs.selection_pos:clear()
1246:     else
1247:         local sp = self.saved_placement
1248:         self.saved_selection_pos = xyz2pos(sp.x1, sp.y1, sp.z1)
1249:         self.saved_pos = xyz2pos(sp.x2, sp.y2, sp.z2)
1250:         self.saved_pos.x = self.saved_pos.x + sp.width - 1
1251:         self.saved_pos.y = self.saved_pos.y + sp.height - 1
1252:     end
1253: end
1254: 
1255: function PlannerOverlay:restore_placement()
1256:     if self.saved_selection_pos_valid then
1257:         uibs.selection_pos = self.saved_selection_pos
1258:         self.saved_selection_pos_valid = nil
1259:     else
1260:         uibs.selection_pos:clear()
1261:     end
1262:     self.saved_selection_pos = nil
1263:     self.saved_pos = nil
1264:     local placement_data = self.saved_placement
1265:     self.saved_placement = nil
1266:     return placement_data
1267: end
1268: 
1269: function PlannerOverlay:onInput(keys)
1270:     if not is_plannable() then return false end
1271:     if PlannerOverlay.super.onInput(self, keys) then
1272:         return true
1273:     end
1274:     if keys.LEAVESCREEN or keys._MOUSE_R then
1275:         if uibs.selection_pos:isValid() then
1276:             uibs.selection_pos:clear()
1277:             return true
1278:         end
1279:         self.selected = 1
1280:         self.subviews.hollow:setOption(false)
1281:         self:reset()
1282:         reset_counts_flag = true
1283:         return false
1284:     end
1285:     if keys.CUSTOM_ALT_M then
1286:         self:toggle_minimized()
1287:         return true
1288:     end
1289:     if self:is_minimized() then return false end
1290:     if keys._MOUSE_L then
1291:         if is_over_options_panel() then return false end
1292:         local detect_rect = copyall(self.frame_rect)
1293:         detect_rect.height = self.subviews.main.frame_rect.height +
1294:                 self.subviews.errors.frame_rect.height
1295:         detect_rect.y2 = detect_rect.y1 + detect_rect.height - 1
1296:         if self.subviews.main:getMousePos(gui.ViewRect{rect=detect_rect})
1297:                 or self.subviews.errors:getMousePos() then
1298:             return true
1299:         end
1300:         if not is_construction() and #uibs.errors > 0 then return true end
1301:         if dfhack.gui.getMousePos() then
1302:             if is_choosing_area() or cur_building_has_no_area() then
1303:                 local filters = get_cur_filters()
1304:                 local num_filters = #filters
1305:                 local choose = self.subviews.choose:getOptionValue()
1306:                 if choose == 0 then
1307:                     self:place_building(get_placement_data())
1308:                 else
1309:                     local bounds = get_selected_bounds()
1310:                     self:save_placement()
1311:                     local autoselect = choose == 2
1312:                     local is_hollow = self.subviews.hollow:getOptionValue()
1313:                     local chosen_items, active_screens = {}, {}
1314:                     local pending = num_filters
1315:                     df.global.game.main_interface.bottom_mode_selected = -1
1316:                     for idx = num_filters,1,-1 do
1317:                         chosen_items[idx] = {}
1318:                         local filter = filters[idx]
1319:                         local get_available_items_fn = function()
1320:                             return require('plugins.buildingplan').getAvailableItems(
1321:                                 uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type, idx-1)
1322:                         end
1323:                         local selection_screen = itemselection.ItemSelectionScreen{
1324:                             get_available_items_fn=get_available_items_fn,
1325:                             desc=require('plugins.buildingplan').get_desc(filter),
1326:                             quantity=get_quantity(filter, is_hollow, bounds),
1327:                             autoselect=autoselect,
1328:                             on_submit=function(items)
1329:                                 chosen_items[idx] = items
1330:                                 if active_screens[idx] then
1331:                                     active_screens[idx]:dismiss()
1332:                                     active_screens[idx] = nil
1333:                                 else
1334:                                     active_screens[idx] = true
1335:                                 end
1336:                                 pending = pending - 1
1337:                                 if pending == 0 then
1338:                                     df.global.game.main_interface.bottom_mode_selected = df.main_bottom_mode_type.BUILDING_PLACEMENT
1339:                                     self:place_building(self:restore_placement(), chosen_items)
1340:                                 end
1341:                             end,
1342:                             on_cancel=function()
1343:                                 for _,scr in pairs(active_screens) do
1344:                                     scr:dismiss()
1345:                                 end
1346:                                 df.global.game.main_interface.bottom_mode_selected = df.main_bottom_mode_type.BUILDING_PLACEMENT
1347:                                 self:restore_placement()
1348:                             end,
1349:                         }
1350:                         if active_screens[idx] then
1351:                             -- we've already returned via autoselect
1352:                             active_screens[idx] = nil
1353:                         else
1354:                             active_screens[idx] = selection_screen:show()
1355:                         end
1356:                     end
1357:                 end
1358:                 return true
1359:             elseif not is_choosing_area() then
1360:                 return false
1361:             end
1362:        end
1363:    end
1364:    return keys._MOUSE_L or keys.SELECT
1365: end
1366: 
1367: function PlannerOverlay:render(dc)
1368:     if not is_plannable() then return end
1369:     self.subviews.errors:updateLayout()
1370:     PlannerOverlay.super.render(self, dc)
1371: end
1372: 
1373: function PlannerOverlay:onRenderFrame(dc, rect)
1374:     PlannerOverlay.super.onRenderFrame(self, dc, rect)
1375: 
1376:     if reset_counts_flag then
1377:         self:reset()
1378:         local buildingplan = require('plugins.buildingplan')
1379:         self.subviews.engraved:setOption(buildingplan.getSpecials(
1380:             uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type).engraved or false)
1381:         self.subviews.empty:setOption(buildingplan.getSpecials(
1382:             uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type).empty or false)
1383:         self.subviews.choose:setOption(buildingplan.getChooseItems(
1384:             uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type))
1385:         self.subviews.safety:setOption(buildingplan.getHeatSafetyFilter(
1386:             uibs.building_type, uibs.building_subtype, uibs.custom_type))
1387:     end
1388: 
1389:     if self:is_minimized() then return end
1390: 
1391:     local bounds = get_selected_bounds(self.saved_selection_pos, self.saved_pos)
1392:     if not bounds then return end
1393: 
1394:     local hollow = self.subviews.hollow:getOptionValue()
1395:     local default_pen = (self.saved_selection_pos or #uibs.errors == 0) and pens.GOOD_TILE_PEN or pens.BAD_TILE_PEN
1396: 
1397:     -- always allow reconstruction if it's a 1x1x1 selection (meaning the player selected that spot specifically)
1398:     local reconstruct = can_reconstruct(bounds)
1399: 
1400:     local get_pen_fn = is_construction() and
1401:         function(pos)
1402:             return can_place_construction(reconstruct, pos) and pens.GOOD_TILE_PEN or pens.BAD_TILE_PEN
1403:         end or function()
1404:             return default_pen
1405:         end
1406: 
1407:     local function get_overlay_pen(pos)
1408:         if not hollow then return get_pen_fn(pos) end
1409:         if pos.x == bounds.x1 or pos.x == bounds.x2 or
1410:                 pos.y == bounds.y1 or pos.y == bounds.y2 then
1411:             return get_pen_fn(pos)
1412:         end
1413:         return gui.TRANSPARENT_PEN
1414:     end
1415: 
1416:     guidm.renderMapOverlay(get_overlay_pen, bounds)
1417: end
1418: 
1419: function PlannerOverlay:get_stairs_subtype(pos, bounds)
1420:     local subtype = uibs.building_subtype
1421:     if pos.z == bounds.z1 then
1422:         local opt = bounds.z1 == bounds.z2 and self.subviews.stairs_only_subtype:getOptionValue() or
1423:             self.subviews.stairs_bottom_subtype:getOptionValue()
1424:         if opt == 'auto' then
1425:             local tt = dfhack.maps.getTileType(pos)
1426:             local shape = df.tiletype.attrs[tt].shape
1427:             if shape ~= df.tiletype_shape.STAIR_DOWN and shape ~= df.tiletype_shape.STAIR_UPDOWN then
1428:                 subtype = df.construction_type.UpStair
1429:             end
1430:         else
1431:             subtype = opt
1432:         end
1433:     elseif pos.z == bounds.z2 then
1434:         local opt = self.subviews.stairs_top_subtype:getOptionValue()
1435:         if opt == 'auto' then
1436:             local tt = dfhack.maps.getTileType(pos)
1437:             local shape = df.tiletype.attrs[tt].shape
1438:             if shape ~= df.tiletype_shape.STAIR_UP and shape ~= df.tiletype_shape.STAIR_UPDOWN then
1439:                 subtype = df.construction_type.DownStair
1440:             end
1441:         else
1442:             subtype = opt
1443:         end
1444:     end
1445:     return subtype
1446: end
1447: 
1448: function PlannerOverlay:place_building(placement_data, chosen_items)
1449:     local pd = placement_data
1450:     local blds = {}
1451:     local hollow = self.subviews.hollow:getOptionValue()
1452:     local subtype = uibs.building_subtype
1453:     local filters = get_cur_filters()
1454:     if is_pressure_plate() or is_spike_trap() then
1455:         filters[1].quantity = get_quantity(filters[1])
1456:     elseif is_weapon_trap() then
1457:         filters[2].quantity = get_quantity(filters[2])
1458:     end
1459:     local reconstruct = can_reconstruct(pd)
1460:     for z=pd.z1,pd.z2 do for y=pd.y1,pd.y2 do for x=pd.x1,pd.x2 do
1461:         if hollow and is_interior(pd, x, y) then
1462:             goto continue
1463:         end
1464:         local pos = xyz2pos(x, y, z)
1465:         if is_construction() and not can_place_construction(reconstruct, pos) then
1466:             goto continue
1467:         end
1468:         if is_stairs() then
1469:             subtype = self:get_stairs_subtype(pos, pd)
1470:         end
1471:         local fields = {}
1472:         if is_siege_engine() then
1473:             local facing = df.global.buildreq.direction
1474:             fields.facing = facing
1475:             fields.resting_orientation = facing
1476:         end
1477:         local bld, err = dfhack.buildings.constructBuilding{pos=pos,
1478:             type=uibs.building_type, subtype=subtype, custom=uibs.custom_type,
1479:             width=pd.width, height=pd.height,
1480:             direction=uibs.direction, filters=filters, fields=fields}
1481:         if err then
1482:             -- it's ok if some buildings fail to build
1483:             goto continue
1484:         end
1485:         -- assign fields for the types that need them. we can't pass them all in
1486:         -- to the call to constructBuilding since attempting to assign unrelated
1487:         -- fields to building types that don't support them causes errors.
1488:         for k in pairs(bld) do
1489:             if k == 'track_stop_info' then utils.assign(bld.track_stop_info, uibs.track_stop) end
1490:             if k == 'speed' then bld.speed = uibs.speed end
1491:             if k == 'plate_info' then utils.assign(bld.plate_info, uibs.plate_info) end
1492:         end
1493:         table.insert(blds, bld)
1494:         ::continue::
1495:     end end end
1496:     local used_quantity = is_construction() and #blds or false
1497:     self.subviews.item1:reduce_quantity(used_quantity)
1498:     self.subviews.item2:reduce_quantity(used_quantity)
1499:     self.subviews.item3:reduce_quantity(used_quantity)
1500:     self.subviews.item4:reduce_quantity(used_quantity)
1501:     local buildingplan = require('plugins.buildingplan')
1502:     for _,bld in ipairs(blds) do
1503:         -- attach chosen items and reduce job_item quantity
1504:         if chosen_items then
1505:             local job = bld.jobs[0]
1506:             local jitems = job.job_items.elements
1507:             local num_filters = #get_cur_filters()
1508:             for idx=1,num_filters do
1509:                 local item_ids = chosen_items[idx]
1510:                 local jitem = jitems[num_filters-idx]
1511:                 while jitem.quantity > 0 and #item_ids > 0 do
1512:                     local item_id = item_ids[#item_ids]
1513:                     local item = df.item.find(item_id)
1514:                     if not item then
1515:                         dfhack.printerr(('item no longer available: %d'):format(item_id))
1516:                         break
1517:                     end
1518:                     if not dfhack.job.attachJobItem(job, item, df.job_role_type.Hauled, idx-1, -1) then
1519:                         dfhack.printerr(('cannot attach item: %d'):format(item_id))
1520:                         break
1521:                     end
1522:                     jitem.quantity = jitem.quantity - 1
1523:                     item_ids[#item_ids] = nil
1524:                 end
1525:             end
1526:         end
1527:         buildingplan.addPlannedBuilding(bld)
1528:         -- the job already exists at designation time; flagging it now means it
1529:         -- is posted with do_now whenever it gets unsuspended
1530:         if self.state.do_now then
1531:             buildingplan.setDoNow(bld, true)
1532:         end
1533:     end
1534:     buildingplan.scheduleCycle()
1535:     uibs.selection_pos:clear()
1536: end
1537: 
1538: 
1539: return _ENV

1: local _ENV = mkmodule("plugins.buildingplan.unlink_mechanisms")

3: local dialogs = require('gui.dialogs')
4: local overlay = require("plugins.overlay")
5: local utils = require("utils")

7: local lever = reqscript("lever")

9: local function mech_iter(b) --iterate mechanisms backwards
10:     local t = b.contained_items
11:     local i_cur = #t - 1
12:     return function()
13:         for i = i_cur, 1, -1 do --index 0 is always building component
14:             local item = t[i].item
15:             if item._type == df.item_trappartsst and
16:                 item.flags.in_building and
17:                 not item.flags.in_job then
18:                     i_cur = i - 1 --resume here
19:                     return item
20:             end
21:         end
22:     end
23: end
24: 
25: local function get_trigger_index(m) --return gref index or nil
26:     for k, gref in ipairs(m.general_refs) do
27:         if gref._type == df.general_ref_building_triggerst or
28:             gref._type == df.general_ref_building_triggertargetst then
29:                 return k
30:         end
31:     end
32: end
33: 
34: local function get_mech_target(m) --mechanism target building if exists
35:     local i = get_trigger_index(m)
36:     return i and df.building.find(m.general_refs[i].building_id) or nil
37: end
38: 
39: local function is_lever(b) --building is a lever
40:     return b and b._type == df.building_trapst and b.trap_type == df.trap_type.Lever
41: end
42: 
43: local function get_pull_job(b) --pending pull job on lever, or nil
44:     for _, j in ipairs(b.jobs) do
45:         if j.job_type == df.job_type.PullLever then
46:             return j
47:         end
48:     end
49: end
50: 
51: local ASCII_LEVER_OFF = string.char(0x95) --ò
52: local ASCII_LEVER_ON = string.char(0xA2) --ó
53: 
54: local function get_lever_state_char(lever) --lever position glyph for the current tileset
55:     -- match the current mode because ASCII and premium lever glyphs differ in directions
56:     if dfhack.screen.inGraphicsMode() then
57:         return lever.state == 0 and "/" or "\\"
58:     end
59:     return lever.state == 0 and ASCII_LEVER_OFF or ASCII_LEVER_ON
60: end
61: 
62: local function get_pull_label(lever) --button label and pen for the lever's pull state
63:     local state_char = get_lever_state_char(lever) --current lever position
64:     local job = get_pull_job(lever)
65:     if not job then
66:         return "Pull    "..state_char, COLOR_WHITE
67:     elseif dfhack.job.getWorker(job) then
68:         return "Pulling "..state_char, COLOR_GREEN --a citizen has taken the job
69:     end
70:     return "Queued  "..state_char, COLOR_YELLOW --queued, not yet taken
71: end
72: 
73: local function has_link_tab(b) --linked building tab exists
74:     if not b then
75:         return
76:     elseif b._type == df.building_trapst and
77:         (b.trap_type == df.trap_type.Lever or
78:         b.trap_type == df.trap_type.PressurePlate) then
79:             return true --always active
80:     end
81: 
82:     for item in mech_iter(b) do
83:         if get_trigger_index(item) then
84:             return true
85:         end
86:     end
87: end
88: 
89: local function unlink_building(b, m) --clean up extra stuff
90:     if b._type == df.building_trapst then
91:         for k, v in ipairs(b.linked_mechanisms) do
92:             if v == m then
93:                 b.linked_mechanisms:erase(k)
94:                 break
95:             end
96:         end
97:     elseif b._type == df.building_doorst or b._type == df.building_hatchst then
98:         local p = utils.getBuildingCenter(b)
99:         local mb = dfhack.maps.getTileBlock(p)
100:         if mb then
101:             b.door_flags.forbidden = false
102:             b.door_flags.operated_by_mechanisms = false
103:             mb.occupancy[p.x % 16][p.y % 16].building = df.tile_building_occ.Dynamic
104:             df.global.world.reindex_pathfinding = true
105:         end
106:     end
107: end
108: 
109: local function unlink_mech(b_src, m_src, b_dst, free_src, free_dst) --unlink two buildings
110:     local m_dst
111:     for item in mech_iter(b_dst) do
112:         local i = get_trigger_index(item)
113:         if i and item.general_refs[i].building_id == b_src.id then
114:             m_dst = item
115:             item.general_refs:erase(i) --unlink from b_src
116:             break
117:         end
118:     end
119: 
120:     unlink_building(b_dst, m_src)
121: 
122:     if m_dst then
123:         unlink_building(b_src, m_dst)
124: 
125:         if free_dst then
126:             m_dst.flags.in_building = false
127:             dfhack.items.moveToGround(m_dst, xyz2pos(dfhack.items.getPosition(m_dst)))
128:         end
129:     else
130:         dfhack.printerr(("MechLinkOverlay: Corresponding mechanism not found in %s!"):format(b_dst))
131:     end
132: 
133:     local i = get_trigger_index(m_src)
134:     if i then
135:         m_src.general_refs:erase(i) --unlink from b_dst
136:     else
137:         dfhack.printerr(("MechLinkOverlay: General ref went missing in %s!"):format(m_src))
138:     end
139: 
140:     if free_src then
141:         m_src.flags.in_building = false
142:         dfhack.items.moveToGround(m_src, xyz2pos(dfhack.items.getPosition(m_src)))
143:     end
144: end
145: 
146: local function confirm_action(self, title, message, pause_var, action_fn)
147:     if self[pause_var] then
148:         action_fn()
149:         return
150:     end
151:     local function pause()
152:         self[pause_var] = true
153:         action_fn()
154:     end
155:     dialogs.showYesNoPrompt(title, message, nil, action_fn, nil, pause)
156: end

236: function MechLinkOverlay:do_scroll(spec)
237:     if type(spec) == "number" then
238:         self:scroll(spec - sheet.scroll_position_linked_buildings-1)
239:     elseif spec == "up_small" then
240:         self:scroll(-1)
241:     elseif spec == "down_small" then
242:         self:scroll(1)
243:     elseif spec == "up_large" then
244:         self:scroll(-self.num_buttons*3)
245:     elseif spec == "down_large" then
246:         self:scroll(self.num_buttons*3)
247:     end
248: end
249: 
250: function MechLinkOverlay:scroll(delta)
251:     local val = sheet.scroll_position_linked_buildings + delta
252:     sheet.scroll_position_linked_buildings = math.min(val, (#self.links - self.num_buttons)*3)
253:     sheet.scroll_position_linked_buildings = math.max(sheet.scroll_position_linked_buildings, 0)
254:     self.subviews.scroll:update(sheet.scroll_position_linked_buildings + 1)
255: end
256: 
257: function MechLinkOverlay:build_links_table()
258:     self.links = {}
259:     for idx = 1, #self.building.contained_items-1 do --index 0 is always building component
260:         local item = self.building.contained_items[idx].item
261:         if item._type == df.item_trappartsst and item.flags.in_building and
262:             not item.flags.in_job and get_trigger_index(item) then --item is linked mechanism
263:                 table.insert(self.links, idx)
264:         end
265:     end
266: 
267:     if self.num_buttons >= #self.links then --no scrolling
268:         sheet.scroll_position_linked_buildings = 0
269:     end
270: end
271: 
272: function MechLinkOverlay:idx_from_offset(offset)
273:     if offset <= 0 or offset >= self.num_buttons*3 - 1 then --linked icons disappear early
274:         return 0 --outside of list
275:     else
276:         return self.links[(offset + sheet.scroll_position_linked_buildings) // 3 + 1] or 0
277:     end
278: end

291:                 visible = false,
292:             },

295:         button:updateLayout(self.frame_body)
296:     end

301: function MechLinkOverlay:activate_button(n)
302:     local button = self:get_button(n)
303:     local saved_mode = self.subviews.unlink_mode:getOptionValue()
304: 
305:     local idx = self:idx_from_offset(button.frame.t)
306:     if idx > 0 and idx < #self.building.contained_items then
307:         local item = self.building.contained_items[idx].item
308:         local target = get_mech_target(item)
309: 
310:         if target then
311:             local free_src = (saved_mode % 2 == 1)
312:             local free_dst = (saved_mode // 2 > 0)
313: 
314:             local message = {
315:                 "Unlink mechanism?",
316:                 NEWLINE, NEWLINE,
317:                 "Mechanism:       ", {text=dfhack.items.getDescription(item, 0, true), pen=COLOR_BLUE},
318:                 NEWLINE,
319:                 "Target building: ", {text=utils.getBuildingName(target), pen=COLOR_BLUE},
320:                 NEWLINE, NEWLINE,
321:                 free_src and {text="This mechanism will be freed and moved to the ground.", pen=COLOR_GREEN} or
322:                     {text="This mechanism will be kept in the building. You can free it later.", pen=COLOR_DARKGRAY},
323:                 NEWLINE,
324:                 free_dst and {text="Mechanism in target will be freed and moved to the ground.", pen=COLOR_GREEN} or
325:                     {text="Mechanism in target will be kept in the building. You can free it later.", pen=COLOR_DARKGRAY},
326:             }
327:             confirm_action(self, "Unlink mechanism", message, 'always_unlink_single', function()
328:                 unlink_mech(self.building, item, target, free_src, free_dst)
329:                 if not has_link_tab(self.building) then
330:                     sheet.show_linked_buildings = false --DF will swap viewscreen
331:                 end
332:             end)
333:         else
334:             dfhack.printerr(("MechLinkOverlay: Mechanism %s had no target!"):format(item))
335:         end
336:     else
337:         dfhack.printerr(("MechLinkOverlay: Invalid button! Offset %d"):format(button.frame.t))
338:     end
339: end

362: function MechLinkOverlay:pull_target(n) --linked lever for button n, or nil

380:         return
381:     end
382:     local job = get_pull_job(target)
383:     if job then
384:         dfhack.job.removeJob(job) --cancel queued pull
385:     else
386:         lever.leverPullJob(target, true) --do now
387:     end
388: end
389: 
390: function MechLinkOverlay:ask_unlink_all()
391:     local saved_mode = self.subviews.unlink_mode:getOptionValue()
392:     local message = {
393:         "Unlink all mechanisms?",
394:         NEWLINE, NEWLINE,
395:         (saved_mode % 2 == 1) and {text="These mechanisms will be freed and moved to the ground.", pen=COLOR_GREEN} or
396:            {text="These mechanisms will be kept in the building. You can free them later.", pen=COLOR_DARKGRAY},
397:         NEWLINE,
398:         (saved_mode // 2 > 0) and {text="Mechanisms in linked buildings will be freed and moved to the ground.", pen=COLOR_GREEN} or
399:             {text="Mechanisms in linked buildings will be kept in the buildings. You can free them later.", pen=COLOR_DARKGRAY},
400:     }
401:     confirm_action(self, "Unlink", message, 'always_unlink_all', function ()
402:         local free_src = (saved_mode % 2 == 1)
403:         local free_dst = (saved_mode // 2 > 0)
404: 
405:         -- freeing a mechanism removes it from contained_items and shifts the
406:         -- indices of the entries after it, so resolve items before mutating
407:         local items = {}
408:         for _, idx in ipairs(self.links) do
409:             local entry = self.building.contained_items[idx]
410:             if entry then
411:                 table.insert(items, entry.item)
412:             end
413:         end
414: 
415:         for _, item in ipairs(items) do
416:             local target = get_mech_target(item)
417: 
418:             if target then
419:                 unlink_mech(self.building, item, target, free_src, free_dst)
420:             else
421:                 dfhack.printerr(("MechLinkOverlay: Mechanism %s had no target!"):format(item))
422:             end
423:         end
424: 
425:         if not has_link_tab(self.building) then
426:             sheet.show_linked_buildings = false --DF will swap viewscreen
427:         end
428:     end)
429: end
430: 
431: function MechLinkOverlay:update_buttons()
432:     self:build_links_table()
433:     local scroll_pos = sheet.scroll_position_linked_buildings
434:     self.subviews.scroll:update(scroll_pos + 1, self.num_buttons*3, #self.links*3)
435:     self:scroll(0)
436: 
437:     local bci_len = #self.building.contained_items
438:     local h_offset = #self.links > self.num_buttons and 8 or 6 --account for scrollbar
439: 
440:     for i=1, self.num_buttons do
441:         local button = self:get_button(i, true)
442:         local offset = i*3 - 1 - ((scroll_pos + 1) % 3)
443:         local idx = self:idx_from_offset(offset)
444: 
445:         button.visible = false
446:         if idx > 0 and idx < bci_len then
447:             button.frame.t = offset
448:             button.frame.r = h_offset
449:             button.visible = true
450:         end
451:         button:updateLayout()
452: 
453:         local pbutton = self:get_pull_button(i, true)
454:         pbutton.visible = false
455:         local target = idx > 0 and idx < bci_len and
456:             get_mech_target(self.building.contained_items[idx].item)
457:         if is_lever(target) then
458:             local label, pen = get_pull_label(target)
459:             pbutton:setLabel(label)
460:             pbutton.label.text_pen = pen
461:             pbutton.frame.t = offset
462:             pbutton.frame.r = h_offset + 9
463:             pbutton.visible = true
464:         end
465:         pbutton:updateLayout()
466:     end
467: 
468:     local b = (self.frame.h % 3) == 1 and #self.links >= self.num_buttons and 0 or 1
469:     self.subviews.unlink_mode.frame.b = b --avoid overlapping list
470:     self.subviews.unlink_all.frame.b = b
471:     self.subviews.unlink_mode:updateLayout()
472:     self.subviews.unlink_all:updateLayout()
473: end
474: 
475: function MechLinkOverlay:preUpdateLayout(parent_rect)
476:     for i=1, self.num_buttons do --hide existing buttons
477:         local button = self:get_button(i)
478:         if button then
479:             button.visible = false
480:         end
481:         local pbutton = self:get_pull_button(i)
482:         if pbutton then
483:             pbutton.visible = false
484:         end
485:     end
486: 
487:     local h = parent_rect.height - 49
488:     self.frame.h = h + 1 --includes lower border
489:     self.num_buttons = h // 3
490: 
491:     self.subviews.scroll.frame.h = self.num_buttons*3
492: end
493: 
494: function MechLinkOverlay:onRenderFrame(dc, rect)
495:     if self.bld_id ~= sheet.viewing_bldid then
496:         self.bld_id = sheet.viewing_bldid
497:         self.building = df.building.find(self.bld_id)
498:     end
499: 
500:     self:update_buttons()
501: 
502:     MechLinkOverlay.super.onRenderFrame(self, dc, rect)
503: end

505: -- ----------------------
506: -- MechItemOverlay
507: --
508: 
509: MechItemOverlay = defclass(MechItemOverlay, overlay.OverlayWidget)
510: MechItemOverlay.ATTRS
511: {
512:     desc = "Allows freeing unlinked mechanisms from buildings.",
513:     default_enabled = true,
514:     default_pos = {x=-41, y=-4},
515:     frame = {w=56, h=27},
516:     viewscreens = {},
517: }
518: 
519: for _,v in ipairs(valid_build) do
520:     table.insert(MechItemOverlay.ATTRS.viewscreens, "dwarfmode/ViewSheets/BUILDING/"..v.."/Items")
521: end
522: valid_build = nil
523: 
524: function MechItemOverlay:init()
525:     self.num_buttons = 0
526: 
527:     self:addviews
528:     {
529:         widgets.HotkeyLabel
530:         {
531:             view_id = "free_all",
532:             frame = {b=1, r=3},
533:             key = "CUSTOM_SHIFT_F",
534:             label = "Free all",
535:             auto_width = true,
536:             on_activate = self:callback("ask_free_all"),
537:         },
538:     }
539: end
540: 
541: function MechItemOverlay:idx_from_offset(offset)
542:     if offset < 0 or offset >= self.num_buttons*3 then
543:         return 0 --outside of list
544:     else
545:         return (offset + sheet.scroll_position_item) // 3
546:     end
547: end
548: 
549: function MechItemOverlay:get_button(n, ensure)
550:     local button = self.subviews["free_"..n]
551:     if not button and ensure then
552:         self:addviews
553:         {
554:             widgets.TextButton
555:             {
556:                 view_id = "free_"..n,
557:                 frame = {t=0, r=21, w=6, h=1},
558:                 label = "Free",
559:                 on_activate = function() self:activate_button(n) end,
560:                 visible = false,
561:             },
562:         }
563:         button = self.subviews["free_"..n]
564:         button:updateLayout(self.frame_body)
565:     end
566: 
567:     return button
568: end
569: 
570: function MechItemOverlay:activate_button(n)
571:     local button = self:get_button(n)
572: 
573:     local idx = self:idx_from_offset(button.frame.t)
574:     if idx > 0 and idx < #self.building.contained_items then
575:         local item = self.building.contained_items[idx].item
576:         local target = get_mech_target(item)
577: 
578:         if target then
579:             dfhack.printerr(("MechItemOverlay: Mechanism %s still linked to %s!"):format(item, target))
580:         else
581:             local message = {
582:                 "Free mechanism and move it to the ground?",
583:                 NEWLINE, NEWLINE,
584:                 "This mechanism: ", {text=dfhack.items.getDescription(item, 0, true), pen=COLOR_BLUE},
585:             }
586:             confirm_action(self, "Free mechanism", message, 'always_free_single', function()
587:                 item.flags.in_building = false
588:                 dfhack.items.moveToGround(item, xyz2pos(dfhack.items.getPosition(item)))
589:             end)
590:         end
591:     else
592:         dfhack.printerr(("MechItemOverlay: Invalid button! Offset %d"):format(button.frame.t))
593:     end
594: end
595: 
596: function MechItemOverlay:ask_free_all()
597:     local message = {
598:         "Free all mechanisms and move them to the ground?",
599:         NEWLINE, NEWLINE,
600:         "This will recover all unlinked mechanisms from the building for reuse.",
601:     }
602:     confirm_action(self, "Free all mechanisms", message, 'always_free_all', function()
603:         for item in mech_iter(self.building) do
604:             if not get_mech_target(item) then
605:                 item.flags.in_building = false
606:                 dfhack.items.moveToGround(item, xyz2pos(dfhack.items.getPosition(item)))
607:             end
608:         end
609:     end)
610: end
611: 
612: function MechItemOverlay:update_buttons()
613:     local scroll_pos = sheet.scroll_position_item
614:     local bci_len = #self.building.contained_items
615:     local h_offset = bci_len > self.num_buttons and 21 or 19 --account for scrollbar
616: 
617:     for i=1, self.num_buttons do
618:         local button = self:get_button(i, true)
619:         local offset = i*3 - 1 - ((scroll_pos + 1) % 3)
620:         local idx = self:idx_from_offset(offset)
621: 
622:         button.visible = false
623:         if idx > 0 and idx < bci_len then
624:             local item = self.building.contained_items[idx].item
625:             if item._type == df.item_trappartsst and item.flags.in_building and
626:                 not item.flags.in_job and not get_trigger_index(item) then
627:                     button.frame.t = offset
628:                     button.frame.r = h_offset
629:                     button.visible = true
630:             end
631:         end
632:         button:updateLayout()
633:     end
634: 
635:     self.subviews.free_all.visible = false
636:     for item in mech_iter(self.building) do
637:         if not get_mech_target(item) then
638:             self.subviews.free_all.visible = true
639: 
640:             local b = (self.frame.h % 3) == 1 and bci_len >= self.num_buttons and 0 or 1
641:             self.subviews.free_all.frame.b = b --avoid overlapping list
642:             self.subviews.free_all:updateLayout()
643:             break
644:         end
645:     end
646: end
647: 
648: function MechItemOverlay:fix_layout()
649:     for i=1, self.num_buttons do --hide existing buttons
650:         local button = self:get_button(i)
651:         if button then
652:             button.visible = false
653:         end
654:     end
655: 
656:     local h = self.parent_height - 46 - (has_link_tab(self.building) and 3 or 0)
657:     self.frame.h = h + 1 --includes lower border
658:     self.num_buttons = h // 3
659: end
660: 
661: function MechItemOverlay:preUpdateLayout(parent_rect)
662:     self.parent_height = parent_rect.height
663:     self:fix_layout()
664: end
665: 
666: function MechItemOverlay:onRenderFrame(dc, rect)
667:     if self.bld_id ~= sheet.viewing_bldid then
668:         self.bld_id = sheet.viewing_bldid
669:         self.building = df.building.find(self.bld_id)
670:     end
671: 
672:     self:fix_layout()
673:     self:update_buttons()
674: 
675:     MechItemOverlay.super.onRenderFrame(self, dc, rect)
676: end
677: 
678: return _ENV

1: local _ENV = mkmodule('plugins.buildingplan')
2: 
3: --[[
4: 
5:  Public native functions:
6: 
7:  * bool isPlannableBuilding(df::building_type type, int16_t subtype, int32_t custom)
8:  * bool isPlannedBuilding(df::building *bld)
9:  * void addPlannedBuilding(df::building *bld)
10:  * void doCycle()
11:  * void scheduleCycle()
12:

15: local argparse = require('argparse')
16: local inspector = require('plugins.buildingplan.inspectoroverlay')
17: local mechanisms = require('plugins.buildingplan.mechanisms')
18: local pens = require('plugins.buildingplan.pens')
19: local planner = require('plugins.buildingplan.planneroverlay')
20: local unlink_mechanisms = require('plugins.buildingplan.unlink_mechanisms')
21: require('dfhack.buildings')
22: 
23: local function process_args(opts, args)
24:     if args[1] == 'help' then
25:         opts.help = true
26:         return
27:     end
28: 
29:     return argparse.processArgsGetopt(args, {
30:             {'h', 'help', handler=function() opts.help = true end},
31:         })
32: end
33: 
34: function parse_commandline(...)
35:     local args, opts = {...}, {}
36:     local positionals = process_args(opts, args)
37: 
38:     if opts.help then
39:         return false
40:     end
41: 
42:     local command = table.remove(positionals, 1)
43:     if not command or command == 'status' then
44:         printStatus()
45:     elseif command == 'set' and positionals then
46:         setSetting(positionals[1], positionals[2] == 'true')
47:     elseif command == 'reset' then
48:         resetFilters()
49:     elseif command == 'ignore' and positionals then
50:         setIgnoreBurrow(positionals[1] or "")
51:     else
52:         return false
53:     end
54: 
55:     return true
56: end
57: 
58: function is_suspendmanager_enabled()
59:     return require('plugins.suspendmanager').isEnabled()
60: end
61: 
62: -- The flag lives on the job, which exists as soon as the building is
63: -- designated, so it can be set before the job is unsuspended and it persists
64: -- with the save like any other job flag.
65: function setDoNow(bld, val)
66:     if not bld or #bld.jobs == 0 then return false end
67:     bld.jobs[0].flags.do_now = val
68:     return true
69: end
70: 
71: function getDoNow(bld)
72:     return bld ~= nil and #bld.jobs > 0 and bld.jobs[0].flags.do_now
73: end
74: 
75: function get_num_filters(btype, subtype, custom)
76:     local filters = dfhack.buildings.getFiltersByType({}, btype, subtype, custom)
77:     return filters and #filters or 0
78: end
79: 
80: function get_job_item(btype, subtype, custom, index)
81:     local filters = dfhack.buildings.getFiltersByType({}, btype, subtype, custom)
82:     if not filters or not filters[index] then return nil end
83:     local obj = df.job_item:new()
84:     obj:assign(filters[index])
85:     return obj
86: end
87: 
88: local function to_title_case(str)
89:     str = str:gsub('(%a)([%w_]*)',
90:         function (first, rest) return first:upper()..rest:lower() end)
91:     str = str:gsub('_', ' ')
92:     return str
93: end
94: 
95: function get_desc(filter)
96:     local desc = 'Unknown'
97:     if filter.has_tool_use and filter.has_tool_use > -1 then
98:         desc = to_title_case(df.tool_uses[filter.has_tool_use])
99:     elseif filter.flags2 and filter.flags2.screw then
100:         desc = 'Screw'
101:     elseif filter.item_type and filter.item_type > -1 then
102:         desc = to_title_case(df.item_type[filter.item_type])
103:     elseif filter.vector_id and filter.vector_id > -1 then
104:         desc = to_title_case(df.job_item_vector_id[filter.vector_id])
105:     elseif filter.flags2 and filter.flags2.building_material then
106:         if filter.flags2.magma_safe then
107:             desc = 'Magma-safe material'
108:         elseif filter.flags2.fire_safe then
109:             desc = 'Fire-safe material'
110:         else
111:             desc = 'Building material'
112:         end
113:     end
114: 
115:     if desc:endswith('s') then
116:         desc = desc:sub(1,-2)
117:     end
118:     if desc == 'Trappart' then
119:         desc = 'Mechanism'
120:     elseif desc == 'Wood' then
121:         desc = 'Log'
122:     elseif desc == 'Any weapon' then
123:         desc = 'Weapon'
124:     elseif desc == 'Any spike' then
125:         desc = 'Spike'
126:     elseif desc == 'Ballistapart' then
127:         desc = 'Ballista part'
128:     elseif desc == 'Catapultpart' then
129:         desc = 'Catapult part'
130:     elseif desc == 'Smallgem' then
131:         desc = 'Small, cut gem'
132:     end
133: 
134:     return desc
135: end
136: 
137: function reload_pens()
138:     pens.reload_pens()
139: end
140: 
141: function signal_reset()
142:     planner.reset_counts_flag = true
143:     inspector.reset_inspector_flag = true
144: end
145: 
146: -- for use during development to reload all buildingplan modules
147: function reload_modules()
148:     reload('plugins.buildingplan.pens')
149:     reload('plugins.buildingplan.filterselection')
150:     reload('plugins.buildingplan.itemselection')
151:     reload('plugins.buildingplan.planneroverlay')
152:     reload('plugins.buildingplan.inspectoroverlay')
153:     reload('plugins.buildingplan.mechanisms')
154:     reload('plugins.buildingplan.unlink_mechanisms')
155:     reload('plugins.buildingplan')
156: end

SOURCE D:/program/steam/steamapps/common/DFHack/hack/lua/plugins/burrow.lua
1: local _ENV = mkmodule('plugins.burrow')
2: 
3: local argparse = require('argparse')
4: local overlay = require('plugins.overlay')
5: local widgets = require('gui.widgets')

7: ---------------------------------
8: -- BurrowDesignationOverlay
9: --
10: 
11: local selection_rect = df.global.selection_rect
12: local if_burrow = df.global.game.main_interface.burrow
13: 
14: local function is_choosing_area(pos)
15:     return if_burrow.doing_rectangle and
16:         selection_rect.start_z >= 0 and
17:         (pos or dfhack.gui.getMousePos())
18: end
19: 
20: local function reset_selection_rect()
21:     selection_rect.start_x = -30000
22:     selection_rect.start_y = -30000
23:     selection_rect.start_z = -30000
24: end
25: 
26: local function clamp(pos)
27:     return xyz2pos(
28:         math.max(0, math.min(df.global.world.map.x_count-1, pos.x)),
29:         math.max(0, math.min(df.global.world.map.y_count-1, pos.y)),
30:         math.max(0, math.min(df.global.world.map.z_count-1, pos.z)))
31: end
32: 
33: local function get_bounds(pos1, pos2)
34:     pos1 = clamp(pos1 or dfhack.gui.getMousePos(true))
35:     pos2 = clamp(pos2 or xyz2pos(selection_rect.start_x, selection_rect.start_y, selection_rect.start_z))
36:     return {
37:         x1=math.min(pos1.x, pos2.x),
38:         x2=math.max(pos1.x, pos2.x),
39:         y1=math.min(pos1.y, pos2.y),
40:         y2=math.max(pos1.y, pos2.y),
41:         z1=math.min(pos1.z, pos2.z),
42:         z2=math.max(pos1.z, pos2.z),
43:     }
44: end
45: 
46: local function get_cur_area_dims()
47:     local bounds = get_bounds()
48:     return bounds.x2 - bounds.x1 + 1,
49:             bounds.y2 - bounds.y1 + 1,
50:             bounds.z2 - bounds.z1 + 1
51: end
52: 
53: BurrowDesignationOverlay = defclass(BurrowDesignationOverlay, overlay.OverlayWidget)
54: BurrowDesignationOverlay.ATTRS{
55:     desc='Adds flood fill and 3D box select functionality to burrow designations.',
56:     default_pos={x=6,y=9},
57:     viewscreens='dwarfmode/Burrow/Paint',
58:     default_enabled=true,
59:     frame={w=53, h=1},
60: }
61: 
62: function BurrowDesignationOverlay:init()
63:     self:addviews{
64:         widgets.BannerPanel{
65:             frame={t=0, l=0},
66:             subviews={
67:                 widgets.CycleHotkeyLabel{
68:                     view_id='fill',
69:                     frame={t=0, l=1, r=1},
70:                     key='CUSTOM_CTRL_F',
71:                     label='Flood fill on double click:',
72:                     options={
73:                         {label='Off', value='off', pen=COLOR_RED},
74:                         {label='2D fill enabled', value='2d', pen=COLOR_GREEN},
75:                         {label='3D fill enabled', value='3d', pen=COLOR_LIGHTGREEN},
76:                     },
77:                 },
78:             },
79:         },
80:     }
81: end
82: 
83: local function flood_fill(pos, erasing, do_3d, painting_burrow)
84:     local opts = {zlevel=not do_3d}
85:     if erasing then
86:         burrow_tiles_flood_remove(painting_burrow, pos, opts)
87:     else
88:         burrow_tiles_flood_add(painting_burrow, pos, opts)
89:     end
90:     reset_selection_rect()
91: end
92: 
93: function BurrowDesignationOverlay:onInput(keys)
94:     if self:inputToSubviews(keys) then
95:         return true
96:     -- don't perform burrow modifications immediately -- painting_burrow may not yet
97:     -- have been initialized. instead, allow clicks to go through so that vanilla
98:     -- behavior is triggered before we modify the burrow further
99:     elseif keys._MOUSE_L then
100:         local pos = dfhack.gui.getMousePos(true)
101:         if pos then
102:             local fill = self.subviews.fill:getOptionValue()
103:             local now_ms = dfhack.getTickCount()
104:             if not same_xyz(pos, self.saved_pos) then
105:                 self.last_click_ms = now_ms
106:                 self.saved_pos = pos
107:             elseif fill ~= 'off' then
108:                 if now_ms - self.last_click_ms <= widgets.getDoubleClickMs() then
109:                     self.last_click_ms = 0
110:                     local do_3d = fill == '3d'
111:                     self.pending_fn = curry(flood_fill, pos, if_burrow.erasing, do_3d)
112:                     return
113:                 else
114:                     self.last_click_ms = now_ms
115:                 end
116:             end
117:             if is_choosing_area(pos) then
118:                 self.last_click_ms = 0
119:                 return
120:             end
121:         end
122:     end
123: end
124: 
125: function BurrowDesignationOverlay:onRenderBody(dc)
126:     BurrowDesignationOverlay.super.onRenderBody(self, dc)
127:     local pending_fn = self.pending_fn
128:     self.pending_fn = nil
129:     if pending_fn and if_burrow.painting_burrow then
130:         pending_fn(if_burrow.painting_burrow)
131:     end
132: end
133: 
134: OVERLAY_WIDGETS = {
135:     designation=BurrowDesignationOverlay,
136: }
137: 
138: rawset_default(_ENV, dfhack.burrows)
139:

141: -- commandline handling
142: --
143: 
144: local function set_add_remove(mode, which, params, _)
145:     local target_burrow = table.remove(params, 1)
146:     _ENV[('burrow_%s_%s'):format(mode, which)](target_burrow, params)
147: end
148: 
149: local function tiles_box_add_remove(which, params, opts)
150:     local target_burrow = table.remove(params, 1)
151:     local pos1 = argparse.coords(params[1] or 'here', 'pos')
152:     local pos2 = opts.cursor or argparse.coords(params[2] or 'here', 'pos')
153:     local bounds = get_bounds(pos1, pos2)
154:     _ENV['burrow_tiles_box_'..which](target_burrow, bounds)
155: end
156: 
157: local function tiles_flood_add_remove(which, params, opts)
158:     local target_burrow = table.remove(params, 1)
159:     local pos = opts.cursor or argparse.coords('here', 'pos')
160:     _ENV['burrow_tiles_flood_'..which](target_burrow, pos, opts)
161: end
162: 
163: local function run_command(mode, command, params, opts)
164:     if mode == 'tiles' then
165:         if command == 'clear' then
166:             burrow_tiles_clear(params)
167:         elseif command == 'set' or command == 'add' or command == 'remove' then
168:             set_add_remove('tiles', command, params, opts)
169:         elseif command == 'box-add' or command == 'box-remove' then
170:             tiles_box_add_remove(command:sub(5), params, opts)
171:         elseif command == 'flood-add' or command == 'flood-remove' then
172:             tiles_flood_add_remove(command:sub(7), params, opts)
173:         else
174:             return false
175:         end
176:     elseif mode == 'units' then
177:         if command == 'clear' then
178:             burrow_units_clear(params)
179:         elseif command == 'set' or command == 'add' or command == 'remove' then
180:             set_add_remove('units', command, params, opts)
181:         else
182:             return false
183:         end
184:     else
185:         return false
186:     end
187:     return true
188: end
189: 
190: function parse_commandline(...)
191:     local args, opts = {...}, {}
192: 
193:     if args[1] == 'help' then
194:         return false
195:     end
196: 
197:     local positionals = argparse.processArgsGetopt(args, {
198:             {'c', 'cursor', hasArg=true,
199:              handler=function(optarg) opts.cursor = argparse.coords(optarg, 'cursor') end},
200:             {'h', 'help', handler=function() opts.help = true end},
201:             {'z', 'cur-zlevel', handler=function() opts.zlevel = true end},
202:         })
203: 
204:     if opts.help then
205:         return false
206:     end
207: 
208:     local mode = table.remove(positionals, 1)
209:     local command = table.remove(positionals, 1)
210:     local ret = run_command(mode, command, positionals, opts)
211: 
212:     if not ret then return false end
213: 
214:     print('done')
215:     return true
216: end
217: 
218: return _ENV

294: local function get_bounds()
295:     local pos1 = dfhack.gui.getMousePos(true)
296:     if not pos1 then return end
297:     pos1 = xyz2pos(
298:         math.max(0, math.min(df.global.world.map.x_count-1, pos1.x)),
299:         math.max(0, math.min(df.global.world.map.y_count-1, pos1.y)),
300:         math.max(0, math.min(df.global.world.map.z_count-1, pos1.z)))
301:     local pos2 = xyz2pos(
302:         math.max(0, math.min(df.global.world.map.x_count-1, selection_rect.start_x)),
303:         math.max(0, math.min(df.global.world.map.y_count-1, selection_rect.start_y)),
304:         math.max(0, math.min(df.global.world.map.z_count-1, selection_rect.start_z)))
305:     local bounds = {
306:         x1=math.min(pos1.x, pos2.x),
307:         x2=math.max(pos1.x, pos2.x),
308:         y1=math.min(pos1.y, pos2.y),
309:         y2=math.max(pos1.y, pos2.y),
310:         z1=math.min(pos1.z, pos2.z),
311:         z2=math.max(pos1.z, pos2.z),
312:     }
313: 
314:     return bounds
315: end
316: 
317: function WarmDampOverlay:onInput(keys)
318:     if WarmDampOverlay.super.onInput(self, keys) then
319:         return true
320:     end
321:     if main_if.main_designation_doing_rectangles then
322:         if keys._MOUSE_L and selection_rect.start_z >= 0 then
323:             local bounds = get_bounds()
324:             if bounds then
325:                 if self.pending_fn then
326:                     self.pending_fn()
327:                 end
328:                 self.pending_fn = function() registerWarmDampBox(bounds) end
329:             end
330:         end
331:     else
332:         if keys._MOUSE_L_DOWN then
333:             local pos = dfhack.gui.getMousePos()
334:             if pos then
335:                 registerWarmDampTile(pos)
336:             end
337:         end
338:     end
339: end
340: 
341: function WarmDampOverlay:onRenderFrame(dc, rect)
342:     WarmDampOverlay.super.onRenderFrame(self, dc, rect)
343:     if self.pending_fn then
344:         self.pending_fn()
345:         self.pending_fn = nil
346:     end
347:     paintScreenWarmDamp()
348: end

25: function WeatherWidget:overlay_onupdate()
26:     local rain, snow = false, false
27:     local cw = df.global.current_weather
28:     for i=0,4 do
29:         for j=0,4 do
30:             weather = cw[i][j]
31:             if weather == df.weather_type.Rain then rain = true end
32:             if weather == df.weather_type.Snow then snow = true end
33:         end
34:     end
35:     self.frame.w = (rain and 4 or 0) + (snow and 4 or 0) +
36:             ((snow and rain) and 1 or 0)
37:     self.rain, self.snow = rain, snow
38: end

40: function WeatherWidget:onRenderBody(dc)
41:     if self.rain then dc:string('Rain', COLOR_LIGHTBLUE):advance(1) end

43: end

50:     local ok, config = pcall(json.decode_file, DWARFMONITOR_CONFIG_FILE)
51:     if not ok or not config.date_format then
52:         return 'Y-M-D'

57: DateWidget = defclass(DateWidget, overlay.OverlayWidget)
58: DateWidget.ATTRS{
59:     default_pos={x=-16,y=1},
60:     viewscreens={'dungeonmode', 'dwarfmode'},
61: }
62: 
63: function DateWidget:init()
64:     self.datestr = ''
65:     self.fmt = get_date_format()
66: end
67: 
68: function DateWidget:overlay_onupdate()
69:     local year = dfhack.world.ReadCurrentYear()
70:     local month = dfhack.world.ReadCurrentMonth() + 1
71:     local day = dfhack.world.ReadCurrentDay()
72: 
73:     local fmt = self.fmt
74:     local datestr = 'Date:'
75:     for i=1,#fmt do
76:         local c = fmt:sub(i, i)
77:         if c == 'y' or c == 'Y' then
78:             datestr = datestr .. year
79:         elseif c == 'm' or c == 'M' then
80:             if c == 'M' and month < 10 then
81:                 datestr = datestr .. '0'
82:             end
83:             datestr = datestr .. month
84:         elseif c == 'd' or c == 'D' then
85:             if c == 'D' and day < 10 then
86:                 datestr = datestr .. '0'
87:             end
88:             datestr = datestr .. day
89:         else
90:             datestr = datestr .. c
91:         end
92:     end
93: 
94:     self.frame.w = #datestr
95:     self.datestr = datestr
96: end
97: 
98: function DateWidget:onRenderBody(dc)
99:     dc:string(self.datestr, COLOR_GREY)
100: end

118: function MiseryWidget:overlay_onupdate()
119:     local counts, num_colors = {}, #self.colors
120:     for _,unit in ipairs(dfhack.units.getCitizens(true, true)) do
121:         local stress_category = math.min(num_colors,
122:                                          dfhack.units.getStressCategory(unit)+1)
123:         counts[stress_category] = (counts[stress_category] or 0) + 1
124:     end
125: 
126:     local width = 2 + num_colors - 1 -- 'H:' plus the slashes
127:     for i=1,num_colors do
128:         width = width + #tostring(counts[i] or 0)
129:     end
130: 
131:     self.stress_category_counts = counts
132:     self.frame.w = width
133: end
134: 
135: function MiseryWidget:onRenderBody(dc)
136:     dc:string('H:', COLOR_WHITE)
137:     local counts = self.stress_category_counts
138:     for i,color in ipairs(self.colors) do
139:         dc:string(tostring(counts[i] or 0), color)
140:         if i < #self.colors then dc:string('/', COLOR_WHITE) end
141:     end
142: end

182: function CursorWidget:onRenderBody(dc)
183:     local text = {}
184:     for k, v in pairs(CursorWidget.COORD_TYPES) do
185:         -- fall back to 'all' behavior if type isn't recognized
186:         if k == self.type or not CursorWidget.COORD_TYPES[self.type] then
187:             local coords = v.get_coords()
188:             if coords then
189:                 local prefix = self.short and '' or (v.description .. ' ')
190:                 local coord_str = table.concat({coords.x, coords.y, coords.z}, ',')
191:                 table.insert(text, ('%s(%s)'):format(prefix, coord_str))
192:             end
193:         end
194:     end
195: 
196:     local width = 0
197:     for i,line in ipairs(text) do
198:         dc:seek(0, i-1):string(line)
199:         width = math.max(width, #line)
200:     end
201:     self.frame.w = width
202:     self.frame.h = #text
203: end
204: 
205: -- register our widgets with the overlay
206: OVERLAY_WIDGETS = {
207:     cursor=CursorWidget,
208:     date=DateWidget,
209:     misery=MiseryWidget,
210:     weather=WeatherWidget,
211: }
212: 
213: return _ENV

135: -- ---- --
136: -- Menu --
137: -- ---- --

139: local ARROW = string.char(26)
140: local MAX_LIST_WIDTH = 45
141: local MAX_LIST_HEIGHT = 15
142: 
143: Menu = defclass(Menu, widgets.Panel)
144: Menu.ATTRS{
145:     hotspot=DEFAULT_NIL,
146: }
147: 
148: -- get a map from the binding string to a list of hotkey strings that all
149: -- point to that binding
150: local function get_bindings_to_hotkeys(hotkeys, bindings)
151:     local bindings_to_hotkeys = {}
152:     for _,hotkey in ipairs(hotkeys) do
153:         local binding = bindings[hotkey]
154:         table.insert(ensure_key(bindings_to_hotkeys, binding), hotkey)
155:     end
156:     return bindings_to_hotkeys
157: end
158: 
159: -- number of non-text tiles: icon, space between cmd and hk, scrollbar+margin
160: local LIST_BUFFER = 2 + 1 + 3
161: 
162: local function get_choices(hotkeys, bindings, is_inverted)
163:     local choices, max_width, seen = {}, 0, {}
164:     local bindings_to_hotkeys = get_bindings_to_hotkeys(hotkeys, bindings)
165: 
166:     -- build list choices
167:     for _,hotkey in ipairs(hotkeys) do
168:         local command = bindings[hotkey]
169:         if seen[command] then goto continue end
170:         seen[command] = true
171:         local hk_width, tokens = 0, {}
172:         for _,hk in ipairs(bindings_to_hotkeys[command]) do
173:             if hk_width ~= 0 then
174:                 table.insert(tokens, ', ')
175:                 hk_width = hk_width + 2
176:             end
177:             table.insert(tokens, {text=hk, pen=COLOR_LIGHTGREEN})
178:             hk_width = hk_width + #hk
179:         end
180:         local command_str = command
181:         if hk_width + #command + LIST_BUFFER > MAX_LIST_WIDTH then
182:             local max_command_len = MAX_LIST_WIDTH - hk_width - LIST_BUFFER
183:             command_str = command:sub(1, max_command_len - 3) .. '...'
184:         end
185:         table.insert(tokens, 1, {text=command_str})
186:         local choice = {icon=ARROW, command=command, text=tokens,
187:                         hk_width=hk_width}
188:         max_width = math.max(max_width, hk_width + #command_str + LIST_BUFFER)
189:         table.insert(choices, is_inverted and 1 or #choices + 1, choice)
190:         ::continue::
191:     end
192: 
193:     -- adjust width of command fields so the hotkey tokens are right justified
194:     for _,choice in ipairs(choices) do
195:         local command_token = choice.text[1]
196:         command_token.width = max_width - choice.hk_width - (LIST_BUFFER - 1)
197:     end
198: 
199:     return choices, max_width
200: end
201: 
202: function Menu:init()
203:     local hotkeys, bindings = getHotkeys()
204:     if #hotkeys == 0 then
205:         hotkeys = {''}
206:         bindings = {['']='gui/launcher'}
207:     end
208: 
209:     local is_inverted = not not self.hotspot.frame.b
210:     local choices,list_width = get_choices(hotkeys, bindings, is_inverted)
211: 
212:     list_width = math.max(35, list_width)
213: 
214:     local list_frame = copyall(self.hotspot.frame)
215:     local list_widget_frame = {h=math.min(#choices, MAX_LIST_HEIGHT)}
216:     local quickstart_frame = {}
217:     list_frame.w = list_width + 2
218:     list_frame.h = list_widget_frame.h + 4
219:     if list_frame.t then
220:         list_frame.t = math.max(0, list_frame.t - 1)
221:         list_widget_frame.t = 0
222:         quickstart_frame.b = 0
223:     else
224:         list_frame.b = math.max(0, list_frame.b - 1)
225:         list_widget_frame.b = 0
226:         quickstart_frame.t = 0
227:     end
228:     if list_frame.l then
229:         list_frame.l = math.max(0, list_frame.l + 5)
230:     else
231:         list_frame.r = math.max(0, list_frame.r + 5)
232:     end
233: 
234:     local help_frame = {w=list_frame.w, l=list_frame.l, r=list_frame.r}
235:     if list_frame.t then
236:         help_frame.t = list_frame.t + list_frame.h
237:     else
238:         help_frame.b = list_frame.b + list_frame.h
239:     end
240: 
241:     self:addviews{
242:         widgets.Panel{
243:             view_id='list_panel',
244:             frame=list_frame,
245:             frame_style=gui.PANEL_FRAME,
246:             frame_background=gui.CLEAR_PEN,
247:             subviews={
248:                 widgets.List{
249:                     view_id='list',
250:                     frame=list_widget_frame,
251:                     choices=choices,
252:                     icon_width=2,
253:                     on_select=self:callback('onSelect'),
254:                     on_submit=self:callback('onSubmit'),
255:                     on_submit2=self:callback('onSubmit2'),
256:                 },
257:                 widgets.Panel{frame={h=1}},
258:                 widgets.HotkeyLabel{
259:                     frame=quickstart_frame,
260:                     label='Quickstart guide',
261:                     key='STRING_A063',
262:                     on_activate=function()
263:                         self:onSubmit(nil, {command='quickstart-guide'})
264:                     end,
265:                 },
266:             },
267:         },
268:         widgets.ResizingPanel{
269:             view_id='help_panel',
270:             autoarrange_subviews=true,
271:             frame=help_frame,
272:             frame_style=gui.PANEL_FRAME,
273:             frame_background=gui.CLEAR_PEN,
274:             subviews={
275:                 widgets.WrappedLabel{
276:                     view_id='help',
277:                     text_to_wrap='',
278:                     scroll_keys={},
279:                 },
280:             },
281:         },
282:     }
283: 
284:     self.initialize = function()
285:         self.subviews.list:setSelected(is_inverted and #choices or 1)
286:     end
287: end
288: 
289: function Menu:onSelect(_, choice)
290:     if not choice or #self.subviews == 0 then return end
291:     local command = get_command(choice.command)
292:     self.subviews.help.text_to_wrap = helpdb.is_entry(command) and
293:             helpdb.get_entry_short_help(command) or 'Command not found'
294:     self.subviews.help_panel:updateLayout()
295: end
296: 
297: function Menu:onSubmit(_, choice)
298:     if not choice then return end
299:     dfhack.screen.hideGuard(self.parent_view, dfhack.run_command, choice.command)
300:     self.parent_view:dismiss()
301: end
302: 
303: function Menu:onSubmit2(_, choice)
304:     if not choice then return end
305:     self.parent_view:dismiss()
306:     dfhack.run_script('gui/launcher', choice.command)
307: end
308: 
309: function Menu:onInput(keys)
310:     if keys.LEAVESCREEN or keys._MOUSE_R then
311:         return false
312:     elseif keys.KEYBOARD_CURSOR_RIGHT then
313:         self:onSubmit2(self.subviews.list:getSelected())
314:         return true
315:     elseif keys._MOUSE_L then
316:         local list = self.subviews.list
317:         local x = list:getMousePos()
318:         if x == 0 then -- clicked on icon
319:             self:onSubmit2(list:getSelected())
320:             return true
321:         end
322:         if not self:getMouseFramePos() then
323:             self.parent_view:dismiss()
324:             return true
325:         end
326:     end
327:     self:inputToSubviews(keys)
328:     return true -- we're modal
329: end
330: 
331: function Menu:onRenderFrame(dc, rect)
332:     if self.initialize then
333:         self.initialize()
334:         self.initialize = nil
335:     end
336:     Menu.super.onRenderFrame(self, dc, rect)
337: end
338: 
339: function Menu:getMouseFramePos()
340:     return self.subviews.list_panel:getMouseFramePos() or
341:             self.subviews.help_panel:getMouseFramePos()
342: end
343: 
344: function Menu:onRenderBody(dc)
345:     Menu.super.onRenderBody(self, dc)
346:     local list = self.subviews.list
347:     local idx = list:getIdxUnderMouse()
348:     if idx and idx ~= self.last_mouse_idx then
349:         -- focus follows mouse, but if cursor keys were used to change the
350:         -- selection, don't override the selection until the mouse moves to
351:         -- another item
352:         list:setSelected(idx)
353:         self.last_mouse_idx = idx
354:     end
355: end
356: 
357: -- ---------- --
358: -- MenuScreen --
359: -- ---------- --
360: 
361: MenuScreen = defclass(MenuScreen, gui.ZScreen)
362: MenuScreen.ATTRS {
363:     focus_path='hotkeys/menu',
364:     initial_pause=false,
365:     hotspot=DEFAULT_NIL,
366: }
367: 
368: function MenuScreen:init()
369:     self:addviews{
370:         Menu{
371:             frame=gui.get_interface_frame(),
372:             hotspot=self.hotspot,
373:         },
374:     }
375: end
376: 
377: function MenuScreen:onDismiss()
378:     cleanupHotkeys()
379: end
380: 
381: return _ENV
