1: local _ENV = mkmodule('plugins.logistics')
2: 
3: local argparse = require('argparse')
4: local gui = require('gui')
5: local overlay = require('plugins.overlay')
6: local utils = require('utils')
7: local widgets = require('gui.widgets')
8: 
9: -------------------------------------
10: -- logistics status console output
11: --
12: 
13: local function make_stat(desig, sp_number, stats, configs)
14:     local stat = {
15:         enabled=(configs[sp_number] ~= nil) and (configs[sp_number][desig] == 'true'),
16:         designated=stats[desig..'_designated'][sp_number] or 0,
17:         can_designate=stats[desig..'_can_designate'][sp_number] or 0,
18:         designatable=nil,
19:     }
20:     stat.designatable = stat.designated + stat.can_designate
21:     return stat
22: end
23: 
24: function getStockpileData()
25:     local stats, configs = logistics_getStockpileData()
26:     local data={sp_stats={}, sp_totals={}, sp_name_max_len=0}
27:     data.sp_totals = {
28:         melt={enabled_count=0, designated=0, designatable=0},
29:         trade={enabled_count=0, designated=0, designatable=0},
30:         dump={enabled_count=0, designated=0, designatable=0},
31:         train={enabled_count=0, designated=0, designatable=0},
32:         forbid={enabled_count=0, designated=0, designatable=0},
33:         claim={enabled_count=0}
34:     }
35:     local function inc_totals(total, sp)
36:         if sp.enabled then total.enabled_count = total.enabled_count + 1 end
37:         if nil == total.designated or nil == total.designatable then return end
38:         total.designated = total.designated + sp.designated
39:         total.designatable = total.designatable + sp.designatable
40:     end
41: 
42:     for i,bld in ipairs(df.global.world.buildings.other.STOCKPILE) do
43:         local sp_number, sp_name = bld.stockpile_number, bld.name
44:         local sort_key = tostring(sp_name):lower()
45:         if #sp_name == 0 then
46:             sp_name = ('Stockpile #%d'):format(sp_number)
47:             sort_key = ('stockpile #%09d'):format(sp_number)
48:         end
49:         data.sp_name_max_len = math.max(data.sp_name_max_len, #sp_name)
50:         table.insert(data.sp_stats, {
51:             sp_number=sp_number,
52:             sp_name=sp_name,
53:             sort_key=sort_key,
54:             melt=make_stat('melt', sp_number, stats, configs),
55:             trade=make_stat('trade', sp_number, stats, configs),
56:             dump=make_stat('dump', sp_number, stats, configs),
57:             train=make_stat('train', sp_number, stats, configs),
58:             forbid=make_stat('forbid', sp_number, stats, configs),
59:             claim=make_stat('claim', sp_number, stats, configs),
60:             melt_masterworks=(configs[sp_number] ~= nil) and (configs[sp_number].melt_masterworks == 'true'),
61:         })
62: 
63:         local j = i+1
64:         inc_totals(data.sp_totals.melt, data.sp_stats[j].melt)
65:         inc_totals(data.sp_totals.trade, data.sp_stats[j].trade)
66:         inc_totals(data.sp_totals.dump, data.sp_stats[j].dump)
67:         inc_totals(data.sp_totals.train, data.sp_stats[j].train)
68:         inc_totals(data.sp_totals.forbid, data.sp_stats[j].forbid)
69:         inc_totals(data.sp_totals.claim, data.sp_stats[j].claim)
70:     end
71: 
72:     table.sort(data.sp_stats, function(a, b) return a.sort_key < b.sort_key end)
73:     return data
74: end
75: 
76: local function print_stockpile_data(data)
77:     -- setup stockpile summary table size and format
78:     local totals_row_label = 'Total Stockpiled Items'
79:     local name_len = math.min(40, math.max(data.sp_name_max_len, #totals_row_label))
80: 
81:     -- --          sp.#  sp.name                    '  melt'     trade       dump      train      forbid  ' -- --
82:     local fmt =  ' %6s  %-' .. name_len .. 's  ' .. '%-6s%-11s  %-6s%-11s  %-6s%-11s  %-6s%-11s  %-6s%-11s' -- --
83:     local col_labels = fmt:format('number' , 'name',
84:     -- --                                           '  [x] ' '#### / ####'                                  -- --
85:                                                     '  melt','   items',
86:                                                     ' trade','   items',
87:                                                     '  dump','   items',
88:                                                     ' train','   items',
89:                                                     ' forbid','  items')
90:     -- --                                           '------' '-----------'                                  -- --
91:     local function uline(len) return ('-'):rep(len) end
92:     local ulines = fmt:format(uline(6), uline(name_len),
93:                                                     uline(6), uline(11),  -- melt
94:                                                     uline(6), uline(11),  -- trade
95:                                                     uline(6), uline(11),  -- dump
96:                                                     uline(6), uline(11),  -- train
97:                                                     uline(6), uline(11))  -- forbid
98:     -- --                                           '------' '-----------'                                  -- --
99:     local totals = data.sp_totals
100:     -- --                                           '  [#] ' '#### / ####'                                  -- --
101:     local tbl_ln_len = name_len + 105
102:     --  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------  -- --
103:     local has_melt_mastworks, has_claim_enabled = false, false
104: 
105:     -- print stockpiles summary table header
106:     local function space(len) return (' '):rep(len) end
107:     print('Stockpiles' .. space(name_len) .. space(1) .. '[Enabled] Auto-Designations' .. space(11) .. 'Designated / Designatable Item Counts')
108:     print(uline(tbl_ln_len))
109:     print(col_labels)
110:     print(ulines)
111: 
112:     -- print stockpiles summary table data
113:     local function get_enab(stats, ch) return ('  [%s]'):format(stats.enabled and (ch or 'x') or ' ') end
114:     local function get_dstat(stats) return ('%4d / %-4d'):format(stats.designated, stats.designatable) end
115:     for _,sp in ipairs(data.sp_stats) do
116:         has_melt_mastworks = has_melt_mastworks or sp.melt_masterworks
117:         has_claim_enabled = has_claim_enabled or sp.claim.enabled
118:         local forbid_or_claim = (sp.claim.enabled and sp.claim) or (sp.forbid.enabled and sp.forbid) or sp.forbid
119:         print(fmt:format(sp.sp_number, sp.sp_name,
120:                 get_enab(sp.melt, sp.melt_masterworks and 'M' or 'm'), get_dstat(sp.melt),
121:                 get_enab(sp.trade,'t'), get_dstat(sp.trade),
122:                 get_enab(sp.dump,'d'), get_dstat(sp.dump),
123:                 get_enab(sp.train,'a'), get_dstat(sp.train),
124:                 get_enab(forbid_or_claim, (sp.claim.enabled and 'C') or (sp.forbid.enabled and 'f')), get_dstat(sp.forbid)))
125:     end
126: 
127:     -- print stockpile summary table footer
128:     print(ulines)
129:     print(fmt:format('', totals_row_label,
130:             '', get_dstat(totals.melt),
131:             '', get_dstat(totals.trade),
132:             '', get_dstat(totals.dump),
133:             '', get_dstat(totals.train),
134:             '', get_dstat(totals.forbid)))
135:     print(uline(tbl_ln_len))
136:     if has_melt_mastworks or has_claim_enabled then
137:         print()
138:         if has_melt_mastworks then print('[M] in the "melt" column indicates that masterworks in the stockpile will be melted.') end
139:         if has_claim_enabled then print('[C] in the "forbid" column indicates that items in the stockpile will be claimed (unforbidden).') end
140:     end
141: end
142: 
143: local function print_status()
144:     -- print plugin enabled/disabled status
145:     print('logistics status')
146:     print((' - %sactively monitoring stockpiles and designating items')
147:             :format(isEnabled() and '' or 'not '))
148:     if df.global.gamemode ~= df.game_mode.DWARF or not dfhack.isMapLoaded() or not dfhack.isSiteLoaded() then
149:         return
150:     end
151:     print((' - %sauto-retraining partially trained animals')
152:             :format(logistics_getFeature('autoretrain') and '' or 'not '))
153: 
154:     -- print stockpile logistics configuration and summary
155:     local data = getStockpileData()
156:     print()
157:     if not data.sp_stats[1] then
158:         print 'No stockpiles configured'
159:     else
160:         print_stockpile_data(data)
161:     end
162: 
163:     -- print fortress logistics summary of all items
164:     local global_sp_stats = logistics_getGlobalCounts()
165:     print()
166:     print('Total fortress items:')
167:     print((' - Items designated for melting: %5d'):format(global_sp_stats.total_melt))
168:     print((' - Items designated for trading: %5d'):format(global_sp_stats.total_trade))
169:     print((' - Items designated for dumping: %5d'):format(global_sp_stats.total_dump))
170:     print((' - Animals designated for train: %5d'):format(global_sp_stats.total_train))  -- TODO: fix to include all animals designated for training
171:     print((' - Forbidden items             : %5d'):format(global_sp_stats.total_forbid)) -- TODO: this should exclude unreachable items
172:     print((' - All (unforbidden) items     : %5d'):format(global_sp_stats.total_claim))  -- TODO: same ^^ implement in logistics.cpp or use `item` script api?
173: end
174: 
175: --------------------------
176: -- configure stockpiles
177: --
178: 
179: local function for_stockpiles(opts, fn)
180:     if not opts.sp then
181:         local selected_sp = dfhack.gui.getSelectedStockpile()
182:         if not selected_sp then qerror('please specify or select a stockpile') end
183:         fn(selected_sp.stockpile_number)
184:         return
185:     end
186:     for _,sp in ipairs(argparse.stringList(opts.sp)) do
187:         fn(sp)
188:     end
189: end
190: 
191: local function do_add_stockpile_config(features, opts)
192:     for_stockpiles(opts, function(sp)
193:         local configs = logistics_getStockpileConfigs(tonumber(sp) or sp)
194:         if not configs then
195:             dfhack.printerr('invalid stockpile: '..sp)
196:         else
197:             for _,config in ipairs(configs) do
198:                 features.claim = features.claim or features.unforbid -- accept 'add unforbid' in addition to 'add claim'
199:                 logistics_setStockpileConfig(config.stockpile_number,
200:                         features.melt and true or config.melt == 1,
201:                         features.trade and true or config.trade == 1,
202:                         features.dump and true or config.dump == 1,
203:                         features.train and true or config.train == 1,
204:                         (features.forbid and 1) or (features.claim and 2) or config.forbid,
205:                         not not opts.melt_masterworks)
206:             end
207:         end
208:     end)
209: end
210: 
211: local function do_clear_stockpile_config(all, opts)
212:     if all then
213:         logistics_clearAllStockpileConfigs()
214:         return
215:     end
216:     for_stockpiles(opts, function(sp)
217:         logistics_clearStockpileConfig(tonumber(sp) or sp)
218:     end)
219: end
220: 
221: local function do_set_feature(enabled, feature)
222:     if not logistics_setFeature(enabled, feature) then
223:         qerror(('unknown feature: "%s"'):format(feature))
224:     end
225: end
226: 
227: ------------------
228: -- command line
229: --
230: 
231: local function process_args(opts, args)
232:     if args[1] == 'help' then
233:         opts.help = true
234:         return
235:     end
236: 
237:     return argparse.processArgsGetopt(args, {
238:         {'h', 'help', handler=function() opts.help = true end},
239:         {'m', 'melt-masterworks', handler=function() opts.melt_masterworks = true end},
240:         {'s', 'stockpile', hasArg=true, handler=function(arg) opts.sp = arg end},
241:     })
242: end
243: 
244: function parse_commandline(args)
245:     local opts = {}
246:     local positionals = process_args(opts, args)
247: 
248:     if opts.help or not positionals then
249:         return false
250:     end
251: 
252:     local command = table.remove(positionals, 1)
253:     if not command or command == 'status' then
254:         print_status()
255:     elseif command == 'now' then
256:         logistics_cycle()
257:     elseif command == 'add' then
258:         do_add_stockpile_config(utils.invert(positionals), opts)
259:     elseif command == 'clear' then
260:         do_clear_stockpile_config(utils.invert(positionals).all, opts)
261:     elseif command == 'enable' or command == 'disable' then
262:         do_set_feature(command == 'enable', positionals[1])
263:     else
264:         return false
265:     end
266: 
267:     return true
268: end

276: local function can_set_skill_level()
277:     local ret = false
278:     for _,fs in ipairs(dfhack.gui.getFocusStrings(dfhack.gui.getDFViewscreen(true))) do
279:         if fs:endswith('/Workers') then
280:             local bld = dfhack.gui.getSelectedBuilding(true)
281:             if not bld then return false end
282:             ret = #bld.profile.permitted_workers == 0
283:         elseif fs:endswith('WORKER_ASSIGNMENT') then
284:             return false
285:         end
286:     end
287:     return ret
288: end
289: 
290: local function set_skill_level(which, val, bld)
291:     bld = bld or dfhack.gui.getSelectedBuilding(true)
292:     if not bld then return end
293:     bld.profile[which] = val
294: end
295: 
296: -- the UI isn't wide enough to accomodate all the skills. select a few to combine
297: -- into the adjacent tier.
298: local SKIP_RATINGS = utils.invert{
299:     df.skill_rating.Adequate,
300:     df.skill_rating.Skilled,
301:     df.skill_rating.Adept,
302:     df.skill_rating.Professional,
303:     df.skill_rating.Great,
304:     df.skill_rating.HighMaster,
305:     df.skill_rating.Legendary1,
306:     df.skill_rating.Legendary2,
307:     df.skill_rating.Legendary3,
308:     df.skill_rating.Legendary4,
309:     df.skill_rating.Legendary5,
310: }
311: 
312: local SKILL_OPTIONS = {}
313: local MIN_SKILL_RATINGS, MAX_SKILL_RATINGS = {}, {}
314: for ridx in ipairs(df.skill_rating) do
315:     if SKIP_RATINGS[ridx] then goto continue end
316:     local idx = #SKILL_OPTIONS + 1
317:     table.insert(SKILL_OPTIONS, {
318:         label=df.skill_rating.attrs[ridx].caption,
319:         value=idx,
320:     })
321:     local prev_max = MAX_SKILL_RATINGS[idx-1]
322:     MIN_SKILL_RATINGS[idx] = prev_max and (prev_max+1) or 0
323:     MAX_SKILL_RATINGS[idx] = ridx
324:     ::continue::
325: end
326: MAX_SKILL_RATINGS[#MAX_SKILL_RATINGS] = 3000 -- DF value for upper cap
327: 
328: 
329: function SkillRestrictionOverlay:init()
330:     local panel = widgets.Panel{
331:         frame_style=gui.FRAME_MEDIUM,
332:         frame_background=gui.CLEAR_PEN,
333:     }
334:     panel:addviews{
335:         widgets.CycleHotkeyLabel{
336:             view_id='min_skill',
337:             frame={l=1, t=0, w=16},
338:             label='Min skill:',
339:             label_below=true,
340:             key_back='CUSTOM_SHIFT_C',
341:             key='CUSTOM_SHIFT_V',
342:             options=SKILL_OPTIONS,
343:             initial_option=SKILL_OPTIONS[1].value,
344:             on_change=function(val)
345:                 local bld = dfhack.gui.getSelectedBuilding(true)
346:                 if self.subviews.max_skill:getOptionValue() < val then
347:                     self.subviews.max_skill:setOption(val)
348:                     set_skill_level('max_level', MAX_SKILL_RATINGS[val], bld)
349:                 end
350:                 set_skill_level('min_level', MIN_SKILL_RATINGS[val], bld)
351:             end,
352:         },
353:         widgets.CycleHotkeyLabel{
354:             view_id='max_skill',
355:             frame={r=1, t=0, w=16},
356:             label='Max skill:',
357:             label_below=true,
358:             key_back='CUSTOM_SHIFT_E',
359:             key='CUSTOM_SHIFT_R',
360:             options=SKILL_OPTIONS,
361:             initial_option=SKILL_OPTIONS[#SKILL_OPTIONS].value,
362:             on_change=function(val)
363:                 local bld = dfhack.gui.getSelectedBuilding(true)
364:                 if self.subviews.min_skill:getOptionValue() > val then
365:                     self.subviews.min_skill:setOption(val)
366:                     set_skill_level('min_level', MIN_SKILL_RATINGS[val], bld)
367:                 end
368:                 set_skill_level('max_level', MAX_SKILL_RATINGS[val], bld)
369:             end,
370:         },
371:         widgets.RangeSlider{
372:             frame={l=1, t=3},
373:             num_stops=#SKILL_OPTIONS,
374:             get_left_idx_fn=function()
375:                 return self.subviews.min_skill:getOptionValue()
376:             end,
377:             get_right_idx_fn=function()
378:                 return self.subviews.max_skill:getOptionValue()
379:             end,
380:             on_left_change=function(idx) self.subviews.min_skill:setOption(idx, true) end,
381:             on_right_change=function(idx) self.subviews.max_skill:setOption(idx, true) end,
382:         },
383:     }
384: 
385:     self:addviews{
386:         panel,
387:         widgets.HelpButton{command='orders'},
388:     }
389: end
390: 
391: function SkillRestrictionOverlay:refresh_slider()
392:     local bld = dfhack.gui.getSelectedBuilding(true)
393:     if not bld then return end
394:     self.subviews.min_skill:setOption(#SKILL_OPTIONS)
395:     for min_idx=1,#SKILL_OPTIONS do
396:         if bld.profile.min_level > MAX_SKILL_RATINGS[min_idx] then goto continue end
397:         self.subviews.min_skill:setOption(min_idx)
398:         self.subviews.max_skill:setOption(min_idx)
399:         for max_idx=min_idx,#SKILL_OPTIONS do
400:             if bld.profile.max_level <= MAX_SKILL_RATINGS[max_idx] then
401:                 self.subviews.max_skill:setOption(max_idx)
402:                 break
403:             end
404:         end
405:         break
406:         ::continue::
407:     end
408: end
409: 
410: function SkillRestrictionOverlay:render(dc)
411:     if can_set_skill_level() then
412:         self:refresh_slider()
413:         SkillRestrictionOverlay.super.render(self, dc)
414:     end
415: end
416: 
417: function SkillRestrictionOverlay:onInput(keys)
418:     if can_set_skill_level() and
419:         not mi.view_sheets.building_entering_nickname
420:     then
421:         return SkillRestrictionOverlay.super.onInput(self, keys)
422:     end
423: end

465:     [df.workshop_type.Carpenters]={'CARPENTER', 'TRAPPER'},
466:     [df.workshop_type.Farmers]={'PROCESS_PLANT', 'MAKE_CHEESE', 'MILK',
467:         'SHEARER', 'SPINNER', 'PAPERMAKING'},
468:     [df.workshop_type.Masons]={'STONECUTTER', 'STONE_CARVER',},
469:     [df.workshop_type.Craftsdwarfs]={'STONE_CRAFT', 'WOOD_CRAFT', 'BONE_CARVE',
470:         'WAX_WORKING', 'BOOKBINDING', 'EXTRACT_STRAND',
471:         'LEATHER', 'CLOTHESMAKER', 'METAL_CRAFT', 'GLASSMAKER'},
472:     [df.workshop_type.Jewelers]={'CUT_GEM', 'ENCRUST_GEM'},
473:     [df.workshop_type.MetalsmithsForge]={'FORGE_WEAPON', 'FORGE_ARMOR',

483:     -- this is specifically for the Screw Press, but we don't need to differentiate (yet)

493: -- used by quickfort
494: function get_profile_labors(bld_type, bld_subtype)
495:     if bld_type == df.building_type.Workshop then
496:         return WORKSHOP_LABORS[bld_subtype] or {}
497:     elseif bld_type == df.building_type.Furnace then
498:         return FURNACE_LABORS[bld_subtype] or {}
499:     end
500:     return {}
501: end
502: 
503: local ENABLED_PEN_LEFT = dfhack.pen.parse{fg=COLOR_CYAN,
504:         tile=curry(textures.tp_control_panel, 1), ch=string.byte('[')}
505: local ENABLED_PEN_CENTER = dfhack.pen.parse{fg=COLOR_LIGHTGREEN,
506:         tile=curry(textures.tp_control_panel, 2) or nil, ch=251} -- check
507: local ENABLED_PEN_RIGHT = dfhack.pen.parse{fg=COLOR_CYAN,
508:         tile=curry(textures.tp_control_panel, 3) or nil, ch=string.byte(']')}
509: local DISABLED_PEN_LEFT = dfhack.pen.parse{fg=COLOR_CYAN,
510:         tile=curry(textures.tp_control_panel, 4) or nil, ch=string.byte('[')}
511: local DISABLED_PEN_CENTER = dfhack.pen.parse{fg=COLOR_RED,
512:         tile=curry(textures.tp_control_panel, 5) or nil, ch=string.byte('x')}
513: local DISABLED_PEN_RIGHT = dfhack.pen.parse{fg=COLOR_CYAN,
514:         tile=curry(textures.tp_control_panel, 6) or nil, ch=string.byte(']')}
515: 
516: local function set_labor(bld, labor, val)
517:     bld.profile.blocked_labors[labor] = val
518: end
519: 
520: local function toggle_labor(_, choice)
521:     if not choice then return end
522:     local bld = dfhack.gui.getSelectedBuilding(true)
523:     if not bld then return end
524:     set_labor(bld, choice.labor, not bld.profile.blocked_labors[choice.labor])
525: end
526: 
527: local function is_labor_blocked(labor, bld)
528:     bld = bld or dfhack.gui.getSelectedBuilding(true)
529:     return bld and bld.profile.blocked_labors[labor]
530: end
531: 
532: function make_labor_panel(bld_type, bld_subtype, labors)
533:     local list = widgets.List{
534:         frame={t=2, l=1, w=28, b=0},
535:         on_double_click=toggle_labor,
536:     }
537: 
538:     local panel = widgets.Panel{
539:         frame_style=gui.FRAME_MEDIUM,
540:         frame_background=gui.CLEAR_PEN,
541:         -- will get clamped to parent frame and a scrollbar will appear if the list
542:         -- is too long
543:         frame={l=0, r=0, t=0, h=math.max(#labors+5,9)},
544:         visible=function()
545:             local bld = dfhack.gui.getSelectedBuilding(true)
546:             return bld and bld:getType() == bld_type and bld.type == bld_subtype
547:         end,
548:         subviews={
549:             widgets.Label{
550:                 frame={t=0, l=0},
551:                 text='Permitted General Work Order Labors',
552:             },
553:             list,
554:             widgets.HotkeyLabel{
555:                 frame={l=30, t=2},
556:                 key='CUSTOM_CTRL_A',
557:                 label='Toggle all',
558:                 auto_width=true,
559:                 on_activate=function()
560:                     local bld = dfhack.gui.getSelectedBuilding(true)
561:                     if not bld then return end
562:                     local choices = list:getChoices()
563:                     local target = not is_labor_blocked(choices[1].labor, bld)
564:                     for _, choice in ipairs(choices) do
565:                         set_labor(bld, choice.labor, target)
566:                     end
567:                 end,
568:             },
569:             widgets.HotkeyLabel{
570:                 frame={l=30, t=4},
571:                 key='SELECT',
572:                 label='Toggle selected',
573:                 auto_width=true,
574:                 on_activate=function() toggle_labor(list:getSelected()) end,
575:             },
576:             widgets.Label{
577:                 frame={l=37, t=5},
578:                 text='(or double click)'
579:             }
580:         },
581:     }
582: 
583:     panel.onInput = function(self, keys)
584:         local handled = widgets.Panel.onInput(self, keys)
585:         if keys._MOUSE_L then
586:             local idx = list:getIdxUnderMouse()
587:             if idx then
588:                 local x = list:getMousePos()
589:                 if x <= 2 then
590:                     toggle_labor(list:getSelected())
591:                     list.last_select_click_ms = 0
592:                 end
593:             end
594:         end
595:         return handled
596:     end
597: 
598:     local choices = {}
599:     for _,labor_name in ipairs(labors) do
600:         local labor = df.unit_labor[labor_name]
601:         local function get_enabled_button_token(e_tile, d_tile)
602:             return {
603:                 tile=function()
604:                     return is_labor_blocked(labor) and d_tile or e_tile
605:                 end,
606:             }
607:         end
608:         table.insert(choices, {
609:             labor=labor,
610:             text={
611:                     get_enabled_button_token(ENABLED_PEN_LEFT, DISABLED_PEN_LEFT),
612:                     get_enabled_button_token(ENABLED_PEN_CENTER, DISABLED_PEN_CENTER),
613:                     get_enabled_button_token(ENABLED_PEN_RIGHT, DISABLED_PEN_RIGHT),
614:                     {gap=1, text=df.unit_labor.attrs[labor].caption},
615:             },
616:         })
617:     end
618:     list:setChoices(choices)
619: 
620:     return panel
621: end
622: 
623: function LaborRestrictionsOverlay:init()
624:     for ws_type, labors in pairs(WORKSHOP_LABORS) do
625:         self:addviews{make_labor_panel(df.building_type.Workshop, ws_type, labors)}
626:     end
627:     for f_type, labors in pairs(FURNACE_LABORS) do
628:         self:addviews{make_labor_panel(df.building_type.Furnace, f_type, labors)}
629:     end
630:     self:addviews{widgets.HelpButton{command='orders'}}
631: end
632: 
633: function LaborRestrictionsOverlay:render(dc)
634:     if can_set_labors() then
635:         LaborRestrictionsOverlay.super.render(self, dc)
636:     end
637: end
638: 
639: function LaborRestrictionsOverlay:onInput(keys)
640:     if can_set_labors() and
641:         not mi.view_sheets.building_entering_nickname
642:     then
643:         return LaborRestrictionsOverlay.super.onInput(self, keys)
644:     end
645: end

712: 
713: --
714: -- OrdersSearchOverlay
715: --
716: 
717: local ORDER_HEIGHT = 3
718: local TABS_WIDTH_THRESHOLD = 155
719: local LIST_START_Y_ONE_TABS_ROW = 8
720: local LIST_START_Y_TWO_TABS_ROWS = 10
721: local BOTTOM_MARGIN = 9
722: local ARROW_X = 10
723: 
724: local SELECTED_PEN = dfhack.pen.parse{fg=COLOR_BLACK, bg=COLOR_WHITE, bold=true}
725: local MATCH_PEN = dfhack.pen.parse{fg=COLOR_WHITE, bg=COLOR_BLACK, bold=true}
726: 
727: local function perform_search(text)
728:     local matches = {}
729: 
730:     if text == '' then
731:         return matches
732:     end
733: 
734:     local orders = df.global.world.manager_orders.all
735:     for i = 0, #orders - 1 do
736:         local order = orders[i]
737:         local search_key = dfhack.job.getManagerOrderName(order)
738:         if search_key and utils.search_text(search_key, text) then
739:             table.insert(matches, i)
740:         end
741:     end
742: 
743:     return matches
744: end
745: 
746: local function concat_order_names()
747:     local orders = df.global.world.manager_orders.all
748:     if #orders == 0 then return "" end
749: 
750:     local names = {}
751:     for i = 0, #orders - 1 do
752:         local name = dfhack.job.getManagerOrderName(orders[i])
753:         table.insert(names, name or "")
754:     end
755: 
756:     return table.concat(names, "|")
757: end
758: 
759: local function getListStartY()
760:     local rect = gui.get_interface_rect()
761: 
762:     if rect.width >= TABS_WIDTH_THRESHOLD then
763:         return LIST_START_Y_ONE_TABS_ROW
764:     else
765:         return LIST_START_Y_TWO_TABS_ROWS
766:     end
767: end
768: 
769: local function getViewportSize()
770:     local rect = gui.get_interface_rect()
771:     local list_start_y = getListStartY()
772: 
773:     local available_height = rect.height - list_start_y - BOTTOM_MARGIN
774:     return math.floor(available_height / ORDER_HEIGHT)
775: end
776: 
777: local function getVisibleOrderIndices()
778:     local orders = df.global.world.manager_orders.all
779:     local scroll_pos = mi.info.work_orders.scroll_position_work_orders
780: 
781:     if #orders == 0 then return 0, -1 end
782: 
783:     local viewport_size = getViewportSize()
784:     local viewport_start = scroll_pos
785:     local viewport_end = scroll_pos + viewport_size - 1
786: 
787:     -- Handle end-of-list case
788:     if viewport_end >= #orders then
789:         viewport_end = #orders - 1
790:         viewport_start = math.max(0, viewport_end - viewport_size + 1)
791:     end
792: 
793:     return viewport_start, viewport_end
794: end
795: 
796: local function calculateOrderY(order_idx)
797:     local orders = df.global.world.manager_orders.all
798: 
799:     if #orders == 0 or order_idx < 0 or order_idx >= #orders then
800:         return nil
801:     end
802: 
803:     local viewport_start, viewport_end = getVisibleOrderIndices()
804: 
805:     -- Check if order is in viewport
806:     if order_idx < viewport_start or order_idx > viewport_end then
807:         return nil
808:     end
809: 
810:     local list_start_y = getListStartY()
811:     local pos_in_viewport = order_idx - viewport_start
812: 
813:     return list_start_y + (pos_in_viewport * ORDER_HEIGHT)
814: end
815: 
816: OrdersSearchOverlay = defclass(OrdersSearchOverlay, overlay.OverlayWidget)
817: OrdersSearchOverlay.ATTRS{
818:     desc='Adds a search box to find and navigate to matching manager orders.',
819:     default_pos={x=85, y=-6},
820:     default_enabled=true,
821:     viewscreens='dwarfmode/Info/WORK_ORDERS/Default',
822:     frame={w=26, h=4},
823:     overlay_onupdate_max_freq_seconds=1,
824: }
825: 
826: function OrdersSearchOverlay:init()
827:     local main_panel = widgets.Panel{
828:         view_id='main_panel',
829:         frame={t=0, l=0, r=0, h=4},
830:         frame_style=gui.MEDIUM_FRAME,
831:         frame_background=gui.CLEAR_PEN,
832:         frame_title='Search',
833:         visible=function() return not self.minimized end,
834:         subviews={
835:             widgets.EditField{
836:                 view_id='filter',
837:                 frame={t=0, l=0},
838:                 key='CUSTOM_ALT_S',
839:                 on_change=self:callback('update_filter'),
840:                 on_submit=self:callback('on_submit'),
841:                 on_submit2=self:callback('on_submit2'),
842:             },
843:             widgets.HotkeyLabel{
844:                 frame={t=1, l=0},
845:                 label='prev',
846:                 key='CUSTOM_ALT_P',
847:                 auto_width=true,
848:                 on_activate=self:callback('cycle_match', -1),
849:                 enabled=function() return #self.matched_indices > 0 end,
850:             },
851:             widgets.HotkeyLabel{
852:                 frame={t=1, l=12},
853:                 label='next',
854:                 key='CUSTOM_ALT_N',
855:                 auto_width=true,
856:                 on_activate=self:callback('cycle_match', 1),
857:                 enabled=function() return #self.matched_indices > 0 end,
858:             },
859:         },
860:     }
861: 
862:     local minimized_panel = widgets.Panel{
863:         frame={t=0, r=0, w=3, h=1},
864:         subviews={
865:             widgets.Label{
866:                 frame={t=0, l=0, w=1, h=1},
867:                 text='[',
868:                 text_pen=COLOR_RED,
869:                 visible=function() return self.minimized end,
870:             },
871:             widgets.Label{
872:                 frame={t=0, l=1, w=1, h=1},
873:                 text={{text=function() return self.minimized and string.char(31) or string.char(30) end}},
874:                 text_pen=dfhack.pen.parse{fg=COLOR_BLACK, bg=COLOR_GREY},
875:                 text_hpen=dfhack.pen.parse{fg=COLOR_BLACK, bg=COLOR_WHITE},
876:                 on_click=function() self.minimized = not self.minimized end,
877:             },
878:             widgets.Label{
879:                 frame={t=0, r=0, w=1, h=1},
880:                 text=']',
881:                 text_pen=COLOR_RED,
882:                 visible=function() return self.minimized end,
883:             },
884:         },
885:     }
886: 
887:     self:addviews{
888:         main_panel,
889:         minimized_panel,
890:     }
891: 
892:     self.minimized = false
893:     self.matched_indices = {}
894:     self.current_match_idx = 0
895:     self.cached_order_names = nil
896: end
897: 
898: function OrdersSearchOverlay:overlay_onupdate()
899:     if self.minimized then return end
900: 
901:     local current_order_names = concat_order_names()
902:     if current_order_names ~= self.cached_order_names then
903:         self.cached_order_names = current_order_names
904:         self:update_filter()
905:     end
906: end
907: 
908: function OrdersSearchOverlay:update_filter()
909:     local text = self.subviews.filter.text
910:     self.matched_indices = perform_search(text)
911:     self.current_match_idx = 0
912: 
913:     if text == '' then
914:         self.subviews.main_panel.frame_title = 'Search'
915:     else
916:         self.subviews.main_panel.frame_title = 'Search' .. self:get_match_text()
917:     end
918: end
919: 
920: function OrdersSearchOverlay:on_submit()
921:     self:cycle_match(1)
922:     self.subviews.filter:setFocus(true)
923: end
924: 
925: function OrdersSearchOverlay:on_submit2()
926:     self:cycle_match(-1)
927:     self.subviews.filter:setFocus(true)
928: end
929: 
930: function OrdersSearchOverlay:cycle_match(direction)
931:     local search_text = self.subviews.filter.text
932: 
933:     local new_matches = perform_search(search_text)
934: 
935:     if #new_matches == 0 then
936:         self.matched_indices = {}
937:         self.current_match_idx = 0
938:         self.subviews.main_panel.frame_title = 'Search'
939:         return
940:     end
941: 
942:     local new_match_idx = self.current_match_idx + direction
943: 
944:     if new_match_idx > #new_matches then
945:         new_match_idx = 1
946:     elseif new_match_idx < 1 then
947:         new_match_idx = #new_matches
948:     end
949: 
950:     self.matched_indices = new_matches
951:     self.current_match_idx = new_match_idx
952: 
953:     -- Scroll to the selected match only if not already visible
954:     local order_idx = self.matched_indices[self.current_match_idx]
955:     local viewport_start, viewport_end = getVisibleOrderIndices()
956:     if order_idx < viewport_start or order_idx > viewport_end then
957:         mi.info.work_orders.scroll_position_work_orders = order_idx
958:     end
959: 
960:     self.subviews.main_panel.frame_title = 'Search' .. self:get_match_text()
961: end
962: 
963: function OrdersSearchOverlay:get_match_text()
964:     local total_matches = #self.matched_indices
965: 
966:     if total_matches == 0 then
967:         return ''
968:     end
969: 
970:     if self.current_match_idx == 0 then
971:         return string.format(': %d matches', total_matches)
972:     end
973: 
974:     return string.format(': %d of %d', self.current_match_idx, total_matches)
975: end
976: 
977: local function is_mouse_key(keys)
978:     return keys._MOUSE_L
979:         or keys._MOUSE_R
980:         or keys._MOUSE_M
981:         or keys.CONTEXT_SCROLL_UP
982:         or keys.CONTEXT_SCROLL_DOWN
983:         or keys.CONTEXT_SCROLL_PAGEUP
984:         or keys.CONTEXT_SCROLL_PAGEDOWN
985: end
986: 
987: function OrdersSearchOverlay:onInput(keys)
988:     if mi.job_details.open then return end
989: 
990:     local filter_field = self.subviews.filter
991:     if not filter_field then return false end
992: 
993:     -- Unfocus search on right-click
994:     if keys._MOUSE_R and filter_field.focus then
995:         filter_field:setFocus(false)
996:         return true
997:     end
998: 
999:     -- Let parent handle input first (for HotkeyLabel clicks and widget interactions)
1000:     if OrdersSearchOverlay.super.onInput(self, keys) then
1001:         return true
1002:     end
1003: 
1004:     -- Unfocus search on left-click when focused (for workshop and number of times changes)
1005:     -- And let the click pass through
1006:     if keys._MOUSE_L and filter_field.focus then
1007:         filter_field:setFocus(false)
1008:         return false
1009:     end
1010: 
1011:     -- Only consume input if search field has focus and it's not a mouse key
1012:     -- This allows scrolling, navigation, and mouse interaction in the orders list
1013:     if filter_field.focus and not is_mouse_key(keys) then
1014:         return true
1015:     end
1016: 
1017:     return false
1018: end
1019: 
1020: function OrdersSearchOverlay:render(dc)
1021:     if mi.job_details.open then return end
1022:     OrdersSearchOverlay.super.render(self, dc)
1023:     self:render_highlights(dc)
1024: end
1025: 
1026: function OrdersSearchOverlay:render_highlights(dc)
1027:     if #self.matched_indices == 0 then return end
1028: 
1029:     local selected_order_idx = self.current_match_idx > 0 and
1030:                                self.matched_indices[self.current_match_idx] or nil
1031: 
1032:     for _, match_order_idx in ipairs(self.matched_indices) do
1033:         local match_y = calculateOrderY(match_order_idx)
1034: 
1035:         if match_y then
1036:             local pen = (match_order_idx == selected_order_idx) and SELECTED_PEN or MATCH_PEN
1037: 
1038:             dc:seek(ARROW_X, match_y):string('|', pen)
1039:             dc:seek(ARROW_X, match_y + 1):string('>', pen)
1040:             dc:seek(ARROW_X, match_y + 2):string('|', pen)
1041:         end
1042:     end
1043: end
1044: 
1045: -- -------------------
1046: 
1047: OVERLAY_WIDGETS = {
1048:     recheck=RecheckOverlay,
1049:     importexport=OrdersOverlay,
1050:     search=OrdersSearchOverlay,
1051:     skillrestrictions=SkillRestrictionOverlay,

32: function register_trigger_lock_screen(scr, desc)

35:             trigger_lock_holder_screen:dismiss()
36:         end
37:         trigger_lock_holder_description = nil

91: -- normalize "short form" viewscreen names to "long form" and remove any focus
92: local function normalize_viewscreen_name(vs_name)
93:     if vs_name == 'all' or vs_name:match('^viewscreen_.*st') then
94:         return vs_name:match('^[^/]+')
95:     end
96:     return 'viewscreen_' .. vs_name:match('^[^/]+') .. 'st'

99: -- reduce "long form" viewscreen names to "short form"; keep focus
100: function simplify_viewscreen_name(vs_name)
101:     local short_name = vs_name:match('^viewscreen_([^/]+)st')
102:     return short_name or vs_name
103: end

105: local function is_empty(tbl)
106:     for _ in pairs(tbl) do
107:         return false
108:     end
109:     return true
110: end

112: local function sanitize_pos(pos)
113:     local x = math.floor(tonumber(pos.x) or DEFAULT_X_POS)
114:     local y = math.floor(tonumber(pos.y) or DEFAULT_Y_POS)
115:     -- if someone accidentally uses 0-based instead of 1-based indexing, fix it
116:     if x == 0 then x = 1 end
117:     if y == 0 then y = 1 end
118:     return {x=x, y=y}
119: end
120: 
121: local function make_frame(pos, old_frame)
122:     old_frame = old_frame or {}
123:     local frame = {w=old_frame.w, h=old_frame.h}
124:     if pos.x < 0 then frame.r = math.abs(pos.x) - 1 else frame.l = pos.x - 1 end
125:     if pos.y < 0 then frame.b = math.abs(pos.y) - 1 else frame.t = pos.y - 1 end
126:     return frame
127: end

147: local function do_by_names_or_numbers(args, fn)

164: local function do_enable(args, quiet, skip_save)
165:     local enable_fn = function(name, db_entry)
166:         overlay_config[name].enabled = true
167:         if db_entry.widget.hotspot then
168:             active_hotspot_widgets[name] = db_entry
169:         end
170:         for _,vs_name in ipairs(normalize_list(db_entry.widget.viewscreens)) do
171:             vs_name = normalize_viewscreen_name(vs_name)
172:             ensure_key(active_viewscreen_widgets, vs_name)[name] = db_entry
173:         end
174:         if db_entry.widget.overlay_onenable then
175:             db_entry.widget.overlay_onenable()
176:         end
177:         if not quiet then
178:             print(('enabled widget %s'):format(name))
179:         end
180:     end
181:     if args[1] == 'all' then
182:         for name,db_entry in pairs(widget_db) do
183:             if not overlay_config[name].enabled then
184:                 enable_fn(name, db_entry)
185:             end
186:         end
187:     else
188:         do_by_names_or_numbers(args, enable_fn)
189:     end
190:     if not skip_save then
191:         save_config()
192:     end
193: end
194: 
195: local function do_disable(args, quiet)
196:     local disable_fn = function(name, db_entry)
197:         overlay_config[name].enabled = false
198:         if db_entry.widget.hotspot then
199:             active_hotspot_widgets[name] = nil
200:         end
201:         for _,vs_name in ipairs(normalize_list(db_entry.widget.viewscreens)) do
202:             vs_name = normalize_viewscreen_name(vs_name)
203:             ensure_key(active_viewscreen_widgets, vs_name)[name] = nil
204:             if is_empty(active_viewscreen_widgets[vs_name]) then
205:                 active_viewscreen_widgets[vs_name] = nil
206:             end
207:         end
208:         if db_entry.widget.overlay_ondisable then
209:             db_entry.widget.overlay_ondisable()
210:         end
211:         if not quiet then
212:             print(('disabled widget %s'):format(name))
213:         end
214:     end
215:     if args[1] == 'all' then
216:         for name,db_entry in pairs(widget_db) do
217:             if overlay_config[name].enabled then
218:                 disable_fn(name, db_entry)
219:             end
220:         end
221:     else
222:         do_by_names_or_numbers(args, disable_fn)
223:     end
224:     save_config()
225: end

227: local function do_list(args)
228:     local filter = args and #args > 0

231:         if filter then

234:                 if name:find(str) then
235:                     passes = true
236:                     break
237:                 end
238:             end
239:             if not passes then
240:                 num_filtered = num_filtered + 1
241:                 goto continue
242:             end
243:         end
244:         local db_entry = widget_db[name]

247:         dfhack.print(enabled and '[enabled] ' or '[disabled]')
248:         dfhack.color()
249:         print((' %d) %s'):format(i, name))
250:         ::continue::
251:     end
252:     if num_filtered > 0 then
253:         print(('(%d widgets filtered out)'):format(num_filtered))

257: local function get_focus_strings(viewscreens)

269: local function load_widget(name, widget_class)
270:     local widget = widget_class{name=name}
271:     widget_db[name] = {
272:         widget=widget,
273:         focus_strings=get_focus_strings(normalize_list(widget.viewscreens)),
274:         next_update_ms=widget.overlay_onupdate and 0 or math.huge,
275:     }
276:     if not overlay_config[name] then overlay_config[name] = {} end
277:     if widget.version ~= overlay_config[name].version then
278:         overlay_config[name] = {}
279:     end
280:     local config = overlay_config[name]
281:     config.version = widget.version
282:     if config.enabled == nil then
283:         config.enabled = widget.default_enabled
284:     end
285:     config.pos = sanitize_pos(config.pos or widget.default_pos)
286:     widget.frame = make_frame(config.pos, widget.frame)
287:     if config.enabled then
288:         do_enable(name, true, true)
289:     else
290:         config.enabled = false
291:     end
292: end

294: local function load_widgets(env_name, env)
295:     local overlay_widgets = env[OVERLAY_WIDGETS_VAR]
296:     if not overlay_widgets then return end
297:     if type(overlay_widgets) ~= 'table' then
298:         dfhack.printerr(
299:                 ('error loading overlay widgets from "%s": %s map is malformed')
300:                 :format(env_name, OVERLAY_WIDGETS_VAR))
301:         return
302:     end
303:     for widget_name,widget_class in pairs(overlay_widgets) do
304:         local name = env_name .. '.' .. widget_name
305:         if not safecall(load_widget, name, widget_class) then
306:             dfhack.printerr(('error loading overlay widget "%s"'):format(name))
307:         end
308:     end
309: end

343:     local viewscreens = normalize_list(widget.viewscreens)

355: local function do_position(args, quiet)
356:     local name_or_number, x, y = table.unpack(args)
357:     local name = get_name(name_or_number)
358:     if not widget_db[name] then
359:         if not name_or_number then
360:             dfhack.printerr('please specify a widget name or list number')
361:         else
362:             dfhack.printerr(('widget not found: "%s"'):format(name))
363:         end
364:         return
365:     end
366:     local widget = widget_db[name].widget
367:     local pos
368:     if x == 'default' then
369:         pos = sanitize_pos(widget.default_pos)
370:     else
371:         x, y = tonumber(x), tonumber(y)
372:         if not x or not y then
373:             dump_widget_config(name, widget)
374:             return
375:         end
376:         pos = sanitize_pos{x=x, y=y}
377:     end
378:     overlay_config[name].pos = pos
379:     widget.frame = make_frame(pos, widget.frame)
380:     local full, scaled = get_interface_rects()
381:     widget:updateLayout(widget.fullscreen and full or scaled)
382:     save_config()
383:     if not quiet then
384:         print(('repositioned widget %s to x=%d, y=%d'):format(name, pos.x, pos.y))
385:     end
386: end

388: -- note that the widget does not have to be enabled to be triggered
389: local function do_trigger(args, quiet)
390:     if triggered_screen_has_lock() then
391:         dfhack.printerr(('cannot trigger widget; widget "%s" is already active')
392:                         :format(trigger_lock_holder_description))
393:         return
394:     end
395:     do_by_names_or_numbers(args[1], function(name, db_entry)
396:         local widget = db_entry.widget
397:         if widget.overlay_trigger then
398:             register_trigger_lock_screen(
399:                 widget:overlay_trigger(table.unpack(args, 2)),
400:                 name
401:             )
402: 
403:             if not quiet then
404:                 print(('triggered widget %s'):format(name))
405:             end
406:         end
407:     end)
408: end

410: local command_fns = {
411:     enable=do_enable,
412:     disable=do_disable,
413:     list=do_list,
414:     position=do_position,
415:     trigger=do_trigger,

418: local HELP_ARGS = utils.invert{'help', '--help', '-h'}
419: 
420: function overlay_command(args, quiet)
421:     local command = table.remove(args, 1) or 'help'
422:     if HELP_ARGS[command] or not command_fns[command] then return false end
423:     command_fns[command](args, quiet)
424:     return true
425: end

427: -- ---------------- --
428: -- event management --
429: -- ---------------- --
430: 
431: local function detect_frame_change(widget, fn)
432:     local frame = widget.frame
433:     local w, h = frame.w, frame.h
434:     local now_ms = dfhack.getTickCount()
435:     local ret = fn()
436:     record_widget_runtime(widget.name, now_ms)
437:     if w ~= frame.w or h ~= frame.h then
438:         widget:updateLayout()
439:     end
440:     return ret
441: end
442: 
443: local function get_next_onupdate_timestamp(now_ms, widget)
444:     local freq_s = widget.overlay_onupdate_max_freq_seconds
445:     if freq_s == 0 then
446:         return now_ms
447:     end
448:     local freq_ms = math.floor(freq_s * 1000)
449:     local jitter = math.random(0, freq_ms // 8) -- up to ~12% jitter
450:     return now_ms + freq_ms - jitter
451: end
452: 
453: -- reduces the next call by a small random amount to introduce jitter into the
454: -- widget processing timings
455: local function do_update(name, db_entry, now_ms, vs)
456:     local w = db_entry.widget
457:     if w.overlay_onupdate_max_freq_seconds ~= 0 and
458:         db_entry.next_update_ms > now_ms
459:     then
460:         return
461:     end
462:     if not utils.getval(w.active) then return end
463:     db_entry.next_update_ms = get_next_onupdate_timestamp(now_ms, w)
464:     if detect_frame_change(w, function() return w:overlay_onupdate(vs) end) then
465:         if register_trigger_lock_screen(w:overlay_trigger(), name) then
466:             return true
467:         end
468:     end
469: end
470: 
471: function update_hotspot_widgets()
472:     if triggered_screen_has_lock() then return end
473:     local now_ms = dfhack.getTickCount()
474:     for name,db_entry in pairs(active_hotspot_widgets) do
475:         if do_update(name, db_entry, now_ms) then return end
476:     end
477: end
478: 
479: local function matches_focus_strings(db_entry, vs_name, vs)
480:     if not db_entry.focus_strings then return true end
481:     local matched = true
482:     local simple_vs_name = simplify_viewscreen_name(vs_name)
483:     for _,fs in ipairs(db_entry.focus_strings) do
484:         if fs:startswith(simple_vs_name) then
485:             matched = false
486:             if dfhack.gui.matchFocusString(fs, vs) then
487:                 return true
488:             end
489:             local lua_focus = fs:match('^[^/]+/(dfhack/lua/.*)$')
490:             if lua_focus and dfhack.gui.matchFocusString(lua_focus) then
491:                 return true
492:             end
493:         end
494:     end
495:     return matched
496: end
497: 
498: local function _update_viewscreen_widgets(vs_name, vs, now_ms)
499:     local vs_widgets = active_viewscreen_widgets[vs_name]
500:     if not vs_widgets then return end
501:     local is_all = vs_name == 'all'
502:     now_ms = now_ms or dfhack.getTickCount()
503:     for name,db_entry in pairs(vs_widgets) do
504:         if (is_all or matches_focus_strings(db_entry, vs_name, vs)) and
505:                 do_update(name, db_entry, now_ms, vs) then
506:             return
507:         end
508:     end
509:     return now_ms
510: end
511: 
512: function update_viewscreen_widgets(vs_name, vs)
513:     if triggered_screen_has_lock() then return end
514:     local now_ms = _update_viewscreen_widgets(vs_name, vs, nil)
515:     if now_ms then
516:         _update_viewscreen_widgets('all', vs, now_ms)
517:     end
518: end
519: 
520: local function _feed_viewscreen_widgets(vs_name, vs, keys)
521:     local vs_widgets = active_viewscreen_widgets[vs_name]
522:     if not vs_widgets then return false end
523:     for _,db_entry in pairs(vs_widgets) do
524:         local w = db_entry.widget
525:         if (not vs or matches_focus_strings(db_entry, vs_name, vs)) and
526:             utils.getval(w.active) and
527:             utils.getval(w.visible) and
528:             detect_frame_change(w, function() return w:onInput(keys) end)
529:         then
530:             --print('widget handled input:', w.name)
531:             return true
532:         end
533:     end
534:     return false
535: end
536: 
537: -- when the native UI is capturing text input (renaming a building, editing a
538: -- workshop job filter, etc.), overlay widgets must not consume keys, since
539: -- their bound hotkeys would otherwise steal typed characters (#5764)
540: local function is_native_text_entry_active()
541:     local mi = df.global.game.main_interface
542:     local vs = mi.view_sheets
543:     return mi.entering_building_name or
544:         vs.building_entering_nickname or
545:         vs.unit_overview_entering_nickname or
546:         vs.unit_overview_entering_profession_nickname or
547:         vs.entering_building_job_filter or
548:         vs.entering_gen_work_order_num or
549:         vs.entering_wq_number
550: end
551: 
552: function feed_viewscreen_widgets(vs_name, vs, keys)
553:     if is_native_text_entry_active() then
554:         return false
555:     end
556:     if not _feed_viewscreen_widgets(vs_name, vs, keys) and
557:             not _feed_viewscreen_widgets('all', nil, keys) then
558:         return false
559:     end
560:     return true
561: end
562: 
563: local function _render_viewscreen_widgets(vs_name, vs)
564:     local vs_widgets = active_viewscreen_widgets[vs_name]
565:     if not vs_widgets then return end
566:     local full, scaled = get_interface_rects()
567:     for _,db_entry in pairs(vs_widgets) do
568:         local w = db_entry.widget
569:         if (not vs or matches_focus_strings(db_entry, vs_name, vs)) and utils.getval(w.visible) then
570:             detect_frame_change(w, function()
571:                 w:render(w.fullscreen and gui.Painter.new(full) or gui.Painter.new(scaled))
572:             end)
573:         end
574:     end
575:     return full_dc, scaled_dc
576: end
577: 
578: local force_refresh
579: 
580: function render_viewscreen_widgets(vs_name, vs)
581:     _render_viewscreen_widgets(vs_name, vs)
582:     _render_viewscreen_widgets('all', nil)
583:     if force_refresh then
584:         force_refresh = nil
585:         df.global.gps.force_full_display_count = 1
586:     end
587: end
588: 
589: -- called when the DF window is resized
590: function reposition_widgets()
591:     local full, scaled = get_interface_rects()
592:     for _,db_entry in pairs(widget_db) do
593:         local widget = db_entry.widget
594:         widget:updateLayout(widget.fullscreen and full or scaled)
595:     end
596:     force_refresh = true
597: end

599: -- ------------------------------------------------- --
600: -- OverlayWidget (base class of all overlay widgets) --
601: -- ------------------------------------------------- --
602: 
603: OverlayWidget = defclass(OverlayWidget, widgets.Panel)
604: OverlayWidget.ATTRS{
605:     name=DEFAULT_NIL, -- this is set by the framework to the widget name
606:     desc=DEFAULT_NIL, -- add a short description (<100 chars); displays in control panel
607:     default_pos={x=DEFAULT_X_POS, y=DEFAULT_Y_POS}, -- 1-based widget screen pos
608:     default_enabled=false, -- initial enabled state if not in config
609:     fullscreen=false, -- true if widget covers entire screen
610:     full_interface=false, -- true if widget covers entire interface area
611:     hotspot=false, -- whether to call overlay_onupdate on all screens
612:     viewscreens={}, -- override with associated viewscreen or list of viewscrens
613:     overlay_onupdate_max_freq_seconds=5, -- throttle calls to overlay_onupdate
614: }
615: 
616: function OverlayWidget:init()
617:     if self.overlay_onupdate_max_freq_seconds < 0 then
618:         error(('overlay_onupdate_max_freq_seconds must be >= 0: %s')
619:               :format(tostring(self.overlay_onupdate_max_freq_seconds)))
620:     end
621: 
622:     -- set defaults for frame. the widget is expected to keep these up to date
623:     -- when display contents change so the widget position can shift if the
624:     -- frame is relative to the right or bottom edges.
625:     self.frame = self.frame or {}

20: function assignToRole(code, bld)
21:     local group_codes = code_lookup[code:lower()]
22:     if not group_codes then
23:         dfhack.printerr('Noble or administrator role not found: ' .. code)
24:     end

32: local function print_status()
33:     local features, stats = preserve_rooms_getState()
34:     print('Features:')
35:     for feature,enabled in pairs(features) do
36:         print(('  %20s: %s'):format(feature, enabled))
37:     end
38:     print()
39:     print('Rooms reserved for traveling units:', stats.reservations)
40:     print('Managing assignments for noble rooms:', stats.nobles)
41: 
42:     if DEBUG then
43:         for k,v in pairs(preserve_rooms_getDebugState()) do
44:             local elems = v:split(',')
45:             if elems[1] == '' then
46:                 table.remove(elems, 1)
47:             end
48:             print(('%s (%d):'):format(k, #elems))
49:             for _,elem in ipairs(elems) do
50:                 print('  ' .. elem)
51:             end
52:             print()
53:         end
54:     end
55: end
56: 
57: local function do_set_feature(enabled, feature)
58:     if not preserve_rooms_setFeature(enabled, feature) then
59:         qerror(('unknown feature: "%s"'):format(feature))
60:     end
61: end
62: 
63: local function do_reset_feature(feature)
64:     if not preserve_rooms_resetFeatureState(feature) then
65:         qerror(('unknown feature: "%s"'):format(feature))
66:     end
67: end
68: 
69: function parse_commandline(args)
70:     local opts = {}
71:     local positionals = argparse.processArgsGetopt(args, {
72:         {'h', 'help', handler=function() opts.help = true end},
73:     })
74: 
75:     if opts.help or not positionals or positionals[1] == 'help' then
76:         return false
77:     end
78: 
79:     local command = table.remove(positionals, 1)
80:     if not command or command == 'status' then
81:         print_status()
82:     elseif command == 'now' then
83:         preserve_rooms_cycle()
84:     elseif command == 'enable' or command == 'disable' then
85:         do_set_feature(command == 'enable', positionals[1])
86:     elseif command == 'reset' then
87:         do_reset_feature(positionals[1])
88:     else
89:         return false
90:     end
91: 
92:     return true
93: end

266: local function to_title_case(str)
267:     return dfhack.capitalizeStringWords(dfhack.lowerCp437(str:gsub('_', ' ')))
268: end
269: 
270: local function add_options(options, choices, codes)
271:     for _,group_codes in ipairs(codes) do
272:         local names = {}
273:         for _,code in ipairs(group_codes) do
274:             table.insert(names, to_title_case(code))
275:         end
276:         local name = table.concat(names, '/')
277:         table.insert(options, {label=name, value=group_codes, pen=COLOR_YELLOW})
278:         table.insert(choices, name)
279:     end
280: end

282: -- updated on world load
283: codes = codes or {}

285: function ReservedWidget:refresh_role_list()
286:     local options, choices = {{label='None', value={''}, pen=COLOR_YELLOW}}, {'None'}
287:     add_options(options, choices, codes)
288: 
289:     self.code_to_idx = {['']=1}
290:     for idx,group_codes in ipairs(codes) do
291:         self.code_to_idx[group_codes[1]] = idx + 1 -- offset for None option
292:     end
293: 
294:     self.subviews.role.options = options
295:     self.subviews.role:setOption(1)
296:     self.subviews.list:setChoices(choices, 1)
297: end

299: OVERLAY_WIDGETS = {
300:     reserved=ReservedWidget,
301: }
302: 
303: local function add_positions(positions, entity)
304:     if not entity then return end
305:     for _,position in ipairs(entity.positions.own) do
306:         positions[position.id] = {
307:             id=position.id,
308:             code=position.code,
309:             replaced_by=position.replaced_by,
310:             required_value=position.required_office + position.required_bedroom + position.required_dining + position.required_tomb,
311:         }
312:     end
313: end
314: 
315: local function get_codes(positions)
316:     -- group by promotion chain
317:     -- grouped[id] is the group of positions that can be (eventually) promoted to the id'd position
318:     local grouped = {}
319:     for id,position in pairs(positions) do
320:         if position.replaced_by == -1 then
321:             ensure_key(grouped, id)[id] = position
322:         else
323:             -- replaced-by links may be cyclic. this code will behave "incorrectly" in the event
324:             -- of a cycle but will not hang. a proper fix is still needed. see issue DFHack/dfhack#5538
325:             local parent = positions[position.replaced_by]
326:             local counter = 0
327:             while parent.replaced_by ~= -1 and counter < 10 do
328:                 parent = positions[parent.replaced_by]
329:                 counter = counter + 1
330:             end
331:             ensure_key(grouped, parent.id)[id] = position
332:         end
333:     end
334: 
335:     -- add reverse links for promotion chains and record whether it has a room requirement
336:     for _,group in pairs(grouped) do
337:         for _,position in pairs(group) do
338:             if position.replaced_by ~= -1 then
339:                 group[position.replaced_by].prev = position.id
340:             end
341:         end
342:     end
343: 
344:     -- produce combined codes
345:     local diplay_codes = {}
346:     for id,group in pairs(grouped) do
347:         local group_codes = {}
348:         local position = group[id]
349:         while position do
350:             table.insert(group_codes, 1, position.code)
351:             group_codes.required_value = math.max(group_codes.required_value or 0, position.required_value)
352:             position=group[position.prev]
353:         end
354:         table.insert(diplay_codes, group_codes)
355:     end
356:     table.sort(diplay_codes, function(a, b)
357:         if a.required_value == b.required_value then
358:             return a[1] < b[1]
359:         end
360:         return a.required_value > b.required_value
361:     end)
362: 
363:     return diplay_codes
364: end
365: 
366: local function get_api_lookup_table(codes)
367:     local lookup = {}
368:     for _,group_codes in ipairs(codes) do
369:         for _,code in ipairs(group_codes) do
370:             lookup[code:lower()] = group_codes
371:         end
372:     end
373:     return lookup
374: end
375: 
376: dfhack.onStateChange[GLOBAL_KEY] = function(sc)
377:     if sc ~= SC_MAP_LOADED or not dfhack.world.isFortressMode() then
378:         return
379:     end
380:     local positions = {}
381:     add_positions(positions, df.historical_entity.find(df.global.plotinfo.civ_id));
382:     add_positions(positions, df.historical_entity.find(df.global.plotinfo.group_id));
383:     codes = get_codes(positions)
384:     code_lookup = get_api_lookup_table(codes)
385:     new_world_loaded = true
386: end
387: 
388: return _ENV

SOURCE D:/program/steam/steamapps/common/DFHack/hack/lua/plugins/sort/deathcause_button.lua
1: local _ENV = mkmodule('plugins.sort.deathcause_button')
2: 
3: local dialogs = require('gui.dialogs')
4: local overlay = require('plugins.overlay')
5: local widgets = require('gui.widgets')
6: 
7: DeathCauseOverlay = defclass(DeathCauseOverlay, overlay.OverlayWidget)
8: DeathCauseOverlay.ATTRS{
9:     desc='Adds a button to view death cause on the dead/missing tab.',
10:     default_pos={x=50, y=-7},
11:     default_enabled=true,
12:     viewscreens='dwarfmode/Info/CREATURES/DECEASED',
13:     frame={w=21, h=1},
14: }
15: 
16: function DeathCauseOverlay:init()
17:     local deathcause = reqscript('deathcause')
18: 
19:     local function get_selected_unit()
20:         -- Navigate to the creatures/deceased widget hierarchy:
21:         -- list_widget - the main deceased list
22:         --   ├─ children[0]: scrollbar widget
23:         --   └─ children[1]: container widget (list_container)
24:         --       ├─ grandchildren[0]: header
25:         --       ├─ grandchildren[1]: header or other UI
26:         --       └─ grandchildren[2]: scrollable rows container (scrollable_list)
27:         --           └─ rows: row widgets (each row = one unit in the list)
28:         --               └─ row.children[x]: unit widget
29:         --                   └─ unit_widget.u: pointer to the df.unit object
30: 
31:         local creatures = df.global.game.main_interface.info.creatures
32:         local list_widget = dfhack.gui.getWidget(creatures, 'Tabs', 'Dead/Missing')
33:         if not list_widget then return nil end
34: 
35:         local children = dfhack.gui.getWidgetChildren(list_widget)
36:         local list_container = children[1]
37:         local grandchildren = dfhack.gui.getWidgetChildren(list_container)
38: 
39:         local scrollable_list = grandchildren[2]
40:         if not scrollable_list then
41:             return nil
42:         end
43: 
44:         local rows = dfhack.gui.getWidgetChildren(scrollable_list)
45: 
46:         local cursor_idx = list_widget.cursor_idx or 0
47: 
48:         if cursor_idx >= 0 and cursor_idx < #rows then
49:             local row = rows[cursor_idx + 1]
50: 
51:             local ok, unit = pcall(function() return dfhack.gui.getWidget(row, 0).u end)
52:             if ok and unit then
53:                 return unit
54:             end
55:         end
56: 
57:         return nil
58:     end
59: 
60:     self:addviews{
61:         widgets.TextButton{
62:             frame={t=0, l=0},
63:             label='Show death cause',
64:             key='CUSTOM_D',
65:             on_activate=function()
66:                 local unit = get_selected_unit()
67:                 if not unit then
68:                     dialogs.showMessage('Death Cause', 'No unit selected.')
69:                     return
70:                 end
71:                 local cause = deathcause.getDeathCause(unit)
72:                 dialogs.showMessage('Death Cause', dfhack.df2console(cause))
73:             end,
74:         },
75:     }
76: end
77: 
78: return _ENV

54: local function to_item_type_str(item_type)
55:     return string.lower(df.item_type[item_type]):gsub('_', ' ')
56: end

58: local function make_item_description(item_type, subtype)
59:     local itemdef = dfhack.items.getSubtypeDef(item_type, subtype)
60:     return itemdef and string.lower(itemdef.name) or to_item_type_str(item_type)

63: local function get_preferences(unit)
64:     if not unit then return {} end
65:     local preferences = {}
66:     for _, pref in ipairs(unit.status.current_soul.preferences) do
67:         if pref.type == df.unitpref_type.LikeItem and pref.flags.visible then
68:             table.insert(preferences, make_item_description(pref.item_type, pref.item_subtype))
69:         end
70:     end
71:     return preferences
72: end

74: local function get_unit(hfid)
75:     local hf = df.historical_figure.find(hfid)
76:     if not hf then return end
77:     return df.unit.find(hf.unit_id)
78: end

80: function DiplomacyOverlay:get_search_key(hfid)
81:     local unit = get_unit(hfid)
82:     if not unit then return end
83:     return ('%s %s'):format(sortoverlay.get_unit_search_key(unit),
84:         table.concat(get_preferences(unit), ' '))
85: end

88: function DiplomacyOverlay:get_sort()
89:     local _, sh = dfhack.screen.getWindowSize()
90:     local list_height = sh - 17
91:     local num_elems = list_height // 3
92:     diplomacy.scroll_position_land_holder_hf = math.max(0,
93:         math.min(diplomacy.scroll_position_land_holder_hf,
94:             #diplomacy.land_holder_avail_hfid - num_elems))
95:     return function(a, b)
96:         local unita = get_unit(a)
97:         local unitb = get_unit(b)
98:         return #get_preferences(unita) < #get_preferences(unitb)
99:     end
100: end

117:     default_enabled=true,
118:     frame={w=29, h=1},
119: }

121: function PreferenceOverlay:init()
122:     self:addviews{
123:         widgets.Label{
124:             view_id='annotations',
125:             frame={t=0, l=0},
126:             text='',
127:             text_pen=COLOR_GRAY,
128:         }
129:     }
130: end

132: function PreferenceOverlay:preUpdateLayout(parent_rect)
133:     local list_height = parent_rect.height - 17
134:     self.frame.w = parent_rect.width - 85
135:     self.frame.h = list_height
136:     local margin = (parent_rect.width - 114) // 3
137:     self.subviews.annotations.frame.l = margin
138: end
139: 
140: function PreferenceOverlay:onRenderFrame(dc, rect)
141:     local margin = self.subviews.annotations.frame.l
142:     local num_elems = self.frame.h // 3
143:     local max_elem = math.min(#diplomacy.land_holder_avail_hfid-1,
144:         diplomacy.scroll_position_land_holder_hf+num_elems-1)
145: 
146:     local annotations = {}
147:     for idx=diplomacy.scroll_position_land_holder_hf,max_elem do
148:         table.insert(annotations, NEWLINE)
149:         table.insert(annotations, NEWLINE)
150:         local prefs = get_preferences(get_unit(diplomacy.land_holder_avail_hfid[idx]))
151:         table.insert(annotations, {text='[', pen=COLOR_RED})
152:         if #prefs == 0 then
153:             table.insert(annotations, 'no item preferences')
154:         else
155:             local pref_str = ('%d preference%s: %s'):format(#prefs,
156:                 #prefs > 1 and 's' or '', table.concat(prefs, ', '))
157:             table.insert(annotations, pref_str:sub(1, self.frame.w-(margin+2)))
158:         end
159:         table.insert(annotations, {text=']', pen=COLOR_RED})
160:         table.insert(annotations, NEWLINE)
161:     end
162:     self.subviews.annotations:setText(annotations)
163:     self.subviews.annotations:updateLayout()
164: 
165:     PreferenceOverlay.super.onRenderFrame(self, dc, rect)
166: end
167: 
168: return _ENV

17: local function get_cri_unit_search_key(cri_unit)
18:     return ('%s %s'):format(
19:         cri_unit.un and sortoverlay.get_unit_search_key(cri_unit.un) or '',
20:         cri_unit.job_sort_name)
21: end
22: 
23: local function get_task_search_key(cri_unit)
24:     local result = {get_cri_unit_search_key(cri_unit)}
25: 
26:     if cri_unit.jb then
27:         local bld = dfhack.job.getHolder(cri_unit.jb)
28:         if bld then
29:             table.insert(result, bld.name)
30:             local btype = bld:getType()
31:             if btype == df.building_type.Workshop then
32:                 -- the display name may differ from the enum item name (e.g.
33:                 -- Masons -> "Stoneworker's Workshop") and is the raw-defined
34:                 -- name for custom workshops
35:                 table.insert(result, dfhack.buildings.getName(bld))
36:                 table.insert(result, df.workshop_type.attrs[bld.type].name or '')
37:                 table.insert(result, df.workshop_type[bld.type])
38:             elseif btype == df.building_type.Furnace then
39:                 table.insert(result, df.furnace_type[bld.type])
40:             elseif btype == df.building_type.Construction then
41:                 table.insert(result, df.construction_type[bld.type])
42:             elseif btype == df.building_type.Trap then
43:                 table.insert(result, df.trap_type[bld.trap_type])
44:             elseif btype == df.building_type.SiegeEngine then
45:                 table.insert(result, df.siegeengine_type[bld.type])
46:             else
47:                 table.insert(result, df.building_type.attrs[btype].name)
48:             end
49:         end
50:     end
51: 
52:     return table.concat(result, ' ')
53: end

77: local function free_allocated_data(data)
78:     if data.saved_visible and data.saved_original and #data.saved_visible ~= #data.saved_original then
79:         for _,elem in ipairs(data.saved_original) do
80:             if not utils.linear_index(data.saved_visible, elem) then
81:                 elem:delete()
82:             end
83:         end
84:     end
85:     data.saved_original, data.saved_visible = nil, nil
86: end

88: local function serialize_skills(unit)
89:     if not unit or not unit.status or not unit.status.current_soul then
90:         return ''
91:     end
92:     local skills = {}
93:     for _, skill in ipairs(unit.status.current_soul.skills) do
94:         if skill.rating > 0 then -- ignore dabbling
95:             table.insert(skills, df.job_skill[skill.id])
96:         end
97:     end
98:     return table.concat(skills, ' ')
99: end

126: function get_squad_options()
127:     local options = {{label='Any', value='all', pen=COLOR_GREEN}}
128:     local fort = df.historical_entity.find(df.global.plotinfo.group_id)
129:     if not fort then return options end
130:     for _, squad_id in ipairs(fort.squads) do
131:         table.insert(options, {
132:             label=dfhack.military.getSquadName(squad_id),
133:             value=squad_id,
134:             pen=COLOR_YELLOW,
135:         })
136:     end
137:     return options
138: end
139: 
140: function get_burrow_options()
141:     local options = {
142:         {label='Any', value='all', pen=COLOR_GREEN},
143:         {label='Unburrowed', value='none', pen=COLOR_LIGHTRED},
144:     }
145:     for _, burrow in ipairs(df.global.plotinfo.burrows.list) do
146:         table.insert(options, {
147:             label=#burrow.name > 0 and burrow.name or ('Burrow %d'):format(burrow.id + 1),
148:             value=burrow.id,
149:             pen=COLOR_YELLOW,
150:         })
151:     end
152:     return options
153: end
154: 
155: function matches_squad_burrow_filters(unit, subset, target_squad_id, target_burrow_id)
156:     if subset == 'all' then
157:         return true
158:     elseif subset == 'civilian' then
159:         return unit.military.squad_id == -1
160:     elseif subset == 'military' then
161:         local squad_id = unit.military.squad_id
162:         if squad_id == -1 then return false end
163:         if target_squad_id == 'all' then return true end
164:         return target_squad_id == squad_id
165:     elseif subset == 'burrow' then
166:         if target_burrow_id == 'all' then return #unit.burrows + #unit.inactive_burrows > 0 end
167:         if target_burrow_id == 'none' then return #unit.burrows + #unit.inactive_burrows == 0 end
168:         return utils.binsearch(unit.burrows, target_burrow_id) or
169:             utils.binsearch(unit.inactive_burrows, target_burrow_id)
170:     end
171:     return true
172: end

284: function resize_overlay(self)
285:     local iw = gui.get_interface_rect().width
286:     local overlay_width = math.min(40, iw - (self.frame_rect.x1 + 30))
287:     if overlay_width ~= self.frame.w then
288:         self.frame.w = overlay_width
289:         return true
290:     end
291: end

293: local function is_tabs_in_two_rows()
294:     return gui.get_interface_rect().width < 155
295: end
296: 
297: function get_panel_offsets()
298:     local tabs_in_two_rows = is_tabs_in_two_rows()
299:     local shift_right = info.current_mode == df.info_interface_mode_type.ARTIFACTS
300:     local l_offset = (not tabs_in_two_rows and shift_right) and 4 or 0
301:     local t_offset = 1
302:     if tabs_in_two_rows then
303:         t_offset = shift_right and 0 or 3
304:     end
305:     if info.current_mode == df.info_interface_mode_type.JOBS then
306:         t_offset = t_offset - 1
307:     end
308:     return l_offset, t_offset
309: end
310: 
311: function InfoOverlay:updateFrames()
312:     local ret = resize_overlay(self)
313:     local l, t = get_panel_offsets()
314:     local frame = self.subviews.panel.frame
315:     if frame.l == l and frame.t == t then return ret end
316:     frame.l, frame.t = l, t
317:     local frame2 = self.subviews.subset_panel.frame
318:     frame2.l, frame2.t = l, t + 1
319:     local frame3 = self.subviews.subfilter_panel.frame
320:     frame3.l, frame3.t = l, t + 2
321:     return true
322: end
323: 
324: function InfoOverlay:do_refresh()
325:     self.refresh_search = nil
326:     if self:get_key() == 'JOBS' then
327:         local data = self.state.JOBS
328:         -- if any jobs have been canceled, fix up our data vectors
329:         if data and data.saved_visible and data.saved_original then
330:             local to_remove = {}
331:             for _,elem in ipairs(data.saved_visible) do
332:                 if not utils.linear_index(tasks.cri_job, elem) then
333:                     table.insert(to_remove, elem)
334:                 end
335:             end
336:             for _,elem in ipairs(to_remove) do
337:                 table.remove(data.saved_visible, utils.linear_index(data.saved_visible, elem))
338:                 data.saved_visible_size = data.saved_visible_size - 1
339:                 table.remove(data.saved_original, utils.linear_index(data.saved_original, elem))
340:                 data.saved_original_size = data.saved_original_size - 1
341:             end
342:         end
343:     end
344:     self:do_search(self.subviews.search.text, true)
345: end
346: 
347: function InfoOverlay:onRenderBody(dc)
348:     if self.refresh_search then
349:         self:do_refresh()
350:     end
351:     InfoOverlay.super.onRenderBody(self, dc)
352:     if self:updateFrames() then
353:         self:updateLayout()
354:     end
355: end
356: 
357: function InfoOverlay:onInput(keys)
358:     if self.refresh_search then
359:         self:do_refresh()
360:     end
361:     if keys._MOUSE_L then
362:         local key = self:get_key()
363:         if key == 'WORK_DETAILS' or key == 'JOBS' then
364:             self.refresh_search = true
365:         end
366:     end
367:     return InfoOverlay.super.onInput(self, keys)
368: end

372:         self.subviews.squad:getOptionValue(), self.subviews.burrow:getOptionValue())
373: end

454: local function get_work_animal_counts()
455:     local counts = {}
456:     for _,unit in ipairs(df.global.world.units.active) do
457:         if not dfhack.units.isOwnCiv(unit) or
458:             (not dfhack.units.isWar(unit) and not dfhack.units.isHunter(unit))
459:         then
460:             goto continue
461:         end
462:         local owner_id = unit.relationship_ids.PetOwner
463:         if owner_id == -1 then goto continue end
464:         counts[owner_id] = (counts[owner_id] or 0) + 1
465:         ::continue::
466:     end
467:     return counts
468: end

490: function WorkAnimalOverlay:onRenderFrame(dc, rect)
491:     local scroll_rows = get_scroll_rows()
492:     if not scroll_rows then return end
493:     local rows = dfhack.gui.getWidgetChildren(scroll_rows)
494:     local scroll_pos = get_scroll_pos(scroll_rows)
495:     local max_elem = math.min(#rows, scroll_pos+scroll_rows.num_visible)
496: 
497:     local annotations = {}
498:     local counts = get_work_animal_counts()
499:     for idx=scroll_pos+1,max_elem do
500:         table.insert(annotations, NEWLINE)
501:         table.insert(annotations, NEWLINE)
502:         local unit = dfhack.gui.getWidget(rows[idx], 0).u
503:         local animal_count = counts[unit.id]
504:         if animal_count and animal_count > 0 then
505:             table.insert(annotations, {text='[', pen=COLOR_RED})
506:             table.insert(annotations, ('Assigned work animals: %d'):format(animal_count))
507:             table.insert(annotations, {text=']', pen=COLOR_RED})
508:         end
509:         table.insert(annotations, NEWLINE)
510:     end
511:     self.subviews.annotations:setText(annotations)
512:     self.subviews.annotations:updateLayout()
513: 
514:     WorkAnimalOverlay.super.onRenderFrame(self, dc, rect)
