556:                 squad.enabled = false
557:                 burrow.enabled = false
558:                 if value == 'military' then
559:                     squad.options = get_squad_options()
560:                     squad.enabled = true
561:                 else
562:                     squad:setOption('all')
563:                 end
564:                 if value == 'burrow' then
565:                     burrow.options = get_burrow_options()
566:                     burrow.enabled = true
567:                 else
568:                     burrow:setOption('all')
569:                 end
570:                 poke_list()
571:             end,
572:         },
573:         widgets.CycleHotkeyLabel{
574:             view_id='squad',
575:             frame={l=0, t=1, r=0},
576:             key='CUSTOM_SHIFT_S',
577:             label=' Squad:',
578:             options={
579:                 {label='Any', value='all', pen=COLOR_GREEN},
580:             },
581:             enabled=false,
582:             on_change=poke_list,
583:         },
584:         widgets.CycleHotkeyLabel{
585:             view_id='burrow',
586:             frame={l=0, t=2, r=0},
587:             key='CUSTOM_SHIFT_B',
588:             label='Burrow:',
589:             options={
590:                 {label='Any', value='all', pen=COLOR_GREEN},
591:             },
592:             enabled=false,
593:             on_change=poke_list,
594:         },
595:     }
596: end
597:

599:     local unitlist = get_hunting_assignment()
600:     require('plugins.sort').sort_set_work_animal_assignment_filter_fn(unitlist)
601:     WorkAnimalFilterOverlay.super.render(self, dc)
602: end

607:         self.subviews.subset:getOptionValue(),
608:         self.subviews.squad:getOptionValue(),
609:         self.subviews.burrow:getOptionValue())
610: end

713: --

715: convict_instance = nil
716: 
717: ConvictionOverlay = defclass(ConvictionOverlay, JusticeOverlay)
718: ConvictionOverlay.ATTRS{
719:     desc='Adds filter capabilities to the conviction screen.',
720:     default_pos={x=1, y=-6},
721:     default_enabled=true,
722:     viewscreens='dwarfmode/Info/JUSTICE/Convicting',
723:     frame={w=30, h=3},
724:     which='Convict'
725: }
726: 
727: function ConvictionOverlay:init()
728:     convict_instance = self
729: end
730: 
731: -- ----------------------
732: -- filtering logic
733: --
734: 
735: local RISKY_PROFESSIONS = utils.invert{
736:     df.profession.THIEF,
737:     df.profession.MASTER_THIEF,
738:     df.profession.CRIMINAL,
739: }
740: 
741: local function is_risky(unit)
742:     if RISKY_PROFESSIONS[unit.profession] or RISKY_PROFESSIONS[unit.profession2] then
743:         return true
744:     end
745:     if dfhack.units.getReadableName(unit):find('necromancer') then return true end
746:     return not dfhack.units.isAlive(unit)  -- detect intelligent undead
747: end
748: 
749: local function filter_matches(unit, subset)
750:     if subset == 'all' then
751:         return true
752:     elseif dfhack.units.isDead(unit) or not dfhack.units.isActive(unit) then
753:         return subset == 'deceased'
754:     elseif dfhack.units.isInvader(unit) or dfhack.units.isOpposedToLife(unit)
755:         or unit.flags2.visitor_uninvited or dfhack.units.isAgitated(unit)
756:     then
757:         return subset == 'others'
758:     elseif dfhack.units.isVisiting(unit) then
759:         local risky = is_risky(unit)
760:         return (subset == 'risky' and risky) or (subset == 'visitors' and not risky)
761:     elseif dfhack.units.isAnimal(unit) then
762:         return subset == 'animals'
763:     elseif dfhack.units.isCitizen(unit) then
764:         return subset == 'citizens'
765:     elseif unit.flags2.roaming_wilderness_population_source then
766:         return subset == 'others'
767:     end
768:     return subset == 'residents'
769: end
770: 
771: function do_justice_filter(unit)
772:     local self
773:     if dfhack.gui.matchFocusString('dwarfmode/Info/JUSTICE/Interrogating') then
774:         self = interrogate_instance
775:         if not self.subviews.include_interviewed:getOptionValue() and
776:             require('plugins.sort').sort_is_interviewed(unit)
777:         then
778:             return false
779:         end
780:     else
781:         self = convict_instance
782:     end
783:     return filter_matches(unit, self.subviews.subset:getOptionValue())
784: end
785: 
786: return _ENV

SOURCE D:/program/steam/steamapps/common/DFHack/hack/lua/plugins/sort/locationselector.lua
1: local _ENV = mkmodule('plugins.sort.locationselector')
2: 
3: local sortoverlay = require('plugins.sort.sortoverlay')
4: local widgets = require('gui.widgets')
5: 
6: local location_selector = df.global.game.main_interface.location_selector
7: 
8: -- ----------------------
9: -- LocationSelectorOverlay
10: --
11: 
12: LocationSelectorOverlay = defclass(LocationSelectorOverlay, sortoverlay.SortOverlay)
13: LocationSelectorOverlay.ATTRS{
14:     desc='Adds search and filter capabilities to the temple and guildhall establishment screens.',
15:     default_pos={x=48, y=6},
16:     viewscreens='dwarfmode/LocationSelector',
17:     frame={w=26, h=3},
18: }
19: 
20: local function add_spheres(hf, spheres)
21:     if not hf then return end
22:     for _, sphere in ipairs(hf.info.metaphysical.spheres) do
23:         spheres[sphere] = true
24:     end
25: end
26: 
27: local function stringify_spheres(spheres)
28:     local strs = {}
29:     for sphere in pairs(spheres) do
30:         table.insert(strs, df.sphere_type[sphere])
31:     end
32:     return table.concat(strs, ' ')
33: end
34: 
35: function get_religion_string(religion_id, religion_type)
36:     if religion_id == -1 then return end
37:     local entity
38:     local spheres = {}
39:     if religion_type == df.religious_practice_type.WORSHIP_HFID then
40:         entity = df.historical_figure.find(religion_id)
41:         add_spheres(entity, spheres)
42:     elseif religion_type == df.religious_practice_type.RELIGION_ENID then
43:         entity = df.historical_entity.find(religion_id)
44:         if entity then
45:             for _, deity in ipairs(entity.relations.deities) do
46:                 add_spheres(df.historical_figure.find(deity), spheres)
47:             end
48:         end
49:     end
50:     if not entity then return end
51:     return ('%s %s'):format(dfhack.translation.translateName(entity.name, true), stringify_spheres(spheres))
52: end
53: 
54: function get_profession_string(profession)
55:     local profession_string = df.profession[profession]:gsub('_', ' ')
56:     local dwarfified_string = profession_string:gsub('[Mm][Aa][Nn]', 'dwarf')
57:     return profession_string .. ' ' .. dwarfified_string
58: end
59: 
60: function LocationSelectorOverlay:init()
61:     local panel = widgets.Panel{
62:         visible=self:callback('get_key'),
63:     }
64:     panel:addviews{
65:         widgets.BannerPanel{
66:             frame={l=0, t=0, r=0, h=1},
67:             subviews={
68:                 widgets.EditField{
69:                     view_id='search',
70:                     frame={l=1, t=0, r=1},
71:                     label_text="Search: ",
72:                     key='CUSTOM_ALT_S',
73:                     on_change=function(text) self:do_search(text) end,
74:                 },
75:             },
76:         },
77:         widgets.BannerPanel{
78:             frame={l=0, t=2, r=0, h=1},
79:             subviews={
80:                 widgets.ToggleHotkeyLabel{
81:                     view_id='hide_established',
82:                     frame={l=1, t=0, r=1},
83:                     label="Hide established:",
84:                     key='CUSTOM_SHIFT_E',
85:                     initial_option=false,
86:                     on_change=function() self:do_search(self.subviews.search.text, true) end,
87:                 },
88:             },
89:         },
90:     }
91:     self:addviews{panel}
92: 
93:     self:register_handler('TEMPLE', location_selector.valid_religious_practice_id,
94:         curry(sortoverlay.flags_vector_search,
95:             {
96:                 get_search_key_fn=get_religion_string,
97:                 matches_filters_fn=self:callback('matches_temple_filter'),
98:             },
99:             location_selector.valid_religious_practice))
100:     self:register_handler('GUILDHALL', location_selector.valid_craft_guild_type,
101:         curry(sortoverlay.single_vector_search,
102:             {
103:                 get_search_key_fn=get_profession_string,
104:                 matches_filters_fn=self:callback('matches_guildhall_filter'),
105:             }))
106: end
107: 
108: function LocationSelectorOverlay:get_key()
109:     if location_selector.choosing_temple_religious_practice then
110:         return 'TEMPLE'
111:     elseif location_selector.choosing_craft_guild then
112:         return 'GUILDHALL'
113:     end
114: end
115: 
116: function LocationSelectorOverlay:reset()
117:     LocationSelectorOverlay.super.reset(self)
118:     self.cache = nil
119:     self.subviews.hide_established:setOption(false, false)
120: end
121: 
122: function LocationSelectorOverlay:get_cache()
123:     if self.cache then return self.cache end
124:     local cache = {}
125:     local site = dfhack.world.getCurrentSite() or {}
126:     for _,location in ipairs(site.buildings or {}) do
127:         if df.abstract_building_templest:is_instance(location) then
128:             ensure_keys(cache, 'temple', location.deity_type)[location.deity_data.Religion] = true
129:         elseif df.abstract_building_guildhallst:is_instance(location) then
130:             ensure_keys(cache, 'guildhall')[location.contents.profession] = true
131:         end
132:     end
133:     self.cache = cache
134:     return self.cache
135: end
136: 
137: function LocationSelectorOverlay:matches_temple_filter(id, flag)
138:     local hide_established = self.subviews.hide_established:getOptionValue()
139:     return not hide_established or not safe_index(self:get_cache(), 'temple', flag, id)
140: end
141: 
142: function LocationSelectorOverlay:matches_guildhall_filter(id)
143:     local hide_established = self.subviews.hide_established:getOptionValue()
144:     return not hide_established or not safe_index(self:get_cache(), 'guildhall', id)
145: end
146: 
147: return _ENV

SOURCE D:/program/steam/steamapps/common/DFHack/hack/lua/plugins/sort/places.lua
1: local _ENV = mkmodule('plugins.sort.places')
2: 
3: local sortoverlay = require('plugins.sort.sortoverlay')
4: local locationselector = require('plugins.sort.locationselector')
5: local info_overlay = require('plugins.sort.info')
6: local widgets = require('gui.widgets')
7: local utils = require('utils')
8: 
9: local info = df.global.game.main_interface.info
10: local buildings = info.buildings
11: 
12: local zone_names = {
13:     [df.civzone_type.MeetingHall] = 'Meeting Area',
14:     [df.civzone_type.Bedroom] = 'Bedroom',
15:     [df.civzone_type.DiningHall] = 'Dining Hall',
16:     [df.civzone_type.Pen] = 'Pen Pasture',
17:     [df.civzone_type.Pond] = 'Pit Pond',
18:     [df.civzone_type.WaterSource] = 'Water Source',
19:     [df.civzone_type.Dungeon] = 'Dungeon',
20:     [df.civzone_type.FishingArea] = 'Fishing',
21:     [df.civzone_type.SandCollection] = 'Sand',
22:     [df.civzone_type.Office] = 'Office',
23:     [df.civzone_type.Dormitory] = 'Dormitory',
24:     [df.civzone_type.Barracks] = 'Barracks',
25:     [df.civzone_type.ArcheryRange] = 'Archery Range',
26:     [df.civzone_type.Dump] = 'Garbage Dump',
27:     [df.civzone_type.AnimalTraining] = 'Animal Training',
28:     [df.civzone_type.Tomb] = 'Tomb',
29:     [df.civzone_type.PlantGathering] = 'Gather Fruit',
30:     [df.civzone_type.ClayCollection] = 'Clay'
31: }
32: 
33: -- I used strings rather than df.civzone_type because nobody is going to search "MeadHall" they're going to search "Tavern"
34: local language_name_types = {
35:     [df.language_name_type.FoodStore] = 'Inn Tavern',
36:     [df.language_name_type.Temple] = 'Temple',
37:     [df.language_name_type.Hospital] = 'Hospital',
38:     [df.language_name_type.Guildhall] = 'Guildhall',
39:     [df.language_name_type.Library] = 'Library',
40: }
41: 
42: local stockpile_settings_flag_names = {
43:     animals = 'Animal',
44:     food = 'Food',
45:     furniture = 'Furniture',
46:     corpses = 'Corpse',
47:     refuse = 'Refuse',
48:     stone = 'Stone',
49:     ammo = 'Ammo',
50:     coins = 'Coins',
51:     bars_blocks = 'Bars/Blocks',
52:     gems = 'Gems',
53:     finished_goods = 'Finished Goods',
54:     leather = 'Leather',
55:     cloth = 'Cloth',
56:     wood = 'Wood',
57:     weapons = 'Weapon',
58:     armor = 'Armor',
59:     sheet = 'Sheet'
60: }

62: local function get_location_religion(religion_id, religion_type)
63:     if religion_type == df.religious_practice_type.NONE then return 'Temple'
64:     else return locationselector.get_religion_string(religion_id, religion_type) or '' end
65: end
66: 
67: local function get_default_zone_name(zone_type)
68:     return zone_names[zone_type] or ''
69: end
70: 
71: local function get_zone_search_key(zone)
72:     local result = {}
73: 
74:     -- allow zones to be searchable by their name
75:     if #zone.name == 0 then
76:         table.insert(result, get_default_zone_name(zone.type))
77:     else
78:         table.insert(result, zone.name)
79:     end
80: 
81:     -- allow zones w/ assignments to be searchable by their assigned unit
82:     local owner = dfhack.buildings.getOwner(zone)
83:     if owner then
84:         table.insert(result, sortoverlay.get_unit_search_key(owner))
85:     end
86: 
87:     -- allow zones to be searchable by type
88:     if zone.location_id == -1 then -- zone is NOT a special location and we don't need to do anything special for type searching
89:         table.insert(result, df.civzone_type[zone.type]);
90:     else -- zone is a special location and we need to get its type from world data
91:         local site = dfhack.world.getCurrentSite() or {}
92:         local building, success = utils.binsearch(site.buildings or {}, zone.location_id, 'id')
93: 
94:         if success and building.name then
95:             table.insert(result, language_name_types[building.name.type] or '')
96:             if building.name.has_name then
97:                 table.insert(result, dfhack.translation.translateName(building.name, true))
98:             end
99:         end
100:     end
101: 
102:     -- allow barracks to be searchable by assigned squad
103:     for _, squad in ipairs(zone.squad_room_info) do
104:         table.insert(result, dfhack.military.getSquadName(squad.squad_id))
105:     end
106: 
107:     return table.concat(result, ' ')
108: end
109: 
110: local function get_location_search_key(zone)
111:     local site = dfhack.world.getCurrentSite() or {}
112:     local result = {}
113: 
114:     -- get language_name and type (we dont need user-given zone name because it does not appear on this page)
115:     local building, success = utils.binsearch(site.buildings or {}, zone.location_id, 'id')
116:     if success and building.name then
117:         table.insert(result, language_name_types[building.name.type] or '')
118:         if building.name.has_name then
119:             table.insert(result, dfhack.translation.translateName(building.name, true))
120:         end
121: 
122:         -- for temples and guildhalls, get assigned organization
123:         if df.abstract_building_templest:is_instance(building) then
124:             table.insert(result, get_location_religion(building.deity_data.Deity or building.deity_data.Religion, building.deity_type))
125:         elseif df.abstract_building_guildhallst:is_instance(building) then
126:             local dwarfified_profession = locationselector.get_profession_string(building.contents.profession)
127:             table.insert(result, dwarfified_profession)
128:         end
129: 
130:     end
131: 
132:     return table.concat(result, ' ')
133: end
134: 
135: local function get_stockpile_search_key(stockpile)
136:     local result = {}
137:     if #stockpile.name ~= 0 then table.insert(result, stockpile.name) end
138: 
139:     local flags = stockpile.settings.flags
140:     for flag, name in pairs(stockpile_settings_flag_names) do
141:         if flags[flag] then table.insert(result, name) end
142:     end
143: 
144:     table.insert(result, ('Stockpile #%s'):format(stockpile.stockpile_number))
145:     return table.concat(result, ' ')
146: end
147: 
148: local function get_workshop_search_key(workshop)
149:     local result = {}
150:     for _, unit_id in ipairs(workshop.profile.permitted_workers) do
151:         local unit = df.unit.find(unit_id)
152:         if unit then table.insert(result, sortoverlay.get_unit_search_key(unit)) end
153:     end
154: 
155:     table.insert(result, workshop.name)
156: 
157:     -- the display name may differ from the enum item name (e.g. Masons ->
158:     -- "Stoneworker's Workshop") and is the raw-defined name for custom
159:     -- workshops
160:     table.insert(result, dfhack.buildings.getName(workshop))
161: 
162:     table.insert(result, df.workshop_type.attrs[workshop.type].name or '')
163:     table.insert(result, df.workshop_type[workshop.type])
164: 
165:     return table.concat(result, ' ')
166: end
167: 
168: local function get_farmplot_search_key(farmplot)
169:     local result = {}
170: 
171:     if #farmplot.name ~= 0 then table.insert(result, farmplot.name) else table.insert(result, 'Farm Plot') end
172: 
173:     local plant = df.plant_raw.find(farmplot.plant_id[farmplot.last_season])
174:     if plant then table.insert(result, plant.name_plural) end
175: 
176:     return table.concat(result, ' ')
177: end

179: ---@param siege_engine df.building_siegeenginest
180: ---@return string
181: local function siege_engine_type(siege_engine)
182:     if siege_engine.type == df.siegeengine_type.BoltThrower then
183:         return 'Bolt Thrower'
184:     end
185:     return df.siegeengine_type[siege_engine.type]
186: end
187: 
188: ---@param siege_engine df.building_siegeenginest
189: ---@return string
190: local function siege_engine_status(siege_engine)
191:     -- portions of return value with with underscores are to allow easier
192:     -- word-anchored matching even when the DFHack full-text search mode is
193:     -- enabled; e.g.
194:     -- - "loaded" would match "Loaded" and "Unloaded",
195:     --   - but "_loaded" would only match "_Loaded"
196:     local count = 0
197:     local count_all = siege_engine.type == df.siegeengine_type.BoltThrower
198:     for _, building_item in ipairs(siege_engine.contained_items) do
199:         if building_item.use_mode == df.building_item_role_type.TEMP then
200:             if not count_all then
201:                 return 'Loaded _Loaded'
202:             end
203:             count = count + building_item.item:getStackSize()
204:         end
205:     end
206:     if count_all and count > 0 then
207:         return ('%d bolts _%d_bolts'):format(count, count)
208:     end
209:     return 'Unloaded'
210: end
211: 
212: ---@param siege_engine df.building_siegeenginest
213: ---@return string
214: local function siege_engine_job_status(siege_engine)
215:     for _, job in ipairs(siege_engine.jobs) do
216:         if job.job_type == df.job_type.LoadCatapult
217:             or job.job_type == df.job_type.LoadBallista
218:             or job.job_type == df.job_type.LoadBoltThrower
219:         then
220:             if dfhack.job.getWorker(job) ~= nil then
221:                 return 'Loading'
222:             else
223:                 return 'Inactive load task'
224:             end
225:         end
226:         local firing_bolt_thrower = job.job_type == df.job_type.FireBoltThrower
227:         local firing = job.job_type == df.job_type.FireCatapult
228:             or job.job_type == df.job_type.FireBallista
229:             or firing_bolt_thrower
230:         if firing then
231:             local unit = dfhack.job.getWorker(job)
232:             if unit == nil then
233:                 return 'No operator'
234:             else
235:                 ---@type integer?, integer?, integer?
236:                 local x, y, z = dfhack.units.getPosition(unit)
237:                 -- DF shows "present" when the unit is inside the building's
238:                 -- footprint (or, for bolt throwers, next to it); the unit does
239:                 -- not need to be at the exact firing position tile (which
240:                 -- varies based on siege engine type and direction)
241:                 if x ~= nil and z == siege_engine.z then
242:                     ---@cast y integer
243:                     if firing_bolt_thrower then
244:                         if siege_engine.x1 - 1 <= x and x <= siege_engine.x2 + 1
245:                             and siege_engine.y1 - 1 <= y and y <= siege_engine.y2 + 1
246:                         then
247:                             return 'Operator present'
248:                         end
249:                     elseif dfhack.buildings.containsTile(siege_engine, x, y) then
250:                         return 'Operator present'
251:                     end
252:                 end
253:                 return 'Operator assigned'
254:             end
255:         end
256:     end
257:     return ''
258: end
259: 
260: ---@param siege_engine df.building_siegeenginest
261: ---@return string
262: local function get_siege_engine_search_key(siege_engine)
263:     -- DF 53.05 Info window, Places tab, Siege Engines subtab shows this info:
264:     --       name: assigned name or siege engine type name
265:     --     status: "Unloaded", "Loaded", "<N> bolts"
266:     -- job status:
267:     --             - "Inactive load task" (load job unassigned),
268:     --             - "Loading" (load job assigned),
269:     --             - "No operator" (fire job unassigned),
270:     --             - "Operator present" (fire job assigned),
271:     --             - "Operator assigned" (fire job assigned, but not in position),
272:     --             - blank
273:     --     action: (icons) fire-at-will, practice, prepare-to-fire, keep-loaded, not-in-use
274:     --             These have associated text blurbs that are shown in the
275:     --             building info window, but those texts are not discoverable
276:     --             from the Info > Places > Siege engine list view.
277:     local result = {}
278: 
279:     if #siege_engine.name ~= 0 then table.insert(result, siege_engine.name) end
280: 
281:     table.insert(result, siege_engine_type(siege_engine))
282: 
283:     table.insert(result, siege_engine_status(siege_engine))
284: 
285:     table.insert(result, siege_engine_job_status(siege_engine))
286: 
287:     return table.concat(result, ' ')
288: end
289:

291: -- PlacesOverlay
292: --
293:

312:                     frame={l=1, t=0, r=1},
313:                     label_text="Search: ",
314:                     key='CUSTOM_ALT_S',
315:                     on_change=function(text) self:do_search(text) end,
316:                 },
317:             },

322:     self:register_handler('LOCATIONS', buildings.list[df.buildings_mode_type.LOCATIONS], curry(sortoverlay.single_vector_search, {get_search_key_fn=get_location_search_key}))

327: end

337:     local l, t = info_overlay.get_panel_offsets()

342: end

70: function SlabOverlay:get_key()
71:     -- DF fails to set building.category back to NONE if there are no units that
72:     -- can be memorialized, so we have to manually check for a populated button vector
73:     if #building.button > 0 and
74:         building.category == df.interface_category_building.SELECT_MEMORIAL_UNIT
75:     then
76:         return 'SLAB'
77:     else
78:         if safe_index(self.state, 'SLAB', 'saved_original') then
79:             -- elements get freed as soon as the screen changes
80:             self.state.SLAB.saved_original = nil
81:         end
82:     end
83: end

88: end

90: local function get_unit(if_button)
91:     local hf = df.historical_figure.find(if_button.specdata.hist_figure_id)
92:     return hf and df.unit.find(hf.unit_id) or nil
93: end
94: 
95: function SlabOverlay:get_search_key(if_button)
96:     local unit = get_unit(if_button)
97:     if not unit then return if_button.filter_str end
98:     return sortoverlay.get_unit_search_key(unit)
99: end
100: 
101: local function needs_slab(if_button)
102:     local unit = get_unit(if_button)
103:     if not unit then return false end
104:     if not dfhack.units.isOwnGroup(unit) then return false end
105:     local info = dfhack.toSearchNormalized(if_button.info)
106:     if not info:find('no slabs engraved', 1, true) then return false end
107:     return info:find('not memorialized', 1, true) or info:find('ghost', 1, true)
108: end
109: 
110: function SlabOverlay:matches_filters(if_button)
111:     local only_needs_slab = self.subviews.only_needs_slab:getOptionValue()
112:     return not only_needs_slab or needs_slab(if_button)
113: end

SOURCE D:/program/steam/steamapps/common/DFHack/hack/lua/plugins/sort/sortoverlay.lua
1: local _ENV = mkmodule('plugins.sort.sortoverlay')
2: 
3: local overlay = require('plugins.overlay')
4: local utils = require('utils')
5: 
6: function get_unit_search_key(unit)
7:     return dfhack.units.getReadableName(unit)
8: end
9: 
10: local function copy_to_lua_table(vec)
11:     local tab = {}
12:     for k,v in ipairs(vec) do
13:         tab[k+1] = v
14:     end
15:     return tab
16: end
17: 
18: -- ----------------------
19: -- SortOverlay
20: --
21: 
22: SortOverlay = defclass(SortOverlay, overlay.OverlayWidget)
23: SortOverlay.ATTRS{
24:     default_enabled=true,
25:     hotspot=true,
26:     overlay_onupdate_max_freq_seconds=0,
27:     -- subclasses expected to provide default_pos, viewscreens, and frame
28:     -- viewscreens should be the top-level scope(s) within which the search widget state is maintained
29:     -- once the player leaves that scope, widget state will be reset
30: }
31: 
32: function SortOverlay:init()
33:     self.state = {}
34:     self.handlers = {}
35:     -- subclasses expected to provide an EditField widget with view_id='search'
36: end
37: 
38: function SortOverlay:register_handler(key, vec, search_fn, cleanup_fn)
39:     self.handlers[key] = {
40:         vec=vec,
41:         search_fn=search_fn,
42:         cleanup_fn=cleanup_fn
43:     }
44: end
45: 
46: local function do_cleanup(handlers, key, data)
47:     if not key or not data then return end
48:     local cleanup_fn = safe_index(handlers, key, 'cleanup_fn')
49:     if cleanup_fn then
50:         cleanup_fn(data)
51:     end
52:     data.saved_original = nil
53: end
54: 
55: local function get_scope(scopes)
56:     if type(scopes) == 'string' then scopes = {scopes} end
57:     local scr = dfhack.gui.getDFViewscreen(true)
58:     for _,scope in ipairs(scopes) do
59:         if dfhack.gui.matchFocusString(scope, scr) then
60:             return scope
61:         end
62:     end
63: end
64: 
65: function SortOverlay:has_exited_scope()
66:     local cur_scope = get_scope(self.viewscreens)
67:     local ret = self.cur_scope and self.cur_scope ~= cur_scope
68:     self.cur_scope = cur_scope
69:     return ret
70: end
71: 
72: -- handles reset and clean up when the player exits the handled scope
73: function SortOverlay:overlay_onupdate()
74:     if self.overlay_onupdate_max_freq_seconds ~= 0 then return end
75:     if self:has_exited_scope() then
76:         for key,data in pairs(self.state) do
77:             if type(data) == 'table' then
78:                 do_cleanup(self.handlers, key, data)
79:             end
80:         end
81:         self:reset()
82:         if self.cur_scope then
83:             self.overlay_onupdate_max_freq_seconds = 300
84:         end
85:     end
86: end
87: 
88: function SortOverlay:reset()
89:     self.state = {}
90:     self.subviews.search:setText('')
91:     self.subviews.search:setFocus(false)
92: end
93: 
94: -- returns the current context key for dereferencing the handler
95: -- subclasses must override
96: function SortOverlay:get_key()
97:     return nil
98: end
99: 
100: -- handles saving/restoring search strings when the player moves between different contexts
101: function SortOverlay:onRenderBody(dc)
102:     local key, group = self:get_key()
103:     if self.state.cur_group ~= group then
104:         self.state.cur_group = group
105:         do_cleanup(self.handlers, self.state.cur_key, self.state[self.state.cur_key])
106:     end
107:     if self.state.cur_key ~= key then
108:         self.state.cur_key = key
109:         local prev_text = key and ensure_key(self.state, key).prev_text or ''
110:         self.subviews.search:setText(prev_text)
111:         self:do_search(self.subviews.search.text, true)
112:     end
113:     self.overlay_onupdate_max_freq_seconds = 0
114:     SortOverlay.super.onRenderBody(self, dc)
115: end
116: 
117: local function is_mouse_key(keys)
118:     return keys._MOUSE_L
119:         or keys._MOUSE_R
120:         or keys._MOUSE_M
121:         or keys.CONTEXT_SCROLL_UP
122:         or keys.CONTEXT_SCROLL_DOWN
123:         or keys.CONTEXT_SCROLL_PAGEUP
124:         or keys.CONTEXT_SCROLL_PAGEDOWN
125: end
126: 
127: function SortOverlay:onInput(keys)
128:     local key = self:get_key()
129:     if keys._MOUSE_R and self.subviews.search.focus and key then
130:         self.subviews.search:setFocus(false)
131:         return true
132:     end
133:     return key and (SortOverlay.super.onInput(self, keys) or
134:         (self.subviews.search.focus and not is_mouse_key(keys)))
135: end
136: 
137: function SortOverlay:do_search(text, force_full_search)
138:     if not force_full_search and not next(self.state) and text == '' then return end
139:     -- the EditField state is guaranteed to be consistent with the current
140:     -- context since when clicking to switch tabs, onRenderBody is always called
141:     -- before this text_input callback, even if a key is pressed before the next
142:     -- graphical frame would otherwise be printed. if this ever becomes untrue,
143:     -- then we can add an on_char handler to the EditField that also checks for
144:     -- context transitions.
145:     local key = self:get_key()
146:     if not key then return end
147:     local prev_text = ensure_key(self.state, key).prev_text
148:     -- some screens reset their contents between context switches; regardless,
149:     -- a switch back to the context should result in an incremental search
150:     local incremental = not force_full_search and prev_text and text:startswith(prev_text)
151:     local handler = self.handlers[key]
152:     handler.search_fn(handler.vec, self.state[key], text, incremental)
153:     self.state[key].prev_text = text
154: end
155: 
156: local function filter_vec(fns, flags_vec, vec, text, erase_fn)
157:     if fns.matches_filters_fn or text ~= '' then
158:         local search_tokens = text:split()
159:         for idx = #vec-1,0,-1 do
160:             local flag = flags_vec and flags_vec[idx] or nil
161:             local search_key = fns.get_search_key_fn and fns.get_search_key_fn(vec[idx], flag) or nil
162:             if (search_key and not utils.search_text(search_key, search_tokens)) or
163:                 (fns.matches_filters_fn and not fns.matches_filters_fn(vec[idx], flag))
164:             then
165:                 erase_fn(idx)
166:             end
167:         end
168:     end
169: end
170: 
171: function single_vector_search(fns, vec, data, text, incremental)
172:     vec = utils.getval(vec)
173:     if not data.saved_original then
174:         data.saved_original = copy_to_lua_table(vec)
175:         data.saved_original_size = #vec
176:     elseif not incremental then
177:         vec:assign(data.saved_original)
178:         vec:resize(data.saved_original_size)
179:     end
180:     filter_vec(fns, nil, vec, text, function(idx) vec:erase(idx) end)
181:     data.saved_visible = copy_to_lua_table(vec)
182:     data.saved_visible_size = #vec
183:     if fns.get_sort_fn then
184:         table.sort(data.saved_visible, fns.get_sort_fn())
185:         vec:assign(data.saved_visible)
186:         vec:resize(data.saved_visible_size)
187:     end
188: end
189: 
190: -- doesn't support sorting since nothing that uses this needs it yet
191: function flags_vector_search(fns, flags_vec, vec, data, text, incremental)
192:     local get_elem_id_fn = fns.get_elem_id_fn or function(elem) return elem end
193:     flags_vec, vec = utils.getval(flags_vec), utils.getval(vec)
194:     if not data.saved_original then
195:         -- we save the sizes since trailing nils get lost in the lua -> vec assignment
196:         data.saved_original = copy_to_lua_table(vec)
197:         data.saved_original_size = #vec
198:         data.saved_flags = copy_to_lua_table(flags_vec)
199:         data.saved_flags_size = #flags_vec
200:         data.saved_idx_map = {}
201:         for idx,elem in ipairs(data.saved_original) do
202:             data.saved_idx_map[get_elem_id_fn(elem)] = idx  -- 1-based idx
203:         end
204:     else  -- sync flag changes to saved vector
205:         for idx,elem in ipairs(vec) do  -- 0-based idx
206:             data.saved_flags[data.saved_idx_map[get_elem_id_fn(elem)]] = flags_vec[idx]
207:         end
208:     end
209: 
210:     if not incremental then
211:         vec:assign(data.saved_original)
212:         vec:resize(data.saved_original_size)
213:         flags_vec:assign(data.saved_flags)
214:         flags_vec:resize(data.saved_flags_size)
215:     end
216: 
217:     filter_vec(fns, flags_vec, vec, text, function(idx)
218:         vec:erase(idx)
219:         flags_vec:erase(idx)
220:     end)
221: end
222: 
223: return _ENV

SOURCE D:/program/steam/steamapps/common/DFHack/hack/lua/plugins/sort/stressicons.lua
1: local _ENV = mkmodule('plugins.sort.stressicons')
2: 
3: local dialogs = require('gui.dialogs')
4: local overlay = require('plugins.overlay')
5: 
6: -- indexed by stress category (0-6, where 0 is most stressed)
7: local STRESS_CATEGORY_NAMES = {
8:     [0]='Miserable', 'Unhappy', 'Displeased', 'Content', 'Pleased', 'Happy',
9:     'Ecstatic',
10: }
11: 
12: -- single-unit equivalent of the dfhack.units.getCitizens(true, true) filter
13: local function is_listable(unit)
14:     return dfhack.units.isCitizen(unit, true) and dfhack.units.isActive(unit)
15: end
16: 
17: local function show_stress_units(category)
18:     local choices = {}
19:     -- living, on-map citizens only (excluding residents), matching the game's
20:     -- own stress icon counts
21:     for _,unit in ipairs(dfhack.units.getCitizens(true, true)) do
22:         if dfhack.units.getStressCategory(unit) == category then
23:             table.insert(choices, {
24:                 text=dfhack.units.getReadableName(unit),
25:                 unit_id=unit.id,
26:             })
27:         end
28:     end
29:     local name = STRESS_CATEGORY_NAMES[category]
30:     if #choices == 0 then
31:         dialogs.showMessage('Citizens: ' .. name,
32:             ('No citizens are currently %s.'):format(name:lower()))
33:         return
34:     end
35:     dialogs.ListBox{
36:         frame_title='Citizens: ' .. name,
37:         with_filter=true,
38:         choices=choices,
39:         on_select=function(_, choice)
40:             -- re-resolve in case the unit died or left the map while the list
41:             -- was open; df.unit.find still resolves dead units
42:             local unit = df.unit.find(choice.unit_id)
43:             if not unit or not is_listable(unit) then return end
44:             dfhack.gui.revealInDwarfmodeMap(unit.pos.x, unit.pos.y, unit.pos.z, true, true)
45:         end,
46:     }:show()
47: end
48: 
49: -- invisible hotspot over the stress icons in the top bar; clicking an icon
50: -- opens a list of the citizens in that stress category
51: StressIconsOverlay = defclass(StressIconsOverlay, overlay.OverlayWidget)
52: StressIconsOverlay.ATTRS{
53:     desc='Click a stress icon in the top bar to list the citizens in that stress category.',
54:     default_pos={x=1, y=1},
55:     default_enabled=true,
56:     viewscreens={'dwarfmode/Default'},
57:     overlay_onupdate_max_freq_seconds=1,
58:     frame={w=1, h=1},
59: }
60: 
61: function StressIconsOverlay:overlay_onupdate()
62:     self.icon_rects = nil
63:     local dimx = df.global.gps.dimx
64:     -- the 'Pop' label immediately precedes the stress icons in the top bar;
65:     -- scan right to left so a fortress name containing 'Pop' doesn't win
66:     local pop_x
67:     for x = math.min(dimx-3, 200), 0, -1 do
68:         if dfhack.screen.readTile(x, 0).ch == 80 and         -- 'P'
69:                 dfhack.screen.readTile(x+1, 0).ch == 111 and -- 'o'
70:                 dfhack.screen.readTile(x+2, 0).ch == 112 then -- 'p'
71:             pop_x = x
72:             break
73:         end
74:     end
75:     if not pop_x then return end
76:     -- the icons are drawn in 3-column cells right of the 'Pop' block, with the
77:     -- happiest category first
78:     local rects = {}
79:     for i = 0, 6 do
80:         local x1 = pop_x + 4 + i * 3
81:         if x1 + 1 >= dimx then break end
82:         table.insert(rects, {x1=x1, y1=0, x2=x1+2, y2=2, category=6-i})
83:     end
84:     self.icon_rects = rects
85:     self.frame = {l=pop_x+4, t=0, w=#rects*3, h=3}
86: end
87: 
88: function StressIconsOverlay:onInput(keys)
89:     if not keys._MOUSE_L or not self.icon_rects then return false end
90:     local mx, my = dfhack.screen.getMousePos()
91:     if not mx then return false end
92:     for _,rect in ipairs(self.icon_rects) do
93:         if my >= rect.y1 and my <= rect.y2 and
94:                 mx >= rect.x1 and mx <= rect.x2 then
95:             show_stress_units(rect.category)
96:             return true
97:         end
98:     end
99:     return false
100: end
101: 
102: return _ENV

SOURCE D:/program/steam/steamapps/common/DFHack/hack/lua/plugins/sort/units.lua
1: local _ENV = mkmodule('plugins.sort.units')
2: 
3: local utils = require('utils')
4: 
5: orders = orders or {}
6: 
7: -- Relies on NULL being auto-translated to NULL, and then sorted
8: orders.exists = {
9:     key = function(unit)
10:         return 1
11:     end
12: }
13: 
14: orders.name = {
15:     key = function(unit)
16:         local name = dfhack.units.getVisibleName(unit)
17:         if name and name.has_name then
18:             return dfhack.translation.translateName(name)
19:         end
20:     end,
21:     compare = utils.compare_name
22: }
23: 
24: orders.age = {
25:     key = function(unit)
26:         return dfhack.units.getAge(unit)
27:     end
28: }
29: 
30: -- This assumes that units are added to active in arrival order
31: orders.arrival = {
32:     key_table = function(units)
33:         local size = units.n or #units
34:         local lookup={}
35:         for i=1,size do
36:             if units[i] then
37:                 lookup[units[i].id] = i
38:             end
39:         end
40:         local idx={}
41:         for i,v in ipairs(df.global.world.units.active) do
42:             if lookup[v.id] then
43:                 idx[lookup[v.id]] = i
44:             end
45:         end
46:         return idx
47:     end
48: }
49: 
50: local function findRaceCaste(unit)
51:     local rraw = df.creature_raw.find(unit.race)
52:     return rraw, safe_index(rraw, 'caste', unit.caste)
53: end
54: 
55: orders.noble = {
56:     key = function(unit)
57:         local info = dfhack.units.getNoblePositions(unit)
58:         if info then
59:             return info[1].position.precedence
60:         end
61:     end
62: }
63: 
64: orders.profession = {
65:     key = function(unit)
66:         local cp = dfhack.units.getProfessionName(unit)
67:         if cp and cp ~= '' then
68:             return string.lower(cp)
69:         end
70:     end
71: }
72: 
73: orders.profession_class = {
74:     key = function(unit)
75:         local pid = unit.profession
76:         local parent = df.profession.attrs[pid].parent
77:         if parent >= 0 then
78:             return parent
79:         else
80:             return pid
81:         end
82:     end
83: }
84: 
85: orders.race = {
86:     key = function(unit)
87:         local rraw = findRaceCaste(unit)
88:         if rraw then
89:             return rraw.name[0]
90:         end
91:     end
92: }
93: 
94: orders.squad = {
95:     key = function(unit)
96:         local sidx = unit.military.squad_id
97:         if sidx >= 0 then
98:             return sidx
99:         end
100:     end
101: }
102: 
103: orders.squad_position = {
104:     key = function(unit)
105:         local sidx = unit.military.squad_id
106:         if sidx >= 0 then
107:             return sidx * 1000 + unit.military.squad_position
108:         end
109:     end
110: }
111: 
112: orders.happiness = {
113:     key = function(unit)

115:     end

SOURCE D:/program/steam/steamapps/common/DFHack/hack/lua/plugins/sort/world.lua
1: local _ENV = mkmodule('plugins.sort.world')
2: 
3: local sortoverlay = require('plugins.sort.sortoverlay')
4: local widgets = require('gui.widgets')
5: 
6: -- ----------------------
7: -- WorldOverlay
8: --
9: 
10: WorldOverlay = defclass(WorldOverlay, sortoverlay.SortOverlay)
11: WorldOverlay.ATTRS{
12:     desc='Adds search functionality to the artifact list on the world raid screen.',
13:     default_pos={x=-18, y=2},
14:     viewscreens='world/ARTIFACTS',
15:     frame={w=40, h=1},
16: }
17: 
18: local function get_world_artifact_search_key(artifact, rumor)
19:     local search_key = ('%s %s'):format(dfhack.translation.translateName(artifact.name, true),
20:         dfhack.items.getDescription(artifact.item, 0))
21:     if rumor then
22:         local hf = df.historical_figure.find(rumor.hfid)
23:         if hf then
24:             search_key = ('%s %s %s'):format(search_key,
25:                 dfhack.translation.translateName(hf.name),
26:                 dfhack.translation.translateName(hf.name, true))
27:         end
28:         local ws = df.world_site.find(rumor.stid)
29:         if ws then
30:             search_key = ('%s %s'):format(search_key,
31:                 dfhack.translation.translateName(ws.name, true))
32:         end
33:     else
34:         local hf = df.historical_figure.find(artifact.holder_hf)
35:         if hf then
36:             local unit = df.unit.find(hf.unit_id)
37:             if unit then
38:                 search_key = ('%s %s'):format(search_key,
39:                     dfhack.units.getReadableName(unit))
40:             end
41:         end
42:     end
43:     return search_key
44: end
45: 
46: local function cleanup_artifact_vectors(data)
47:     local vs_world = dfhack.gui.getDFViewscreen(true)
48:     if not df.viewscreen_worldst:is_instance(vs_world) then return end
49:     vs_world.artifact:assign(data.saved_original)
50:     vs_world.artifact:resize(data.saved_original_size)
51:     vs_world.artifact_arl:assign(data.saved_flags)
52:     vs_world.artifact_arl:resize(data.saved_flags_size)
53: end
54: 
55: function WorldOverlay:init()
56:     self:addviews{
57:         widgets.BannerPanel{
58:             frame={l=0, t=0, r=0, h=1},
59:             visible=self:callback('get_key'),
60:             subviews={
61:                 widgets.EditField{
62:                     view_id='search',
63:                     frame={l=1, t=0, r=1},
64:                     label_text="Search: ",
65:                     key='CUSTOM_ALT_S',
66:                     on_change=function(text) self:do_search(text) end,
67:                 },
68:             },
69:         },
70:     }
71: 
72:     self:register_handler('ARTIFACTS',
73:         function() return dfhack.gui.getDFViewscreen(true).artifact end,
74:         curry(sortoverlay.flags_vector_search,
75:             {
76:                 get_search_key_fn=get_world_artifact_search_key,
77:                 get_elem_id_fn=function(artifact_record) return artifact_record.id end,
78:             },
79:             function() return dfhack.gui.getDFViewscreen(true).artifact_arl end),
80:         cleanup_artifact_vectors)
81: end
82: 
83: function WorldOverlay:get_key()
84:     local scr = dfhack.gui.getDFViewscreen(true)
85:     if scr.view_mode == df.world_view_mode_type.ARTIFACTS then
86:         return 'ARTIFACTS'
87:     end
88: end
89: 
90: return _ENV

SOURCE D:/program/steam/steamapps/common/DFHack/hack/lua/plugins/sort.lua
1: local _ENV = mkmodule('plugins.sort')
2: 
3: local gui = require('gui')
4: local overlay = require('plugins.overlay')
5: local setbelief = reqscript('modtools/set-belief')
6: local utils = require('utils')
7: local widgets = require('gui.widgets')
8: 
9: local GLOBAL_KEY = 'sort'
10: 
11: local function get_rating(val, baseline, range, highest, high, med, low)
12:     val = val - (baseline or 0)
13:     range = range or 100
14:     local percentile = (math.min(range, val) * 100) // range
15:     if percentile < (low or 25) then return percentile, COLOR_RED end
16:     if percentile < (med or 50) then return percentile, COLOR_LIGHTRED end
17:     if percentile < (high or 75) then return percentile, COLOR_YELLOW end
18:     if percentile < (highest or 90) then return percentile, COLOR_GREEN end
19:     return percentile, COLOR_LIGHTGREEN
20: end
21: 
22: local function get_name(unit)
23:     return unit and dfhack.toSearchNormalized(dfhack.translation.translateName(dfhack.units.getVisibleName(unit)))
24: end
25: 
26: local function sort_by_name_desc(unit1, unit2)
27:     local name1 = get_name(unit1)
28:     local name2 = get_name(unit2)
29:     return utils.compare_name(name1, name2)
30: end
31: 
32: local active_units = df.global.world.units.active
33: local active_idx_cache = {}
34: local function get_active_idx_cache()
35:     local num_active_units = #active_units
36:     if num_active_units == 0 or active_idx_cache[active_units[num_active_units-1].id] ~= num_active_units-1 then
37:         active_idx_cache = {}
38:         for i,active_unit in ipairs(active_units) do
39:             active_idx_cache[active_unit.id] = i
40:         end
41:     end
42:     return active_idx_cache
43: end
44: 
45: local function is_original_dwarf(unit)
46:     return df.global.plotinfo.fortress_age == unit.usable_interaction.time_on_site // 10
47: end
48: 
49: local WAVE_END_GAP = 10000
50: 
51: local function get_most_recent_wave_oldest_active_idx(cache)
52:     local oldest_unit
53:     for idx=#active_units-1,0,-1 do
54:         local unit = active_units[idx]
55:         if not dfhack.units.isCitizen(unit) then goto continue end
56:         if oldest_unit and unit.usable_interaction.time_on_site - oldest_unit.usable_interaction.time_on_site > WAVE_END_GAP then
57:             return cache[oldest_unit.id]
58:         else
59:             oldest_unit = unit
60:         end
61:         ::continue::
62:     end
63: end
64: 
65: -- return green for most recent wave, red for the first wave, yellow for all others
66: -- rating is an up to three digit number that indicates the (approximate) order
67: local function get_arrival_rating(unit)
68:     local cache = get_active_idx_cache()
69:     local unit_active_idx = cache[unit.id]
70:     if not unit_active_idx then return end
71:     local most_recent_wave_oldest_active_idx = get_most_recent_wave_oldest_active_idx(cache)
72:     if not most_recent_wave_oldest_active_idx then return end
73:     local num_active_units = #active_units
74:     local rating = num_active_units < 1000 and unit_active_idx or ((unit_active_idx * 1000) // #active_units)
75:     if most_recent_wave_oldest_active_idx < unit_active_idx then
76:         return rating, COLOR_LIGHTGREEN
77:     end
78:     if is_original_dwarf(unit) then
79:         return rating, COLOR_RED
80:     end
81:     return rating, COLOR_YELLOW
82: end
83: 
84: local function sort_by_arrival_desc(unit1, unit2)
85:     local cache = get_active_idx_cache()
86:     if not cache[unit1.id] then return -1 end
87:     if not cache[unit2.id] then return 1 end
88:     return utils.compare(cache[unit2.id], cache[unit1.id])
89: end
90: 
91: local function sort_by_arrival_asc(unit1, unit2)
92:     local cache = get_active_idx_cache()
93:     if not cache[unit1.id] then return -1 end
94:     if not cache[unit2.id] then return 1 end
95:     return utils.compare(cache[unit1.id], cache[unit2.id])
96: end
97: 
98: local function get_stress(unit)
99:     return unit and
100:         unit.status.current_soul and
101:         unit.status.current_soul.personality.stress
102: end
103: 
104: local function get_stress_rating(unit)
105:     return get_rating(dfhack.units.getStressCategory(unit), 0, 100, 4, 3, 2, 1)
106: end
107: 
108: local function sort_by_stress_desc(unit1, unit2)
109:     local happiness1 = get_stress(unit1)
110:     local happiness2 = get_stress(unit2)
111:     if happiness1 == happiness2 then
112:         return sort_by_name_desc(unit1, unit2)
113:     end
114:     if not happiness2 then return -1 end
115:     if not happiness1 then return 1 end
116:     return utils.compare(happiness2, happiness1)
117: end
118: 
119: local function sort_by_stress_asc(unit1, unit2)
120:     local happiness1 = get_stress(unit1)
121:     local happiness2 = get_stress(unit2)
122:     if happiness1 == happiness2 then
123:         return sort_by_name_desc(unit1, unit2)
124:     end
125:     if not happiness2 then return 1 end
126:     if not happiness1 then return -1 end
127:     return utils.compare(happiness1, happiness2)
128: end
129: 
130: local function get_skill(skill, unit)
131:     return unit and
132:         unit.status.current_soul and
133:         (utils.binsearch(unit.status.current_soul.skills, skill, 'id'))
134: end
135: 
136: local function get_skill_rating(skill, unit)
137:     local uskill = get_skill(skill, unit)
138:     if not uskill then return nil end
139:     return get_rating(uskill.rating, 0, 100, 10, 5, 1, 0)
140: end
141: 
142: local MELEE_WEAPON_SKILLS = {
143:     df.job_skill.AXE,
144:     df.job_skill.SWORD,
145:     df.job_skill.MACE,
146:     df.job_skill.HAMMER,
147:     df.job_skill.SPEAR,
148: }
149: 
150: local function melee_skill_effectiveness(unit)
151:     -- Physical attributes
152:     local strength = dfhack.units.getPhysicalAttrValue(unit, df.physical_attribute_type.STRENGTH)
153:     local agility = dfhack.units.getPhysicalAttrValue(unit, df.physical_attribute_type.AGILITY)
154:     local toughness = dfhack.units.getPhysicalAttrValue(unit, df.physical_attribute_type.TOUGHNESS)
155:     local endurance = dfhack.units.getPhysicalAttrValue(unit, df.physical_attribute_type.ENDURANCE)
156:     local body_size_base = unit.body.size_info.size_base
157: 
158:     -- Mental attributes
159:     local willpower = dfhack.units.getMentalAttrValue(unit, df.mental_attribute_type.WILLPOWER)
160:     local spatial_sense = dfhack.units.getMentalAttrValue(unit, df.mental_attribute_type.SPATIAL_SENSE)
161:     local kinesthetic_sense = dfhack.units.getMentalAttrValue(unit, df.mental_attribute_type.KINESTHETIC_SENSE)
162: 
163:     -- Skills
164:     -- Finding the highest skill
165:     local skill_rating = 0
166:     for _, skill in ipairs(MELEE_WEAPON_SKILLS) do
167:         local melee_skill = dfhack.units.getNominalSkill(unit, skill, true)
168:         skill_rating = math.max(skill_rating, melee_skill)
169:     end
170:     local melee_combat_rating = dfhack.units.getNominalSkill(unit, df.job_skill.MELEE_COMBAT, true)
171: 
172:     local rating = skill_rating * 27000 + melee_combat_rating * 9000
173:             + strength * 180 + body_size_base * 100 + kinesthetic_sense * 50 + endurance * 50
174:             + agility * 30 + toughness * 20 + willpower * 20 + spatial_sense * 20
175:     return rating
176: end
177: 
178: local function get_melee_skill_effectiveness_rating(unit)
179:     return get_rating(melee_skill_effectiveness(unit), 350000, 2750000, 64, 52, 40, 28)
180: end
181: 
182: local function make_sort_by_melee_skill_effectiveness_desc()
183:     return function(unit1, unit2)
184:         local rating1 = melee_skill_effectiveness(unit1)
185:         local rating2 = melee_skill_effectiveness(unit2)
186:         if rating1 == rating2 then return sort_by_name_desc(unit1, unit2) end
187:         return utils.compare(rating2, rating1)
188:     end
189: end
190: 
191: local function make_sort_by_melee_skill_effectiveness_asc()
192:     return function(unit1, unit2)
193:         local rating1 = melee_skill_effectiveness(unit1)
194:         local rating2 = melee_skill_effectiveness(unit2)
195:         if rating1 == rating2 then return sort_by_name_desc(unit1, unit2) end
196:         return utils.compare(rating1, rating2)
197:     end
198: end
199: 
200: local RANGED_WEAPON_SKILLS = {
201:     df.job_skill.CROSSBOW,
202: }
203: 
204: -- Function could easily be adapted to different weapon types.
205: local function ranged_skill_effectiveness(unit)
206:     -- Physical attributes
207:     local agility = dfhack.units.getPhysicalAttrValue(unit, df.physical_attribute_type.AGILITY)
208: 
209:     -- Mental attributes
210:     local spatial_sense = dfhack.units.getMentalAttrValue(unit, df.mental_attribute_type.SPATIAL_SENSE)
211:     local kinesthetic_sense = dfhack.units.getMentalAttrValue(unit, df.mental_attribute_type.KINESTHETIC_SENSE)
212:     local focus = dfhack.units.getMentalAttrValue(unit, df.mental_attribute_type.FOCUS)
213: 
214:     -- Skills
215:     -- Finding the highest skill
216:     local skill_rating = 0
217:     for _, skill in ipairs(RANGED_WEAPON_SKILLS) do
218:         local ranged_skill = dfhack.units.getNominalSkill(unit, skill, true)
219:         skill_rating = math.max(skill_rating, ranged_skill)
220:     end
221:     local ranged_combat = dfhack.units.getNominalSkill(unit, df.job_skill.RANGED_COMBAT, true)
222: 
223:     local rating = skill_rating * 24000 + ranged_combat * 8000
224:     + agility * 15 + spatial_sense * 15 + kinesthetic_sense * 6 + focus * 6
225:     return rating
226: end
227: 
228: local function get_ranged_skill_effectiveness_rating(unit)
229:     return get_rating(ranged_skill_effectiveness(unit), 0, 800000, 72, 52, 31, 11)
230: end
231: 
232: local function make_sort_by_ranged_skill_effectiveness_desc()
233:     return function(unit1, unit2)
234:         local rating1 = ranged_skill_effectiveness(unit1)
235:         local rating2 = ranged_skill_effectiveness(unit2)
236:         if rating1 == rating2 then return sort_by_name_desc(unit1, unit2) end
237:         return utils.compare(rating2, rating1)
238:     end
239: end
240: 
241: local function make_sort_by_ranged_skill_effectiveness_asc()
242:     return function(unit1, unit2)
243:         local rating1 = ranged_skill_effectiveness(unit1)
244:         local rating2 = ranged_skill_effectiveness(unit2)
245:         if rating1 == rating2 then return sort_by_name_desc(unit1, unit2) end
246:         return utils.compare(rating1, rating2)
247:     end
248: end
249: 
250: local function make_sort_by_skill_desc(sort_skill)
251:     return function(unit1, unit2)
252:         local s1 = get_skill(sort_skill, unit1)
253:         local s2 = get_skill(sort_skill, unit2)
254:         if s1 == s2 then return sort_by_name_desc(unit1, unit2) end
255:         if not s2 then return -1 end
256:         if not s1 then return 1 end
257:         if s1.rating ~= s2.rating then
258:             return utils.compare(s2.rating, s1.rating)
259:         end
260:         if s1.experience ~= s2.experience then
261:             return utils.compare(s2.experience, s1.experience)
262:         end
263:         return sort_by_name_desc(unit1, unit2)
264:     end
265: end
266: 
267: local function make_sort_by_skill_asc(sort_skill)
268:     return function(unit1, unit2)
269:         local s1 = get_skill(sort_skill, unit1)
270:         local s2 = get_skill(sort_skill, unit2)
271:         if s1 == s2 then return sort_by_name_desc(unit1, unit2) end
272:         if not s2 then return 1 end
273:         if not s1 then return -1 end
274:         if s1.rating ~= s2.rating then
275:             return utils.compare(s1.rating, s2.rating)
276:         end
277:         if s1.experience ~= s2.experience then
278:             return utils.compare(s1.experience, s2.experience)
279:         end
280:         return sort_by_name_desc(unit1, unit2)
281:     end
282: end
283: 
284: -- Statistical rating that is higher for dwarves that are mentally stable
285: local function get_mental_stability(unit)
286:     local altruism = unit.status.current_soul.personality.traits.ALTRUISM
287:     local anxiety_propensity = unit.status.current_soul.personality.traits.ANXIETY_PROPENSITY
288:     local bravery = unit.status.current_soul.personality.traits.BRAVERY
289:     local cheer_propensity = unit.status.current_soul.personality.traits.CHEER_PROPENSITY
290:     local curious = unit.status.current_soul.personality.traits.CURIOUS
291:     local discord = unit.status.current_soul.personality.traits.DISCORD
292:     local dutifulness = unit.status.current_soul.personality.traits.DUTIFULNESS
293:     local emotionally_obsessive = unit.status.current_soul.personality.traits.EMOTIONALLY_OBSESSIVE
294:     local humor = unit.status.current_soul.personality.traits.HUMOR
295:     local love_propensity = unit.status.current_soul.personality.traits.LOVE_PROPENSITY
296:     local perseverance = unit.status.current_soul.personality.traits.PERSEVERANCE
297:     local politeness = unit.status.current_soul.personality.traits.POLITENESS
298:     local privacy = unit.status.current_soul.personality.traits.PRIVACY
299:     local stress_vulnerability = unit.status.current_soul.personality.traits.STRESS_VULNERABILITY
300:     local tolerant = unit.status.current_soul.personality.traits.TOLERANT
301: 
302:     local craftsmanship = setbelief.getUnitBelief(unit, df.value_type['CRAFTSMANSHIP'])
303:     local family = setbelief.getUnitBelief(unit, df.value_type['FAMILY'])
304:     local harmony = setbelief.getUnitBelief(unit, df.value_type['HARMONY'])
305:     local independence = setbelief.getUnitBelief(unit, df.value_type['INDEPENDENCE'])
306:     local knowledge = setbelief.getUnitBelief(unit, df.value_type['KNOWLEDGE'])
307:     local leisure_time = setbelief.getUnitBelief(unit, df.value_type['LEISURE_TIME'])
308:     local nature = setbelief.getUnitBelief(unit, df.value_type['NATURE'])
309:     local skill = setbelief.getUnitBelief(unit, df.value_type['SKILL'])
310: 
311:     -- calculate the rating using the defined variables
312:     local rating = (craftsmanship * -0.01) + (family * -0.09) + (harmony * 0.05)
313:                  + (independence * 0.06) + (knowledge * -0.30) + (leisure_time * 0.24)
314:                  + (nature * 0.27) + (skill * -0.21) + (altruism * 0.13)
315:                  + (anxiety_propensity * -0.06) + (bravery * 0.06)
316:                  + (cheer_propensity * 0.41) + (curious * -0.06) + (discord * 0.14)
317:                  + (dutifulness * -0.03) + (emotionally_obsessive * -0.13)
318:                  + (humor * -0.05) + (love_propensity * 0.15) + (perseverance * -0.07)
319:                  + (politeness * -0.14) + (privacy * 0.03) + (stress_vulnerability * -0.20)
320:                  + (tolerant * -0.11)
321: 
322:     return rating
323: end
324: 
325: local function sort_by_mental_stability_desc(unit1, unit2)
326:     local rating1 = get_mental_stability(unit1)
327:     local rating2 = get_mental_stability(unit2)
328:     if rating1 == rating2 then
329:         -- sorting by stress is opposite
330:         -- more mental stable dwarves should have less stress
331:         return sort_by_stress_asc(unit1, unit2)
332:     end
333:     return utils.compare(rating2, rating1)
334: end
335: 
336: local function sort_by_mental_stability_asc(unit1, unit2)
337:     local rating1 = get_mental_stability(unit1)
338:     local rating2 = get_mental_stability(unit2)
339:     if rating1 == rating2 then
340:         return sort_by_stress_desc(unit1, unit2)
341:     end
342:     return utils.compare(rating1, rating2)
343: end
344: 
345: -- Statistical rating that is higher for more potent dwarves in long run melee military training
346: -- Rating considers fighting melee opponents
347: -- Wounds are not considered!
348: local function get_melee_combat_potential(unit)
349:     -- Physical attributes
350:     local strength = unit.body.physical_attrs.STRENGTH.max_value
351:     local agility = unit.body.physical_attrs.AGILITY.max_value
352:     local toughness = unit.body.physical_attrs.TOUGHNESS.max_value
353:     local endurance = unit.body.physical_attrs.ENDURANCE.max_value
354:     local body_size_base = unit.body.size_info.size_base
355: 
356:     -- Mental attributes
357:     local willpower = unit.status.current_soul.mental_attrs.WILLPOWER.max_value
358:     local spatial_sense = unit.status.current_soul.mental_attrs.SPATIAL_SENSE.max_value
359:     local kinesthetic_sense = unit.status.current_soul.mental_attrs.KINESTHETIC_SENSE.max_value
360: 
361:     -- assume highest skill ratings
362:     local skill_rating = df.skill_rating.Legendary5
363:     local melee_combat_rating = df.skill_rating.Legendary5
364: 
365:     -- melee combat potential rating
366:     local rating = skill_rating * 27000 + melee_combat_rating * 9000
367:             + strength * 180 + body_size_base * 100 + kinesthetic_sense * 50 + endurance * 50
368:             + agility * 30 + toughness * 20 + willpower * 20 + spatial_sense * 20
369:     return rating
370: end
371: 
372: local function get_melee_combat_potential_rating(unit)
373:     return get_rating(get_melee_combat_potential(unit), 350000, 2750000, 64, 52, 40, 28)
374: end
375: 
376: local function sort_by_melee_combat_potential_desc(unit1, unit2)
377:     local rating1 = get_melee_combat_potential(unit1)
378:     local rating2 = get_melee_combat_potential(unit2)
379:     if rating1 == rating2 then
380:         return sort_by_mental_stability_desc(unit1, unit2)
381:     end
382:     return utils.compare(rating2, rating1)
383: end
384: 
385: local function sort_by_melee_combat_potential_asc(unit1, unit2)
386:     local rating1 = get_melee_combat_potential(unit1)
387:     local rating2 = get_melee_combat_potential(unit2)
388:     if rating1 == rating2 then
389:         return sort_by_mental_stability_asc(unit1, unit2)
390:     end
391:     return utils.compare(rating1, rating2)
392: end
393: 
394: -- Statistical rating that is higher for more potent dwarves in long run ranged military training
395: -- Wounds are not considered!
396: local function get_ranged_combat_potential(unit)
397:     -- Physical attributes
398:     local agility = unit.body.physical_attrs.AGILITY.max_value
399: 
400:     -- Mental attributes
401:     local focus = unit.status.current_soul.mental_attrs.FOCUS.max_value
402:     local spatial_sense = unit.status.current_soul.mental_attrs.SPATIAL_SENSE.max_value
403:     local kinesthetic_sense = unit.status.current_soul.mental_attrs.KINESTHETIC_SENSE.max_value
404: 
405:     -- assume highest skill ratings
406:     local skill_rating = df.skill_rating.Legendary5
407:     local ranged_combat = df.skill_rating.Legendary5
408: 
409:     -- ranged combat potential formula
410:     local rating = skill_rating * 24000 + ranged_combat * 8000
411:             + agility * 15 + spatial_sense * 15 + kinesthetic_sense * 6 + focus * 6
412:     return rating
413: end
414: 
415: local function get_ranged_combat_potential_rating(unit)
416:     return get_rating(get_ranged_combat_potential(unit), 0, 800000, 72, 52, 31, 11)
417: end
418: 
419: local function sort_by_ranged_combat_potential_desc(unit1, unit2)
420:     local rating1 = get_ranged_combat_potential(unit1)
421:     local rating2 = get_ranged_combat_potential(unit2)
422:     if rating1 == rating2 then
423:         return sort_by_mental_stability_desc(unit1, unit2)
424:     end
425:     return utils.compare(rating2, rating1)
426: end
427: 
428: local function sort_by_ranged_combat_potential_asc(unit1, unit2)
429:     local rating1 = get_ranged_combat_potential(unit1)
430:     local rating2 = get_ranged_combat_potential(unit2)
431:     if rating1 == rating2 then
432:         return sort_by_mental_stability_asc(unit1, unit2)
433:     end
434:     return utils.compare(rating1, rating2)
435: end
436: 
437: local function get_need(unit)
438:     if not unit or not unit.status.current_soul then return end
439:     for _, need in ipairs(unit.status.current_soul.personality.needs) do
440:         if need.id == df.need_type.MartialTraining and need.focus_level < 0 then
441:             return -need.focus_level
442:         end
443:     end
444: end
445: 
446: local function get_need_rating(unit)
447:     local focus_level = get_need(unit)
448:     if not focus_level then return end
449:     -- convert to stress ratings so we can use stress faces as labels
450:     if focus_level > 100000 then return 0 end
451:     if focus_level > 10000 then return 1 end
452:     if focus_level > 1000 then return 2 end
453:     if focus_level > 100 then return 3 end
454:     return 6
455: end
456: 
457: local function sort_by_need_desc(unit1, unit2)
458:     local rating1 = get_need(unit1)
459:     local rating2 = get_need(unit2)
460:     if rating1 == rating2 then
461:         return sort_by_stress_desc(unit1, unit2)
462:     end
463:     if not rating2 then return -1 end
464:     if not rating1 then return 1 end
465:     return utils.compare(rating2, rating1)
466: end
467: 
468: local function sort_by_need_asc(unit1, unit2)
469:     local rating1 = get_need(unit1)
470:     local rating2 = get_need(unit2)
471:     if rating1 == rating2 then
472:         return sort_by_stress_asc(unit1, unit2)
473:     end
474:     if not rating2 then return 1 end
475:     if not rating1 then return -1 end
476:     return utils.compare(rating1, rating2)
477: end

522: }
523: for _, v in ipairs(SORT_LIBRARY) do
524:     SORT_LIBRARY[v.widget] = v
525: end
526: 
527: local unit_selector = df.global.game.main_interface.unit_selector
528: 
529: local use_stress_faces = false
530: local rating_annotations = {}
531: 
532: local function get_unit_selector()
533:     return dfhack.gui.getWidget(unit_selector, 'Unit selector')
534: end
535: 
536: local function get_scroll_rows()
537:     return dfhack.gui.getWidget(unit_selector, 'Unit selector', 'Unit List', 1)
538: end
539: 
540: local function get_scroll_pos(scroll_rows)
541:     return (scroll_rows or get_scroll_rows()).scroll
542: end
543: 
544: local function get_num_slots(screen_height)
545:     if not screen_height then
546:         _, screen_height = dfhack.screen.getWindowSize()
547:     end
548:     return (screen_height - 17) // 3
549: end
550: 
551: local function annotate_visible_units(sort_id)
552:     local opt = SORT_LIBRARY[sort_id]
553:     use_stress_faces = opt.use_stress_faces
554:     rating_annotations = {}
555:     rating_fn = opt.rating_fn
556:     local scroll_rows = get_scroll_rows()
557:     local rows = dfhack.gui.getWidgetChildren(scroll_rows)
558:     local scroll_pos = get_scroll_pos(scroll_rows)
559:     local max_idx = math.min(#rows, scroll_pos+scroll_rows.num_visible+1)
560:     for idx = scroll_pos+1, max_idx do
561:         local annotation_idx = idx - scroll_pos
562:         rating_annotations[annotation_idx] = nil
563:         local row = rows[idx]
564:         if rating_fn and df.widget_container:is_instance(row) then
565:             local unit = dfhack.gui.getWidget(row, 0).u
566:             local val, color = rating_fn(unit)
567:             if val then
568:                 rating_annotations[annotation_idx] = {val=val, color=color}
569:             end
570:         end
571:     end
572: end
573: 
574: -- ----------------------
575: -- SortButton
576: --
577: 
578: SortButton = defclass(SortButton, widgets.Panel)
579: SortButton.ATTRS{
580:     on_click=DEFAULT_NIL,
581: }
582: 
583: local to_pen = dfhack.pen.parse
584: 
585: local function make_sort_pens(ascending, selected)
586:     local init = df.global.init
587:     local name = ('texpos_sort_%s_%s'):format(
588:         ascending and 'ascending' or 'descending',
589:         selected and 'active' or 'inactive'
590:     )
591:     -- this is backwards, but it matches vanilla
592:     local ch = ascending and 31 or 30
593:     local fg = selected and COLOR_WHITE or COLOR_BLACK
594:     return {
595:         to_pen{tile=init[name][0], ch=32, fg=COLOR_BLACK, bg=COLOR_GRAY},
596:         to_pen{tile=init[name][1], ch=ch, fg=fg, bg=COLOR_GRAY},
597:         to_pen{tile=init[name][2], ch=ch, fg=fg, bg=COLOR_GRAY},
598:         to_pen{tile=init[name][3], ch=32, fg=COLOR_BLACK, bg=COLOR_GRAY},
599:     }
600: end
601: 
602: local SORT_PENS = {
603:     [true]={[true]=make_sort_pens(true, true), [false]=make_sort_pens(true, false)},
604:     [false]={[true]=make_sort_pens(false, true), [false]=make_sort_pens(false, false)},
605: }
606: 
607: local function sort_button_get_tile(self, idx)
608:     return SORT_PENS[self.ascending][self.selected][idx]
609: end
610: 
611: function SortButton:init()
612:     self.frame = self.frame or {}
613:     self.frame.w, self.frame.h = 4, 1
614: 
615:     self.ascending = false
616:     self.selected = false
617: 
618:     self:addviews{
619:         widgets.Label{
620:             text={
621:                 {width=1, tile=curry(sort_button_get_tile, self, 1)},
622:                 {width=1, tile=curry(sort_button_get_tile, self, 2)},
623:                 {width=1, tile=curry(sort_button_get_tile, self, 3)},
624:                 {width=1, tile=curry(sort_button_get_tile, self, 4)},
625:             },
626:             on_click=function()
627:                 if not self.selected then
628:                     self.selected = true
629:                 else
630:                     self.ascending = not self.ascending
631:                 end
632:                 self.on_click()
633:             end,
634:         }
635:     }
636: end
637: 
638: -- ----------------------
639: -- SquadAnnotationOverlay
640: --
641: 
642: annotation_instance = nil
643: 
644: SquadAnnotationOverlay = defclass(SquadAnnotationOverlay, overlay.OverlayWidget)
645: SquadAnnotationOverlay.ATTRS{
646:     desc='Adds sort and annotation capabilities to the squad assignment panel.',
647:     default_pos={x=15, y=5},
648:     version='2',
649:     default_enabled=true,
650:     viewscreens='dwarfmode/UnitSelector/SQUAD_FILL_POSITION',
651:     frame={w=82, h=35},
652: }
653: 
654: function get_annotation_text(idx)
655:     local elem = rating_annotations[idx]
656:     if not elem or not tonumber(elem.val) then return ' -- ' end
657:     return tostring(math.tointeger(elem.val))
658: end
659: 
660: function get_annotation_color(idx)
661:     local elem = rating_annotations[idx]
662:     return elem and elem.color or nil
663: end
664: 
665: local to_pen = dfhack.pen.parse
666: local DASH_PEN = to_pen{ch='-', fg=COLOR_WHITE, keep_lower=true}
667: 
668: local FACE_TILES = {}
669: local function init_face_tiles()
670:     for idx=0,6 do
671:         FACE_TILES[idx] = {}
672:         local face_off = (6 - idx) * 2
673:         for y=0,1 do
674:             for x=0,1 do
675:                 local tile = dfhack.screen.findGraphicsTile('INTERFACE_BITS', 32 + face_off + x, 6 + y)
676:                 ensure_key(FACE_TILES[idx], y)[x] = tile
677:             end
678:         end
679:     end
680: 
681:     for idx,color in ipairs{COLOR_RED, COLOR_LIGHTRED, COLOR_YELLOW, COLOR_WHITE, COLOR_GREEN, COLOR_LIGHTGREEN, COLOR_LIGHTCYAN} do
682:         local face = {}
683:         ensure_key(face, 0)[0] = to_pen{tile=FACE_TILES[idx-1][0][0], ch=1, fg=color, keep_lower=true}
684:         ensure_key(face, 0)[1] = to_pen{tile=FACE_TILES[idx-1][0][1], ch='\\', fg=color, keep_lower=true}
685:         ensure_key(face, 1)[0] = to_pen{tile=FACE_TILES[idx-1][1][0], ch='\\', fg=color, keep_lower=true}
686:         ensure_key(face, 1)[1] = to_pen{tile=FACE_TILES[idx-1][1][1], ch='/', fg=color, keep_lower=true}
687:         FACE_TILES[idx-1] = face
688:     end
689: end
690: init_face_tiles()
691: 
692: function get_stress_face_tile(idx, x, y)
693:     local elem = rating_annotations[idx]
694:     if not elem or not elem.val or elem.val < 0 then
695:         return y == 1 and DASH_PEN or gui.CLEAR_PEN
696:     end
697:     local val = math.min(6, elem.val)
698:     return safe_index(FACE_TILES, val, y, x)
699: end
700: 
701: local function get_label_text(screen_height)
702:     local text = {}
703:     for idx=1, get_num_slots(screen_height) do
704:         table.insert(text, NEWLINE)
705:         if use_stress_faces then
706:             table.insert(text, {gap=1, width=1, tile=curry(get_stress_face_tile, idx, 0, 0)})
707:             table.insert(text, {width=1, tile=curry(get_stress_face_tile, idx, 1, 0)})
708:             table.insert(text, NEWLINE)
709:             table.insert(text, {gap=1, width=1, tile=curry(get_stress_face_tile, idx, 0, 1)})
710:             table.insert(text, {width=1, tile=curry(get_stress_face_tile, idx, 1, 1)})
711:             table.insert(text, NEWLINE)
712:         else
713:             table.insert(text, {
714:                 text=curry(get_annotation_text, idx),
715:                 pen=curry(get_annotation_color, idx),
716:                 width=3,
717:                 rjustify=true,
718:             })
719:             table.insert(text, NEWLINE)
720:             table.insert(text, NEWLINE)
721:         end
722:     end
723:     return text
724: end
725: 
726: local function make_options(label, view_id)
727:     return {
728:         {label=label, value='noop'},
729:         {label=label, value=view_id, pen=COLOR_GREEN},
730:     }
731: end
732:

928: 
929: function SquadAnnotationOverlay:sync_widgets(sort_widget, sort_id)
930:     if sort_widget then
931:         if sort_id == 'noop' then
932:             self.subviews[sort_widget]:cycle()
933:             return
934:         end
935:         self.subviews.sort:setOption(sort_id)
936:         for _,opt in ipairs(SORT_LIBRARY) do
937:             self.subviews[opt.widget]:setOption(sort_id)
938:         end
939:     end
940:     sort_set_sort_fn()
941:     self.dirty = true
942: end
943: 
944: function do_sort(a, b)
945:     local self = annotation_instance
946:     local opt = SORT_LIBRARY[self.subviews.sort:getOptionValue()]
947:     local fn = self.subviews.sort_button.ascending and opt.asc_fn or opt.desc_fn
948:     return fn(a, b) < 0
949: end
950: 
951: function SquadAnnotationOverlay:mouse_over_ours()
952:     if self.subviews.annotation_panel:getMouseFramePos() then return true end
953:     if self.subviews.hover_panel.visible and self.subviews.hover_panel:getMouseFramePos() then
954:         return true
955:     end
956:     return self.subviews.banner_panel:getMouseFramePos()
957: end
958: 
959: function SquadAnnotationOverlay:onInput(keys)
960:     local clicked_on_us = keys._MOUSE_L and self:mouse_over_ours()
961:     if keys._MOUSE_R or (keys._MOUSE_L and not clicked_on_us) then
962:         -- if any click is made, we may need to refresh our annotations
963:         self.dirty = true
964:     end
965:     return SquadAnnotationOverlay.super.onInput(self, keys) or clicked_on_us
966: end
967: 
968: function SquadAnnotationOverlay:onRenderFrame(dc, rect)
969:     if self.dirty or
970:         self.saved_scroll_position ~= get_scroll_pos() or
971:         self.saved_num_visible ~= get_scroll_rows().num_visible
972:     then
973:         self.subviews.sort_button.selected = sort_get_sort_active()
974:         annotate_visible_units(self.subviews.sort:getOptionValue())
975:         self.saved_scroll_position = get_scroll_pos()
976:         self.dirty = false
977:     end
978:     self.subviews.label:setText(get_label_text(self.frame_parent_rect.height))
979:     if self.subviews.sort:getMousePos() then
980:         self.subviews.hover_panel.visible = true
981:     elseif self.subviews.hover_panel.visible and
982:         not self.subviews.hover_panel:getMouseFramePos()
983:     then
984:         self.subviews.hover_panel.visible = false
985:     end
986:     SquadAnnotationOverlay.super.onRenderFrame(self, dc, rect)
987: end
988: 
989: function SquadAnnotationOverlay:preUpdateLayout(parent_rect)
990:     self.frame.h = get_num_slots(parent_rect.height) * 3 + 4
991: end

1001: local function get_shifter_text(ch)
1002:     return {
1003:         ' ', NEWLINE,
1004:         ch, NEWLINE,
1005:         ch, NEWLINE,
1006:         ' ', NEWLINE,
1007:     }
1008: end

1200: function SquadFilterOverlay:preUpdateLayout(parent_rect)
1201:     if parent_rect.width >= 153 and self.frame.w == NARROW_WIDTH then

1207:     elseif parent_rect.width < 153 and self.frame.w == WIDE_WIDTH then
1208:         self.frame.w = NARROW_WIDTH
1209:         self.subviews.shifter.visible = true
1210:         self.subviews.shifter:setText(get_shifter_text(TO_THE_RIGHT))
1211:         self.subviews.divider.visible = false
1212:         self.subviews.left_panel.visible = true
1213:         self.subviews.right_panel.visible = false
