1217: local function is_in_military(unit)
1218:     return unit.military.squad_id > -1
1219: end
1220: 
1221: local function is_elected_or_appointed_official(unit)
1222:     for _,occupation in ipairs(unit.occupations) do
1223:         if occupation.type ~= df.occupation_type.MERCENARY then
1224:             return true
1225:         end
1226:     end
1227:     for _, noble_pos in ipairs(dfhack.units.getNoblePositions(unit) or {}) do
1228:         if noble_pos.position.flags.ELECTED or
1229:             (noble_pos.position.mandate_max == 0 and noble_pos.position.demand_max == 0)
1230:         then
1231:             return true
1232:         end
1233:     end
1234:     return false
1235: end
1236: 
1237: local function is_nobility(unit)
1238:     for _, noble_pos in ipairs(dfhack.units.getNoblePositions(unit) or {}) do
1239:         if not noble_pos.position.flags.ELECTED and
1240:             (noble_pos.position.mandate_max > 0 or noble_pos.position.demand_max > 0)
1241:         then
1242:             return true
1243:         end
1244:     end
1245:     return false
1246: end
1247: 
1248: local function has_infant(unit)
1249:     for _, baby in ipairs(df.global.world.units.other.ANY_BABY) do
1250:         if baby.relationship_ids.Mother == unit.id then
1251:             return true
1252:         end
1253:     end
1254:     return false
1255: end
1256: 
1257: local function is_unstable(unit)
1258:     -- stddev percentiles are 61, 48, 35, 23
1259:     -- let's go with one stddev below the mean (35) as the cutoff
1260:     local _, color = get_rating(get_mental_stability(unit), -40, 80, 35, 0, 0, 0)
1261:     return color ~= COLOR_LIGHTGREEN
1262: end
1263: 
1264: local function is_maimed(unit)
1265:     return not unit.flags2.vision_good or
1266:         unit.status2.limbs_grasp_count < 2 or
1267:         unit.status2.limbs_stand_count == 0
1268: end
1269: 
1270: local function has_labor_conflict(unit)
1271:     return unit.status.labors[df.unit_labor.MINE] or
1272:            unit.status.labors[df.unit_labor.CUTWOOD] or
1273:            unit.status.labors[df.unit_labor.HUNT]
1274: end
1275: 
1276: local function filter_matches(unit, filter)
1277:     if filter.military == 'only' and not is_in_military(unit) then return false end
1278:     if filter.military == 'exclude' and is_in_military(unit) then return false end
1279:     if filter.officials == 'only' and not is_elected_or_appointed_official(unit) then return false end
1280:     if filter.officials == 'exclude' and is_elected_or_appointed_official(unit) then return false end
1281:     if filter.nobles == 'only' and not is_nobility(unit) then return false end
1282:     if filter.nobles == 'exclude' and is_nobility(unit) then return false end
1283:     if filter.infant == 'only' and not has_infant(unit) then return false end
1284:     if filter.infant == 'exclude' and has_infant(unit) then return false end
1285:     if filter.unstable == 'only' and not is_unstable(unit) then return false end
1286:     if filter.unstable == 'exclude' and is_unstable(unit) then return false end
1287:     if filter.maimed == 'only' and not is_maimed(unit) then return false end
1288:     if filter.maimed == 'exclude' and is_maimed(unit) then return false end
1289:     if filter.labor_conflict == 'only' and not has_labor_conflict(unit) then return false end
1290:     if filter.labor_conflict == 'exclude' and has_labor_conflict(unit) then return false end
1291:     return true
1292: end
1293: 
1294: function do_squad_filter(unit)
1295:     if annotation_instance then
1296:         annotation_instance.dirty = true
1297:     end
1298:     local self = filter_instance
1299:     local filter = {
1300:         military=self.subviews.military:getOptionValue(),
1301:         officials=self.subviews.officials:getOptionValue(),
1302:         nobles=self.subviews.nobles:getOptionValue(),
1303:         infant=self.subviews.infant:getOptionValue(),
1304:         unstable=self.subviews.unstable:getOptionValue(),
1305:         maimed=self.subviews.maimed:getOptionValue(),
1306:         labor_conflict=self.subviews.labor_conflict:getOptionValue(),
1307:     }
1308:     return filter_matches(unit, filter)
1309: end

76: local function load_state()
77:     local state = get_default_state()
78:     local config_file = json.open(dfhack.getConfigPath() .. '/spectate.json')
79:     for key in pairs(config_file.data) do
80:         if state[key] == nil then
81:             config_file.data[key] = nil
82:         end
83:     end
84:     utils.assign(state, config_file.data)
85:     config_file.data = state
86:     return config_file.data,
87:            function() config_file:write() end
88: end

94:     local elem = config[name]
95:     if elem == nil then return end
96:     if type(elem) == 'table' then
97:         return elem[key]
98:     end
99:     return elem

103:     for name,value in pairs(config) do
104:         if not name:startswith(lua_only_settings_prefix) then
105:             if type(value) == 'boolean' then
106:                 value = value and 1 or 0
107:             end
108:             spectate_setSetting(name, value)
109:         end
110:     end

123: -----------------------------
124: -- commandline interface

126: local function pairsByKeys(t, f)
127:     local a = {}
128:     for n in pairs(t) do table.insert(a, n) end
129:     table.sort(a, f)
130:     local i = 0      -- iterator variable
131:     local iter = function ()   -- iterator function
132:         i = i + 1
133:         if a[i] == nil then return nil
134:         else return a[i], t[a[i]]
135:         end
136:     end
137:     return iter
138: end
139: 
140: -- no recursion protection, but it shouldn't be needed for a config...
141: local function print_table(t, indent)
142:     indent = indent or ''
143:     for key, value in pairsByKeys(t) do
144:         if type(value) == 'table' then
145:             print(indent .. key .. ':')
146:             print_table(value, indent .. '  ')
147:         else
148:             print(indent .. key .. ': ' .. tostring(value))
149:         end
150:     end
151: end
152: 
153: local function print_status()
154:     print('spectate is:', isEnabled() and 'enabled' or 'disabled')
155:     print()
156:     print('settings:')
157: 
158:     print_table(config, '  ')
159: end
160: 
161: local function do_toggle()
162:     if isEnabled() then
163:         dfhack.run_command('disable', 'spectate')
164:     else
165:         dfhack.run_command('enable', 'spectate')
166:     end
167: end
168: 
169: local function set_setting(args)
170:     local n = #args
171:     if n == 0 then
172:         qerror('missing key')
173:     end
174: 
175:     local cfg = config
176:     local v
177:     for i = 1, n do
178:         v = cfg[args[i]]
179:         if v == nil then
180:             -- probably an unknown option, but we may allow adding new keys
181:             break
182:         elseif type(v) == 'table' then
183:             if i == n then
184:                 -- arrived at the very last argument, but have a table
185:                 qerror('missing value for ' .. table.concat(args, '/', 1, i))
186:             end
187:             cfg = v
188:         else
189:             -- arrived at something that's not a table
190:             if i == n-1 then
191:                 -- if there is exactly 1 argument left, we're good
192:                 break
193:             elseif i == n then
194:                 qerror('missing value for ' .. table.concat(args, '/', 1, i))
195:             else -- i < n-1 then
196:                 qerror('too many arguments for ' .. table.concat(args, '/', 1, i))
197:             end
198:         end
199:     end
200:     if v == nil then
201:         if n == 3 and args[1] == 'tooltip-follow-job-shortenings' then
202:             -- user should be able to add new shortenings, but not other things
203:         else
204:             qerror('unknown option: ' .. table.concat(args, '/', 1, i))
205:         end
206:     end
207: 
208:     local path = table.concat(args, '/', 1, n-1)
209:     local key = args[n-1]
210:     local value = args[n]
211:     local entry_type = type(cfg[key])
212:     if entry_type == 'table' then
213:         -- here just in case, is already checked in the loop above
214:         qerror('missing value for ' .. path)
215:     elseif entry_type == 'boolean' then
216:         if value == 'toggle' then
217:             value = not cfg[key]
218:         else
219:             value = argparse.boolean(value, path)
220:         end
221:     elseif entry_type == 'number' then
222:         if path == 'follow-seconds' then
223:             value = argparse.positiveInt(value, path)
224:         else
225:             value = argparse.nonnegativeInt(value, path)
226:         end
227:     end
228: 
229:     cfg[key] = value
230: 
231:     if n == 2 and not key:startswith(lua_only_settings_prefix) then
232:         if type(value) == 'boolean' then
233:             value = value and 1 or 0
234:         end
235:         spectate_setSetting(key, value)
236:     end
237: 
238:     save_state()
239: end
240: 
241: local function set_overlay(value)
242:     value = argparse.boolean(value, name)
243:     dfhack.run_command('overlay', value and 'enable' or 'disable', 'spectate.tooltip')
244: end
245: 
246: function parse_commandline(args)
247:     local command = table.remove(args, 1)
248:     if not command or command == 'status' then
249:         print_status()
250:     elseif command == 'toggle' then
251:         if #args == 0 then
252:             do_toggle()
253:         else
254:             args[#args+1] = 'toggle'
255:             set_setting(args)
256:         end
257:     elseif command == 'set' then
258:         set_setting(args)
259:     elseif command == 'overlay' then
260:         if #args == 0 then qerror('missing option') end
261:         set_overlay(args[1])
262:     else
263:         return false
264:     end
265: 
266:     return true
267: end

272: local function GetUnitStress(unit, stress_levels)
273:     local stressCat = dfhack.units.getStressCategory(unit)
274:     if stressCat > 6 then stressCat = 6 end
275:     stressCat = tostring(stressCat)
276:     if not stress_levels[stressCat] then return end
277: 
278:     local level_cfg = config['tooltip-stress-levels'][stressCat]
279:     return {text=level_cfg.text, pen=level_cfg.pen}
280: end

283:     return dfhack.units.getReadableName(unit)

286: local function GetUnitJob(unit)
287:     local job = unit.job.current_job
288:     return job and dfhack.job.getName(job)
289: end

291: -- there is no equivalent of `dfhack.job.GetName` yet
292: -- this function is by no means a replacement, it's
293: -- here to show something at least
294: local activityNames = {
295:     [df.activity_entry_type.TrainingSession] = "Training Session",
296:     [df.activity_entry_type.IndividualSkillDrill] = "Individual Skill Drill",
297:     [df.activity_entry_type.FillServiceOrder] = "Fill Service Order",
298:     [df.activity_entry_type.StoreObject] = "Store Object",
299:     -- other types are single words
300: }
301: local function activity_GetName(activity)
302:     local t = activity.type
303:     local n = df.activity_entry_type[t]
304:     n = activityNames[n] or n
305:     return {text = n, pen = COLOR_LIGHTGREEN}
306: end

308: local function GetUnitActivity(unit)
309:     local activity = dfhack.units.getMainSocialActivity(unit)
310:     return activity and activity_GetName(activity)
311: end

313: local function GetRelevantSettings(key)
314:     return config['tooltip-' .. key .. '-name'],
315:            config['tooltip-' .. key .. '-job'],
316:            config['tooltip-' .. key .. '-activity'],
317:            config['tooltip-' .. key .. '-stress'],
318:            config['tooltip-' .. key .. '-stress-levels'],
319:            config['tooltip-' .. key .. '-job-shortenings']
320: end
321: 
322: local function GetUnitInfoText(unit, settings_group_name)
323:     local show_name, show_job, show_activity, show_stress, stress_levels, job_shortenings = GetRelevantSettings(settings_group_name)
324: 
325:     local stress = show_stress and GetUnitStress(unit, stress_levels) or nil
326:     local name = show_name and GetUnitName(unit) or nil
327:     local job = show_job and GetUnitJob(unit) or nil
328:     if job_shortenings then job = job_shortenings[job] or job end
329:     local activity = show_activity and GetUnitActivity(unit) or nil
330: 
331:     local job_or_activity = job or activity
332: 
333:     local txt = {}
334:     if stress then
335:         txt[#txt+1] = stress
336:         if name or job_or_activity then txt[#txt+1] = ' ' end
337:     end
338:     if name then
339:         txt[#txt+1] = name
340:     end
341:     if job_or_activity then
342:         if name then txt[#txt+1] = ": " end
343:         txt[#txt+1] = job_or_activity
344:     end
345: 
346:     return txt
347: end

349: local function unit_filter(unit)

352: 
353: local function GetHoverText(pos)
354:     if not pos then return end
355: 
356:     local txt = {}
357:     local units = dfhack.units.getUnitsInBox(pos, pos, unit_filter) or {}
358: 
359:     for _,unit in ipairs(units) do
360:         local info = GetUnitInfoText(unit, 'hover')
361:         if not next(info) then goto continue end
362: 
363:         for _,t in ipairs(info) do
364:             txt[#txt+1] = t
365:         end
366:         txt[#txt+1] = NEWLINE
367: 
368:         ::continue::
369:     end
370: 
371:     return txt
372: end
373:

377: TooltipOverlay = defclass(TooltipOverlay, overlay.OverlayWidget)
378: TooltipOverlay.ATTRS{
379:     desc='Adds info tooltips that follow units or appear when you hover the mouse.',
380:     default_pos={x=1,y=1},
381:     fullscreen=true,
382:     viewscreens='dwarfmode/Default',
383: }
384: 
385: function TooltipOverlay:init()
386:     self:addviews{MouseTooltip{view_id = 'tooltip'}}
387: end
388: 
389: function TooltipOverlay:preUpdateLayout(parent_rect)
390:     -- this is required, otherwise there is no room to draw child widgets in
391:     self.frame.w = parent_rect.width
392:     self.frame.h = parent_rect.height
393: end
394: 
395: function TooltipOverlay:render(dc)
396:     self:render_unit_banners(dc)
397:     TooltipOverlay.super.render(self, dc)
398: end
399: 
400: -- map coordinates -> interface layer coordinates
401: local function GetScreenCoordinates(map_coord)
402:     -- -> map viewport offset
403:     local vp = df.global.world.viewport
404:     local vp_Coord = vp.corner
405:     local map_offset_by_vp = {
406:         x = map_coord.x - vp_Coord.x,
407:         y = map_coord.y - vp_Coord.y,
408:         z = map_coord.z - vp_Coord.z,
409:     }
410: 
411:     if not dfhack.screen.inGraphicsMode() then
412:         return map_offset_by_vp
413:     else
414:         -- -> pixel offset
415:         local gps = df.global.gps
416:         local map_tile_pixels = gps.viewport_zoom_factor // 4;
417:         local screen_coord_px = {
418:             x = map_tile_pixels * map_offset_by_vp.x,
419:             y = map_tile_pixels * map_offset_by_vp.y,
420:         }
421:         -- -> interface layer coordinates
422:         local screen_coord_text = {
423:             x = math.ceil( screen_coord_px.x / gps.tile_pixel_x ),
424:             y = math.ceil( screen_coord_px.y / gps.tile_pixel_y ),
425:         }
426: 
427:         return screen_coord_text
428:     end
429: end
430: 
431: local function GetString(tokens)
432:     local sb = {}
433:     for _, tok in ipairs(tokens) do
434:         if type(tok) == "string" then
435:             sb[#sb+1] = tok
436:         else -- must be a table token
437:             sb[#sb+1] = tok.text
438:         end
439:     end
440:     if not next(sb) then return nil end
441:     return table.concat(sb)
442: end
443: 
444: function TooltipOverlay:render_unit_banners(dc)
445:     if not config['tooltip-follow'] then return end
446: 
447:     local hold_to_show = config['tooltip-follow-hold-to-show']
448:     if hold_to_show and hold_to_show ~= 'none' then
449:         if not dfhack.internal.getModifiers()[hold_to_show] then
450:             return
451:         end
452:     else
453:         local blink_duration = config['tooltip-follow-blink-milliseconds']
454:         if blink_duration > 0 and not gui.blink_visible(blink_duration) then
455:             return
456:         end
457: 
458:         if not dfhack.screen.inGraphicsMode() and not gui.blink_visible(500) then
459:             return
460:         end
461:     end
462: 
463:     local vp = df.global.world.viewport
464:     local topleft = vp.corner
465:     local width = vp.max_x
466:     local height = vp.max_y
467:     local bottomright = {x = topleft.x + width, y = topleft.y + height, z = topleft.z}
468: 
469:     local units = dfhack.units.getUnitsInBox(topleft, bottomright, unit_filter)
470:     if not units or #units == 0 then return end
471: 
472:     local oneTileOffset = GetScreenCoordinates({x = topleft.x + 1, y = topleft.y + 1, z = topleft.z + 0})
473:     local pen = COLOR_WHITE
474: 
475:     local _, screenHeight = dfhack.screen:getWindowSize()
476: 
477:     local used_tiles = {}
478:     -- reverse order yields better offsets for overlapping texts
479:     for i = #units, 1, -1 do
480:         local unit = units[i]
481: 
482:         local posX, posY, posZ = dfhack.units.getPosition(unit)
483:         if not posX then goto continue end
484:         local pos = xyz2pos(posX, posY, posZ)
485: 
486:         local info = GetUnitInfoText(unit, 'follow')
487:         if not info or not next(info) then goto continue end
488: 
489:         local str = GetString(info)
490:         if not str then goto continue end
491: 
492:         local scrPos = GetScreenCoordinates(pos)
493:         local y = scrPos.y - 1 -- subtract 1 to move the text over the heads
494:         local x = scrPos.x + oneTileOffset.x - 1 -- subtract 1 to move the text inside the map tile
495: 
496:         -- do not write anything in the top rows, where DF's interface is.
497:         -- todo: use precise rectangles
498:         if y < 4 then goto continue end
499: 
500:         -- to resolve overlaps, we'll mark every coordinate we write anything in,
501:         -- and then check if the new tooltip will overwrite any used coordinate.
502:         -- if it will, try the next row, to a maximum offset of 4.
503:         local row
504:         local dy = 0
505:         -- todo: search for the "best" offset instead, f.e. max `usedAt` value, with `-1` the best
506:         local usedAt = -1
507:         for yOffset = 0, max_banner_y_offset do
508:             dy = yOffset
509: 
510:             row = used_tiles[y + dy]
511:             if not row then
512:                 row = {}
513:                 used_tiles[y + dy] = row
514:             end
515: 
516:             usedAt = -1
517:             for j = 0, #str - 1 do
518:                 if row[x + j] then
519:                     usedAt = j
520:                     break
521:                 end
522:             end
523: 
524:             if usedAt == -1 then break end
525:         end -- for dy
526:         -- if other text starts at the same position, or even 2 to the right,
527:         -- we can't place any useful information, and will ignore it instead.
528:         if 0 <= usedAt and usedAt <= 2 then goto continue end
529: 
530:         -- do not write anything over DF's interface
531:         -- todo: use precise rectangles
532:         if y + dy > screenHeight - 4 then goto continue end
533: 
534:         dc:seek(x, y + dy)
535:         local ix = 0
536:         for _, tok in ipairs(info) do
537:             local s
538:             if type(tok) == "string" then
539:                 dc:pen(pen)
540:                 s = tok
541:             else
542:                 dc:pen(tok.pen)
543:                 s = tok.text
544:             end
545: 
546:             -- in case there isn't enough space, cut the text off
547:             local len = #s
548:             if usedAt > 0 and ix + len + 1 >= usedAt then
549:                 -- last position we can write is `usedAt - len - ix - 1`
550:                 -- we want to replace it with an `_`, so we need another `- 1`
551:                 s = s:sub(1, usedAt - len - ix - 1 - 1) .. '_'
552: 
553:                 dc:string(s)
554:                 break -- nothing more will fit
555:             else
556:                 dc:string(s)
557:             end
558: 
559:             ix = ix + len
560:         end
561: 
562:         -- mark coordinates as used
563:         for j = 0, #str - 1 do
564:             row[x + j] = true
565:         end
566: 
567:         ::continue::
568:     end
569: end
570: 
571: -- MouseTooltip is an almost copy&paste of the DimensionsTooltip
572: MouseTooltip = defclass(MouseTooltip, widgets.ResizingPanel)
573: 
574: MouseTooltip.ATTRS{
575:     frame_style=gui.FRAME_THIN,
576:     frame_background=gui.CLEAR_PEN,
577:     no_force_pause_badge=true,
578:     auto_width=true,
579:     display_offset={x=3, y=3},
580: }
581: 
582: function MouseTooltip:init()
583:     ensure_key(self, 'frame').w = 17
584:     self.frame.h = 4
585: 
586:     self.label = widgets.Label{
587:         frame={t=0},
588:         auto_width=true,
589:     }
590: 
591:     self:addviews{
592:         widgets.Panel{
593:             -- set minimum size for tooltip frame so the DFHack frame badge fits
594:             frame={t=0, l=0, w=7, h=2},
595:         },
596:         self.label,
597:     }
598: end
599: 
600: function MouseTooltip:render(dc)
601:     if not config['tooltip-hover'] then return end
602: 
603:     local x, y = dfhack.screen.getMousePos()
604:     if not x then return end
605: 
606:     local pos = dfhack.gui.getMousePos()
607:     local text = GetHoverText(pos)
608:     if not text or not next(text) then return end
609:     self.label:setText(text)
610: 
611:     local sw, sh = dfhack.screen.getWindowSize()
612:     local frame_width = math.max(9, self.label:getTextWidth() + 2)
613:     self.frame.l = math.min(x + self.display_offset.x, sw - frame_width)
614:     self.frame.t = math.min(y + self.display_offset.y, sh - self.frame.h)
615:     self:updateLayout()
616:     MouseTooltip.super.render(self, dc)
617: end

664:             frame={l=26, t=0},
665:             on_click=function() dfhack.run_script('gui/spectate') end,

673:         return true
674:     elseif keys.KEYBOARD_CURSOR_RIGHT then
675:         spectate_followNext()
676:         return true

11: local PERSIST_KEY = 'stockflow/orders'
12: 
13: local function serialize_orders(orders)
14:     local stored = {}
15:     for spid, spec in pairs(orders) do
16:         stored[tostring(spid)] = {
17:             name = spec.name,
18:             order_number = spec.order_number,
19:             trigger_number = spec.trigger_number,
20:         }
21:     end
22:     return stored
23: end

25: local function persist_orders()
26:     dfhack.persistent.saveSiteData(PERSIST_KEY, serialize_orders(saved_orders))
27: end

29: triggers = {
30:     {filled = false, divisor = 1, name = "Per empty space"},
31:     {filled = true,  divisor = 1, name = "Per stored item"},
32:     {filled = false, divisor = 2, name = "Per two empty spaces"},
33:     {filled = true,  divisor = 2, name = "Per two stored items"},
34:     {filled = false, divisor = 3, name = "Per three empty spaces"},
35:     {filled = true,  divisor = 3, name = "Per three stored items"},
36:     {filled = false, divisor = 4, name = "Per four empty spaces"},

45: -- Populate the reaction and stockpile order lists.
46: -- To be called whenever a world is loaded.
47: function initialize_world()
48:     -- Clear old reactions, just in case.
49:     clear_caches()
50:     reaction_list = collect_reactions()
51:     saved_orders = collect_orders()
52: end
53: 
54: -- Clear all caches.
55: -- Called when a world is loaded, or when the plugin is disabled.
56: function clear_caches()
57:     -- Free the C++ objects in the reaction list.
58:     for _, value in ipairs(reaction_list) do
59:         value.order:delete()
60:     end
61:     reaction_list = {}
62:     saved_orders = {}
63:     jobs_to_create = {}
64: end
65: 
66: function trigger_name(spec)
67:     local trigger = triggers[spec.trigger_number]
68:     return trigger and trigger.name or "Never"
69: end
70: 
71: function list_orders()
72:     local listed = false
73:     for _, spec in pairs(saved_orders) do
74:         local num = spec.stockpile.stockpile_number
75:         local name = spec.name
76:         local trigger = trigger_name(spec)
77:         print("Stockpile #"..num, name, trigger)
78:         listed = true
79:     end
80: 
81:     if not listed then
82:         print("No manager jobs have been set for your stockpiles.")
83:         print("Select a stockpile and use the stockflow overlay to create one...")
84:     end
85: end
86: 
87: -- Gather the stockpile jobs for later creation.
88: -- Called at the start of each periodic update.
89: function start_bookkeeping()
90:     local result = {}
91:     for reaction_id, quantity in pairs(check_stockpiles()) do
92:         local amount = order_quantity(reaction_list[reaction_id].order, quantity)
93:         if amount > 0 then
94:             result[reaction_id] = amount
95:         end
96:     end
97: 
98:     jobs_to_create = result
99: end
100: 
101: -- Insert the gathered jobs.
102: -- Called at the end of each periodic update.
103: function finish_bookkeeping()
104:     for reaction, amount in pairs(jobs_to_create) do
105:         create_orders(reaction_list[reaction].order, amount)
106:     end
107: 
108:     jobs_to_create = {}
109: end
110: 
111: function stockpile_settings(sp)
112:     local order = saved_orders[sp.id]
113:     if not order then
114:         return "No job selected", ""
115:     end
116: 
117:     return order.name, trigger_name(order)
118: end
119: 
120: -- Toggle the trigger condition for a stockpile.
121: function toggle_trigger(sp)
122:     local saved = saved_orders[sp.id]
123:     if saved then
124:         saved.trigger_number = (saved.trigger_number % #triggers) + 1
125:         persist_orders()
126:     end
127: end
128: 
129: function collect_orders()
130:     local result = {}
131:     local dirty = false
132:     local stored = dfhack.persistent.getSiteData(PERSIST_KEY) or {}
133:     for spid_str, spec in pairs(stored) do
134:         local spid = tonumber(spid_str)
135:         local stockpile = spid and df.building.find(spid)
136:         if stockpile and not df.building_stockpilest:is_instance(stockpile) then
137:             stockpile = nil
138:         end
139:         if stockpile then
140:             local order_number = spec.order_number
141:             local trigger_number = spec.trigger_number or 1
142:             if reaction_list[order_number] and spec.name == reaction_list[order_number].name then
143:                 result[spid] = {
144:                     stockpile = stockpile,
145:                     name = spec.name,
146:                     order_number = order_number,
147:                     trigger_number = trigger_number,
148:                 }
149:             else
150:                 -- Search reaction_list for the name.
151:                 -- This can happen when loading an old save in a new version.
152:                 -- It's even possible that the reaction has been removed.
153:                 local found = false
154:                 for number, reaction in ipairs(reaction_list) do
155:                     if reaction.name == spec.name then
156:                         print("Adjusting stockflow entry for stockpile #"..stockpile.stockpile_number..": "..spec.name.." ("..tostring(order_number).." => "..number..")")
157:                         result[spid] = {
158:                             stockpile = stockpile,
159:                             name = spec.name,
160:                             order_number = number,
161:                             trigger_number = trigger_number,
162:                         }
163: 
164:                         dirty = true
165:                         found = true
166:                         break
167:                     end
168:                 end
169: 
170:                 if not found then
171:                     print("Unmatched stockflow entry for stockpile #"..stockpile.stockpile_number..": "..tostring(spec.name).." ("..tostring(order_number)..")")
172:                     dirty = true
173:                 end
174:             end
175:         else
176:             -- The stockpile no longer exists.
177:             -- Perhaps it has been deleted, or perhaps this is a different fortress.
178:             dirty = true
179:         end
180:     end
181: 
182:     if dirty then
183:         dfhack.persistent.saveSiteData(PERSIST_KEY, serialize_orders(result))
184:     end
185: 
186:     return result
187: end
188: 
189: -- Choose an order that the stockpile should add to the manager queue.
190: function select_order(stockpile)
191:     screen:reset(stockpile)
192:     screen:show()
193: end

195: function reaction_entry(reactions, job_type, values, name)
196:     if not job_type then
197:         -- Perhaps df.job_type.something returned nil for an unknown job type.
198:         -- We could warn about it; in any case, don't add it to the list.
199:         return
200:     end
201: 
202:     local order = df.manager_order:new()
203:     -- These defaults differ from the newly created order's.
204:     order:assign{
205:         frequency = df.workquota_frequency_type.OneTime,
206:         job_type = job_type,
207:         item_type = -1,
208:         item_subtype = -1,
209:         mat_type = -1,
210:         mat_index = -1,
211:     }
212: 
213:     if values then
214:         -- Override default attributes.
215:         order:assign(values)
216:     end
217: 
218:     table.insert(reactions, {
219:         name = name or df.job_type.attrs[job_type].caption,
220:         order = order,
221:     })
222: end
223: 
224: function resource_reactions(reactions, job_type, mat_info, keys, items, options)
225:     local values = {}
226:     for key, value in pairs(mat_info.management) do
227:         values[key] = value
228:     end
229: 
230:     for _, itemid in ipairs(keys) do
231:         local itemdef = items[itemid]
232:         local start = options.verb or mat_info.verb or "Make"
233:         if options.adjective then
234:             start = start.." "..itemdef.adjective
235:         end
236: 
237:         if (not options.permissible) or options.permissible(itemdef) then
238:             local item_name = " "..itemdef[options.name_field or "name"]
239:             if options.capitalize then
240:                 item_name = string.gsub(item_name, " .", string.upper)
241:             end
242: 
243:             values.item_subtype = itemid
244:             reaction_entry(reactions, job_type, values, start.." "..mat_info.adjective..item_name)
245:         end
246:     end
247: end
248: 
249: function material_reactions(reactions, itemtypes, mat_info)
250:     -- Expects a list of {job_type, verb, item_name} tuples.
251:     for _, row in ipairs(itemtypes) do
252:         local line = row[2].." "..mat_info.adjective
253:         if row[3] then
254:             line = line.." "..row[3]
255:         end
256: 
257:         reaction_entry(reactions, row[1], mat_info.management, line)
258:     end
259: end

261: function clothing_reactions(reactions, mat_info, filter)
262:     local resources = df.historical_entity.find(df.global.plotinfo.civ_id).resources
263:     local itemdefs = df.global.world.raws.itemdefs
264:     local job_types = df.job_type
265:     resource_reactions(reactions, job_types.MakeArmor,  mat_info, resources.armor_type,  itemdefs.armor,  {permissible = filter})
266:     resource_reactions(reactions, job_types.MakePants,  mat_info, resources.pants_type,  itemdefs.pants,  {permissible = filter})
267:     resource_reactions(reactions, job_types.MakeGloves, mat_info, resources.gloves_type, itemdefs.gloves, {permissible = filter})
268:     resource_reactions(reactions, job_types.MakeHelm,   mat_info, resources.helm_type,   itemdefs.helms,  {permissible = filter})
269:     resource_reactions(reactions, job_types.MakeShoes,  mat_info, resources.shoes_type,  itemdefs.shoes,  {permissible = filter})
270: end

272: -- Find the reaction types that should be listed in the management interface.
273: function collect_reactions()
274:     -- The sequence here tries to match the native manager screen.
275:     -- It should also be possible to collect the sequence from somewhere native,
276:     -- but I currently can only find it while the job selection screen is active.
277:     -- Even that list doesn't seem to include their names.
278:     local result = {}
279: 
280:     -- Caching the enumeration might not be important, but saves lookups.
281:     local job_types = df.job_type
282: 
283:     local materials = {
284:         rock = {
285:             adjective = "rock",
286:             management = {mat_type = 0},
287:         },
288:     }
289: 
290:     for _, name in ipairs{"wood", "cloth", "leather", "silk", "yarn", "bone", "shell", "tooth", "horn", "pearl"} do
291:         materials[name] = {
292:             adjective = name,
293:             management = {material_category = {[name] = true}},
294:         }
295:     end
296: 
297:     materials.wood.adjective = "wooden"
298:     materials.tooth.adjective = "ivory/tooth"
299:     materials.leather.clothing_flag = "LEATHER"
300:     materials.shell.short = true
301:     materials.pearl.short = true
302: 
303:     -- Collection and Entrapment
304:     reaction_entry(result, job_types.CollectWebs)
305:     reaction_entry(result, job_types.CollectSand)
306:     reaction_entry(result, job_types.CollectClay)
307:     reaction_entry(result, job_types.CatchLiveLandAnimal)
308:     reaction_entry(result, job_types.CatchLiveFish)
309: 
310:     -- Cutting, encrusting, and metal extraction.
311:     local rock_types = df.global.world.raws.inorganics.all
312:     for rock_id = #rock_types-1, 0, -1 do
313:         local material = rock_types[rock_id].material
314:         local rock_name = material.state_adj.Solid
315:         if material.flags.IS_STONE or material.flags.IS_GEM then
316:             reaction_entry(result, job_types.CutGems, {
317:                 mat_type = 0,
318:                 mat_index = rock_id,
319:             }, "Cut "..rock_name)
320: 
321:             reaction_entry(result, job_types.EncrustWithGems, {
322:                 mat_type = 0,
323:                 mat_index = rock_id,
324:                 specflag = {encrust_flags={finished_goods=true}},
325:             }, "Encrust Finished Goods With "..rock_name)
326: 
327:             reaction_entry(result, job_types.EncrustWithGems, {
328:                 mat_type = 0,
329:                 mat_index = rock_id,
330:                 specflag = {encrust_flags={furniture=true}},
331:             }, "Encrust Furniture With "..rock_name)
332: 
333:             reaction_entry(result, job_types.EncrustWithGems, {
334:                 mat_type = 0,
335:                 mat_index = rock_id,
336:                 specflag = {encrust_flags={ammo=true}},
337:             }, "Encrust Ammo With "..rock_name)
338:         end
339: 
340:         if #rock_types[rock_id].metal_ore.mat_index > 0 then
341:             reaction_entry(result, job_types.SmeltOre, {mat_type = 0, mat_index = rock_id}, "Smelt "..rock_name.." Ore")
342:         end
343: 
344:         if #rock_types[rock_id].thread_metal.mat_index > 0 then
345:             reaction_entry(result, job_types.ExtractMetalStrands, {mat_type = 0, mat_index = rock_id})
346:         end
347:     end
348: 
349:     -- Glass cutting and encrusting, with different job numbers.
350:     -- We could search the entire table, but glass is less subject to raws.
351:     local glass_types = df.global.world.raws.mat_table.builtin
352:     local glasses = {}
353:     for glass_id = 3, 5 do
354:         local material = glass_types[glass_id]
355:         local glass_name = material.state_adj.Solid
356:         if material.flags.IS_GLASS then
357:             -- For future use.
358:             table.insert(glasses, {
359:                 adjective = glass_name,
360:                 management = {mat_type = glass_id},
361:             })
362: 
363:             reaction_entry(result, job_types.CutGlass, {mat_type = glass_id}, "Cut "..glass_name)
364: 
365:             reaction_entry(result, job_types.EncrustWithGlass, {
366:                 mat_type = glass_id,
367:                 specflag = {encrust_flags={finished_goods=true}},
368:             }, "Encrust Finished Goods With "..glass_name)
369: 
370:             reaction_entry(result, job_types.EncrustWithGlass, {
371:                 mat_type = glass_id,
372:                 specflag = {encrust_flags={furniture=true}},
373:             }, "Encrust Furniture With "..glass_name)
374: 
375:             reaction_entry(result, job_types.EncrustWithGlass, {
376:                 mat_type = glass_id,
377:                 specflag = {encrust_flags={ammo=true}},
378:             }, "Encrust Ammo With "..glass_name)
379:         end
380:     end

461:         local rock_name = material.state_adj.Solid

481:                     adjective = true,

801:         resource_reactions(result, job_types.MakeAmmo, mat, entity.resources.ammo_type, itemdefs.ammo, {
802:             name_field = "name_plural",
803:         })
804:     end
805: 
806:     -- BARRED and SCALED as flag names don't quite seem to fit, here.
807:     clothing_reactions(result, materials.bone, (function(itemdef) return itemdef.props.flags.BARRED end))
808:     clothing_reactions(result, materials.shell, (function(itemdef) return itemdef.props.flags.SCALED end))
809: 
810:     for _, mat in ipairs{materials.wood, materials.leather} do
811:         resource_reactions(result, job_types.MakeShield, mat, entity.resources.shield_type, itemdefs.shields, {})
812:     end
813: 
814:     -- Melt a Metal Object
815:     reaction_entry(result, job_types.MeltMetalObject)
816: 
817:     return result
818: end

820: screen = gui.FramedScreen {
821:     frame_title = "Select Stockpile Order",

823: 
824: function screen:onRenderBody(dc)
825:     -- Emulates the built-in manager screen.
826: 
827:     if not (self.page_size == self.frame_rect.height - ExtraLines) then
828:         -- The screen size has changed.
829:         self:refilter()
830:     end
831: 
832:     -- Top instruction line.
833:     dc:seek(1, 1):string("Type in parts of the name to narrow your search.  ", COLOR_WHITE)
834:     dc:key("LEAVESCREEN"):string(" to abort.", COLOR_WHITE)
835: 
836:     -- Search term, if any.
837:     dc:seek(1, FirstRow + self.page_size + 1):string(self.search_string, COLOR_LIGHTCYAN)
838: 
839:     -- Bottom instruction line.
840:     dc:seek(1, FirstRow + self.page_size + 2)
841:     dc:key("STANDARDSCROLL_UP"):key("STANDARDSCROLL_DOWN")
842:     dc:key("STANDARDSCROLL_PAGEUP"):key("STANDARDSCROLL_PAGEDOWN")
843:     dc:key("STANDARDSCROLL_LEFT"):key("STANDARDSCROLL_RIGHT")
844:     dc:string(": Select", COLOR_WHITE)
845: 
846:     dc:seek(CenterCol, FirstRow + self.page_size + 2)
847:     dc:key("CUSTOM_SHIFT_N"):string(": No order", COLOR_WHITE)
848: 
849:     -- Reaction lines.
850:     for _, item in ipairs(self.displayed) do
851:         dc:seek(item.x, item.y):string(item.name, item.color)
852:     end
853: end
854: 
855: function screen:onInput(keys)
856:     if keys.LEAVESCREEN then
857:         self:dismiss()
858:     elseif keys.SELECT then
859:         self:dismiss()
860:         local selected = self.reactions[self.position]
861:         if selected then
862:             store_order(self.stockpile, selected.index)
863:         end
864:     elseif keys.CUSTOM_SHIFT_N then
865:         self:dismiss()
866:         clear_order(self.stockpile)
867:     elseif keys.STANDARDSCROLL_UP then
868:         self.position = self.position - 1
869:     elseif keys.STANDARDSCROLL_DOWN then
870:         self.position = self.position + 1
871:     elseif keys.STANDARDSCROLL_LEFT then
872:         if self.position == 1 then
873:             -- Move from the very first item to the very last item.
874:             self.position = #self.reactions
875:         elseif self.position < self.page_size then
876:             -- On the first column, move to the very first item.
877:             self.position = 1
878:         else
879:             -- Move to the same position on the previous column.
880:             self.position = self.position - self.page_size
881:         end
882:     elseif keys.STANDARDSCROLL_RIGHT then
883:         if self.position == #self.reactions then
884:             -- Move from the very last item to the very first item.
885:             self.position = 1
886:         else
887:             -- Move to the same position on the next column.
888:             self.position = self.position + self.page_size
889:             if self.position > #self.reactions then
890:                 -- If that's past the end, move to the very last item.
891:                 self.position = #self.reactions
892:             end
893:         end
894:     elseif keys.STANDARDSCROLL_PAGEUP then
895:         if self.position == 1 then
896:             -- Move from the very first item to the very last item.
897:             self.position = #self.reactions
898:         elseif self.position < self.page_size*2 then
899:             -- On the first page, move to the very first item.
900:             self.position = 1
901:         else
902:             -- Move to the same position on the previous page.
903:             self.position = self.position - self.page_size*2
904:         end
905:     elseif keys.STANDARDSCROLL_PAGEDOWN then
906:         if self.position == #self.reactions then
907:             -- Move from the very last item to the very first item.
908:             self.position = 1
909:         else
910:             -- Move to the same position on the next page.
911:             self.position = self.position + self.page_size*2
912:             if self.position > #self.reactions then
913:                 -- If that's past the end, move to the very last item.
914:                 self.position = #self.reactions
915:             end
916:         end
917:     elseif keys.STRING_A000 then
918:         -- This seems like an odd way to check for Backspace.
919:         self.search_string = string.sub(self.search_string, 1, -2)
920:         self.position = 1
921:     elseif keys._STRING and keys._STRING >= 32 then
922:         -- This interface only accepts letters and spaces.
923:         local char = string.char(keys._STRING)
924:         if char == " " or string.find(char, "^%a") then
925:             self.search_string = self.search_string .. string.upper(char)
926:             self.position = 1
927:         end
928:     end
929: 
930:     self:refilter()
931: end
932: 
933: function screen:reset(stockpile)
934:     self.stockpile = stockpile
935:     self.search_string = ""
936:     self.position = 1
937:     self:refilter()
938: end
939: 
940: function matchall(haystack, needles)
941:     for _, needle in ipairs(needles) do
942:         if not string.find(haystack, needle) then
943:             return false
944:         end
945:     end
946: 
947:     return true
948: end
949: 
950: function screen:refilter()
951:     -- Determine which rows to show, and in which colors.
952:     -- Todo: The official one now has three categories of search results:
953:     -- * Cyan: Contains at least one exact word from the search terms
954:     -- * Yellow: At least one word starts with at least one search term
955:     -- * Grey: Each search term is found in the middle of a word
956:     self.page_size = self.frame_rect.height - ExtraLines
957: 
958:     local filtered = {}
959:     local needles = self.search_string:split()
960:     for key, value in ipairs(reaction_list) do
961:         if matchall(string.upper(value.name), needles) then
962:             filtered[#filtered+1] = {
963:                 index = key,
964:                 name = value.name
965:             }
966:         end
967:     end
968: 
969:     if self.position < 1 then
970:         self.position = #filtered
971:     elseif self.position > #filtered then
972:         self.position = 1
973:     end
974: 
975:     local start = 1
976:     while self.position >= start + self.page_size*2 do
977:         start = start + self.page_size*2
978:     end
979: 
980:     local displayed = {}
981:     for n = 0, self.page_size*2 - 1 do
982:         local item = filtered[start + n]
983:         if not item then
984:             break
985:         end
986:         local name = item.name
987: 
988:         local x = 1
989:         local y = FirstRow + n
990:         if n >= self.page_size then
991:             x = CenterCol
992:             y = y - self.page_size
993:             name = " "..name
994:         end
995: 
996:         local color = COLOR_CYAN
997:         if start + n == self.position then
998:             color = COLOR_LIGHTCYAN
999:         end
1000: 
1001:         displayed[n + 1] = {
1002:             x = x,
1003:             y = y,
1004:             name = name,
1005:             color = color,
1006:         }
1007:     end
1008: 
1009:     self.reactions = filtered
1010:     self.displayed = displayed
1011: end
1012: 
1013: function clear_order(stockpile)
1014:     if saved_orders[stockpile.id] then
1015:         saved_orders[stockpile.id] = nil
1016:         persist_orders()
1017:     end
1018: end
1019: 
1020: function store_order(stockpile, order_number)
1021:     local name = reaction_list[order_number].name
1022:     -- print("Setting stockpile #"..stockpile.stockpile_number.." to "..name.." (#"..order_number..")")
1023:     local saved = saved_orders[stockpile.id]
1024:     if saved then
1025:         saved.name = name
1026:         saved.order_number = order_number
1027:     else
1028:         saved_orders[stockpile.id] = {
1029:             stockpile = stockpile,
1030:             name = name,
1031:             order_number = order_number,
1032:             trigger_number = 1,
1033:         }
1034:     end
1035:     persist_orders()
1036: end
1037: 
1038: -- Compare the job specification of two orders.
1039: function orders_match(a, b)
1040:     local fields = {
1041:         "job_type",
1042:         "item_subtype",
1043:         "reaction_name",
1044:         "mat_type",
1045:         "mat_index",
1046:     }
1047: 
1048:     for _, fieldname in ipairs(fields) do
1049:         if a[fieldname] ~= b[fieldname] then
1050:             return false
1051:         end
1052:     end
1053: 
1054:     for key, value in ipairs(a.specflag.encrust_flags) do
1055:         if b.specflag.encrust_flags[key] ~= value then
1056:             return false
1057:         end
1058:     end
1059: 
1060:     for key, value in ipairs(a.material_category) do
1061:         if b.material_category[key] ~= value then
1062:             return false
1063:         end
1064:     end
1065: 
1066:     return true
1067: end
1068: 
1069: -- Reduce the quantity by the number of matching orders in the queue.
1070: function order_quantity(order, quantity)
1071:     local amount = quantity
1072:     for _, managed in ipairs(df.global.world.manager_orders.all) do
1073:         if orders_match(order, managed) then
1074:             amount = amount - managed.amount_left
1075:             if amount < 0 then
1076:                 return 0
1077:             end
1078:         end
1079:     end
1080: 
1081:     return amount
1082: end
1083: 
1084: -- Place a new copy of the order onto the manager's queue.
1085: function create_orders(order, amount)
1086:     local new_order = order:new()
1087:     new_order.frequency = df.workquota_frequency_type.OneTime
1088:     amount = math.floor(amount)
1089:     new_order.amount_left = amount
1090:     new_order.amount_total = amount
1091:     -- Todo: Create in a validated state if the fortress is small enough?
1092:     new_order.status.validated = false
1093:     new_order.status.active = false
1094:     new_order.id = df.global.world.manager_orders.manager_order_next_id
1095:     df.global.world.manager_orders.manager_order_next_id = df.global.world.manager_orders.manager_order_next_id + 1
1096:     df.global.world.manager_orders.all:insert('#', new_order)
1097: end
1098: 
1099: function countContents(container, settings)
1100:     local total = 0
1101:     for _, item in ipairs(dfhack.items.getContainedItems(container)) do
1102:         if item.flags.container then
1103:             -- Recursively count the total of items contained.
1104:             -- Not likely to go more than two levels deep.
1105:             local subtotal = countContents(item, settings)
1106:             if subtotal > 0 then
1107:                 -- Ignore the inner container itself;
1108:                 -- generally, only the contained items matter.
1109:                 total = total + subtotal
1110:             elseif matches_stockpile(item, settings) then
1111:                 -- The container may or may not be empty,
1112:                 -- but is stockpiled as a container itself.
1113:                 total = total + 1
1114:             end
1115:         elseif matches_stockpile(item, settings) then
1116:             total = total + 1
1117:         end
1118:     end
1119: 
1120:     return total
1121: end
1122: 
1123: function check_stockpiles(verbose)
1124:     local result = {}
1125:     for _, spec in pairs(saved_orders) do
1126:         local trigger = triggers[spec.trigger_number]
1127:         if trigger and trigger.divisor then
1128:             local reaction = spec.order_number
1129:             local filled, empty = check_pile(spec.stockpile, verbose)
1130:             local amount = math.floor((trigger.filled and filled or empty) / trigger.divisor)
1131:             result[reaction] = (result[reaction] or 0) + amount
1132:         end
1133:     end
1134: 
1135:     return result
1136: end
1137: 
1138: function check_pile(sp, verbose)
1139:     local numspaces = 0
1140:     local filled = 0
1141:     local empty = 0
1142:     for y = sp.y1, sp.y2 do
1143:         for x = sp.x1, sp.x2 do
1144:             if dfhack.buildings.containsTile(sp, x, y) then
1145:                 numspaces = numspaces + 1
1146:                 local designation, occupancy = dfhack.maps.getTileFlags(x, y, sp.z)
1147:                 if not designation.liquid_type then
1148:                     if not occupancy.item then
1149:                         empty = empty + 1
1150:                     end
1151:                 end
1152:             end
1153:         end
1154:     end
1155: 
1156:     for _, item in ipairs(dfhack.buildings.getStockpileContents(sp)) do
1157:         if item:isAssignedToThisStockpile(sp.id) then
1158:             -- This is a bin or barrel associated with the stockpile.
1159:             filled = filled + countContents(item, sp.settings)
1160:         elseif matches_stockpile(item, sp.settings) then
1161:             filled = filled + 1
1162:         end
1163:     end
1164: 
1165:     if verbose then
1166:         print("Stockpile #"..sp.stockpile_number,
1167:             string.format("%3d spaces", numspaces),
1168:             string.format("%4d items", filled),
1169:             string.format("%4d empty spaces", empty))
1170:     end
1171: 
1172:     return filled, empty
1173: end
1174: 
1175: function matches_stockpile(item, settings)
1176:     -- Check whether the item matches the stockpile.
1177:     -- FIXME: This is starting to look like a whole lot of work.
1178:     if df.item_barst:is_instance(item) then
1179:         return settings.flags.bars_blocks
1180:     elseif df.item_blocksst:is_instance(item) then
1181:         return settings.flags.bars_blocks
1182:     elseif df.item_smallgemst:is_instance(item) then
1183:         return settings.flags.gems
1184:     elseif df.item_boulderst:is_instance(item) then
1185:         return settings.flags.stone
1186:     elseif df.item_woodst:is_instance(item) then
1187:         return settings.flags.wood
1188:     elseif df.item_seedsst:is_instance(item) then
1189:         return settings.flags.food
1190:     elseif df.item_meatst:is_instance(item) then
1191:         return settings.flags.food
1192:     elseif df.item_plantst:is_instance(item) then
1193:         return settings.flags.food
1194:     elseif df.item_cheesest:is_instance(item) then
1195:         return settings.flags.food
1196:     elseif df.item_globst:is_instance(item) then
1197:         return settings.flags.food
1198:     elseif df.item_fishst:is_instance(item) then
1199:         return settings.flags.food
1200:     elseif df.item_fish_rawst:is_instance(item) then
1201:         return settings.flags.food
1202:     elseif df.item_foodst:is_instance(item) then
1203:         return settings.flags.food
1204:     elseif df.item_drinkst:is_instance(item) then
1205:         return settings.flags.food
1206:     elseif df.item_eggst:is_instance(item) then
1207:         return settings.flags.food
1208:     elseif df.item_skin_tannedst:is_instance(item) then
1209:         return settings.flags.leather
1210:     elseif df.item_remainsst:is_instance(item) then
1211:         return settings.flags.refuse
1212:     elseif df.item_verminst:is_instance(item) then
1213:         return settings.flags.animals
1214:     elseif df.item_petst:is_instance(item) then
1215:         return settings.flags.animals
1216:     elseif df.item_threadst:is_instance(item) then
1217:         return settings.flags.cloth
1218:     end
1219: 
1220:     return true
1221: end

38: local function list_dir(path, prefix, filters, cb)
39:     local paths = dfhack.filesystem.listdir_recursive(path, 0, false)
40:     if not paths then
41:         dfhack.printerr(('Cannot find stockpile settings directory: "%s"'):format(path))
42:         return
43:     end
44:     local normalized_filters = {}
45:     for _, filter in ipairs(filters or {}) do table.insert(normalized_filters, filter:lower()) end
46:     for _, v in ipairs(paths) do
47:         local normalized_path = prefix .. v.path:lower()
48:         if v.isdir or not normalized_path:endswith('.dfstock') then goto continue end
49:         normalized_path = normalized_path:sub(1, -9)
50:         if #normalized_filters > 0 then
51:             local matched = false
52:             for _, filter in ipairs(normalized_filters) do
53:                 if normalized_path:find(filter, 1, true) then
54:                     matched = true
55:                     break
56:                 end
57:             end
58:             if not matched then goto continue end
59:         end
60:         cb(('%s%s'):format(prefix, v.path:sub(1, -9)))
61:         ::continue::
62:     end
63: end
64: 
65: local function list_settings_files(filters, cb)
66:     cb = cb or print
67:     list_dir(STOCKPILES_DIR, '', filters, cb)
68:     list_dir(STOCKPILES_LIBRARY_DIR, 'library/', filters, cb)
69: end

71: local function assert_safe_name(name)
72:     if not name or #name == 0 then qerror('name missing or empty') end
73:     if name:find(BAD_FILENAME_REGEX) then
74:         qerror('name can only contain numbers, letters, periods, and underscores')
75:     end
76: end

78: local function get_sp_id(opts)
79:     if opts.id then return opts.id end
80:     local sp = dfhack.gui.getSelectedStockpile(true)
81:     if not sp then
82:         qerror('Please select a stockpile or specify the stockpile ID')
83:     end
84:     return sp.id
85: end

87: local included_elements = {containers=1, general=2, categories=4, types=8}
88: 
89: function export_settings(name, opts)
90:     opts = opts or {}
91: 
92:     assert_safe_name(name)
93:     local fname = STOCKPILES_DIR .. '/' .. name
94: 
95:     local includedElements = 0
96:     for _, inc in ipairs(opts.includes or {}) do
97:         includedElements = includedElements | included_elements[inc]
98:     end
99: 
100:     if includedElements == 0 then
101:         for _, v in pairs(included_elements) do includedElements = includedElements | v end
102:     end
103: 
104:     if opts.route_id then
105:         stockpiles_route_export(fname, opts.route_id, opts.stop_id, includedElements)
106:     else
107:         stockpiles_export(fname, get_sp_id(opts), includedElements)
108:     end
109: end
110: 
111: local function normalize_name(name)
112:     local is_library = false
113:     if name:startswith('library/') then
114:         name = name:sub(9)
115:         is_library = true
116:     end
117:     assert_safe_name(name)
118:     if not is_library and dfhack.filesystem.exists(STOCKPILES_DIR .. '/' .. name .. '.dfstock') then
119:         return STOCKPILES_DIR .. '/' .. name
120:     end
121:     return STOCKPILES_LIBRARY_DIR .. '/' .. name
122: end
123: 
124: function import_settings(name, opts)
125:     local fname = normalize_name(name)
126:     opts = opts or {}
127:     local mode = opts.mode or 'set'
128:     local filters = table.concat(opts.filters or {}, ',')
129:     if opts.route_id then
130:         stockpiles_route_import(fname, opts.route_id, opts.stop_id, mode, filters)
131:     else
132:         stockpiles_import(fname, get_sp_id(opts), mode, filters)
133:     end
134: end
135: 
136: local function parse_include(arg)
137:     local includes = argparse.stringList(arg, 'include')
138:     for _, v in ipairs(includes) do
139:         if not included_elements[v] then qerror(('invalid included element: "%s"'):format(v)) end
140:     end
141:     return includes
142: end
143: 
144: local valid_modes = {set=true, enable=true, disable=true}
145: 
146: local function parse_mode(arg)
147:     if not valid_modes[arg] then qerror(('invalid mode: "%s"'):format(arg)) end
148:     return arg
149: end
150: 
151: local function parse_stockpile_opt(id_or_name)
152:     if tonumber(id_or_name) then
153:         return argparse.nonnegativeInt(id_or_name, 'stockpile')
154:     end
155:     for _, sp in ipairs(df.global.world.buildings.other.STOCKPILE) do
156:         if sp.name == id_or_name then
157:             return sp.id
158:         end
159:     end
160:     qerror('could not find stockpile with name: ' .. tostring(id_or_name))
161: end
162: 
163: local function get_route(id_or_name)
164:     local id = tonumber(id_or_name)
165:     if id then
166:         return df.hauling_route.find(id)
167:     end
168:     for _, route in ipairs(df.global.plotinfo.hauling.routes) do
169:         if route.name == id_or_name then
170:             return route
171:         end
172:     end
173:     qerror('could not find route with name: ' .. tostring(id_or_name))
174: end
175: 
176: local function get_stop_id(route, id_or_name)
177:     local id = tonumber(id_or_name)
178:     if tonumber(id_or_name) then
179:         return argparse.nonnegativeInt(id_or_name, 'stop id')
180:     end
181:     if not id_or_name and #route.stops > 0 then
182:         return route.stops[0].id
183:     end
184:     for _, stop in ipairs(route.stops) do
185:         if stop.name == id_or_name then
186:             return stop.id
187:         end
188:     end
189:     qerror('could not find stop with name: ' .. tostring(id_or_name))
190: end
191: 
192: local function parse_route_opt(arg)
193:     local ids = argparse.stringList(arg, 'route') or {}
194:     local route = get_route(ids[1])
195:     return route.id, get_stop_id(route, ids[2])
196: end
197: 
198: local function process_args(opts, args)
199:     if args[1] == 'help' then
200:         opts.help = true
201:         return
202:     end
203: 
204:     opts.includes = {}
205:     opts.mode = 'set'
206:     opts.filters = {}
207: 
208:     return argparse.processArgsGetopt(args, {
209:         {'h', 'help', handler=function() opts.help = true end},
210:         {'m', 'mode', hasArg=true,
211:          handler=function(arg) opts.mode = parse_mode(arg) end},
212:         {'f', 'filter', hasArg=true,
213:          handler=function(arg) opts.filters = argparse.stringList(arg) end},
214:         {'i', 'include', hasArg=true,
215:          handler=function(arg) opts.includes = parse_include(arg) end},
216:         {'s', 'stockpile', hasArg=true,
217:          handler=function(arg) opts.id = parse_stockpile_opt(arg) end},
218:         {'r', 'route', hasArg=true,
219:          handler=function(arg) opts.route_id, opts.stop_id = parse_route_opt(arg) end},
220:     })
221: end
222: 
223: function parse_commandline(args)
224:     local opts = {}
225:     local positionals = process_args(opts, args)
226: 
227:     if opts.help or not positionals then return false end
228: 
229:     local command = table.remove(positionals, 1)
230:     if not command or command == 'status' then
231:         print_status()
232:     elseif command == 'list' then
233:         list_settings_files(positionals)
234:     elseif command == 'export' then
235:         export_settings(positionals[1], opts)
236:     elseif command == 'import' then
237:         import_settings(positionals[1], opts)
238:     else
239:         return false
240:     end
241: 
242:     return true
243: end
244: 
245: -------------------------
246: -- import/export dialogs
247: -------------------------
248: 
249: local function get_import_choices()
250:     local filenames = {}
251:     list_settings_files({}, function(fname) table.insert(filenames, fname) end)
252:     return filenames
253: end
254: 
255: local function do_import(make_opts, title)
256:     local dlg
257:     local function get_dlg() return dlg end
258:     dlg = dialogs.ListBox{
259:         frame_title=title or 'Import/Delete Stockpile Settings',
260:         with_filter=true,
261:         choices=get_import_choices(),
262:         on_select=function(_, choice) import_settings(choice.text, make_opts()) end,
263:         dismiss_on_select2=false,
264:         on_select2=function(_, choice)
265:             if choice.text:startswith('library/') then return end
266:             local fname = ('%s/%s.dfstock'):format(STOCKPILES_DIR, choice.text)
267:             if not dfhack.filesystem.isfile(fname) then return end
268:             dialogs.showYesNoPrompt('Delete saved stockpile settings file?',
269:                 'Are you sure you want to delete "' .. fname .. '"?', nil,
270:                 function()
271:                     print('deleting ' .. fname)
272:                     os.remove(fname)
273:                     local list = get_dlg().subviews.list
274:                     local filter = list:getFilter()
275:                     list:setChoices(get_import_choices(), list:getSelected())
276:                     list:setFilter(filter)
277:                 end)
278:         end,
279:         select2_hint='Delete file',
280:     }:show()
281: end
282: 
283: local function do_export(make_opts, title)
284:     dialogs.InputBox{
285:         frame_title=title or 'Export Stockpile Settings',
286:         text='Please enter a filename',
287:         on_input=function(text) export_settings(text, make_opts()) end,
288:     }:show()
289: end
290: 
291: local function do_stockpile_import()
292:     local sp = dfhack.gui.getSelectedStockpile(true)
293:     do_import(function() return {id=sp and sp.id} end)
294: end
295: 
296: local function do_stockpile_export()
297:     local sp = dfhack.gui.getSelectedStockpile(true)
298:     do_export(function() return {id=sp and sp.id} end)
299: end
300: 
301: local function get_route_target()
302:     local hsc = df.global.game.main_interface.hauling_stop_conditions
303:     return {route_id=hsc.route_id, stop_id=hsc.stop_id}
304: end
305: 
306: local function do_route_import()
307:     do_import(get_route_target, 'Import/Delete Stop Settings')
308: end
309:

311:     do_export(get_route_target, 'Export Stop Settings')

353: --------------------
354: -- MinimizeButton
355: --------------------
356: 
357: MinimizeButton = defclass(MinimizeButton, widgets.Widget)
358: MinimizeButton.ATTRS{
359:     label_unminimized='minimize',
360:     label_minimized='restore',
361:     label_pos='left',
362:     symbol_minimize=string.char(31),
363:     symbol_restore=string.char(30),
364:     get_minimized_fn=DEFAULT_NIL,
365:     on_click=DEFAULT_NIL,
366: }
367: 
368: function MinimizeButton:init()
369:     self.hovered = false
370: 
371:     ensure_key(self, 'frame').h = 1
372: 
373:     local is_hovered = function()
374:         return self.hovered
375:     end
376:     local is_not_hovered = function()
377:         return not self.hovered
378:     end
379:     local get_action_symbol = function()
380:         return self.get_minimized_fn() and self.symbol_minimize or self.symbol_restore
381:     end
382:     local get_label = function()
383:         local label = self.get_minimized_fn() and self.label_minimized or self.label_unminimized
384:         return (' %s '):format(label)
385:     end
386: 
387:     local hovered_text = {'[', {text=get_action_symbol}, ']'}
388:     table.insert(hovered_text, self.label_pos == 'left' and 1 or #hovered_text + 1,
389:             {text=get_label, hpen=dfhack.pen.parse{fg=COLOR_BLACK, bg=COLOR_WHITE}})
390: 
391:     self:addviews{
392:         widgets.Label{
393:             view_id='unhovered_label',
394:             frame={t=0, w=3, h=1},
395:             text={'[', {text=get_action_symbol}, ']'},
396:             text_pen=dfhack.pen.parse{fg=COLOR_BLACK, bg=COLOR_LIGHTRED},
397:             text_hpen=dfhack.pen.parse{fg=COLOR_WHITE, bg=COLOR_RED},
398:             on_click=function()
399:                 self.on_click()
400:                 self:updateLayout()
401:             end,
402:             visible=is_not_hovered,
403:         }, widgets.Label{
404:             view_id='hovered_label',
405:             frame={t=0, h=1},
406:             text=hovered_text,
407:             auto_width=true,
408:             text_pen=dfhack.pen.parse{fg=COLOR_BLACK, bg=COLOR_LIGHTRED},
409:             text_hpen=dfhack.pen.parse{fg=COLOR_WHITE, bg=COLOR_RED},
410:             on_click=function()
411:                 self.on_click()
412:                 self:updateLayout()
413:             end,
414:             visible=is_hovered,
415:         },
416:     }
417: 
418:     if self.label_pos == 'left' then
419:         self.subviews.unhovered_label.frame.r = 0
420:         self.subviews.hovered_label.frame.r = 0
421:     else
422:         self.subviews.unhovered_label.frame.l = 0
423:         self.subviews.hovered_label.frame.l = 0
424:     end
425: end
426: 
427: function MinimizeButton:onRenderFrame()
428:     local prev_hovered = self.hovered
429:     if self.hovered then
430:         self.hovered = self.subviews.hovered_label:getMousePos()
431:     else
432:         self.hovered = self.subviews.unhovered_label:getMousePos()
433:     end
434:     if self.hovered ~= prev_hovered then
435:         self:updateLayout()
436:         df.global.gps.force_full_display_count = 1
437:     end
438: end

562: 
563: function StockpilesOverlay:overlay_onupdate()
564:     -- periodically pick up changes made from other interfaces
565:     self.cur_stockpile = nil
566: end
567: 
568: function StockpilesOverlay:onRenderFrame()
569:     local sp = dfhack.gui.getSelectedStockpile(true)
570:     if sp and self.cur_stockpile ~= sp then
571:         local config = logistics.logistics_getStockpileConfigs(sp.stockpile_number)[1]
572:         self.subviews.melt:setOption(config.melt == 1)
573:         self.subviews.trade:setOption(config.trade == 1)
574:         self.subviews.dump:setOption(config.dump == 1)
575:         self.subviews.forbid:setOption(config.forbid)
576:         self.subviews.train:setOption(config.train == 1)
577:         self.cur_stockpile = sp
578:     end
579: end
580: 
581: function StockpilesOverlay:toggleLogisticsFeature(feature)
582:     self.cur_stockpile = nil
583:     local sp = dfhack.gui.getSelectedStockpile(true)
584:     if not sp then return end
585:     local config = logistics.logistics_getStockpileConfigs(sp.stockpile_number)[1]
586:     -- logical xor
587:     logistics.logistics_setStockpileConfig(config.stockpile_number,
588:             (feature == 'melt') ~= (config.melt == 1), (feature == 'trade') ~= (config.trade == 1),
589:             (feature == 'dump') ~= (config.dump == 1), (feature == 'train') ~= (config.train == 1),
590:             self.subviews.forbid:getOptionValue(), config.melt_masterworks == 1)
591: end
592: 
593: function StockpilesOverlay:on_custom_config(custom)
594:     local sp = dfhack.gui.getSelectedStockpile(true)
595:     if not sp then return end
596:     local config = logistics.logistics_getStockpileConfigs(sp.stockpile_number)[1]
597:     logistics.logistics_setStockpileConfig(config.stockpile_number,
598:             config.melt == 1, config.trade == 1,
599:             config.dump == 1, config.train == 1,
600:             config.forbid, custom.melt_masterworks)
601: end
602: 
603: function StockpilesOverlay:toggleMinimized()
604:     self.minimized = not self.minimized
605:     self.cur_stockpile = nil
606: end
607: 
608: function StockpilesOverlay:onInput(keys)
609:     if keys.CUSTOM_ALT_M then
610:         self:toggleMinimized()
611:         return true
612:     end
613:     return StockpilesOverlay.super.onInput(self, keys)
614: end

SOURCE D:/program/steam/steamapps/common/DFHack/hack/lua/plugins/stocks.lua
1: local _ENV = mkmodule('plugins.stocks')
2: 
3: local gui = require('gui')
4: local overlay = require('plugins.overlay')
5: local widgets = require('gui.widgets')
6: 
7: local stocks = df.global.game.main_interface.stocks
8: 
9: -- these are only safe to call while the stocks page is open; guard against
10: -- stray activations as the page closes
11: local function collapse_all()
12:     if not stocks.open then return end
13:     local num_sections = #stocks.current_type_a_expanded
14:     for idx=0,num_sections-1 do
15:         stocks.current_type_a_expanded[idx] = false
16:     end
17:     stocks.i_height = num_sections * 3
18:     -- the collapsed list is much shorter; reset the scroll position so the
19:     -- view isn't left pointing past the end of the list
20:     stocks.scroll_position_item = 0
21: end
22: 
23: local function expand_all()
24:     if not stocks.open then return end
25:     local num_sections = #stocks.current_type_a_expanded
26:     for idx=0,num_sections-1 do
27:         stocks.current_type_a_expanded[idx] = true
28:     end
29:     local num_items = #stocks.current_type_i_list
30:     stocks.i_height = (num_items + num_sections) * 3
31: end
32: 
33: local function all_collapsed()
34:     if not stocks.open then return true end
35:     for idx=0,#stocks.current_type_a_expanded-1 do
36:         if stocks.current_type_a_expanded[idx] then return false end
37:     end
38:     return true
39: end
40: 
41: local function toggle_all()
42:     if not stocks.open then return end
43:     if all_collapsed() then expand_all() else collapse_all() end
44: end
45: 
46: local function remove_empty()
47:     if not stocks.open then return end
48:     local empties = {}
49:     for itype,v in ipairs(stocks.storeamount) do
50:         if v == 0 and stocks.badamount[itype] == 0 then
51:             empties[itype] = true
52:         end
53:     end
54:     for idx=#stocks.type_list-1,0,-1 do
55:         if empties[stocks.type_list[idx]] then stocks.type_list:erase(idx) end
56:     end
57:     for idx=#stocks.filtered_type_list-1,0,-1 do
58:         if empties[stocks.filtered_type_list[idx]] then stocks.filtered_type_list:erase(idx) end
59:     end
60:     -- removing types shortens the type list; keep the scroll position in bounds
61:     if stocks.scroll_position_type >= #stocks.filtered_type_list then
62:         stocks.scroll_position_type = math.max(0, #stocks.filtered_type_list - 1)
63:     end
64: end
65: 
66: -- -------------------
67: -- StocksOverlay
68: --
69: 
70: StocksOverlay = defclass(StocksOverlay, overlay.OverlayWidget)
71: StocksOverlay.ATTRS{
72:     desc='Adds productivity actions to the stocks page.',
73:     default_pos={x=-3,y=-20},
74:     default_enabled=true,
75:     viewscreens='dwarfmode/Stocks',
76:     frame={w=27, h=7},
77:     frame_style=gui.MEDIUM_FRAME,
78:     frame_background=gui.CLEAR_PEN,
79: }
80: 
81: function StocksOverlay:init()
82:     self:addviews{
83:         widgets.HotkeyLabel{
84:             frame={t=0, l=0},
85:             label=function()
86:                 return all_collapsed() and 'expand all' or 'collapse all'
87:             end,
88:             key='CUSTOM_CTRL_X',
89:             on_activate=toggle_all,
90:         },
91:         widgets.HotkeyLabel{
92:             frame={t=1, l=0},
93:             label='expand all',
94:             key='CUSTOM_CTRL_Z',
95:             on_activate=expand_all,
96:         },
97:         widgets.HotkeyLabel{
98:             frame={t=2, l=0},
99:             label='remove empties',
100:             key='CUSTOM_CTRL_E',
101:             on_activate=remove_empty,
102:         },
103:         widgets.Label{
104:             frame={b=0, l=0},
105:             text = 'Shift+Scroll',
106:             text_pen=COLOR_LIGHTGREEN,
