SOURCE D:/program/steam/steamapps/common/DFHack/hack/lua/plugins/suspendmanager.lua
1: local _ENV = mkmodule('plugins.suspendmanager')
2: 
3: local utils = require('utils')
4: local gui = require('gui')
5: local guidm = require('gui.dwarfmode')
6: local overlay = require('plugins.overlay')
7: local widgets = require('gui.widgets')
8: 
9: local argparse = require('argparse')
10: 
11: --- Loop over all the construction jobs
12: ---@param fn function A function taking a job as argument
13: function foreach_construction_job(fn)
14:     for _, job in utils.listpairs(df.global.world.jobs.list) do
15:         if job.job_type == df.job_type.ConstructBuilding then
16:             fn(job)
17:         end
18:     end
19: end
20: 
21: function isKeptSuspended(job)
22:     return suspendmanager_isKeptSuspended(job)
23: end
24: 
25: function isBuildingPlanJob(job)
26:     return suspendmanager_isBuildingPlanJob(job)
27: end
28: 
29: --- Return the selected construction job
30: local function getSelectedBuildingJob()
31:     -- This is not relying on dfhack.gui.getSelectedJob() because we don't want
32:     -- the job of a selected or followed unit, only of a selected building
33:     local building = dfhack.gui.getSelectedBuilding(true)
34:     if not building then
35:         return nil
36:     end
37: 
38:     -- Find if the building is being constructed
39:     for _, job in ipairs(building.jobs) do
40:         if job.job_type == df.job_type.ConstructBuilding
41:                 or (job.job_type == df.job_type.DestroyBuilding
42:                     and job.flags.suspend) then
43:             return job
44:         end
45:     end
46: 
47:     return nil
48: end
49: 
50: function runOnce(prevent_blocking, quiet, unsuspend_everything)
51:     suspendmanager_runOnce(prevent_blocking, unsuspend_everything)
52:     if (not quiet) then
53:         print(suspendmanager_getStatus())
54:     end
55: end
56: 
57: function unsuspend_command(...)
58:     local quiet, skipblocking, unsuspend_everything = false, false, false
59:     argparse.processArgsGetopt({ ... }, {
60:         { 'q', 'quiet',        handler = function() quiet = true end },
61:         { 's', 'skipblocking', handler = function() skipblocking = true end },
62:         { 'f', 'force', handler = function() unsuspend_everything = true end },
63:     })
64: 
65:     runOnce(not skipblocking, quiet, unsuspend_everything)
66: end
67: 
68: 
69: -- Overlay Widgets
70: StatusOverlay = defclass(StatusOverlay, overlay.OverlayWidget)
71: StatusOverlay.ATTRS{
72:     desc='Adds information to suspended building panels about why it is suspended.',
73:     default_pos={x=-39,y=16},
74:     default_enabled=true,
75:     viewscreens='dwarfmode/ViewSheets/BUILDING',
76:     frame={w=59, h=3},
77:     frame_style=gui.MEDIUM_FRAME,
78:     frame_background=gui.CLEAR_PEN,
79: }
80: 
81: function StatusOverlay:init()
82:     self:addviews{
83:         widgets.Label{
84:             frame={t=0, l=0},
85:             text={
86:                 {text=self:callback('get_status_string')}
87:             }
88:         },
89:     }
90: end
91: 
92: function StatusOverlay:get_status_string()
93:     local job = getSelectedBuildingJob()
94:     if job and job.flags.suspend then
95:         return "Suspended because: " .. suspendmanager_suspensionDescription(job) .. "."
96:     end
97:     return "Not suspended."
98: end
99: 
100: function StatusOverlay:render(dc)
101:     if not isEnabled() then
102:         return
103:     end
104:     local job = getSelectedBuildingJob()
105:     if not job or isBuildingPlanJob(job) then
106:         return
107:     end
108:     StatusOverlay.super.render(self, dc)
109: end
110: 
111: ToggleOverlay = defclass(ToggleOverlay, overlay.OverlayWidget)
112: ToggleOverlay.ATTRS{
113:     desc='Adds a link to suspended building panels for enabling suspendmanager.',
114:     default_pos={x=-57,y=23},
115:     default_enabled=true,
116:     viewscreens='dwarfmode/ViewSheets/BUILDING',
117:     frame={w=40, h=1},
118:     frame_background=gui.CLEAR_PEN,
119: }
120: 
121: function ToggleOverlay:init()
122:     self:addviews{
123:         widgets.ToggleHotkeyLabel{
124:             view_id="enable_toggle",
125:             frame={t=0, l=0, w=34},
126:             label="Suspendmanager is",
127:             key="CUSTOM_CTRL_M",
128:             options={{value=true, label="Enabled"},
129:                      {value=false, label="Disabled"}},
130:             initial_option = isEnabled(),
131:             on_change=function(val) dfhack.run_command{val and "enable" or "disable", "suspendmanager"} end
132:         },
133:     }
134: end
135: 
136: function ToggleOverlay:shouldRender()
137:     local job = getSelectedBuildingJob()
138:     return job and not isBuildingPlanJob(job)
139: end
140: 
141: function ToggleOverlay:render(dc)
142:     if not self:shouldRender() then
143:         return
144:     end
145:     -- Update the option: the "initial_option" value is not up to date since the widget
146:     -- is not reinitialized for overlays
147:     self.subviews.enable_toggle:setOption(isEnabled(), false)
148:     ToggleOverlay.super.render(self, dc)
149: end
150: 
151: function ToggleOverlay:onInput(keys)
152:     if not self:shouldRender() then
153:         return
154:     end
155:     ToggleOverlay.super.onInput(self, keys)
156: end
157: 
158: -- suspend overlay (formerly in unsuspend.lua)
159: 
160: local textures = dfhack.textures.loadTileset(dfhack.getHackPath()..'/data/art/unsuspend.png', 32, 32, true)
161: 
162: local ok, buildingplan = pcall(require, 'plugins.buildingplan')
163: if not ok then
164:     buildingplan = nil
165: end
166: 
167: local function show_suspend_overlay()
168:     return dfhack.screen.inGraphicsMode() or
169:         dfhack.gui.matchFocusString('dwarfmode/Default', dfhack.gui.getDFViewscreen(true))
170: end
171: 
172: SuspendOverlay = defclass(SuspendOverlay, overlay.OverlayWidget)
173: SuspendOverlay.ATTRS{
174:     desc='Annotates suspended buildings with a visible marker.',
175:     viewscreens='dwarfmode',
176:     default_enabled=true,
177:     frame={w=0, h=0},
178:     visible=show_suspend_overlay,
179:     overlay_onupdate_max_freq_seconds=30,
180: }
181: 
182: function SuspendOverlay:init()
183:     self:reset()
184:     -- there can only be one of these widgets allocated at a time, so this is
185:     -- safe
186:     dfhack.onStateChange.unsuspend = function(code)
187:         if code ~= SC_MAP_LOADED then return end
188:         self:reset()
189:     end
190: end
191: 
192: function SuspendOverlay:reset()
193:     -- value of df.global.building_next_id on last scan
194:     self.prev_building_next_id = 0
195: 
196:     -- increments on every refresh so we can clear old data from the map
197:     self.data_version = 0
198: 
199:     -- map of building id -> {suspended=bool, suspend_count=int, version=int}
200:     -- suspended is the job suspension state as of the last refresh
201:     -- if suspend_count > 1 then this is a repeat offender (red 'X')
202:     -- only buildings whose construction is in progress should be in this map.
203:     self.in_progress_buildings = {}
204: 
205:     -- viewport for cached screen_buildings
206:     self.viewport = {}
207: 
208:     -- map of building ids to current visible screen position
209:     self.screen_buildings = {}
210: end
211: 
212: function SuspendOverlay:overlay_onupdate()
213:     local added = false
214:     self.data_version = self.data_version + 1
215:     foreach_construction_job(function(job)
216:         self:update_building(dfhack.job.getHolder(job).id, job)
217:         added = true
218:     end)
219:     self.prev_building_next_id = df.global.building_next_id
220:     if added then
221:         -- invalidate screen_buildings cache
222:         self.viewport = {}
223:     end
224:     -- clear out old data
225:     for bld_id,data in pairs(self.in_progress_buildings) do
226:         if data.version ~= self.data_version then
227:             self.in_progress_buildings[bld_id] = nil
228:         end
229:     end
230: end
231: 
232: function SuspendOverlay:update_building(bld_id, job)
233:     local suspended = job.flags.suspend
234:     local data = self.in_progress_buildings[bld_id]
235:     if not data then
236:         self.in_progress_buildings[bld_id] = {suspended=suspended,
237:                 suspend_count=suspended and 1 or 0, version=self.data_version}
238:     else
239:         if suspended and suspended ~= data.suspended then
240:             data.suspend_count = data.suspend_count + 1
241:         end
242:         data.suspended = suspended
243:         data.version = self.data_version
244:     end
245: end
246: 
247: function SuspendOverlay:process_new_buildings()
248:     local added = false
249:     for bld_id=self.prev_building_next_id,df.global.building_next_id-1 do
250:         local bld = df.building.find(bld_id)
251:         if not bld or #bld.jobs ~= 1 then
252:             goto continue
253:         end
254:         local job = bld.jobs[0]
255:         if job.job_type ~= df.job_type.ConstructBuilding then
256:             goto continue
257:         end
258:         self:update_building(bld_id, job)
259:         added = true
260:         ::continue::
261:     end
262:     self.prev_building_next_id = df.global.building_next_id
263:     if added then
264:         -- invalidate screen_buildings cache
265:         self.viewport = {}
266:     end
267: end
268: 
269: -- returns true if viewport has changed
270: function SuspendOverlay:update_viewport(viewport)
271:     local pviewport = self.viewport
272:     if viewport.z == pviewport.z
273:             and viewport.x1 == pviewport.x1 and viewport.x2 == pviewport.x2
274:             and viewport.y1 == pviewport.y1 and viewport.y2 == pviewport.y2 then
275:         return false
276:     end
277:     self.viewport = viewport
278:     return true
279: end
280: 
281: function SuspendOverlay:refresh_screen_buildings()
282:     local viewport = guidm.Viewport.get()
283:     if not self:update_viewport(viewport) then return end
284:     local screen_buildings, z = {}, viewport.z
285:     for bld_id,data in pairs(self.in_progress_buildings) do
286:         local bld = df.building.find(bld_id)
287:         if bld then
288:             local pos = {x=bld.centerx, y=bld.centery, z=bld.z}
289:             if viewport:isVisible(pos) then
290:                 screen_buildings[bld_id] = viewport:tileToScreen(pos)
291:             end
292:         end
293:     end
294:     self.screen_buildings = screen_buildings
295: end
296: 
297: local tp = function(offset)
298:     return dfhack.textures.getTexposByHandle(textures[offset])
299: end
300: 
301: function SuspendOverlay:render_marker(dc, bld, screen_pos)
302:     if not bld or #bld.jobs ~= 1 then return end
303:     local data = self.in_progress_buildings[bld.id]
304:     if not data then return end
305:     local job = bld.jobs[0]
306:     if job.job_type ~= df.job_type.ConstructBuilding
307:             or not job.flags.suspend then
308:         return
309:     end
310:     local color, ch, texpos = COLOR_YELLOW, 'x', tp(2)
311:     if buildingplan and buildingplan.isPlannedBuilding(bld) then
312:         color, ch, texpos = COLOR_GREEN, 'P', tp(4)
313:     elseif isKeptSuspended(job) then
314:         color, ch, texpos = COLOR_WHITE, 'x', tp(3)
315:     elseif data.suspend_count > 1 then
316:         color, ch, texpos = COLOR_RED, 'X', tp(1)
317:     end
318:     dc:seek(screen_pos.x, screen_pos.y):tile(ch, texpos, color)
319: end
320: 
321: function SuspendOverlay:onRenderFrame(dc)
322:     if not df.global.pause_state and not dfhack.screen.inGraphicsMode() then
323:         return
324:     end
325: 
326:     self:process_new_buildings()
327:     self:refresh_screen_buildings()
328: 
329:     dc:map(true)
330:     for bld_id,screen_pos in pairs(self.screen_buildings) do
331:         self:render_marker(dc, df.building.find(bld_id), screen_pos)
332:     end
333:     dc:map(false)
334: end
335: 
336: -- register widgets
337: OVERLAY_WIDGETS = {
338:     status=StatusOverlay,
339:     toggle=ToggleOverlay,
340:     overlay=SuspendOverlay
341: }
342: 
343: 
344: return _ENV

57: local function sort_noop(a, b)
58:     -- this function is used as a marker and never actually gets called
59:     error('sort_noop should not be called')
60: end
61: 
62: local function sort_by_race_desc(a, b)
63:     if a.data.race == b.data.race then
64:         return a.data.gender < b.data.gender
65:     end
66:     return a.data.race < b.data.race
67: end
68: 
69: local function sort_by_race_asc(a, b)
70:     if a.data.race == b.data.race then
71:         return a.data.gender < b.data.gender
72:     end
73:     return a.data.race > b.data.race
74: end
75: 
76: local function sort_by_dist_desc(a, b)
77:     local adist, bdist = a.data.dist_fn(), b.data.dist_fn()
78:     if adist == bdist then
79:         return sort_by_race_desc(a, b)
80:     end
81:     return adist < bdist
82: end
83: 
84: local function sort_by_dist_asc(a, b)
85:     local adist, bdist = a.data.dist_fn(), b.data.dist_fn()
86:     if adist == bdist then
87:         return sort_by_race_desc(a, b)
88:     end
89:     return adist > bdist
90: end
91: 
92: local function sort_by_name_desc(a, b)
93:     if a.search_key == b.search_key then
94:         return sort_by_race_desc(a, b)
95:     end
96:     return a.search_key < b.search_key
97: end
98: 
99: local function sort_by_name_asc(a, b)
100:     if a.search_key == b.search_key then
101:         return sort_by_race_desc(a, b)
102:     end
103:     return a.search_key > b.search_key
104: end
105: 
106: local function sort_by_gender_desc(a, b)
107:     if a.data.gender == b.data.gender then
108:         local ag = a.data.gelded or false
109:         local bg = b.data.gelded or false
110:         if ag == bg then
111:             return sort_by_race_desc(a, b)
112:         end
113:         return bg
114:     end
115:     return a.data.gender < b.data.gender
116: end
117: 
118: local function sort_by_gender_asc(a, b)
119:     if a.data.gender == b.data.gender then
120:         local ag = a.data.gelded or false
121:         local bg = b.data.gelded or false
122:         if ag == bg then
123:             return sort_by_race_desc(a, b)
124:         end
125:         return ag
126:     end
127:     return a.data.gender > b.data.gender
128: end
129: 
130: local function sort_by_disposition_desc(a, b)
131:     if a.data.disposition == b.data.disposition then
132:         return sort_by_race_desc(a, b)
133:     end
134:     return a.data.disposition < b.data.disposition
135: end
136: 
137: local function sort_by_disposition_asc(a, b)
138:     if a.data.disposition == b.data.disposition then
139:         return sort_by_race_desc(a, b)
140:     end
141:     return a.data.disposition > b.data.disposition
142: end
143: 
144: local function sort_by_status_desc(a, b)
145:     if a.data.status == b.data.status then
146:         return sort_by_race_desc(a, b)
147:     end
148:     return a.data.status < b.data.status
149: end
150: 
151: local function sort_by_status_asc(a, b)
152:     if a.data.status == b.data.status then
153:         return sort_by_race_desc(a, b)
154:     end
155:     return a.data.status > b.data.status
156: end

271:                     label='Max disposition:',
272:                     label_below=true,
273:                     key_back='CUSTOM_SHIFT_E',
274:                     key='CUSTOM_SHIFT_R',
275:                     options=disposition_options,
276:                     initial_option=DISPOSITION.HOSTILE.value,
277:                     on_change=function(val)
278:                         if self.subviews.min_disposition:getOptionValue() > val then
279:                             self.subviews.min_disposition:setOption(val)
280:                         end
281:                         self:refresh_list()
282:                     end,
283:                 },
284:                 widgets.RangeSlider{
285:                     frame={l=0, t=3},
286:                     num_stops=6,
287:                     get_left_idx_fn=function()
288:                         return self.subviews.min_disposition:getOptionValue()
289:                     end,
290:                     get_right_idx_fn=function()
291:                         return self.subviews.max_disposition:getOptionValue()
292:                     end,
293:                     on_left_change=function(idx) self.subviews.min_disposition:setOption(idx, true) end,
294:                     on_right_change=function(idx) self.subviews.max_disposition:setOption(idx, true) end,
295:                 },
296:             },
297:         },
298:         widgets.Panel{
299:             frame={t=3, l=SLIDER_WIDTH+2, r=0, h=5},
300:             subviews={
301:                 widgets.CycleHotkeyLabel{
302:                     view_id='egg',
303:                     frame={l=0, t=0, w=23},
304:                     key_back='CUSTOM_SHIFT_B',
305:                     key='CUSTOM_SHIFT_N',
306:                     label='Egg layers:',
307:                     options={
308:                         {label='Include', value='include', pen=COLOR_GREEN},
309:                         {label='Only', value='only', pen=COLOR_YELLOW},
310:                         {label='Exclude', value='exclude', pen=COLOR_RED},
311:                     },
312:                     initial_option='include',
313:                     on_change=function() self:refresh_list() end,
314:                 },
315:                 widgets.CycleHotkeyLabel{
316:                     view_id='graze',
317:                     frame={l=0, t=2, w=20},
318:                     key_back='CUSTOM_SHIFT_T',
319:                     key='CUSTOM_SHIFT_Y',
320:                     label='Grazers:',
321:                     options={
322:                         {label='Include', value='include', pen=COLOR_GREEN},
323:                         {label='Only', value='only', pen=COLOR_YELLOW},
324:                         {label='Exclude', value='exclude', pen=COLOR_RED},
325:                     },
326:                     initial_option='include',
327:                     on_change=function() self:refresh_list() end,
328:                 },
329:                 widgets.CycleHotkeyLabel{
330:                     view_id='juvenile',
331:                     frame={l=0, t=4, w=22},
332:                     key_back='CUSTOM_SHIFT_U',
333:                     key='CUSTOM_SHIFT_I',
334:                     label='Juveniles:',
335:                     options={
336:                         {label='Include', value='include', pen=COLOR_GREEN},
337:                         {label='Only', value='only', pen=COLOR_YELLOW},
338:                         {label='Exclude', value='exclude', pen=COLOR_RED},
339:                     },
340:                     initial_option='include',
341:                     on_change=function() self:refresh_list() end,
342:                 },
343:             },
344:         },
345:         widgets.Panel{
346:             view_id='list_panel',
347:             frame={t=12, l=0, r=0, b=4+(can_assign_pets and 0 or 1)},
348:             subviews={
349:                 widgets.CycleHotkeyLabel{
350:                     view_id='sort_status',
351:                     frame={t=0, l=0, w=7},
352:                     options={
353:                         {label='status', value=sort_noop},
354:                         {label='status'..CH_DN, value=sort_by_status_desc},
355:                         {label='status'..CH_UP, value=sort_by_status_asc},
356:                     },
357:                     initial_option=sort_by_status_desc,
358:                     option_gap=0,
359:                     on_change=self:callback('refresh_list', 'sort_status'),
360:                 },
361:                 widgets.CycleHotkeyLabel{
362:                     view_id='sort_dist',
363:                     frame={t=0, l=STATUS_COL_WIDTH+2, w=5},
364:                     options={
365:                         {label='dist', value=sort_noop},
366:                         {label='dist'..CH_DN, value=sort_by_dist_desc},
367:                         {label='dist'..CH_UP, value=sort_by_dist_asc},
368:                     },
369:                     option_gap=0,
370:                     on_change=self:callback('refresh_list', 'sort_dist'),
371:                 },
372:                 widgets.CycleHotkeyLabel{
373:                     view_id='sort_disposition',
374:                     frame={t=0, l=STATUS_COL_WIDTH+2+DIST_COL_WIDTH+2, w=12},
375:                     options={
376:                         {label='disposition', value=sort_noop},
377:                         {label='disposition'..CH_DN, value=sort_by_disposition_desc},
378:                         {label='disposition'..CH_UP, value=sort_by_disposition_asc},
379:                     },
380:                     option_gap=0,
381:                     on_change=self:callback('refresh_list', 'sort_disposition'),
382:                 },
383:                 widgets.CycleHotkeyLabel{
384:                     view_id='sort_gender',
385:                     frame={t=0, l=STATUS_COL_WIDTH+2+DIST_COL_WIDTH+2+DISPOSITION_COL_WIDTH+2, w=7},
386:                     options={
387:                         {label='gender', value=sort_noop},
388:                         {label='gender'..CH_DN, value=sort_by_gender_desc},
389:                         {label='gender'..CH_UP, value=sort_by_gender_asc},
390:                     },
391:                     option_gap=0,
392:                     on_change=self:callback('refresh_list', 'sort_gender'),
393:                 },
394:                 widgets.CycleHotkeyLabel{
395:                     view_id='sort_name',
396:                     frame={t=0, l=STATUS_COL_WIDTH+2+DIST_COL_WIDTH+2+DISPOSITION_COL_WIDTH+2+GENDER_COL_WIDTH+2, w=5},
397:                     options={
398:                         {label='name', value=sort_noop},
399:                         {label='name'..CH_DN, value=sort_by_name_desc},
400:                         {label='name'..CH_UP, value=sort_by_name_asc},
401:                     },
402:                     option_gap=0,
403:                     on_change=self:callback('refresh_list', 'sort_name'),
404:                 },
405:                 widgets.CycleHotkeyLabel{
406:                     view_id='sort_race',
407:                     frame={t=0, l=STATUS_COL_WIDTH+2+DIST_COL_WIDTH+2+DISPOSITION_COL_WIDTH+2+GENDER_COL_WIDTH+2+7, w=5},
408:                     options={
409:                         {label='race', value=sort_noop},
410:                         {label='race'..CH_DN, value=sort_by_race_desc},
411:                         {label='race'..CH_UP, value=sort_by_race_asc},
412:                     },
413:                     option_gap=0,
414:                     on_change=self:callback('refresh_list', 'sort_race'),
415:                 },
416:                 widgets.FilteredList{
417:                     view_id='list',
418:                     frame={l=0, t=2, r=0, b=0},
419:                     on_submit=self:callback('toggle_item'),
420:                     on_submit2=self:callback('toggle_range'),
421:                     on_select=self:callback('select_item'),
422:                 },
423:             }
424:         },
425:         widgets.HotkeyLabel{
426:             frame={l=0, b=2+(can_assign_pets and 0 or 1)},
427:             label='Assign all/none',
428:             key='CUSTOM_CTRL_N',
429:             on_activate=self:callback('toggle_visible'),
430:             visible=self.get_multi_select,
431:             auto_width=true,
432:         },
433:         widgets.Label{
434:             frame={l=30, b=2+(can_assign_pets and 0 or 1)},
435:             text={
436:                 {text=self:callback('get_num_assigned_here'), pen=COLOR_YELLOW},
437:                 ' creature(s) assigned here.'
438:             },
439:         },
440:         widgets.WrappedLabel{
441:             frame={b=0, l=0, r=0},
442:             text_to_wrap=function()
443:                 return 'Click to assign/unassign to ' .. self.target_name .. '.' ..
444:                     (self.get_multi_select() and ' Shift click to assign/unassign a range.' or '') ..
445:                     (not can_assign_pets and '\nNote that pets cannot be assigned to cages or restraints.' or '')
446:             end,
447:         },
448:     }
449: 
450:     -- replace the FilteredList's built-in EditField with our own
451:     self.subviews.list.list.frame.t = 0
452:     self.subviews.list.edit.visible = false
453:     self.subviews.list.edit = self.subviews.search
454:     self.subviews.search.on_change = self.subviews.list:callback('onFilterChange')
455: 
456:     self.subviews.list:setChoices(self:get_choices())
457: end
458: 
459: function AssignAnimal:get_num_assigned_here()
460:     local count = 0
461:     for _,choice in ipairs(self.subviews.list:getChoices()) do
462:         if choice.data.status == 1 then
463:             count = count + 1
464:         end
465:     end
466:     return count
467: end
468: 
469: function AssignAnimal:refresh_list(sort_widget, sort_fn)
470:     sort_widget = sort_widget or 'sort'
471:     sort_fn = sort_fn or self.subviews.sort:getOptionValue()
472:     if sort_fn == sort_noop then
473:         self.subviews[sort_widget]:cycle()
474:         return
475:     end
476:     for _,widget_name in ipairs{'sort', 'sort_status', 'sort_dist', 'sort_disposition', 'sort_gender', 'sort_race', 'sort_name'} do
477:         self.subviews[widget_name]:setOption(sort_fn)
478:     end
479:     local list = self.subviews.list
480:     local saved_filter = list:getFilter()
481:     list:setFilter('')
482:     list:setChoices(self:get_choices(), list:getSelected())
483:     list:setFilter(saved_filter)
484: end
485: 
486: function add_words(words, str)
487:     for word in dfhack.toSearchNormalized(str):gmatch("[%w-]+") do
488:         table.insert(words, word:lower())
489:     end
490: end
491: 
492: function make_search_key(desc, race_raw)
493:     local words = {}
494:     add_words(words, desc)
495:     if race_raw then
496:         add_words(words, race_raw.name[0])
497:     end
498:     return table.concat(words, ' ')
499: end
500: 
501: function AssignAnimal:make_choice_text(data)
502:     local gender_ch = CH_NEUTER
503:     if data.gender == df.pronoun_type.she then
504:         gender_ch = CH_FEMALE
505:     elseif data.gender == df.pronoun_type.he then
506:         gender_ch = CH_MALE
507:     end
508:     gender_ch = string.format(data.gelded and 'x%sx' or ' %s ', gender_ch)
509:     return {
510:         {width=STATUS_COL_WIDTH, text=function() return self.status[self.status_revmap[data.status]].label end},
511:         {gap=2, width=DIST_COL_WIDTH, text=data.dist_fn},
512:         {gap=2, width=DISPOSITION_COL_WIDTH, text=function() return DISPOSITION[DISPOSITION_REVMAP[data.disposition]].label end},
513:         {gap=2, width=GENDER_COL_WIDTH, text=gender_ch},
514:         {gap=2, text=data.desc},
515:     }
516: end
517: 
518: local function get_general_ref(unit_or_vermin, ref_type)
519:     local is_unit = df.unit:is_instance(unit_or_vermin)
520:     return dfhack[is_unit and 'units' or 'items'].getGeneralRef(unit_or_vermin, ref_type)
521: end
522: 
523: local function get_cage_ref(unit_or_vermin)
524:     return get_general_ref(unit_or_vermin, df.general_ref_type.CONTAINED_IN_ITEM)
525: end
526: 
527: local function get_built_cage(item_cage)
528:     if not item_cage then return end
529:     local built_cage_ref = dfhack.items.getGeneralRef(item_cage, df.general_ref_type.BUILDING_HOLDER)
530:     if not built_cage_ref then return end
531:     local built_cage = df.building.find(built_cage_ref.building_id)
532:     if not built_cage then return end
533:     if built_cage:getType() == df.building_type.Cage then
534:         return built_cage
535:     end
536: end
537: 
538: local function get_bld_assignments()
539:     local assignments = {}
540:     for _,cage in ipairs(df.global.world.buildings.other.CAGE) do
541:         for _,unit_id in ipairs(cage.assigned_units) do
542:             assignments[unit_id] = cage
543:         end
544:     end
545:     for _,chain in ipairs(df.global.world.buildings.other.CHAIN) do
546:         if chain.assigned then
547:             assignments[chain.assigned.id] = chain
548:         end
549:     end
550:     return assignments
551: end
552: 
553: local function get_unit_disposition(unit)
554:     local disposition = DISPOSITION.NONE
555:     if dfhack.units.isDanger(unit) then
556:         disposition = DISPOSITION.HOSTILE
557:     elseif dfhack.units.isPet(unit) then
558:         disposition = DISPOSITION.PET
559:     elseif dfhack.units.isDomesticated(unit) then
560:         disposition = DISPOSITION.TAME
561:     elseif dfhack.units.isTame(unit) then
562:         disposition = DISPOSITION.TRAINED
563:     elseif dfhack.units.isTamable(unit) then
564:         disposition = DISPOSITION.WILD_TRAINABLE
565:     else
566:         disposition = DISPOSITION.WILD_UNTRAINABLE
567:     end
568:     return disposition.value
569: end
570: 
571: local function get_item_disposition(item)
572:     if dfhack.units.casteFlagSet(item.race, item.caste, df.caste_raw_flags.OPPOSED_TO_LIFE) then
573:         return DISPOSITION.HOSTILE.value
574:     end
575: 
576:     if df.item_petst:is_instance(item) then
577:         if item.owner_id > -1 then
578:             return DISPOSITION.PET.value
579:         end
580:         return DISPOSITION.TAME.value
581:     end
582: 
583:     if dfhack.units.casteFlagSet(item.race, item.caste, df.caste_raw_flags.PET) or
584:         dfhack.units.casteFlagSet(item.race, item.caste, df.caste_raw_flags.PET_EXOTIC)
585:     then
586:         return DISPOSITION.WILD_TRAINABLE.value
587:     end
588:     return DISPOSITION.WILD_UNTRAINABLE.value
589: end
590: 
591: local function is_assignable_unit(unit)
592:     if not dfhack.units.isActive(unit) or dfhack.units.isDead(unit) then
593:         return false
594:     end
595:     local caged = get_cage_ref(unit)
596:     if dfhack.units.isAnimal(unit) then
597:         return (dfhack.units.isOwnCiv(unit) or caged) and
598:             not dfhack.units.isMerchant(unit) and
599:             not dfhack.units.isForest(unit)
600:     end
601:     return caged
602: end
603: 
604: local function is_assignable_item(item)
605:     -- all vermin/small pets are assignable
606:     return true
607: end
608: 
609: local function get_vermin_desc(vermin, raw)
610:     if not raw then return 'Unknown vermin' end
611:     if vermin.stack_size > 1 then
612:         return ('%s [%d]'):format(raw.name[1], vermin.stack_size)
613:     end
614:     return ('%s'):format(raw.name[0])
615: end
616: 
617: local function get_small_pet_desc(raw)
618:     if not raw then return 'Unknown small pet' end
619:     return ('tame %s'):format(raw.name[0])
620: end
621: 
622: function AssignAnimal:cache_choices()
623:     if self.choices then return self.choices end
624: 
625:     local bld_assignments = get_bld_assignments()
626:     local choices = {}
627:     for _, unit in ipairs(df.global.world.units.active) do
628:         if not get_general_ref(unit, df.general_ref_type.BUILDING_CIVZONE_ASSIGNED) then
629:             if not is_assignable_unit(unit) then goto continue end
630:         end
631:         local raw = df.creature_raw.find(unit.race)
632:         local desc = dfhack.units.getReadableName(unit)
633:         if #unit.custom_profession > 0 then
634:             desc = unit.custom_profession .. ', ' .. desc
635:         end
636:         local data = {
637:             unit=unit,
638:             desc=desc,
639:             dist_fn=curry(self.get_distance, xyz2pos(dfhack.units.getPosition(unit))),
640:             gender=unit.sex,
641:             race=raw and raw.creature_id or '',
642:             status=self.get_status(unit, bld_assignments),
643:             disposition=get_unit_disposition(unit),
644:             egg=dfhack.units.isEggLayerRace(unit),
645:             graze=dfhack.units.isGrazer(unit),
646:             juvenile=not dfhack.units.isAdult(unit),
647:             gelded=dfhack.units.isGelded(unit),
648:         }
649:         local choice = {
650:             search_key=make_search_key(data.desc, raw),
651:             data=data,
652:             text=self:make_choice_text(data),
653:         }
654:         table.insert(choices, choice)
655:         ::continue::
656:     end
657:     for _, vermin in ipairs(df.global.world.items.other.VERMIN) do
658:         if not is_assignable_item(vermin) then goto continue end
659:         local raw = df.creature_raw.find(vermin.race)
660:         local data = {
661:             vermin=vermin,
662:             desc=get_vermin_desc(vermin, raw),
663:             dist_fn=curry(self.get_distance, xyz2pos(dfhack.items.getPosition(vermin))),
664:             gender=df.pronoun_type.it,
665:             race=raw and raw.creature_id or '',
666:             status=self.get_status(vermin, bld_assignments),
667:             disposition=get_item_disposition(vermin),
668:         }
669:         local choice = {
670:             search_key=make_search_key(data.desc, raw),
671:             data=data,
672:             text=self:make_choice_text(data),
673:         }
674:         table.insert(choices, choice)
675:         ::continue::
676:     end
677:     for _, small_pet in ipairs(df.global.world.items.other.PET) do
678:         if not is_assignable_item(small_pet) then goto continue end
679:         local raw = df.creature_raw.find(small_pet.race)
680:         local data = {
681:             vermin=small_pet,
682:             desc=get_small_pet_desc(raw),
683:             dist_fn=curry(self.get_distance, xyz2pos(dfhack.items.getPosition(small_pet))),
684:             gender=df.pronoun_type.it,
685:             race=raw and raw.creature_id or '',
686:             status=self.get_status(small_pet, bld_assignments),
687:             disposition=get_item_disposition(small_pet),
688:         }
689:         local choice = {
690:             search_key=make_search_key(data.desc, raw),
691:             data=data,
692:             text=self:make_choice_text(data),
693:         }
694:         table.insert(choices, choice)
695:         ::continue::
696:     end
697: 
698:     self.choices = choices
699:     return choices
700: end
701: 
702: function matches_filter(filter, value)
703:     if filter == 'only' and not value then return false end
704:     if filter == 'exclude' and value then return false end
705:     return true
706: end
707: 
708: function AssignAnimal:get_choices()
709:     local raw_choices = self:cache_choices()
710:     local show_vermin = self.get_allow_vermin()
711:     local min_status = self.subviews.min_status:getOptionValue()
712:     local max_status = self.subviews.max_status:getOptionValue()
713:     local min_disposition = self.subviews.min_disposition:getOptionValue()
714:     local max_disposition = self.subviews.max_disposition:getOptionValue()
715:     local egg = self.subviews.egg:getOptionValue()
716:     local graze = self.subviews.graze:getOptionValue()
717:     local juvenile = self.subviews.juvenile:getOptionValue()
718:     local choices = {}
719:     for _,choice in ipairs(raw_choices) do
720:         local data = choice.data
721:         if not show_vermin and data.vermin then goto continue end
722:         if min_status > data.status then goto continue end
723:         if max_status < data.status then goto continue end
724:         if min_disposition > data.disposition then goto continue end
725:         if max_disposition < data.disposition then goto continue end
726:         if not matches_filter(egg, data.egg) then goto continue end
727:         if not matches_filter(graze, data.graze) then goto continue end
728:         if not matches_filter(juvenile, data.juvenile) then goto continue end
729:         table.insert(choices, choice)
730:         ::continue::
731:     end
732:     table.sort(choices, self.subviews.sort:getOptionValue())
733:     return choices
734: end
735: 
736: local function get_bld_assigned_vec(bld, unit_or_vermin)
737:     if not bld then return end
738:     return df.unit:is_instance(unit_or_vermin) and bld.assigned_units or bld.assigned_items
739: end
740: 
741: local function get_assigned_unit_or_vermin_idx(bld, unit_or_vermin, vec)
742:     vec = vec or get_bld_assigned_vec(bld, unit_or_vermin)
743:     if not vec then return end
744:     for assigned_idx, assigned_id in ipairs(vec) do
745:         if assigned_id == unit_or_vermin.id then
746:             return assigned_idx
747:         end
748:     end
749: end
750: 
751: local function unassign_unit_or_vermin(bld, unit_or_vermin)
752:     if not bld then return end
753:     if df.building_chainst:is_instance(bld) then
754:         if bld.assigned == unit_or_vermin then
755:             bld.assigned = nil
756:         end
757:         return
758:     end
759:     local vec = get_bld_assigned_vec(bld, unit_or_vermin)
760:     local idx = get_assigned_unit_or_vermin_idx(bld, unit_or_vermin, vec)
761:     if vec and idx then
762:         vec:erase(idx)
763:     end
764: end
765: 
766: local function detach_unit_or_vermin(unit_or_vermin, bld_assignments)
767:     for idx = #unit_or_vermin.general_refs-1, 0, -1 do
768:         local ref = unit_or_vermin.general_refs[idx]
769:         if df.general_ref_building_civzone_assignedst:is_instance(ref) then
770:             unassign_unit_or_vermin(df.building.find(ref.building_id), unit_or_vermin)
771:             unit_or_vermin.general_refs:erase(idx)
772:             ref:delete()
773:         elseif df.general_ref_contained_in_itemst:is_instance(ref) then
774:             local built_cage = get_built_cage(df.item.find(ref.item_id))
775:             if built_cage and built_cage:getType() == df.building_type.Cage then
776:                 unassign_unit_or_vermin(built_cage, unit_or_vermin)
777:                 -- unit's general ref will be removed when the unit is released from the cage
778:             end
779:         end
780:     end
781:     bld_assignments = bld_assignments or get_bld_assignments()
782:     if bld_assignments[unit_or_vermin.id] then
783:         unassign_unit_or_vermin(bld_assignments[unit_or_vermin.id], unit_or_vermin)
784:     end
785: end
786: 
787: function AssignAnimal:toggle_item_base(choice, target_value, bld_assignments)
788:     local true_value = self.status[self.status_revmap[1]].value
789: 
790:     if target_value == nil then
791:         target_value = choice.data.status ~= true_value
792:     end
793: 
794:     if target_value and choice.data.status == true_value then
795:         return target_value
796:     end
797:     if not target_value and choice.data.status ~= true_value then
798:         return target_value
799:     end
800: 
801:     if self.initial_min_disposition ~= DISPOSITION.PET.value and
802:         choice.data.disposition == DISPOSITION.PET.value and
803:         choice.data.status ~= true_value
804:     then
805:         return target_value
806:     end
807: 
808:     local unit_or_vermin = choice.data.unit or choice.data.vermin
809:     detach_unit_or_vermin(unit_or_vermin, bld_assignments)
810: 
811:     if target_value then
812:         local displaced_unit = self.attach(unit_or_vermin)
813:         if displaced_unit then
814:             -- assigning a unit to a restraint can unassign a different unit
815:             for _, c in ipairs(self.subviews.list:getChoices()) do
816:                 if c.data.unit == displaced_unit then
817:                     c.data.status = self.get_status(displaced_unit)
818:                 end
819:             end
820:         end
821:     end
822: 
823:     -- don't pass bld_assignments since it may no longer be valid
824:     choice.data.status = self.get_status(unit_or_vermin)
825: 
826:     return target_value
827: end
828: 
829: function AssignAnimal:select_item(idx, choice)
830:     if not dfhack.internal.getModifiers().shift then
831:         self.prev_list_idx = self.subviews.list.list:getSelected()
832:     end
833: end
834: 
835: function AssignAnimal:toggle_item(idx, choice)
836:     self:toggle_item_base(choice)
837: end
838: 
839: function AssignAnimal:toggle_range(idx, choice)
840:     if not self.get_multi_select() then
841:         self:toggle_item(idx, choice)
842:         return
843:     end
844:     if not self.prev_list_idx then
845:         self:toggle_item(idx, choice)
846:         return
847:     end
848:     local choices = self.subviews.list:getVisibleChoices()
849:     local list_idx = self.subviews.list.list:getSelected()
850:     local bld_assignments = get_bld_assignments()
851:     local target_value
852:     for i = list_idx, self.prev_list_idx, list_idx < self.prev_list_idx and 1 or -1 do
853:         target_value = self:toggle_item_base(choices[i], target_value, bld_assignments)
854:     end
855:     self.prev_list_idx = list_idx
856: end
857: 
858: function AssignAnimal:toggle_visible()
859:     local bld_assignments = get_bld_assignments()
860:     local target_value
861:     for _, choice in ipairs(self.subviews.list:getVisibleChoices()) do
862:         target_value = self:toggle_item_base(choice, target_value, bld_assignments)
863:     end
864: end
865: 
866: -- -------------------
867: -- AssignAnimalScreen
868: --
869: 
870: view = view or nil
871: 
872: AssignAnimalScreen = defclass(AssignAnimalScreen, gui.ZScreen)
873: AssignAnimalScreen.ATTRS {
874:     focus_path='zone/assign',
875:     is_valid_ui_state=DEFAULT_NIL,
876:     status=DEFAULT_NIL,
877:     status_revmap=DEFAULT_NIL,
878:     get_status=DEFAULT_NIL,
879:     get_distance=DEFAULT_NIL,
880:     get_allow_vermin=DEFAULT_NIL,
881:     get_multi_select=DEFAULT_NIL,
882:     attach=DEFAULT_NIL,
883:     initial_min_disposition=DEFAULT_NIL,
884:     target_name=DEFAULT_NIL,
885: }
886: 
887: function AssignAnimalScreen:init()
888:     self:addviews{
889:         AssignAnimal{
890:             status=self.status,
891:             status_revmap=self.status_revmap,
892:             get_status=self.get_status,
893:             get_distance=self.get_distance,
894:             get_allow_vermin=self.get_allow_vermin,
895:             get_multi_select=self.get_multi_select,
896:             attach=self.attach,
897:             initial_min_disposition=self.initial_min_disposition,
898:             target_name=self.target_name,
899:         }
900:     }
901: end
902: 
903: function AssignAnimalScreen:onInput(keys)
904:     local handled = AssignAnimalScreen.super.onInput(self, keys)
905:     if not self.is_valid_ui_state() then
906:         if view then
907:             view:dismiss()
908:         end
909:         return
910:     end
911:     if keys._MOUSE_L then
912:         -- if any click is made outside of our window, we need to recheck unit properties
913:         local window = self.subviews[1]
914:         if not window:getMouseFramePos() then
915:             for _, choice in ipairs(self.subviews.list:getChoices()) do
916:                 choice.data.status = self.get_status(choice.data.unit or choice.data.vermin)
917:             end
918:             window:refresh_list()
919:         end
920:     end
921:     return handled
922: end
923: 
924: function AssignAnimalScreen:onRenderFrame()
925:     if view and not self.is_valid_ui_state() then
926:         view:dismiss()
927:     end
928: end
929: 
930: function AssignAnimalScreen:onDismiss()
931:     view = nil
932: end

934: -- -------------------
935: -- PasturePondOverlay
936: --
937: 
938: PasturePondOverlay = defclass(PasturePondOverlay, overlay.OverlayWidget)
939: PasturePondOverlay.ATTRS{
940:     desc='Adds a link to launch the animal assignment UI to pastures and ponds.',
941:     default_pos={x=7,y=13},
942:     default_enabled=true,
943:     viewscreens={'dwarfmode/Zone/Some/Pen', 'dwarfmode/Zone/Some/Pond'},
944:     frame={w=31, h=4},
945: }
946: 
947: local function is_valid_zone()
948:     return df.global.game.main_interface.bottom_mode_selected == df.main_bottom_mode_type.ZONE and
949:         df.global.game.main_interface.civzone.cur_bld and
950:         (df.global.game.main_interface.civzone.cur_bld.type == df.civzone_type.Pen or
951:          df.global.game.main_interface.civzone.cur_bld.type == df.civzone_type.Pond)
952: end
953: 
954: local function is_pit_selected()
955:     return df.global.game.main_interface.bottom_mode_selected == df.main_bottom_mode_type.ZONE and
956:         df.global.game.main_interface.civzone.cur_bld and
957:         df.global.game.main_interface.civzone.cur_bld.type == df.civzone_type.Pond
958: end
959: 
960: local function attach_to_zone(unit_or_vermin)
961:     local zone = df.global.game.main_interface.civzone.cur_bld
962:     local ref = df.new(df.general_ref_building_civzone_assignedst)
963:     ref.building_id = zone.id;
964:     unit_or_vermin.general_refs:insert('#', ref)
965:     local is_unit = df.unit:is_instance(unit_or_vermin)
966:     local vec = is_unit and zone.assigned_units or zone.assigned_items
967:     utils.insert_sorted(vec, unit_or_vermin.id)
968: end
969: 
970: local PASTURE_STATUS = {
971:     NONE={label='Unknown', value=0},
972:     ASSIGNED_HERE={label='Assigned here', value=1},
973:     PASTURED={label='In other pasture', value=2},
974:     PITTED={label='In other pit/pond', value=3},
975:     RESTRAINED={label='On restraint', value=4},
976:     BUILT_CAGE={label='In built cage', value=5},
977:     ITEM_CAGE={label='In loose cage', value=6},
978:     ROAMING={label='Roaming', value=7},
979: }
980: local PASTURE_STATUS_REVMAP = {}
981: for k, v in pairs(PASTURE_STATUS) do
982:     PASTURE_STATUS_REVMAP[v.value] = k
983: end
984: 
985: local function get_zone_status(unit_or_vermin, bld_assignments)
986:     local assigned_zone_ref = get_general_ref(unit_or_vermin, df.general_ref_type.BUILDING_CIVZONE_ASSIGNED)
987:     if assigned_zone_ref then
988:         if df.global.game.main_interface.civzone.cur_bld.id == assigned_zone_ref.building_id then
989:             return PASTURE_STATUS.ASSIGNED_HERE.value
990:         else
991:             local civzone = df.building.find(assigned_zone_ref.building_id)
992:             if not civzone or not df.building_civzonest:is_instance(civzone) then
993:                 return PASTURE_STATUS.NONE.value
994:             elseif civzone.type == df.civzone_type.Pen then
995:                 return PASTURE_STATUS.PASTURED.value
996:             elseif civzone.type == df.civzone_type.Pond then
997:                 return PASTURE_STATUS.PITTED.value
998:             else
999:                 return PASTURE_STATUS.NONE.value
1000:             end
1001:         end
1002:     end
1003:     if get_general_ref(unit_or_vermin, df.general_ref_type.BUILDING_CHAIN) then
1004:         return PASTURE_STATUS.RESTRAINED.value
1005:     end
1006:     local cage_ref = get_cage_ref(unit_or_vermin)
1007:     if cage_ref then
1008:         if get_built_cage(df.item.find(cage_ref.item_id)) then
1009:             return PASTURE_STATUS.BUILT_CAGE.value
1010:         else
1011:             return PASTURE_STATUS.ITEM_CAGE.value
1012:         end
1013:     end
1014:     bld_assignments = bld_assignments or get_bld_assignments()
1015:     if bld_assignments and bld_assignments[unit_or_vermin.id] then
1016:         if df.building_chainst:is_instance(bld_assignments[unit_or_vermin.id]) then
1017:             return PASTURE_STATUS.RESTRAINED.value
1018:         end
1019:         return PASTURE_STATUS.BUILT_CAGE.value
1020:     end
1021:     return PASTURE_STATUS.ROAMING.value
1022: end
1023: 
1024: local function get_zone_distance(pos)
1025:     local zone = df.global.game.main_interface.civzone.cur_bld
1026:     if not zone then return 0 end
1027:     if zone.z == pos.z and dfhack.buildings.containsTile(zone, pos.x, pos.y) then return 0 end
1028:     return math.max(math.abs(zone.centerx - pos.x), math.abs(zone.centery - pos.y)) + math.abs(zone.z - pos.z)
1029: end
1030: 
1031: local function show_pasture_pond_screen()
1032:     return AssignAnimalScreen{
1033:         is_valid_ui_state=is_valid_zone,
1034:         status=PASTURE_STATUS,
1035:         status_revmap=PASTURE_STATUS_REVMAP,
1036:         get_status=get_zone_status,
1037:         get_distance=get_zone_distance,
1038:         get_allow_vermin=is_pit_selected,
1039:         get_multi_select=function() return true end,
1040:         attach=attach_to_zone,
1041:         target_name='pasture/pond/pit',
1042:     }:show()
1043: end
1044: 
1045: function PasturePondOverlay:init()
1046:     self:addviews{
1047:         widgets.TextButton{
1048:             frame={t=0, l=0, w=23, h=1},
1049:             label='DFHack assign',
1050:             key='CUSTOM_CTRL_T',
1051:             on_activate=function() view = view and view:raise() or show_pasture_pond_screen() end,
1052:         },
1053:         widgets.TextButton{
1054:             frame={b=0, l=0, w=28, h=1},
1055:             label='DFHack autobutcher',
1056:             key='CUSTOM_CTRL_B',
1057:             on_activate=function() dfhack.run_script('gui/autobutcher') end,
1058:         },
1059:     }
1060: end

1062: -- -------------------
1063: -- CageChainOverlay
1064: --
1065: 
1066: CageChainOverlay = defclass(CageChainOverlay, overlay.OverlayWidget)
1067: CageChainOverlay.ATTRS{
1068:     desc='Adds a link to launch the animal assignment UI to cages and chains.',
1069:     default_pos={x=-64,y=34},
1070:     version=2,
1071:     default_enabled=true,
1072:     viewscreens={'dwarfmode/ViewSheets/BUILDING/Cage', 'dwarfmode/ViewSheets/BUILDING/Chain'},
1073:     frame={w=23, h=1},
1074:     frame_background=gui.CLEAR_PEN,
1075: }
1076: 
1077: local function is_valid_building()
1078:     local bld = dfhack.gui.getSelectedBuilding(true)
1079:     if not bld or bld:getBuildStage() ~= bld:getMaxBuildStage() then return false end
1080:     local bt = bld:getType()
1081:     if bt ~= df.building_type.Cage and bt ~= df.building_type.Chain then return false end
1082:     for _,zone in ipairs(bld.relations) do
1083:         if zone.type == df.civzone_type.Dungeon then return false end
1084:     end
1085:     return true
1086: end
1087: 
1088: local function is_cage_selected()
1089:     local bld = dfhack.gui.getSelectedBuilding(true)
1090:     return bld and bld:getType() == df.building_type.Cage
1091: end
1092: 
1093: local function attach_to_building(unit_or_vermin)
1094:     local bld = dfhack.gui.getSelectedBuilding(true)
1095:     if not bld then return end
1096:     local is_unit = df.unit:is_instance(unit_or_vermin)
1097:     if is_unit and unit_or_vermin.relationship_ids[df.unit_relationship_type.PetOwner] ~= -1 then
1098:         -- pet owners would just release them
1099:         return
1100:     end
1101:     if bld:getType() == df.building_type.Cage then
1102:         local vec = is_unit and bld.assigned_units or bld.assigned_items
1103:         vec:insert('#', unit_or_vermin.id)
1104:     elseif is_unit and bld:getType() == df.building_type.Chain then
1105:         local prev_unit = bld.assigned
1106:         bld.assigned = unit_or_vermin
1107:         return prev_unit
1108:     end
1109: end
1110: 
1111: local function get_built_chain(unit_or_vermin)
1112:     local built_chain_ref = get_general_ref(unit_or_vermin, df.general_ref_type.BUILDING_CHAIN)
1113:     if not built_chain_ref then return end
1114:     return df.building.find(built_chain_ref.building_id)
1115: end
1116: 
1117: local CAGE_STATUS = {
1118:     NONE={label='Unknown', value=0},
1119:     ASSIGNED_HERE={label='Assigned here', value=1},
1120:     PASTURED={label='In pasture', value=2},
1121:     PITTED={label='In pit/pond', value=3},
1122:     RESTRAINED={label='On restraint', value=4},
1123:     BUILT_CAGE={label='In built cage', value=5},
1124:     ITEM_CAGE={label='In stockpiled cage', value=6},
1125:     ROAMING={label='Roaming', value=7},
1126: }
1127: local CAGE_STATUS_REVMAP = {}
1128: for k, v in pairs(CAGE_STATUS) do
1129:     CAGE_STATUS_REVMAP[v.value] = k
1130: end
1131: 
1132: local function get_cage_status(unit_or_vermin, bld_assignments)
1133:     local bld = dfhack.gui.getSelectedBuilding(true)
1134:     local is_chain = bld and df.building_chainst:is_instance(bld)
1135: 
1136:     if is_chain and bld.assigned == unit_or_vermin then
1137:         return CAGE_STATUS.ASSIGNED_HERE.value
1138:     elseif not is_chain and get_assigned_unit_or_vermin_idx(bld, unit_or_vermin) then
1139:         return CAGE_STATUS.ASSIGNED_HERE.value
1140:     end
1141:     local cage_ref = get_cage_ref(unit_or_vermin)
1142:     if cage_ref then
1143:         if get_built_cage(df.item.find(cage_ref.item_id)) then
1144:             return CAGE_STATUS.BUILT_CAGE.value
1145:         end
1146:         return CAGE_STATUS.ITEM_CAGE.value
1147:     end
1148:     local built_chain = get_built_chain(unit_or_vermin)
1149:     if built_chain then
1150:         return CAGE_STATUS.RESTRAINED.value
1151:     end
1152:     local assigned_zone_ref = get_general_ref(unit_or_vermin, df.general_ref_type.BUILDING_CIVZONE_ASSIGNED)
1153:     if assigned_zone_ref then
1154:         local civzone = df.building.find(assigned_zone_ref.building_id)
1155:         if not civzone or not df.building_civzonest:is_instance(civzone) then
1156:             return CAGE_STATUS.NONE.value
1157:         elseif civzone.type == df.civzone_type.Pen then
1158:             return CAGE_STATUS.PASTURED.value
1159:         elseif civzone.type == df.civzone_type.Pond then
1160:             return CAGE_STATUS.PITTED.value
1161:         else
1162:             return CAGE_STATUS.NONE.value
1163:         end
1164:     end
1165:     bld_assignments = bld_assignments or get_bld_assignments()
1166:     if bld_assignments and bld_assignments[unit_or_vermin.id] then
1167:         if df.building_chainst:is_instance(bld_assignments[unit_or_vermin.id]) then
1168:             return CAGE_STATUS.RESTRAINED.value
1169:         end
1170:         return CAGE_STATUS.BUILT_CAGE.value
1171:     end
1172:     return CAGE_STATUS.ROAMING.value
1173: end
1174: 
1175: local function get_cage_distance(pos)
1176:     local bld = dfhack.gui.getSelectedBuilding(true)
1177:     if not bld then return 0 end
1178:     return math.max(math.abs(bld.centerx - pos.x), math.abs(bld.centery - pos.y)) + math.abs(bld.z - pos.z)
1179: end
1180: 
1181: local function show_cage_chain_screen()
1182:     return AssignAnimalScreen{
1183:         is_valid_ui_state=is_valid_building,
1184:         status=CAGE_STATUS,
1185:         status_revmap=CAGE_STATUS_REVMAP,
1186:         get_status=get_cage_status,
1187:         get_distance=get_cage_distance,
1188:         get_allow_vermin=is_cage_selected,
1189:         get_multi_select=is_cage_selected,
1190:         attach=attach_to_building,
1191:         initial_min_disposition=DISPOSITION.TAME.value,
1192:         target_name='cage/restraint',
1193:     }:show()
1194: end
1195: 
1196: function CageChainOverlay:init()
1197:     self:addviews{
1198:         widgets.TextButton{
1199:             frame={t=0, l=0, r=0, h=1},
1200:             label='DFHack assign',
1201:             key='CUSTOM_CTRL_T',
1202:             visible=is_valid_building,
1203:             on_activate=function() view = view and view:raise() or show_cage_chain_screen() end,
1204:         },
1205:     }
1206: end

1208: -- ---------------------
1209: -- RetireLocationOverlay
1210: --
1211: 
1212: local mi = df.global.game.main_interface
1213: 
1214: local function location_details_is_on_top()
1215:     local vs = dfhack.gui.getDFViewscreen(true)
1216:     return not dfhack.gui.matchFocusString('dwarfmode/NameCreator', vs) and
1217:         not dfhack.gui.matchFocusString('dwarfmode/UnitSelector', vs)
1218: end
1219: 
1220: RetireLocationOverlay = defclass(RetireLocationOverlay, overlay.OverlayWidget)
1221: RetireLocationOverlay.ATTRS{
1222:     desc='Adds a button to retire unneeded locations to the location details screen.',
1223:     default_pos={x=-39,y=6},
1224:     default_enabled=true,
1225:     viewscreens='dwarfmode/LocationDetails',
1226:     frame={w=25, h=3},
1227: }
1228: 
1229: function RetireLocationOverlay:init()
1230:     self:addviews{
1231:         widgets.Panel{
1232:             frame_background=gui.CLEAR_PEN,
1233:             frame_style=gui.FRAME_MEDIUM,
1234:             visible=location_details_is_on_top,
1235:             subviews={
1236:                 widgets.HotkeyLabel{
1237:                     frame={t=0, l=0},
1238:                     label='Retire location',
1239:                     key='CUSTOM_CTRL_D',
1240:                     on_activate=self:callback('confirm_retire'),
1241:                 },
1242:             },
1243:         },
1244:     }
1245: end
1246: 
1247: local function retire(location)
1248:     location.flags.DOES_NOT_EXIST = true
1249:     for idx, loc in ipairs(mi.location_selector.valid_ab) do
1250:         if loc.id == location.id then
1251:             mi.location_selector.valid_ab:erase(idx)
1252:             break
1253:         end
1254:     end
1255:     local occupations = df.global.world.occupations.all
1256:     for idx = #occupations-1,0,-1 do
1257:         local occupation = occupations[idx]
1258:         if occupation.site_id == location.site_id and
1259:             occupation.location_id == location.id
1260:         then
1261:             occupations:erase(idx)
1262:         end
1263:     end
1264:     mi.location_details.open = false
1265: end
1266: 
1267: function RetireLocationOverlay:confirm_retire()
1268:     local details = mi.location_details
1269:     local location = details.selected_ab
1270:     local num_occupations, num_zones = 0, 0
1271:     for _, occupation in ipairs(location.occupations) do
1272:         if occupation.histfig_id ~= -1 then
1273:             num_occupations = num_occupations + 1
1274:         end
1275:     end
1276:     for _, zone_id in ipairs(location.contents.building_ids) do
1277:         -- there can be dangling references in this list; only count
1278:         -- "attached" zones that actually exist
1279:         if df.building.find(zone_id) then
1280:             num_zones = num_zones + 1
1281:         end
1282:     end
1283:     if num_occupations + num_zones > 0 then
1284:         local messages = {'Cannot retire location! Please:', ''}
1285:         if num_occupations > 0 then
1286:             table.insert(messages, ('- unassign %d location occupation(s)'):format(num_occupations))
1287:         end
1288:         if num_zones > 0 then
1289:             table.insert(messages, ('- detach this location from %d zone(s)'):format(num_zones))
1290:         end
1291:         table.insert(messages, '')
1292:         table.insert(messages, 'and try again')
1293:         dialogs.showMessage('Location in use', table.concat(messages, NEWLINE))
1294:         return
1295:     end
1296:     dialogs.showYesNoPrompt('Confirm retire location',
1297:         'Are you sure you want to retire this location?'..NEWLINE..'You won\'t be able to use it again.',
1298:         COLOR_WHITE,
1299:         curry(retire, location))
1300: end
1301: 
1302: -- -------------------
1303: -- AnimalActionsWidget
1304: --
1305: 
1306: local function isFortAnimal(unit)
1307:     return dfhack.units.isFortControlled(unit)
1308:         and dfhack.units.isAlive(unit)
1309:         and dfhack.units.isAnimal(unit)
1310: end
1311: 
1312: local function isCagedWildAnimal(unit)
1313:     return dfhack.units.isTamable(unit)
1314:         and unit.flags1.caged
1315:         and not unit.flags1.tame
1316: end
1317: 
1318: -- Make sure an animal unit of your civ is selected
1319: local function check_valid_unit()
1320:     local unit = dfhack.gui.getSelectedUnit(true)
1321: 
1322:     return unit and (isFortAnimal(unit) or isCagedWildAnimal(unit))
1323: end
1324: 
1325: local function is_geldable()
1326:     local unit = dfhack.gui.getSelectedUnit(true)
1327: 
1328:     return unit and dfhack.units.isGeldable(unit) and dfhack.units.isFortControlled(unit) and not dfhack.units.isGelded(unit)
1329: end
1330: 
1331: local function is_not_pet()
1332:     local unit = dfhack.gui.getSelectedUnit(true)
1333: 
1334:     return not unit or not dfhack.units.isPet(unit) and dfhack.units.isFortControlled(unit)
1335: end
1336: 
1337: local function is_avail_adoption()
1338:     local unit = dfhack.gui.getSelectedUnit(true)
1339:     if not unit then return false end
1340: 
1341:     if dfhack.units.isPet(unit) then return false end
1342: 
1343:     local raw = df.creature_raw.find(unit.race)
1344:     if not raw then return false end
1345: 
1346:     if not dfhack.units.isFortControlled(unit) then return false end
1347: 
1348:     -- cats adopt owners; owners can't adopt cats
1349:     return raw.creature_id ~= 'CAT'
1350: end
1351: 
1352: local function is_tamable()
1353:     local unit = dfhack.gui.getSelectedUnit(true)
1354: 
1355:     return dfhack.units.isTamable(unit) and not dfhack.units.isDomesticated(unit)
1356: end
1357: 
1358: AnimalActionsWidget=defclass(AnimalActionsWidget, overlay.OverlayWidget)
1359: AnimalActionsWidget.ATTRS {
1360:     desc="Add options to tamed animals view sheet.",
1361:     default_pos={x=-41,y=37},
1362:     default_enabled=true,
1363:     viewscreens='dwarfmode/ViewSheets/UNIT/Overview',
1364:     frame={w=25, h=8},
1365: }
1366: 
1367: -- The above function already handles checking if valid unit
1368: -- so just set slaughter flag
1369: function AnimalActionsWidget:set_slaughter_flag(option)
1370:     local unit = dfhack.gui.getSelectedUnit(true)
1371: 
1372:     if not unit then return end
1373: 
1374:     -- emulate vanilla game behavior by only being able to have
1375:     -- 1 set at a time
1376:     if option then
1377:         self:set_geld_flag(false)
1378:         self:set_adoption_flag(false)
1379:     end
1380: 
1381:     unit.flags2.slaughter = option
1382: end
1383: 
1384: -- set units marked for gelding flag
1385: function AnimalActionsWidget:set_geld_flag(option)
1386:     local unit = dfhack.gui.getSelectedUnit(true)
1387: 
1388:     if not unit then return end
1389: 
1390:     if option then
1391:         self:set_slaughter_flag(false)
1392:     end
1393: 
1394:     unit.flags3.marked_for_gelding = option
1395: end
1396: 
1397: -- set available for adoption flag
1398: function AnimalActionsWidget:set_adoption_flag(option)
1399:     local unit = dfhack.gui.getSelectedUnit(true)
1400: 
1401:     if not unit then return end
1402: 
1403:     if option then
1404:         self:set_slaughter_flag(false)
1405:     end
1406: 
1407:     unit.flags3.available_for_adoption = option
1408: end
1409: 
1410: -- Assign any trainer
1411: function AnimalActionsWidget:set_tame(option)
1412:     local unit = dfhack.gui.getSelectedUnit(true)
1413: 
1414:     if not unit then return end
1415: 
1416:     if option then
1417:         dfhack.units.assignTrainer(unit)
1418:     else
1419:         dfhack.units.unassignTrainer(unit)
1420:     end
1421: end
1422: 
1423: -- Use render to set On/Off dynamically for each unit
1424: function AnimalActionsWidget:render(dc)
1425:     local unit = dfhack.gui.getSelectedUnit(true)
1426: 
1427:     if unit then
1428:         self.subviews.butcher_animal:setOption(dfhack.units.isMarkedForSlaughter(unit))
1429:         self.subviews.geld_animal:setOption(dfhack.units.isMarkedForGelding(unit))
1430:         self.subviews.adopt_animal:setOption(dfhack.units.isAvailableForAdoption(unit))
1431:         self.subviews.tame_animal:setOption(dfhack.units.isMarkedForTraining(unit))
1432: 
1433:         local frame = self.subviews.panel.frame
1434:         local t = unit.portrait_texpos > 0 and 2 or 0
1435:         if t ~= frame.t then
1436:             frame.t = t
1437:             frame.b = 2 - t
1438:             self:updateLayout()
1439:         end
1440:     end
1441: 
1442:     AnimalActionsWidget.super.render(self, dc)
1443: end
1444: 
1445: function AnimalActionsWidget:init()
1446:     self:addviews{
1447:         widgets.Panel{
1448:             view_id='panel',
1449:             frame={t=0, b=2},
1450:             frame_background=gui.CLEAR_PEN,
1451:             frame_style=gui.FRAME_MEDIUM,
1452:             visible=check_valid_unit,
1453:             subviews={
1454:                 widgets.ToggleHotkeyLabel{
1455:                     frame={t=0,l=0},
1456:                     label='Butcher    ',
1457:                     key='CUSTOM_CTRL_B',
1458:                     options={
1459:                         {label='No', value=false, pen=COLOR_WHITE},
1460:                         {label='Yes', value=true, pen=COLOR_YELLOW},
1461:                     },
1462:                     view_id='butcher_animal',
1463:                     enabled=is_not_pet,
1464:                     on_change=self:callback('set_slaughter_flag'),
1465:                 },
1466:                 widgets.ToggleHotkeyLabel{
1467:                     frame={t=1,l=0},
1468:                     label='Geld       ',
1469:                     key='CUSTOM_CTRL_G',
1470:                     options={
1471:                         {label='No', value=false, pen=COLOR_WHITE},
1472:                         {label='Yes', value=true, pen=COLOR_YELLOW},
1473:                     },
1474:                     view_id='geld_animal',
1475:                     enabled=is_geldable,
1476:                     on_change=self:callback('set_geld_flag'),
1477:                 },
1478:                 widgets.ToggleHotkeyLabel{
1479:                     frame={t=2,l=0},
1480:                     label='Adopt      ',
1481:                     key='CUSTOM_CTRL_A',
1482:                     options={
1483:                         {label='No', value=false, pen=COLOR_WHITE},
1484:                         {label='Yes', value=true, pen=COLOR_YELLOW},
1485:                     },
1486:                     view_id='adopt_animal',
1487:                     enabled=is_avail_adoption,
1488:                     on_change=self:callback('set_adoption_flag'),
1489:                 },
1490:                 widgets.ToggleHotkeyLabel{
1491:                     frame={t=3,l=0},
1492:                     label='Has Trainer',
1493:                     key='CUSTOM_CTRL_T',
1494:                     options={
1495:                         {label='No', value=false, pen=COLOR_WHITE},
1496:                         {label='Yes', value=true, pen=COLOR_YELLOW},
1497:                     },
1498:                     view_id='tame_animal',
1499:                     enabled=is_tamable,
1500:                     on_change=self:callback('set_tame'),
1501:                 },
1502:             },
1503:         },
1504:     }
1505: end
1506: 
1507: OVERLAY_WIDGETS = {
1508:     pasturepond=PasturePondOverlay,
1509:     cagechain=CageChainOverlay,
1510:     retirelocation=RetireLocationOverlay,
1511:     animal_actions=AnimalActionsWidget,
1512: }
1513: 
1514: return _ENV

CATALOG GEOMETRY AND REACHABILITY SOURCE NOTES
plugin-fields.tsv now retains label_below, option_gap and label_width expressions. Calls retain all arguments including commas within parentheses.
Reachable helper panels: dig WarmDampDigConfigScreen/WarmDampDigConfig via warmdamptoolbar launch; buildingplan FilterSelectionScreen and ItemSelectionScreen via planner/mechanisms actions; stockpiles ConfigModal via ConfigureButton; zone AssignAnimalScreen/AssignAnimal via pasturepond/cagechain assignment actions. Unregistered sort BurrowAssignmentOverlay remains excluded from runtime catalog.

RESOLVED RUNTIME CATALOG COMPOSITIONS
Last used (<material>) uses Last used ({material}) -> 上次使用（{material}）;
fallback Last used (pick manually) remains an explicit constant row.
Order: <job> uses {job} and the existing DFHack job grammar.
Suspended because: <native reason>. uses {suspension}. -> {suspension}。.
The matching reader preserves fixed prefixes/suffixes around grammar tokens.
orders skill labels are enumerated from actual dfhack.dll type attribute entries
(native-skill-rating-captions.tsv) and the source SKIP_RATINGS list:
Dabbling, Novice, Competent, Proficient, Talented, Expert, Accomplished,
Master, Grand Master, Legendary. The corresponding existing Skill rating:
translations are reused. There is no wildcard preserving English skill captions.
Zone direction labels originate from string.char(30)/string.char(31); catalog
sources encode these as \x1e/\x1f through the existing TSV byte decoder, and
targets use UTF-8 ↑/↓. User-defined squad/burrow/role names remain wildcard *.
Planner minimize/restore and stockpiles hover-only minimize/restore captions
are also registered as plain Label caption/option combinations.

HELPER REACHABILITY INDEX
dig.WarmDampDigConfig -> dig.warmdamptoolbar (dig.lua:122-143,259-262)
buildingplan.QualityAndMaterialsPage -> buildingplan.planner -> FilterSelectionScreen (planneroverlay.lua:set_filter; filterselection.lua:545-617)
buildingplan.GlobalSettingsPage -> buildingplan.planner -> FilterSelectionScreen (planneroverlay.lua:set_filter; filterselection.lua:545-617)
buildingplan.ItemSelection -> buildingplan.planner;buildingplan.mechanisms -> ItemSelectionScreen (itemselection.lua:416+)
buildingplan.ItemLine -> buildingplan.planner (planneroverlay.lua:735-747)
buildingplan.QuickFilter -> buildingplan.planner (planneroverlay.lua:996-1023)
buildingplan.InspectorLine -> buildingplan.inspector (inspectoroverlay.lua:79-84)
stockpiles.ConfigModal -> stockpiles.overlay ConfigureButton (stockpiles.lua:551-554)
stockpiles.MinimizeButton -> stockpiles.overlay (stockpiles.lua:544-550)
zone.AssignAnimal -> zone.pasturepond;zone.cagechain -> AssignAnimalScreen (zone.lua:1028-1041,1178-1191)
sort.SortButton -> sort.squad_annotation (sort.lua:759-763)
plugin-fields.tsv state_update_sources also includes local subviews aliases, e.g. GlobalSettingsPage:init_settings getGlobalSettings().blocks/logs/boulders/bars/reconstruct, not only self.subviews syntax.
